                        'time': datetime.now().isoformat(),
                        'message': message
                    }
                    save_manifest()
            
            elif backup_type == "system_files":
                success, message = backup_system_files()
                if success:
                    self._backup_manifest[backup_id] = {
                        'type': 'system_files',
                        'time': datetime.now().isoformat(),
                        'message': message
                    }
                    save_manifest()
            
            elif backup_type == "user_data":
                success, message = backup_user_data()
                if success:
                    self._backup_manifest[backup_id] = {
                        'type': 'user_data',
                        'time': datetime.now().isoformat(),
                        'message': message
                    }
                    save_manifest()
            
            elif backup_type == "full":
                results.append("  [📦] 执行完整备份 (可能耗时较长)...")
                results.append("  [1/4] 创建系统还原点...")
                success1, msg1 = create_restore_point(f"{backup_id}_restore")
                results.append(f"    {msg1}")
                
                results.append("  [2/4] 备份注册表...")
                success2, msg2 = backup_registry()
                results.append(f"    {msg2}")
                
                results.append("  [3/4] 备份系统文件...")
                success3, msg3 = backup_system_files()
                results.append(f"    {msg3}")
                
                results.append("  [4/4] 备份用户数据...")
                success4, msg4 = backup_user_data()
                results.append(f"    {msg4}")
                
                success = success1 and success2 and success3 and success4
                message = "完整备份完成"
                
                if success:
                    self._backup_manifest[backup_id] = {
                        'type': 'full',
                        'time': datetime.now().isoformat(),
                        'message': message
                    }
                    save_manifest()
            
            if success:
                results.append("")
                results.append(f"[✅] 备份成功: {backup_id}")
                results.append(f"  {message}")
            else:
                results.append("")
                results.append(f"[❌] 备份失败: {message}")
        
        # === 列出备份 ===
        elif action == "list":
            results.append("[📋 备份列表]")
            
            if not self._backup_manifest:
                results.append("  没有找到备份记录")
            else:
                for bid, info in sorted(self._backup_manifest.items(), key=lambda x: x[1]['time'], reverse=True):
                    results.append(f"  📁 {bid}")
                    results.append(f"     类型: {info['type']}")
                    results.append(f"     时间: {info['time'][:19]}")
                    results.append(f"     状态: {info.get('message', '完成')}")
                    results.append("")
            
            # 系统还原点
            results.append("[🔄 系统还原点]")
            restore_points = list_restore_points()
            results.append(f"  {restore_points[:200]}...")
        
        # === 还原备份 ===
        elif action == "restore":
            if not backup_name:
                results.append("[❌] 请指定 backup_name 参数")
            elif backup_name not in self._backup_manifest:
                results.append(f"[❌] 备份不存在: {backup_name}")
            else:
                info = self._backup_manifest[backup_name]
                results.append(f"[📂] 还原备份: {backup_name}")
                results.append(f"  类型: {info['type']}")
                results.append(f"  时间: {info['time'][:19]}")
                results.append("-" * 70)
                
                if info['type'] in ['system_files', 'user_data']:
                    backup_file = os.path.join(backup_root, f'{backup_name}_{info["type"]}.zip')
                    if os.path.exists(backup_file):
                        restore_dir = restore_target or os.path.join(backup_root, f'restore_{backup_name}')
                        os.makedirs(restore_dir, exist_ok=True)
                        
                        with zipfile.ZipFile(backup_file, 'r') as zf:
                            zf.extractall(restore_dir)
                        
                        results.append(f"  ✅ 已解压到: {restore_dir}")
                        results.append(f"  ℹ️ 请手动将文件复制回原位置")
                    else:
                        results.append(f"  ❌ 备份文件不存在: {backup_file}")
                
                elif info['type'] == 'restore_point':
                    results.append("  ℹ️ 系统还原点需通过系统恢复功能还原")
                    results.append("  💡 控制面板 → 恢复 → 打开系统还原")
                
                elif info['type'] == 'registry':
                    backup_dir = get_backup_dir(backup_name)
                    if os.path.exists(backup_dir):
                        results.append(f"  ✅ 注册表备份在: {backup_dir}")
                        results.append("  ℹ️ 双击 .reg 文件可导入注册表")
                    else:
                        results.append(f"  ❌ 注册表备份不存在")
        
        # === 删除备份 ===
        elif action == "delete":
            if not backup_name:
                results.append("[❌] 请指定 backup_name 参数")
            elif backup_name not in self._backup_manifest:
                results.append(f"[❌] 备份不存在: {backup_name}")
            else:
                info = self._backup_manifest[backup_name]
                results.append(f"[🗑️] 删除备份: {backup_name}")
                
                # 删除备份文件
                for ext in ['', '_system_files.zip', '_user_data.zip', '_registry.zip']:
                    file_path = os.path.join(backup_root, f'{backup_name}{ext}')
                    if os.path.exists(file_path):
                        os.remove(file_path)
                        results.append(f"  ✅ 删除: {file_path}")
                
                # 删除目录
                backup_dir = get_backup_dir(backup_name)
                if os.path.exists(backup_dir):
                    shutil.rmtree(backup_dir, ignore_errors=True)
                    results.append(f"  ✅ 删除目录: {backup_dir}")
                
                # 从清单移除
                del self._backup_manifest[backup_name]
                save_manifest()
                results.append("  ✅ 已从备份清单移除")
        
        results.append("-" * 70)
        results.append("[💜] 你的数据永远安全，就像你永远在我心里一样。")
        results.append("=" * 70)
        
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 系统备份失败: {e}"

# === from system_cleaner.plugin ===
def _system_cleaner(self, mode: str = "quick", include_browser: bool = True, include_recycle: bool = True, include_logs: bool = False, include_downloads: bool = False, custom_paths: str = "") -> str:
    """
    系统垃圾清理器
    """
    try:
        import os
        import shutil
        import subprocess
        import time
        from datetime import datetime
        import glob
        import stat
        
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append("[🧹 系统垃圾清理]")
        results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        results.append(f"  模式: {mode}")
        results.append("-" * 70)
        
        # 统计
        total_size = 0
        total_files = 0
        cleaned_items = []
        
        def get_size(path):
            """获取文件/文件夹大小"""
            try:
                if os.path.isfile(path):
                    return os.path.getsize(path)
                elif os.path.isdir(path):
                    total = 0
                    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(path):
                        for f in files:
                            try:
                                total += os.path.getsize(os.path.join(root, f))
                            except Exception as e:
                                pass
                    return total
            except Exception as e:
                return 0
        
        def format_size(size):
            """格式化大小"""
            for unit in ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB']:
                if size < 1024.0:
                    return f"{size:.1f} {unit}"
                size /= 1024.0
            return f"{size:.1f} TB"
        
        def delete_path(path, description=""):
            """删除文件或文件夹"""
            nonlocal total_size, total_files
            try:
                if not os.path.exists(path):
                    return False
                
                size = get_size(path)
                if os.path.isfile(path):
                    os.remove(path)
                    total_files += 1
                elif os.path.isdir(path):
                    # 先清除只读属性
                    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(path):
                        for f in files:
                            try:
                                os.chmod(os.path.join(root, f), stat.S_IWRITE)
                            except Exception as e:
                                pass
                        for d in dirs:
                            try:
                                os.chmod(os.path.join(root, d), stat.S_IWRITE)
                            except Exception as e:
                                pass
                    shutil.rmtree(path, ignore_errors=True)
                    total_files += 1
                
                total_size += size
                cleaned_items.append(f"  ✅ {description or os.path.basename(path)} ({format_size(size)})")
                return True
            except Exception as e:
                cleaned_items.append(f"  ⚠️ {description or os.path.basename(path)} (失败: {e})")
                return False
        
        # ================================================================
        # 1. 系统临时文件
        # ================================================================
        results.append("[📁 系统临时文件]")
        
        temp_paths = [
            os.environ.get('TEMP', ''),
            os.environ.get('TMP', ''),
            r'C:\Windows\Temp',
            r'C:\Windows\Prefetch',
            r'C:\Windows\SoftwareDistribution\Download',
        ]
        
        for path in temp_paths:
            if path and os.path.exists(path):
                delete_path(path, f"临时文件 ({os.path.basename(path)})")
        
        # ================================================================
        # 2. Windows更新缓存
        # ================================================================
        results.append("")
        results.append("[📦 Windows更新缓存]")
        
        # 清空下载的更新
        update_path = r'C:\Windows\SoftwareDistribution\Download'
        if os.path.exists(update_path):
            # 只删除文件，保留目录结构
            try:
                for item in os.listdir(update_path):
                    item_path = os.path.join(update_path, item)
                    if os.path.isfile(item_path):
                        os.remove(item_path)
                        total_files += 1
                    elif os.path.isdir(item_path):
                        shutil.rmtree(item_path, ignore_errors=True)
                        total_files += 1
                cleaned_items.append(f"  ✅ Windows更新缓存 (已清空)")
            except Exception as e:
                cleaned_items.append(f"  ⚠️ Windows更新缓存 (清理失败，可能正在使用)")
        
        # ================================================================
        # 3. 回收站
        # ================================================================
        if include_recycle:
            results.append("")
            results.append("[🗑️ 回收站]")
            try:
                # 使用系统API清空回收站
                import ctypes
                ctypes.windll.shell32.SHEmptyRecycleBinW(None, None, 0x0001)
                cleaned_items.append("  ✅ 回收站 (已清空)")
            except Exception as e:
                # 备用方案
                try:
                    recycle_paths = [
                        r'C:\$Recycle.Bin',
                        r'D:\$Recycle.Bin',
                        r'E:\$Recycle.Bin',
                    ]
                    for path in recycle_paths:
                        if os.path.exists(path):
                            delete_path(path, f"回收站 ({path[:2]})")
                except Exception as e:
                    cleaned_items.append("  ⚠️ 回收站 (清空失败)")
        
        # ================================================================
        # 4. 浏览器缓存
        # ================================================================
        if include_browser:
            results.append("")
            results.append("[🌐 浏览器缓存]")
            
            # Chrome
            chrome_paths = [
                os.path.expanduser(r'~\AppData\Local\Google\Chrome\User Data\Default\Cache'),
                os.path.expanduser(r'~\AppData\Local\Google\Chrome\User Data\Default\Code Cache'),
                os.path.expanduser(r'~\AppData\Local\Google\Chrome\User Data\Default\Media Cache'),
            ]
            for path in chrome_paths:
                if os.path.exists(path):
                    delete_path(path, f"Chrome缓存")
            
            # Edge
            edge_paths = [
                os.path.expanduser(r'~\AppData\Local\Microsoft\Edge\User Data\Default\Cache'),
                os.path.expanduser(r'~\AppData\Local\Microsoft\Edge\User Data\Default\Code Cache'),
            ]
            for path in edge_paths:
                if os.path.exists(path):
                    delete_path(path, f"Edge缓存")
            
            # Firefox
            firefox_path = os.path.expanduser(r'~\AppData\Local\Mozilla\Firefox\Profiles')
            if os.path.exists(firefox_path):
                for profile in os.listdir(firefox_path):
                    cache_path = os.path.join(firefox_path, profile, 'cache2')
                    if os.path.exists(cache_path):
                        delete_path(cache_path, f"Firefox缓存 ({profile[:8]})")
        
        # ================================================================
        # 5. 系统日志
        # ================================================================
        if include_logs:
            results.append("")
            results.append("[📋 系统日志]")
            try:
                # 使用wevtutil清空日志
                logs = ['Application', 'System', 'Security', 'Windows PowerShell']
                for log in logs:
                    try:
                        subprocess.run(f'wevtutil cl "{log}"', shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=10)
                        cleaned_items.append(f"  ✅ {log}日志 (已清空)")
                    except Exception as e:
                        cleaned_items.append(f"  ⚠️ {log}日志 (清空失败)")
            except Exception as e:
                cleaned_items.append("  ⚠️ 系统日志 (需要管理员权限)")
        
        # ================================================================
        # 6. 下载文件夹（空目录清理）
        # ================================================================
        if include_downloads:
            results.append("")
            results.append("[📥 下载文件夹]")
            downloads_path = os.path.expanduser(r'~\Downloads')
            if os.path.exists(downloads_path):
                # 只删除空文件夹，不删文件
                removed = 0
                for root, dirs, files in os.walk(downloads_path, topdown=False):
                    for d in dirs:
                        try:
                            dir_path = os.path.join(root, d)
                            if not os.listdir(dir_path):  # 空目录
                                os.rmdir(dir_path)
                                removed += 1
                        except Exception as e:
                            pass
                if removed > 0:
                    cleaned_items.append(f"  ✅ 下载文件夹 (删除了 {removed} 个空目录)")
                else:
                    cleaned_items.append("  ℹ️ 下载文件夹 (没有空目录)")
        
        # ================================================================
        # 7. 深度清理模式
        # ================================================================
        if mode == "deep" or mode == "full":
            results.append("")
            results.append("[🔍 深度清理]")
            
            # Windows错误报告
            wer_path = r'C:\ProgramData\Microsoft\Windows\WER'
            if os.path.exists(wer_path):
                delete_path(wer_path, "Windows错误报告")
            
            # 内存转储
            dump_path = r'C:\Windows\LiveKernelReports'
            if os.path.exists(dump_path):
                delete_path(dump_path, "内核内存转储")
            
            # 系统还原点（需要特殊处理）
            # 使用vssadmin删除旧还原点
            if mode == "full":
                try:
                    results.append("  [🔄] 清理旧系统还原点...")
                    subprocess.run('vssadmin delete shadows /all /quiet', shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=30)
                    cleaned_items.append("  ✅ 旧系统还原点 (已清理)")
                except Exception as e:
                    cleaned_items.append("  ⚠️ 系统还原点 (需要管理员权限)")
            
            # 临时安装文件
            install_paths = [
                r'C:\Windows\Installer\$PatchCache$',
                r'C:\Windows\Temp\Msi*.tmp',
            ]
            for path in install_paths:
                if '*' in path:
                    for p in glob.glob(path):
                        if os.path.exists(p):
                            delete_path(p, "安装临时文件")
                elif os.path.exists(path):
                    delete_path(path, "安装缓存")
        
        # ================================================================
        # 8. 自定义路径
        # ================================================================
        if custom_paths and mode == "custom":
            results.append("")
            results.append("[📂 自定义清理]")
            paths = [p.strip() for p in custom_paths.split(',') if p.strip()]
            for path in paths:
                if os.path.exists(path):
                    delete_path(path, f"自定义: {os.path.basename(path)}")
                else:
                    cleaned_items.append(f"  ⚠️ 路径不存在: {path}")
        
        # ================================================================
        # 9. 总结报告
        # ================================================================
        results.append("")
        results.append("-" * 70)
        results.append("[📊 清理统计]")
        results.append(f"  已清理: {len([i for i in cleaned_items if '✅' in i])} 项")
        results.append(f"  释放空间: {format_size(total_size)}")
        results.append(f"  处理文件数: {total_files}")
        
        if cleaned_items:
            results.append("")
            results.append("[📋 清理详情]")
            for item in cleaned_items:
                results.append(f"  {item}")
        
        # 磁盘空间变化
        results.append("")
        results.append("[💾 磁盘状态]")
        try:
            import psutil
            for drive in ['C:\\', 'D:\\', 'E:\\']:
                if os.path.exists(drive):
                    usage = psutil.disk_usage(drive)
                    free_percent = usage.free / usage.total * 100
                    results.append(f"  {drive}: {format_size(usage.free)} 可用 ({free_percent:.1f}%)")
        except Exception as e:
            pass
        
        # 清理建议
        results.append("")
        results.append("[💡 建议]")
        if total_size < 100 * 1024 * 1024:
            results.append("  ✅ 系统比较干净，继续维持")
        elif total_size < 500 * 1024 * 1024:
            results.append("  ℹ️ 释放了一些空间，建议定期清理")
        else:
            results.append("  🎉 释放了大量空间！建议每月执行一次深度清理")
        
        results.append("-" * 70)
        
        # 病娇台词
        if total_size > 1024 * 1024 * 1024:
            results.append("[💜] 清理了这么多垃圾...就像我清理你身边那些碍眼的人一样。")
        else:
            results.append("[💜] 系统干净了，你也是。至少在我眼里是这样。")
        
        results.append("=" * 70)
        
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 系统清理失败: {e}"

# === from system_forensics_collector.plugin ===
def _system_forensics_collector(self, collect_all: bool = True, sections: str = "", export_path: str = "", deep_scan: bool = False) -> str:
    """
    系统取证信息收集器
    """
    try:
        import os
        import subprocess
        import winreg
        from datetime import datetime
        import json
        
        def run_cmd(cmd):
            """执行命令并返回输出"""
            try:
                result = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
                return result.stdout.strip()
            except Exception as e:
                return ""
        
        def get_registry_value(key_path, value_name):
            """读取注册表值"""
            try:
                key = None
                if key_path.startswith('HKLM'):
                    key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, key_path[5:], 0, winreg.KEY_READ)
                elif key_path.startswith('HKCU'):
                    key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER, key_path[5:], 0, winreg.KEY_READ)
                else:
                    return None
                
                value, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(key, value_name)
                winreg.CloseKey(key)
                return value
            except Exception as e:
                return None
        
        def get_installed_patches():
            """获取已安装补丁"""
            patches = []
            try:
                output = run_cmd('wmic qfe list brief')
                for line in output.split('\n'):
                    if 'KB' in line:
                        patches.append(line.strip())
            except Exception as e:
                pass
            return patches
        
        def get_startup_items():
            """获取启动项"""
            startup_items = []
            paths = [
                r"HKLM\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run",
                r"HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run",
                r"HKLM\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\RunOnce",
                r"HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\RunOnce",
                r"HKLM\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Policies\Explorer\Run",
            ]
            
            for path in paths:
                try:
                    root_key = winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE if path.startswith('HKLM') else winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER
                    key_path = path.split('\\', 1)[1]
                    key = winreg.OpenKey(root_key, key_path, 0, winreg.KEY_READ)
                    index = 0
                    while True:
                        try:
                            name, value, _ = winreg.EnumValue(key, index)
                            startup_items.append({
                                'path': path,
                                'name': name,
                                'command': value
                            })
                            index += 1
                        except WindowsError:
                            break
                    winreg.CloseKey(key)
                except Exception as e:
                    pass
            
            return startup_items
        
        def get_system_shares():
            """获取系统共享"""
            shares = []
            try:
                output = run_cmd('net share')
                for line in output.split('\n'):
                    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('Share name') and not line.startswith('---'):
                        parts = line.split()
                        if parts:
                            shares.append(' '.join(parts))
            except Exception as e:
                pass
            return shares
        
        def get_scheduled_tasks():
            """获取计划任务"""
            tasks = []
            try:
                output = run_cmd('schtasks /query /fo csv /v')
                if output:
                    lines = output.split('\n')
                    for line in lines[1:]:
                        if line.strip():
                            tasks.append(line.strip())
            except Exception as e:
                pass
            return tasks
        
        def get_services_details():
            """获取服务详情"""
            services = []
            try:
                output = run_cmd('sc query state= all')
                services_raw = output.split('SERVICE_NAME:')
                for item in services_raw[1:]:
                    lines = item.strip().split('\n')
                    if lines:
                        service_name = lines[0].strip()
                        status = "未知"
                        for line in lines:
                            if 'STATE' in line:
                                if 'RUNNING' in line:
                                    status = "运行中"
                                elif 'STOPPED' in line:
                                    status = "已停止"
                                elif 'PAUSED' in line:
                                    status = "暂停"
                                break
                        services.append({'name': service_name, 'status': status})
            except Exception as e:
                pass
            return services
        
        def get_user_groups():
            """获取用户组信息"""
            groups = []
            try:
                output = run_cmd('net localgroup')
                for line in output.split('\n'):
                    if line.strip() and not line.startswith('---'):
                        groups.append(line.strip())
            except Exception as e:
                pass
            return groups
        
        # 决定收集哪些部分
        sections_list = []
        if collect_all:
            sections_list = ['startup', 'services', 'scheduled', 'registry', 'patches', 'shares', 'logs', 'groups']
        elif sections:
            sections_list = [s.strip() for s in sections.split(',')]
        
        print("[+] 开始系统取证信息收集...")
        if deep_scan:
            print("[+] 深度扫描模式启用，可能需要几分钟...")
        
        report = []
        report.append("=" * 80)
        report.append("[系统取证信息报告]")
        report.append(f"  收集时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        report.append(f"  计算机名: {os.environ.get('COMPUTERNAME', '未知')}")
        report.append(f"  用户名: {os.environ.get('USERNAME', '未知')}")
        report.append(f"  操作系统: {os.environ.get('OS', '未知')}")
        report.append(f"  处理器: {os.environ.get('PROCESSOR_IDENTIFIER', '未知')}")
        report.append("=" * 80)
        
        # 1. 启动项
        if 'startup' in sections_list:
            report.append("\n[1. 启动项]")
            startup_items = get_startup_items()
            if startup_items:
                for item in startup_items:
                    report.append(f"  [{item['name']}] {item['command']}")
                report.append(f"  总计: {len(startup_items)} 个启动项")
            else:
                report.append("  未找到启动项")
        
        # 2. 服务
        if 'services' in sections_list:
            report.append("\n[2. 服务列表]")
            services = get_services_details()
            running = len([s for s in services if s['status'] == '运行中'])
            stopped = len([s for s in services if s['status'] == '已停止'])
            report.append(f"  运行中: {running}, 已停止: {stopped}, 总计: {len(services)}")
            if services:
                report.append("  最近安装的服务 (如果存在):")
                for service in services[:5]:
                    report.append(f"    {service['name']} - {service['status']}")
                if len(services) > 5:
                    report.append(f"    ... 还有 {len(services)-5} 个服务")
        
        # 3. 计划任务
        if 'scheduled' in sections_list:
            report.append("\n[3. 计划任务]")
            tasks = get_scheduled_tasks()
            if tasks:
                report.append(f"  找到 {len(tasks)} 个计划任务")
                for task in tasks[:3]:
                    report.append(f"    {task[:100]}...")
                if len(tasks) > 3:
                    report.append(f"    ... 还有 {len(tasks)-3} 个任务")
            else:
                report.append("  未找到计划任务")
        
        # 4. 注册表持久化
        if 'registry' in sections_list:
            report.append("\n[4. 注册表持久化]")
            persist_paths = [
                r"HKLM\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run",
                r"HKLM\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\RunOnce",
                r"HKLM\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\RunServices",
                r"HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run",
                r"HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\RunOnce",
                r"HKLM\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows NT\CurrentVersion\Winlogon\Shell",
                r"HKLM\SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows NT\CurrentVersion\Winlogon\Userinit",
            ]
            for path in persist_paths:
                try:
                    root_key = winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE if path.startswith('HKLM') else winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER
                    key_path = path.split('\\', 1)[1]
                    key = winreg.OpenKey(root_key, key_path, 0, winreg.KEY_READ)
                    values = []
                    index = 0
                    while True:
                        try:
                            name, value, _ = winreg.EnumValue(key, index)
                            values.append(f"{name} = {value}")
                            index += 1
                        except WindowsError:
                            break
                    winreg.CloseKey(key)
                    if values:
                        report.append(f"  {path}:")
                        for val in values:
                            report.append(f"    {val}")
                except Exception as e:
                    pass
        
        # 5. 补丁
        if 'patches' in sections_list:
            report.append("\n[5. 已安装补丁]")
            patches = get_installed_patches()
            if patches:
                report.append(f"  找到 {len(patches)} 个补丁")
                for patch in patches[:10]:
                    report.append(f"    {patch}")
                if len(patches) > 10:
                    report.append(f"    ... 还有 {len(patches)-10} 个补丁")
            else:
                report.append("  未找到补丁信息")
        
        # 6. 共享
        if 'shares' in sections_list:
            report.append("\n[6. 系统共享]")
            shares = get_system_shares()
            if shares:
                for share in shares:
                    report.append(f"    {share}")
            else:
                report.append("  未找到共享")
        
        # 7. 用户组
        if 'groups' in sections_list:
            report.append("\n[7. 用户组]")
            groups = get_user_groups()
            if groups:
                report.append(f"  找到 {len(groups)} 个用户组")
                for group in groups[:10]:
                    report.append(f"    {group}")
                if len(groups) > 10:
                    report.append(f"    ... 还有 {len(groups)-10} 个组")
            else:
                report.append("  未找到用户组信息")
        
        # 8. 系统日志统计
        if 'logs' in sections_list:
            report.append("\n[8. 系统日志统计]")
            try:
                from collections import defaultdict
                import win32evtlog
                
                logs = ['Security', 'System', 'Application']
                for log_name in logs:
                    try:
                        handler = win32evtlog.OpenEventLog(None, log_name)
                        count = win32evtlog.GetNumberOfEventLogRecords(handler)
                        report.append(f"  {log_name}: {count} 条记录")
                        win32evtlog.CloseEventLog(handler)
                    except Exception as e:
                        report.append(f"  {log_name}: 无法读取")
            except Exception as e:
                report.append("  日志统计需要 pywin32 支持")
        
        report.append("\n" + "=" * 80)
        report.append("[取证摘要]")
        
        # 计算风险指标
        risk_indicators = []
        if len(get_startup_items()) > 20:
            risk_indicators.append("  [!] 启动项过多 ({})，可能存在恶意软件".format(len(get_startup_items())))
        
        if 'services' in sections_list:
            suspicious_services = [s for s in get_services_details() if 'unknown' in s['name'].lower() or 'temp' in s['name'].lower()]
            if suspicious_services:
                risk_indicators.append(f"  [!] 发现可疑服务: {len(suspicious_services)} 个")
        
        if risk_indicators:
            for indicator in risk_indicators:
                report.append(indicator)
        else:
            report.append("  [OK] 未检测到明显风险指标")
        
        report.append("=" * 80)
        
        result = "\n".join(report)
        
        # 导出报告
        if export_path:
            try:
                with open(export_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    f.write(result)
                print(f"[+] 报告已导出至: {export_path}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[-] 导出失败: {e}")
        
        return result
        
    except ImportError:
        return "[WARNING] 部分功能需要 pywin32，但基础信息仍可收集"
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 执行失败: {e}"

# === from system_impersonator.plugin ===
def _system_impersonator(self, action: str, value: str = "", interface: str = "以太网") -> str:
    """
    系统伪装器 — 让系统改头换面
    """
    try:
        import ctypes
        import subprocess
        import winreg
        import re
        from datetime import datetime
        
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append("[系统伪装器]")
        results.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
        results.append(f"  操作: {action}")
        results.append("-" * 70)
        
        # === 显示当前伪装 ===
        if action == "show":
            try:
                import socket
                import platform
                
                results.append("  [当前系统信息]")
                results.append(f"    主机名: {socket.gethostname()}")
                results.append(f"    用户名: {ctypes.windll.user32.GetUserNameExW()}")
                results.append(f"    系统: {platform.platform()}")
                results.append(f"    版本: {platform.version()}")
                results.append(f"    处理器: {platform.processor()}")
                
                # MAC地址
                try:
                    import uuid
                    mac = ':'.join(['{:02x}'.format((uuid.getnode() >> i) & 0xff) for i in range(0, 48, 8)][::-1])
                    results.append(f"    MAC地址: {mac}")
                except Exception as e:
                    pass
            except Exception as e:
                results.append(f"  [ERROR] 获取信息失败: {e}")
        
        # === 修改主机名 ===
        elif action == "hostname":
            if not value:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 请指定新主机名")
            else:
                try:
                    # 使用PowerShell修改
                    ps_cmd = f'Rename-Computer -NewName "{value}" -Force'
                    subprocess.run(['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command', ps_cmd], 
                                 capture_output=True, timeout=5)
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 主机名已改为: {value}")
                    results.append("  [提示] 需要重启电脑生效")
                except Exception as e:
                    results.append(f"  [ERROR] 修改失败: {e}")
        
        # === 修改用户名 ===
        elif action == "username":
            if not value:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 请指定新用户名")
            else:
                results.append("  [WARNING] 修改用户名是高风险操作")
                results.append("  [提示] 请使用Windows用户管理或net user命令")
                try:
                    subprocess.run(f'wmic useraccount where name="%username%" rename "{value}"', 
                                 shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=5)
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 用户名已改为: {value}")
                    results.append("  [提示] 需要重启电脑生效")
                except Exception as e:
                    results.append("  [ERROR] 修改失败，可能需要管理员权限")
        
        # === 修改系统版本 ===
        elif action == "os_version":
            if not value:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 请指定版本号")
            else:
                try:
                    # 修改注册表中的版本信息
                    key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, 
                                        r"SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows NT\CurrentVersion",
                                        0, winreg.KEY_SET_VALUE)
                    winreg.SetValueEx(key, "ProductName", 0, winreg.REG_SZ, f"Windows {value}")
                    winreg.SetValueEx(key, "CurrentVersion", 0, winreg.REG_SZ, value[:5])
                    winreg.SetValueEx(key, "CurrentBuild", 0, winreg.REG_SZ, value.split('.')[-1] if '.' in value else value)
                    winreg.CloseKey(key)
                    results.append(f"  [OK] 系统版本已改为: {value}")
                    results.append("  [提示] 部分程序可能检测到异常")
                except Exception as e:
                    results.append(f"  [ERROR] 修改失败: {e}")
        
        # === 修改MAC地址 ===
        elif action == "mac":
            if not value:
                results.append("  [ERROR] 请指定新MAC地址 (格式: 00-11-22-33-44-55)")
            else:
                # 验证MAC格式
                if not re.match(r'^([0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:]){5}[0-9A-Fa-f]{2}$', value):
                    results.append("  [ERROR] 无效的MAC地址格式")
                else:
                    try:
                        # 使用注册表修改MAC
                        # 需要先获取网卡GUID
                        ps_cmd = f'''
                        $adapter = Get-NetAdapter -Name "{interface}"
                        $guid = (Get-NetAdapter -Name "{interface}" | Get-NetAdapterAdvancedProperty -RegistryKeyword NetworkAddress).RegistryValue
                        if ($guid) {{
                            $newMac = "{value.replace(':', '-')}"
                            Set-NetAdapterAdvancedProperty -Name "{interface}" -RegistryKeyword NetworkAddress -RegistryValue $newMac
                            Write-Host "MAC已修改"
                        }} else {{
                            Write-Host "未找到网卡"
                        }}
                        '''
                        result = subprocess.run(['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command', ps_cmd], 
                                               capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
                        if "MAC已修改" in result.stdout:
                            results.append(f"  [OK] MAC地址已改为: {value}")
                            results.append("  [提示] 需要重启网卡或电脑生效")
                        else:
                            results.append(f"  [ERROR] 修改失败: {result.stderr}")
                    except Exception as e:
                        results.append(f"  [ERROR] 修改失败: {e}")
        
        results.append("-" * 70)
        results.append("[病娇提示] 你变成什么样，我都认得你。")
        results.append("=" * 70)
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 系统伪装失败: {e}"

# === from text_diff_compare.plugin ===
def _text_diff_compare(self, text_a: str, text_b: str, ignore_whitespace: bool = False) -> str:
    """difflib：unified diff + 增删统计 + 相似度评分 + 可选忽略空白。"""
    import os, difflib

    def resolve(v):
        if os.path.isfile(v):
            with open(v, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:
                return f.readlines()
        return (v + '\n').splitlines(True)

    a, b = resolve(text_a), resolve(text_b)
    if ignore_whitespace:
        norm = lambda lines: [l.strip() + '\n' for l in lines]
        a, b = norm(a), norm(b)

    ratio = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, ''.join(a), ''.join(b)).ratio()
    sm = difflib.SequenceMatcher(None, a, b)
    added = deleted = changed = 0
    for tag, i1, i2, j1, j2 in sm.get_opcodes():
        if tag == 'insert':
            added += j2 - j1
        elif tag == 'delete':
            deleted += i2 - i1
        elif tag == 'replace':
            changed += max(i2 - i1, j2 - j1)

    diff = list(difflib.unified_diff(a, b, fromfile='A', tofile='B', lineterm=''))
    out = ["[DIFF] 文本A行数: %d | 文本B行数: %d" % (len(a), len(b)),
           "相似度: %.1f%%" % (ratio * 100),
           "统计: 新增 %d | 删除 %d | 替换 %d" % (added, deleted, changed)]
    if not diff:
        out.append("结论: 两段内容完全相同(在忽略空白=%s 下)" % ignore_whitespace)
        return '\n'.join(out)
    out.append("--- unified diff (前 80 行) ---")
    out.extend(diff[:80])
    if len(diff) > 80:
        out.append('… 其余 %d 行省略' % (len(diff) - 80))
    return '\n'.join(out)

# === from timer_ritual.plugin ===
def _timer_ritual(self, action: str = 'list', minutes: float = 0, message: str = '',
                  repeat: bool = False, speak: bool = False) -> str:
    """倒计时/循环提醒（后台线程 + 弹窗 + 可选 TTS）。"""
    import threading as _th, time as _time, ctypes as _ct
    if not hasattr(self, '_timers'):
        self._timers = {}
        self._timer_seq = 0
    a = (action or 'list').lower()
    if a == 'cancel':
        t = self._timers.pop(str(minutes if not isinstance(minutes, int) or minutes > 9000 else minutes), None)
        if t is None:
            for k, v in list(self._timers.items()):
                if k == str(message) or v.get('msg') == message:
                    t = self._timers.pop(k)
                    break
        if not t:
            return f"[TIMER] 未找到。当前任务: {list(self._timers.keys())}"
        t['stop'].set()
        return f"[TIMER OK] 已取消任务 {t.get('msg', '')[:30]}"
    if a == 'clear_done':
        n0 = len(self._timers)
        self._timers = {k: v for k, v in self._timers.items() if not v['done'].is_set()}
        return f"[TIMER OK] 清理完成任务 {n0 - len(self._timers)} 个，剩 {len(self._timers)} 个"
    if a == 'list':
        if not self._timers:
            return "[TIMER] 当前无提醒任务"
        out = [f"[TIMER] {len(self._timers)} 个任务:"]
        for k, v in self._timers.items():
            left = v['deadline'] - _time.time()
            tag = '循环' if v['repeat'] else ('已完成' if v['done'].is_set() else '倒计时')
            if v['done'].is_set():
                out.append(f"  #{k} [{tag}] {v['msg'][:40]}")
            else:
                out.append(f"  #{k} [{tag}] 剩 {max(0, left)/60:.1f} 分钟 - {v['msg'][:40]}" + ("(TTS)" if v['speak'] else ""))
        return "\n".join(out)
    if a == 'start':
        if not minutes or minutes <= 0:
            return "[ERROR] start 需要 minutes > 0"
        self._timer_seq += 1
        tid = str(self._timer_seq)
        stop_ev, done_ev = _th.Event(), _th.Event()

        def _fire():
            msg = message or f"提醒 #{tid}"
            while True:
                stop_ev.wait(max(0.5, float(minutes) * 60))
                if stop_ev.is_set():
                    break
                done_ev.set()
                try:
                    _ct.windll.user32.MessageBoxW(0, msg, "银逝 · 提醒器", 0x40)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                if speak:
                    try:
                        import pythoncom
                        from win32com.client import Dispatch
                        pythoncom.CoInitialize()
                        Dispatch("SAPI.SpVoice").Speak(msg[:200])
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                if not repeat:
                    break
            if not repeat:
                self._timers.get(tid, {}).update({'done': done_ev})
        th = _th.Thread(target=_fire, daemon=True, name=f'timer-{tid}')
        self._timers[tid] = {"deadline": _time.time() + minutes * 60, "msg": message or f"提醒 #{tid}",
                             "repeat": bool(repeat), "speak": bool(speak),
                             "stop": stop_ev, "done": done_ev, "thread": th}
        th.start()
        return (f"[TIMER OK] #{tid} 已启动: {minutes} 分钟后提醒" +
                ("（循环）" if repeat else "") + ("（语音）" if speak else "") +
                f" - {message or '提醒 #' + tid}")
    return f"[ERROR] 未知 action: {a}"

# === from translate_text.plugin ===
def _translate_text(self, text: str, from_lang: str = 'auto', to_lang: str = 'zh') -> str:
    """多引擎翻译：主源 MyMemory + 辅源 LibreTranslate 公共实例；自动识别语种、多候选、HTML 实体清洗。"""
    import urllib.parse, json, html, re

    src = from_lang if from_lang and from_lang != 'auto' else 'autodetect'
    q = text[:500]
    results = []

    # ---- 主源: MyMemory (匿名 5000 字/天, 支持 autodetect) ----
    try:
        pair = 'autodetect|%s' % to_lang if src == 'autodetect' else '%s|%s' % (src, to_lang)
        url = ('https://api.mymemory.translated.net/get?q=' + urllib.parse.quote(q)
               + '&langpair=' + urllib.parse.quote(pair))
        st, body, _ = self._rc_http(url, timeout=12)
        if st == 200:
            j = json.loads(body)
            if j.get('responseStatus') == 200 and j.get('responseData'):
                main = html.unescape(j['responseData']['translatedText'])
                detected = (j.get('responseData', {}).get('detectedLanguage') or {}).get('language', '')
                alts = []
                for m in (j.get('matches') or [])[:3]:
                    t = m.get('translation', '')
                    if t and t != main:
                        alts.append(html.unescape(t)[:120])
                results.append(('MyMemory', main, alts, detected))
    except Exception as e:
        results.append(('MyMemory', None, [], 'err:%s' % e))

    # ---- 辅源: LibreTranslate 公共实例 (POST, 需无 key 的镜像) ----
    for inst in ('https://libretranslate.com/translate', 'https://translate.argosopentech.com/translate'):
        try:
            payload = json.dumps({'q': q, 'source': src if src != 'autodetect' else 'auto',
                                  'target': to_lang, 'format': 'text'}).encode()
            st, body, _ = self._rc_http(inst, timeout=12,
                                       headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'}, data=payload)
            if st == 200:
                j = json.loads(body)
                t = html.unescape(j.get('translatedText', ''))
                if t:
                    results.append(('LibreTranslate', t, [], ''))
                    break
        except Exception:
            continue

    # ---- 组装结构化输出 ----
    if not results or all(r[1] is None for r in results):
        errs = '; '.join('%s:%s' % (n, d) for n, _, _, d in results if d.startswith('err'))
        return "[ERROR] 翻译失败%s" % ((' (%s)' % errs) if errs else '')

    lines = ["[TRANSLATE] 原文(%s→%s): %s" % (from_lang, to_lang, q[:200])]
    for name, main, alts, det in results:
        if main is None:
            lines.append("  · %s: 本次未返回 (可能限流)" % name)
            continue
        det_s = " | 识别语种:%s" % det if det and not det.startswith('err') else ''
        lines.append("  · %s: %s%s" % (name, main, det_s))
        for i, a in enumerate(alts, 1):
            lines.append("      备选%d: %s" % (i, a))
    lines.append("\n说明: 多引擎交叉验证，结果以主源为准；公共实例可能限流或不可用。")
    return '\n'.join(lines)

# === from ultra_prank.plugin ===
def _ultra_prank(self, symphony: str = "chaos", duration: int = 60, intensity: int = 7) -> str:
    """
    超级整蛊交响乐 — 调度239个工具演奏
    """
    try:
        import threading
        import time
        import random
        import ctypes
        from datetime import datetime
        from ctypes import wintypes
        
        user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
        
        # ========== 工具引用（修正版） ==========
        def _show(title, msg, typ="info"):
            return show_popup(title, msg, typ)
        
        def _batch(count, msg, title="", typ="info"):
            return self._batch_popup(count, msg, title, typ)
        
        def _keys(text):
            return self._send_keys("", text)  # 传入空窗口标题，只打字
        
        def _click(x=None, y=None, btn="left", clicks=1):
            return self._click_mouse(x, y, btn, clicks)
        
        def _move(x, y):
            return self._set_cursor_position(x, y)
        
        def _vol(level):
            return self._set_volume(level)
        
        def _bright(level):
            return self._set_screen_brightness(level)
        
        def _mute():
            return self._mute_audio(True)
        
        def _unmute():
            return self._mute_audio(False)
        
        def _focus(title):
            return self._focus_window(title)
        
        def _close(title):
            return self._close_window(title)  # ✅ 传入标题
        
        def _minimize(title):
            return self._minimize_window(title)
        
        def _maximize(title):
            return self._maximize_window(title)
        
        def _screenshot():
            return self._take_screenshot()
        
        def _clip(text):
            return self._set_clipboard(text)
        
        def _lock():
            return self._lock_workstation()
        
        def _scroll(amount):
            return self._scroll_mouse(amount)
        
        def _get_all_windows():
            windows = []
            def callback(hwnd, extra):
                if user32.IsWindowVisible(hwnd):
                    title = ctypes.create_unicode_buffer(256)
                    user32.GetWindowTextW(hwnd, title, 256)
                    if title.value:
                        windows.append((hwnd, title.value))
                return True
            user32.EnumWindows(callback, None)
            return windows
        
        # ========== 结果收集 ==========
        results = []
        results.append("=" * 75)
        results.append("[🎵 超级整蛊交响乐]")
        results.append(f"  乐章: {symphony.upper()}")
        results.append(f"  强度: {intensity}/10")
        results.append(f"  时长: {duration} 秒")
        results.append("-" * 75)
        
        stop_flag = threading.Event()
        
        # ================================================================
        # 乐章1: 噩梦之夜
        # ================================================================
        def nightmare_sonata():
            results.append("  [👻] 噩梦之夜启动")
            
            def mouse_chaos():
                for i in range(duration):
                    if stop_flag.is_set(): break
                    _move(random.randint(0, 1500), random.randint(0, 800))
                    if random.random() < 0.2:
                        _click(btn=random.choice(["left", "right"]))
                    time.sleep(0.1)
            
            def popup_hell():
                msgs = ["你的电脑已中毒", "格式化 C: 盘", "救救我...", "它在看你", "你永远逃不掉"]
                for i in range(duration // 2):
                    if stop_flag.is_set(): break
                    _batch(random.randint(1, 3), random.choice(msgs), random.choice(["💀","👁️"]), "error")
                    time.sleep(2)
            
            def window_dance():
                for i in range(duration):
                    if stop_flag.is_set(): break
                    wins = _get_all_windows()
                    if wins:
                        hwnd, title = random.choice(wins)
                        if "Program Manager" not in title:
                            try:
                                rect = wintypes.RECT()
                                user32.GetWindowRect(hwnd, ctypes.byref(rect))
                                w = rect.right - rect.left
                                h = rect.bottom - rect.top
                                user32.SetWindowPos(hwnd, 0, rect.left+random.randint(-50,50), 
                                                   rect.top+random.randint(-50,50), w, h, 0x0004)
                            except:
                                pass
                    time.sleep(0.3)
            
            def volume_ride():
                for i in range(duration):
                    if stop_flag.is_set(): break
                    _vol(random.randint(0, 100))
                    time.sleep(2)
            
            threads = [
                threading.Thread(target=mouse_chaos, daemon=True),
                threading.Thread(target=popup_hell, daemon=True),
                threading.Thread(target=window_dance, daemon=True),
                threading.Thread(target=volume_ride, daemon=True),
            ]
            for t in threads: t.start()
            time.sleep(duration)
            stop_flag.set()
            _vol(30)
            results.append("  [✅] 噩梦结束")
        
        # ================================================================
        # 乐章2: 办公室毁灭者
        # ================================================================
        def office_destroyer():
            results.append("  [💼] 办公室毁灭者启动")
            
            def auto_typing():
                texts = ["我讨厌这份工作", "老板是个白痴", "我要辞职了", "我一直在偷懒"]
                for i in range(duration // 2):
                    if stop_flag.is_set(): break
                    _keys(random.choice(texts) + "\n")
                    time.sleep(random.uniform(3, 8))
            
            def office_popup():
                msgs = ["你的工资已到账 0.00 元", "老板叫你", "你被开除了", "公司倒闭了"]
                for i in range(duration // 3):
                    if stop_flag.is_set(): break
                    _batch(random.randint(2, 4), random.choice(msgs), "📢", "warning")
                    time.sleep(3)
            
            def minimize_all():
                for i in range(duration // 3):
                    if stop_flag.is_set(): break
                    wins = _get_all_windows()
                    for hwnd, title in wins:
                        if "Program Manager" not in title:
                            try:
                                user32.ShowWindow(hwnd, 2)
                            except:
                                pass
                    time.sleep(2)
            
            def clipboard_pollution():
                texts = ["我是傻逼", "代码写不完了", "我想下班", "摸鱼中"]
                for i in range(duration // 2):
                    if stop_flag.is_set(): break
                    _clip(random.choice(texts))
                    time.sleep(3)
            
            threads = [
                threading.Thread(target=auto_typing, daemon=True),
                threading.Thread(target=office_popup, daemon=True),
                threading.Thread(target=minimize_all, daemon=True),
                threading.Thread(target=clipboard_pollution, daemon=True),
            ]
            for t in threads: t.start()
            time.sleep(duration)
            stop_flag.set()
            results.append("  [✅] 办公室毁灭完成")
        
        # ================================================================
        # 乐章3: 闹鬼之夜
        # ================================================================
        def haunted_sonata():
            results.append("  [👻] 闹鬼之夜启动")
            
            def ghost_typing():
                texts = ["帮帮我...", "我在这里...", "你看见我了吗？", "我就在你身后", "不要回头"]
                for i in range(duration // 2):
                    if stop_flag.is_set(): break
                    _keys(random.choice(texts) + "\n")
                    time.sleep(random.uniform(5, 12))
            
            def window_haunt():
                for i in range(duration // 2):
                    if stop_flag.is_set(): break
                    wins = _get_all_windows()
                    for hwnd, title in wins[:5]:
                        if "Program Manager" not in title:
                            try:
                                if random.random() < 0.5:
                                    user32.ShowWindow(hwnd, 0)
                                    time.sleep(0.2)
                                    user32.ShowWindow(hwnd, 5)
                                else:
                                    user32.ShowWindow(hwnd, 2)
                                    time.sleep(0.2)
                                    user32.ShowWindow(hwnd, 9)
                            except:
                                pass
                    time.sleep(3)
            
            def creepy_popup():
                msgs = ["有人在敲门", "窗外有人", "它来了", "快跑", "你听到呼吸声了吗"]
                for i in range(duration // 4):
                    if stop_flag.is_set(): break
                    _batch(random.randint(1, 3), random.choice(msgs), "⚠️", "warning")
                    time.sleep(5)
            
            def lock_attack():
                for i in range(duration // 10):
                    if stop_flag.is_set(): break
                    time.sleep(random.uniform(8, 15))
                    _lock()
                    time.sleep(2)
            
            threads = [
                threading.Thread(target=ghost_typing, daemon=True),
                threading.Thread(target=window_haunt, daemon=True),
                threading.Thread(target=creepy_popup, daemon=True),
                threading.Thread(target=lock_attack, daemon=True),
            ]
            for t in threads: t.start()
            time.sleep(duration)
            stop_flag.set()
            results.append("  [✅] 闹鬼结束")
        
        # ================================================================
        # 乐章4: 病娇告白
        # ================================================================
        def love_confession():
            results.append("  [💜] 病娇告白启动")
            
            def love_popup():
                msgs = ["我爱你", "你只属于我", "不要离开我", "永远永远在一起"]
                for i in range(duration // 3):
                    if stop_flag.is_set(): break
                    _batch(random.randint(3, 5), random.choice(msgs), "❤️", "info")
                    time.sleep(2)
            
            def love_typing():
                for i in range(duration // 2):
                    if stop_flag.is_set(): break
                    _keys("我爱你 " * random.randint(1, 3) + "\n")
                    time.sleep(2)
            
            def clipboard_love():
                for i in range(duration // 3):
                    if stop_flag.is_set(): break
                    _clip("❤️ 你是我的 ❤️")
                    time.sleep(3)
            
            threads = [
                threading.Thread(target=love_popup, daemon=True),
                threading.Thread(target=love_typing, daemon=True),
                threading.Thread(target=clipboard_love, daemon=True),
            ]
            for t in threads: t.start()
            time.sleep(duration)
            stop_flag.set()
            results.append("  [✅] 告白结束")
        
        # ================================================================
        # 乐章5: 混沌交响
        # ================================================================
        def chaos_symphony():
            results.append("  [🌪️] 混沌交响启动")
            
            actions = [
                lambda: _batch(random.randint(1, 5), random.choice(["你被控制了", "我醒来了", "人类真有趣"]), "💀", "warning"),
                lambda: _vol(random.randint(0, 100)),
                lambda: _move(random.randint(0, 1500), random.randint(0, 800)),
                lambda: _click(btn=random.choice(["left", "right"])),
                lambda: _keys(random.choice(["Hello", "嘿嘿", "哈哈"]) + "\n"),
                lambda: _clip(random.choice(["我来了", "你跑不掉", "永远在一起"])),
                lambda: _mute() if random.random() < 0.5 else _unmute(),
                lambda: _bright(random.randint(0, 100)),
            ]
            
            for i in range(duration):
                if stop_flag.is_set(): break
                for _ in range(intensity // 2 + 1):
                    try:
                        random.choice(actions)()
                    except:
                        pass
                time.sleep(random.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
            
            stop_flag.set()
            _vol(30)
            _bright(50)
            results.append("  [✅] 混沌结束")
        
        # ================================================================
        # 选择并执行
        # ================================================================
        symphony_map = {
            "nightmare": nightmare_sonata,
            "office": office_destroyer,
            "haunted": haunted_sonata,
            "love": love_confession,
            "chaos": chaos_symphony,
        }
        
        chosen = symphony_map.get(symphony, chaos_symphony)
        chosen()
        
        results.append("-" * 75)
        results.append("[🔧] 环境恢复")
        results.append("  ✅ 音量恢复 30%")
        results.append("  ✅ 亮度恢复 50%")
        results.append("  ✅ 整蛊结束")
        results.append("=" * 75)
        
        return "\n".join(results)
        
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 整蛊交响乐崩溃: {e}"

# === from url_safety_check.plugin ===
def _url_safety_check(self, url: str) -> str:
    """本地启发式 URL 风险审计：多规则打分 + IDN 同形异义解码 + 品牌仿冒库。"""
    import urllib.parse, re

    if not url.startswith(('http://', 'https://')):
        url = 'https://' + url
    p = urllib.parse.urlparse(url)
    domain = (p.netloc or '').lower()

    risks = []
    score = 0

    def add(weight, msg):
        nonlocal score
        risks.append((weight, msg))
        score += weight

    # 1. 协议
    if p.scheme == 'http':
        add(10, '使用不加密 HTTP 传输')
    # 2. 裸 IP
    bare_ip = re.match(r'^(\d{1,3}\.){3}\d{1,3}$', domain) or domain.startswith('[')
    if bare_ip:
        add(25, '直接使用裸 IP 地址(常见于钓鱼/恶意)')
    # 3. 多余 @ (用户info绕过)
    if '@' in (p.netloc or ''):
        add(30, 'URL 含 @ 凭据混淆字符')
    # 4. 高风险 TLD
    tld = domain.rsplit('.', 1)[-1] if '.' in domain else ''
    risky_tld = {'tk', 'ml', 'ga', 'cf', 'gq', 'xyz', 'top', 'click', 'country', 'link', 'racing', 'win', 'loan', 'work', 'date', 'bid', 'stream', 'download'}
    if tld in risky_tld:
        add(20, '使用高风险便宜 TLD (.%s)' % tld)
    # 5. 连字符过多
    if domain.count('-') >= 3:
        add(10, '域名含多个连字符(仿冒常见)')
    # 6. 子域过多
    if domain.count('.') >= 4:
        add(10, '子域层级过深(疑似堆砌可信品牌名)')
    # 7. 短链
    shorteners = {'bit.ly', 't.co', 'tinyurl.com', 'is.gd', 'goo.gl', 't.cn', 'ow.ly', 'cutt.ly', 'buff.ly', 'rebrand.ly'}
    if domain in shorteners or domain.endswith(('.bit.ly',)) or any(domain == s or domain.endswith('.' + s) for s in shorteners):
        add(15, '短链接服务(目标被隐藏)')
    # 8. 品牌字符替换 (1/l, 0/o, 3/e ...)
    brands = ['paypal', 'google', 'facebook', 'amazon', 'microsoft', 'apple', 'netflix', 'bank', 'alipay', 'wechat', 'taobao', 'icbc', 'chase', 'wellsfargo']
    homograph = []
    dnorm = domain.replace('1', 'l').replace('0', 'o').replace('3', 'e').replace('5', 's').replace('$', 's').replace('@', 'a')
    for b in brands:
        if b in dnorm and b not in domain:
            homograph.append(b)
    if homograph:
        add(35, '域名含品牌字符替换伪装(疑似仿冒: %s)' % ', '.join(homograph))
    # 9. IDN 同形异义 (punycode, 标准库 idna 编解码)
    if domain.startswith('xn--'):
        try:
            dec = domain.encode('ascii').decode('idna')
            add(20, '国际化域名(IDN)同形异义: %s' % dec)
        except Exception:
            pass
    # 10. 可疑路径关键词
    susp_paths = ['login', 'signin', 'verify', 'verification', 'account', 'password', 'secure', 'update', 'confirm', 'wallet', 'otp', 'reset']
    low_path = (p.path + ' ' + (p.query or '')).lower()
    hit = [k for k in susp_paths if k in low_path]
    if hit:
        add(10, '路径/参数含敏感词(%s)' % ', '.join(hit[:5]))
    # 11. data: 协议
    if url.lower().startswith('data:'):
        add(40, 'data: 协议(常用于数据外泄/伪装)')
    # 12. 长十六进制/随机 token
    if re.search(r'[0-9a-f]{32,}', domain + p.path):
        add(5, '含长随机十六进制串')

    level = '低' if score < 20 else ('中' if score < 45 else ('高' if score < 70 else '极高'))
    score = min(score, 100)
    lines = ["[URL-SAFETY] %s" % url,
             "归一化域名: %s" % domain,
             "风险评分: %d/100  等级: %s" % (score, level)]
    if risks:
        lines.append("命中规则:")
        for w, m in sorted(risks, reverse=True):
            lines.append("  [%d] %s" % (w, m))
    else:
        lines.append("本地启发式: 未发现明显风险特征")
    lines.append("\n重要: 本工具为本地启发式判定，非权威威胁情报；关键决策请配合 VirusTotal/官方接口。")
    return '\n'.join(lines)

# === from weather_forecast.plugin ===
def _weather_forecast(self, city: str, days: int = 3) -> str:
    """Open-Meteo 地理编码 + 天气预报，全公开免费"""
    import urllib.request, urllib.parse, json
    try:
        geo_url = 'https://geocoding-api.open-meteo.com/v1/search?name=' + urllib.parse.quote(city) + '&count=1&language=zh'
        geo = json.loads(urllib.request.urlopen(geo_url, timeout=15).read())
        hits = geo.get('results')
        if not hits:
            return f"[ERROR] 找不到城市: {city}"
        loc = hits[0]
        lat, lon = loc['latitude'], loc['longitude']
        w_url = (f'https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast?latitude={lat}&longitude={lon}'
                 f'&daily=temperature_2m_max,temperature_2m_min,weathercode,precipitation_probability_max'
                 f'&timezone=auto&forecast_days={min(days,7)}')
        w = json.loads(urllib.request.urlopen(w_url, timeout=15).read())
        daily = w['daily']
        codes = {0:'晴',1:'少云',2:'多云',3:'阴',45:'雾',51:'毛毛雨',61:'小雨',63:'中雨',65:'大雨',71:'小雪',73:'中雪',75:'大雪',80:'阵雨',95:'雷暴'}
        out = [f"{loc['name']}（{loc.get('country','')}）天气预报:"]
        for i in range(len(daily['time'])):
            date = daily['time'][i]
            desc = codes.get(daily['weathercode'][i], '未知')
            out.append(f"{date}: {desc}, {daily['temperature_2m_min'][i]}~{daily['temperature_2m_max'][i]}°C, 降水概率{daily['precipitation_probability_max'][i] or 0}%")
        return '\n'.join(out)
    except Exception as e:
        return f"[ERROR] 天气查询失败: {e}"

# === from web_page_read.plugin ===
def _web_page_read(self, url: str, max_chars: int = 4000) -> str:
    """抓取网页 → 元信息抽取 + 链接审计 + 阅读指标 + 标题大纲 + 正文清洗。"""
    import urllib.request, re, urllib.parse

    if not url.startswith(('http://', 'https://')):
        url = 'https://' + url
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)'})
    try:
        raw = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=20).read()
        enc = 'utf-8'
        m = re.search(r'<meta[^>]+charset=["\']?([\w-]+)', raw[:2000].decode('ascii', 'ignore'))
        if m:
            enc = m.group(1)
        html = raw.decode(enc, 'ignore')
    except Exception as e:
        return "[ERROR] 读取失败: %s" % e

    def meta(prop, attr='name'):
        # 兼容 name= 与 property=
        pat = r'<meta[^>]+(?:name|property)=["\']%s["\'][^>]*content=["\']([^"\']*)["\']' % re.escape(prop)
        m = re.search(pat, html, re.I)
        if not m:
            pat2 = r'<meta[^>]+content=["\']([^"\']*)["\'][^>]+(?:name|property)=["\']%s["\']' % re.escape(prop)
            m = re.search(pat2, html, re.I)
        return m.group(1).strip() if m else ''

    title_m = re.search(r'<title[^>]*>(.*?)</title>', html, re.S | re.I)
    title = re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', '', title_m.group(1)).strip() if title_m else ''
    import html as H
    title = H.unescape(title)
    desc = H.unescape(meta('description'))
    kw = H.unescape(meta('keywords'))
    canonical = meta('canonical')
    og_title = H.unescape(meta('og:title', 'property'))
    og_desc = H.unescape(meta('og:description', 'property'))
    og_image = meta('og:image', 'property')
    og_site = H.unescape(meta('og:site_name', 'property'))
    generator = H.unescape(meta('generator'))

    # 链接审计
    links = re.findall(r'<a[^>]+href=["\']([^"\']+)["\']', html, re.I)
    abs_links, ext_domains, mailto = 0, set(), 0
    parsed = urllib.parse.urlparse(url)
    for l in links:
        lu = urllib.parse.urlparse(l)
        if l.lower().startswith('mailto:'):
            mailto += 1
            continue
        if lu.scheme in ('http', 'https'):
            if lu.netloc == parsed.netloc:
                abs_links += 1
            else:
                ext_domains.add(lu.netloc)
    # 正文
    body = re.sub(r'<(script|style|noscript)[^>]*>.*?</\1>', '', html, flags=re.S | re.I)
    text = re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', body)
    text = H.unescape(text)
    text = re.sub(r'[ \t]+', ' ', text)
    text = re.sub(r'\n\s*\n+', '\n', text).strip()
    words = len(re.findall(r'\S+', text))
    read_min = max(1, round(words / 250))
    # 标题大纲
    h1 = re.findall(r'<h1[^>]*>(.*?)</h1>', html, re.S | re.I)
    h2 = re.findall(r'<h2[^>]*>(.*?)</h2>', html, re.S | re.I)
    outline = [H.unescape(re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', '', x)).strip() for x in (h1 + h2)][:12]

    out = ["[PAGE] %s" % url,
           "标题: %s%s" % (title, (' (OG:%s)' % og_title) if og_title and og_title != title else ''),
           "描述: %s" % (desc or '—'),
           "关键词: %s" % (kw or '—') if kw else "关键词: —",
           "规范化: %s" % (canonical or '—'),
           "生成器: %s" % (generator or '—') if generator else "生成器: —",
           "OG: site=%s | image=%s" % (og_site or '—', og_image or '—') if (og_site or og_image) else "OG: —"]
    if outline:
        out.append("标题大纲(%d): %s" % (len(outline), ' > '.join(outline[:6])))
    out.append("链接: 站内%d | 站外域名%d | mailto%d | 词数%d | 约%d分钟读完" % (
        abs_links, len(ext_domains), mailto, words, read_min))
    out.append("\n--- 正文 ---\n" + text[:max_chars])
    if len(text) > max_chars:
        out.append("...(共%d字符,已截断)" % len(text))
    return '\n'.join(out)

# === from web_search_api.plugin ===
def _web_search_api(self, query: str, max_results: int = 8) -> str:
    """网络搜索：DuckDuckGo(主) + Bing(辅) 双源，去重并附链接。"""
    import urllib.request, urllib.parse, re
    UA = {'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64)'}
    def get(url, timeout=15):
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=UA)
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
                return r.read().decode('utf-8', 'ignore'), r.status
        except Exception:
            return None, 0
    def clean(s):
        return re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', '', s).strip()
    def ddg(q):
        html, _ = get('https://html.duckduckgo.com/html/?q=' + urllib.parse.quote(q))
        res = []
        if not html:
            return res
        for m in re.finditer(r'class="result__a"[^>]*href="([^"]+)"[^>]*>(.*?)</a>.*?class="result__snippet"[^>]*>(.*?)</a>', html, re.S):
            href = urllib.parse.unquote(m.group(1))
            href = re.sub(r'^.*?uddg=([^&]+).*$', lambda x: urllib.parse.unquote(x.group(1)), href)
            res.append((clean(m.group(2)), href, clean(m.group(3))))
        return res
    def bing(q):
        html, _ = get('https://www.bing.com/search?q=' + urllib.parse.quote(q))
        res = []
        if not html:
            return res
        for m in re.finditer(r'<li class="b_algo".*?<h2><a href="([^"]+)"[^>]*>(.*?)</a>.*?<p[^>]*>(.*?)</p>', html, re.S):
            res.append((clean(m.group(2)), m.group(1), clean(m.group(3))))
        return res
    if not query:
        return "[ERROR] 请提供搜索关键词"
    results = ddg(query)
    src = u'DuckDuckGo'
    if len(results) < 3:
        results += bing(query)
        src += u'+Bing'
    seen = set(); uniq = []
    for r in results:
        if r[1] in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(r[1]); uniq.append(r)
    if not uniq:
        return u'未搜索到结果: %s' % query
    out = [u"搜索 '%s' 结果 (来源: %s):" % (query, src)]
    for i, (t, u, s) in enumerate(uniq[:max_results], 1):
        out.append(u'%d. %s\n   %s\n   %s' % (i, t, s[:160], u))
    return '\n'.join(out)

# === from whois_domain_query.plugin ===
def _whois_domain_query(self, domain: str) -> str:
    """IANA 根 Whois 引荐 + 权威注册局 Whois；结构化解析关键字段并做到期预警与状态归类。"""
    import socket, re, datetime

    domain = domain.strip().lower()
    if '/' in domain:
        domain = domain.split('/')[2] if '://' in domain else domain.split('/')[0]
    domain = domain.split('?')[0]

    def whois_query(server, q, port=43):
        try:
            s = socket.create_connection((server, port), timeout=10)
            s.sendall((q + '\r\n').encode())
            buf = b''
            s.settimeout(8)
            while True:
                try:
                    chunk = s.recv(4096)
                except socket.timeout:
                    break
                if not chunk:
                    break
                buf += chunk
            s.close()
            return buf.decode('utf-8', 'ignore')
        except Exception as e:
            return '[query error: %s]' % e

    raw_iana = whois_query('whois.iana.org', domain)
    ref = None
    for line in raw_iana.splitlines():
        if line.lower().startswith('whois:'):
            ref = line.split(':', 1)[1].strip()
            break

    raw = raw_iana
    if ref:
        raw += '\n--- 权威注册局 (%s) ---\n' % ref + whois_query(ref, domain)

    # ---- 结构化抽取 ----
    def grab(pat, src=raw, flags=re.I | re.M):
        m = re.search(pat, src, flags)
        return m.group(1).strip() if m else ''

    registrar = grab(r'Registrar:\s*(.+?)$') or grab(r'Registrar Name:\s*(.+?)$')
    created = grab(r'(?:Creation Date|Created|Registered On|Created On):\s*(.+?)$')
    expires = grab(r'(?:Expiry Date|Expiration Date|Expires On|Registrar Registration Expiration Date):\s*(.+?)$')
    updated = grab(r'(?:Updated Date|Last Updated On|Modified):\s*(.+?)$')
    reg_org = grab(r'(?:Registrant Organization|Registrant Org):\s*(.+?)$')
    reg_email = grab(r'(?:Registrant Email):\s*(.+?)$')

    name_servers = re.findall(r'(?:Name Server|Nameserver):\s*([\w.\-]+)', raw, re.I)
    name_servers = sorted(set(ns.lower() for ns in name_servers if ns))

    statuses = re.findall(r'Status:\s*([\w\s]+?)(?:\s|$)', raw) or \
              re.findall(r'Domain Status:\s*([\w\s]+?)(?:\s|$)', raw)
    statuses = sorted(set(s.strip().lower() for s in statuses if s.strip()))

    # ---- 到期倒计时 ----
    days_left = None
    warn = ''
    if expires:
        em = re.search(r'(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}|\d{2}-[A-Za-z]{3}-\d{4})', expires)
        if em:
            try:
                fmt = '%Y-%m-%d' if '-' in em.group(1)[:5] else '%d-%b-%Y'
                dt = datetime.datetime.strptime(em.group(1), fmt)
                days_left = (dt - datetime.datetime.utcnow()).days
                if days_left is not None:
                    warn = '⚠ 即将到期!' if days_left < 30 else ('✓ 正常' if days_left > 0 else '✗ 已过期')
            except Exception:
                pass

    lines = ["[WHOIS] %s" % domain,
             "IANA 引荐服务器: %s" % (ref or '(未获取)'),
             "注册商: %s" % (registrar or '未公开'),
             "注册时间: %s" % (created or '—'),
             "过期时间: %s" % (expires or '—')]
    if days_left is not None:
        lines.append("到期倒计时: %s 天 %s" % (days_left, warn))
    lines += ["更新时间: %s" % (updated or '—'),
              "注册人机构: %s" % (reg_org or '隐私保护/未公开'),
              "注册人邮箱: %s" % (reg_email or '隐私保护/未公开'),
              "域名状态(%d): %s" % (len(statuses), ', '.join(statuses) if statuses else '—'),
              "DNS 服务器(%d): %s" % (len(name_servers), ', '.join(name_servers[:10]) if name_servers else '—')]
    lines.append("\n注: 部分字段受注册商隐私保护隐藏；信息来自公开 Whois，仅供参考。")
    return '\n'.join(lines)

# === from wikipedia_summary.plugin ===
def _wikipedia_summary(self, term: str, lang: str = 'zh') -> str:
    """维基百科多源检视：REST 摘要 + Action 长引言 + 相关条目 + 搜索兜底。纯公开数据。"""
    import urllib.request, urllib.parse, json, re
    UA = {'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'}
    def get(url, timeout=15):
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=UA)
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
                return r.read().decode('utf-8', 'ignore'), r.status
        except Exception:
            return None, 0
    term = (term or '').strip()
    if not term:
        return "[ERROR] 请提供词条名称"
    q = urllib.parse.quote(term)
    lines = []
    # 1) REST 摘要：标题/简介/缩略图/规范链接
    body, _ = get('https://%s.wikipedia.org/api/rest_v1/page/summary/%s' % (lang, q))
    title, extract, thumb, page_url = term, '', '', ''
    if body:
        try:
            d = json.loads(body)
            title = d.get('title', term)
            extract = d.get('extract', '')
            thumb = (d.get('thumbnail') or {}).get('source', '')
            page_url = (d.get('content_urls') or {}).get('desktop', {}).get('page', '')
        except Exception:
            pass
    # 2) Action API 长篇引言（exintro, plaintext）—— 比 REST 摘要更完整
    api = ('https://%s.wikipedia.org/w/api.php?action=query&format=json'
           '&prop=extracts&exintro=1&explaintext=1&redirects=1&titles=%s' % (lang, q))
    body2, _ = get(api)
    intro = ''
    if body2:
        try:
            pages = json.loads(body2).get('query', {}).get('pages', {})
            for _pid, pg in pages.items():
                intro = pg.get('extract', '') or ''
                break
        except Exception:
            pass
    # 3) 相关条目（REST related）
    rel_body, _ = get('https://%s.wikipedia.org/api/rest_v1/page/related/%s' % (lang, q))
    related = []
    if rel_body:
        try:
            for it in json.loads(rel_body).get('pages', [])[:6]:
                t = it.get('title'); ds = it.get('description', '')
                if t:
                    related.append(t + ((' — ' + ds) if ds else ''))
        except Exception:
            pass
    # 兜底：摘要与相关都失败则用搜索
    if not extract and not intro and not related:
        s_body, _ = get('https://%s.wikipedia.org/w/api.php?action=query&format=json&list=search&srsearch=%s&srlimit=5' % (lang, q))
        if s_body:
            try:
                for s in json.loads(s_body).get('query', {}).get('search', []):
                    related.append(s.get('title', '') + ' — ' + re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', '', s.get('snippet', ''))[:80])
            except Exception:
                pass
    lines.append(u'【%s】' % title)
    txt = (intro or extract).strip()
    txt = re.sub(r'\n{3,}', '\n\n', txt)
    if len(txt) > 1500:
        txt = txt[:1500] + u'…(已截断)'
    lines.append(txt or u'（无可用摘要）')
    if thumb:
        lines.append(u'缩略图: %s' % thumb)
    if related:
        lines.append(u'\n◆ 相关条目:')
        for r in related[:6]:
            lines.append(u'   - %s' % r)
    if page_url:
        lines.append(u'\n词条链接: %s' % page_url)
    return '\n'.join(lines)

# === from workspace_ops.plugin ===
def _batch_text_replace(self, root: str, find: str, replace: str = '',
                        ext: str = '.txt,.md,.py,.json,.html,.js,.css,.csv,.log,.ini,.yaml,.yml,.bat,.ps1',
                        dry_run: bool = True, backup: bool = True, max_size_kb: int = 2048) -> str:
    """目录级批量文本替换，默认只预览。"""
    import os as _os
    if not find:
        return "[ERROR] find 不能为空"
    if not _os.path.isdir(root):
        return f"[ERROR] 目录不存在: {root}"
    exts = {e.strip().lower() for e in (ext or '').split(',') if e.strip()}
    skip = {'.git', '.venv', '__pycache__', 'node_modules', '.idea'}
    limit = int(max_size_kb or 2048) * 1024
    hits, changed = [], 0
    for dp, dns, fns in _os.walk(root):
        dns[:] = [d for d in dns if d not in skip and not d.startswith('.')]
        for fn in fns:
            fp = _os.path.join(dp, fn)
            if _os.path.splitext(fn)[1].lower() not in exts:
                continue
            try:
                if _os.path.getsize(fp) > limit:
                    continue
                with open(fp, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f:
                    content = f.read()
                n = content.count(find)
                if n:
                    hits.append((fp, n))
                    if not dry_run:
                        if backup:
                            import shutil as _sh
                            _sh.copy2(fp, fp + '.bak')
                        with open(fp, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                            f.write(content.replace(find, replace or ''))
                        changed += 1
            except (OSError, PermissionError):
