                continue
    mode = '预览(dry_run)' if dry_run else '已写入'
    out = [f"[REPLACE {mode}] 命中 {len(hits)} 个文件，共 {sum(n for _, n in hits)} 处:"]
    for fp, n in hits[:50]:
        out.append(f"  {fp}  ×{n}")
    if len(hits) > 50:
        out.append(f"  ...其余 {len(hits) - 50} 个略")
    if not dry_run:
        out.append(f"实际修改 {changed} 个文件" + ("（已留 .bak 备份）" if backup else ""))
    else:
        out.append("确认无误后带 replace 与 dry_run=false 再执行")
    return "\n".join(out)


def _duplicate_sweeper(self, root: str, action: str = 'scan', keep: str = 'oldest',
                       quarantine_dir: str = '', max_files: int = 5000) -> str:
    """按内容哈希找重复文件，clean 时把多余份隔离到回收目录。"""
    import os as _os, hashlib as _hl, shutil as _sh, time as _time
    if not _os.path.isdir(root):
        return f"[ERROR] 目录不存在: {root}"
    buckets = {}
    n = 0
    for dp, dns, fns in _os.walk(root):
        dns[:] = [d for d in dns if d != '_duplicates_quarantine' and not d.startswith('.')]
        for fn in fns:
            if n >= int(max_files or 5000):
                break
            fp = _os.path.join(dp, fn)
            try:
                h = _hl.md5()
                with open(fp, 'rb') as f:
                    while True:
                        b = f.read(1 << 20)
                        if not b:
                            break
                        h.update(b)
                st = _os.stat(fp)
                buckets.setdefault(h.hexdigest(), []).append((fp, st.st_mtime, st.st_size))
                n += 1
            except (OSError, PermissionError):
                continue
    dup_groups = {k: v for k, v in buckets.items() if len(v) > 1}
    out = [f"[DEDUPE] 扫描 {n} 文件，{len(dup_groups)} 组重复，浪费 "
           f"{sum((len(v)-1)*v[0][2] for v in dup_groups.values())/1024/1024:.2f} MB:"]
    for k, v in dup_groups.items():
        keeper = (min if keep == 'oldest' else max)(v, key=lambda x: x[1])
        out.append(f"  保留: {keeper[0]}")
        for fp, mt, sz in v:
            if fp != keeper[0]:
                out.append(f"    重复: {fp} ({sz/1024:.0f} KB)")
                if action == 'clean':
                    qd = _os.path.abspath(quarantine_dir or _os.path.join(root, '_duplicates_quarantine'))
                    _os.makedirs(qd, exist_ok=True)
                    dst = _os.path.join(qd, f"{_time.strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}_{_os.path.basename(fp)}")
                    _sh.move(fp, dst)
                    out[-1] += f" → 已隔离至 {dst}"
    if not dup_groups:
        out.append("  无重复文件")
    elif action == 'scan':
        out.append("确认后带 action=clean 执行隔离（不会直接删除）")
    return "\n".join(out)


def _startup_shortcut_forge(self, action: str = 'list', name: str = '', target: str = '',
                            method: str = 'startup', args: str = '') -> str:
    """登录自启管理：Startup 文件夹 / HKCU Run。"""
    import os as _os, win32api as _wa, win32con as _wc
    RUN_KEY = r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run"
    a = (action or 'list').lower()
    if a == 'list':
        out = ["[STARTUP] HKCU Run 注册表:"]
        try:
            key = _wa.RegOpenKeyEx(_wc.HKEY_CURRENT_USER, RUN_KEY, 0, _wc.KEY_READ)
            i = 0
            while True:
                try:
                    vn, vv, _ = _wa.RegEnumValue(key, i)
                    out.append(f"  [run]  {vn} = {vv}")
                    i += 1
                except Exception:
                    break
            _wa.RegCloseKey(key)
        except Exception:
            pass
        startup = _os.path.join(_os.path.expanduser('~'),
                                r'AppData\Roaming\Microsoft\Windows\Start Menu\Programs\Startup')
        lnks = [f for f in _os.listdir(startup) if f.lower().endswith('.lnk')] if _os.path.isdir(startup) else []
        out.append(f"[STARTUP] Startup 文件夹 ({len(lnks)} 个):")
        for f in lnks:
            out.append(f"  [lnk]  {f}")
        return "\n".join(out)
    if not name:
        return "[ERROR] 需要 name"
    if a == 'add':
        if not target:
            return "[ERROR] add 需要 target"
        if (method or 'startup').lower() == 'run':
            key = _wa.RegOpenKeyEx(_wc.HKEY_CURRENT_USER, RUN_KEY, 0, _wc.KEY_SET_VALUE)
            _wa.RegSetValueEx(key, name, 0, _wc.REG_SZ, f'"{target}"' + (f' {args}' if args else ''))
            _wa.RegCloseKey(key)
            return f"[STARTUP OK] 已注册 HKCU Run: {name} → {target}"
        startup = _os.path.join(_os.path.expanduser('~'),
                                r'AppData\Roaming\Microsoft\Windows\Start Menu\Programs\Startup')
        lnk = _os.path.join(startup, f"{name}.lnk")
        import pythoncom
        from win32com.client import Dispatch
        pythoncom.CoInitialize()
        shell = Dispatch("WScript.Shell")
        sc = shell.CreateShortCut(lnk)
        sc.Targetpath = target
        if args:
            sc.Arguments = args
        sc.save()
        return f"[STARTUP OK] 已创建快捷方式: {lnk} → {target} {args}".rstrip()
    if a == 'remove':
        removed = []
        try:
            key = _wa.RegOpenKeyEx(_wc.HKEY_CURRENT_USER, RUN_KEY, 0, _wc.KEY_SET_VALUE)
            _wa.RegDeleteValue(key, name)
            _wa.RegCloseKey(key)
            removed.append('HKCU Run')
        except Exception:
            pass
        startup = _os.path.join(_os.path.expanduser('~'),
                                r'AppData\Roaming\Microsoft\Windows\Start Menu\Programs\Startup')
        lnk = _os.path.join(startup, f"{name}.lnk")
        if _os.path.exists(lnk):
            _os.remove(lnk)
            removed.append('Startup.lnk')
        return f"[STARTUP OK] 已移除 {name}: " + ('; '.join(removed) if removed else '未找到该自启项')
    return f"[ERROR] 未知 action: {a}"


# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
#  侦察平台层 P0~P10 工具集（依托 银逝.py 的 ReconPlatform）
#  这些工具都是「薄壳」：真正的逻辑在 ReconPlatform 里，
#  工具只负责取参 + 调用 + 渲染，避免逻辑散落在插件里。
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

def _rp(self):
    """取侦察平台层实例（未挂载时返回 None）。"""
    return getattr(self, 'recon', None)


def _rp_fmt(self, tag, data, note=''):
    return self._rc_fmt('P' + tag, data, note=note)


def _recon_capabilities(self, force='false'):
    """P6 侦察能力自检：实测当前环境能扫什么/扫不了什么。"""
    rp = _rp(self)
    if not rp:
        return '[ERROR] 侦察平台层未挂载'
    force = str(force).lower() in ('1', 'true', 'yes')
    cap = rp.capabilities(force=force)
    return _rp_fmt(self, '6', cap, note=cap.get('summary', ''))


def _recon_advise(self, asset=''):
    """P8 侦察知识库：针对资产给出风险点与建议下一步。"""
    rp = _rp(self)
    if not rp:
        return '[ERROR] 侦察平台层未挂载'
    return _rp_fmt(self, '8', rp.advise(asset), note='知识库建议')


def _recon_assets(self, type='', min_confidence='', limit=200):
    """P4 标准化资产查询：统一 schema，可直接喂 recon_store_save。"""
    rp = _rp(self)
    if not rp:
        return '[ERROR] 侦察平台层未挂载'
    items = rp.assets(type, min_confidence, limit)
    return _rp_fmt(self, '4', {'assets': items, 'count': len(items)},
                   note='标准化资产 %d 条' % len(items))


def _recon_diff(self, type='', session_a='', session_b=''):
    """P1 资产差异对比：新增/消失/变更。"""
    rp = _rp(self)
    if not rp:
        return '[ERROR] 侦察平台层未挂载'
    d = rp.diff(type, session_a, session_b)
    return _rp_fmt(self, '1', d, note=rp.diff_text(d))


def _recon_session_start(self, goal=''):
    """P5 侦察会话：开启一个带目标的侦察上下文。"""
    rp = _rp(self)
    if not rp:
        return '[ERROR] 侦察平台层未挂载'
    return _rp_fmt(self, '5', rp.session_start(goal), note='会话已开启')


def _recon_session_status(self, action='status'):
    """P5 侦察会话：status 查看 / close 收尾。"""
    rp = _rp(self)
    if not rp:
        return '[ERROR] 侦察平台层未挂载'
    a = (action or 'status').lower()
    if a == 'close':
        return _rp_fmt(self, '5', rp.session_close(), note='会话已收尾')
    return _rp_fmt(self, '5', rp.session_status(), note='当前侦察会话状态')


def _recon_plan(self, mode='', target='', custom=''):
    """P2 侦察 DAG：按预置模板或自定义 DAG 执行条件分支侦察链路。"""
    rp = _rp(self)
    if not rp:
        return '[ERROR] 侦察平台层未挂载'
    res = rp.plan(mode, target, custom)
    return _rp_fmt(self, '2', res,
                   note='DAG %s ok=%s 耗时=%sms' % (res.get('mode'),
                                                  res.get('ok'), res.get('elapsed_ms')))


def _recon_batch(self, targets='', mode='domain_full'):
    """P10 批量侦察：对多目标并发跑同一模式并汇总。"""
    rp = _rp(self)
    if not rp:
        return '[ERROR] 侦察平台层未挂载'
    res = rp.batch(targets, mode)
    return _rp_fmt(self, '10', res,
                   note='批量完成 ok=%s failed=%s' % (res.get('ok_count'),
                                                     res.get('failed_count')))


def _recon_timeline(self, target='', days=30):
    """P9 资产时间线：该资产的历史事件与关键时刻。"""
    rp = _rp(self)
    if not rp:
        return '[ERROR] 侦察平台层未挂载'
    res = rp.timeline(target, days)
    return _rp_fmt(self, '9', res, note='时间线 %s 事件' % res.get('count', 0))


def _recon_visualize(self, type='tree', target='', format='ascii'):
    """P7 侦察可视化：tree/graph/table，ascii 给模型、mermaid 给人。"""
    rp = _rp(self)
    if not rp:
        return '[ERROR] 侦察平台层未挂载'
    res = rp.visualize(type, target, format)
    if isinstance(res, dict) and res.get('error'):
        return '[ERROR] %s' % res['error']
    out = ['[RECON VIS %s] target=%s' % (res.get('type'), res.get('target')),
           '--- 给模型（ASCII）---', str(res.get('model_view', '')),
           '--- 给人（可渲染）---', str(res.get('human_view', ''))]
    return '\n'.join(out)

def _delete_file(self, file_path: str) -> str:
        try:
            path = Path(file_path)
            if not path.exists():
                return f"[ERROR] File not found: {file_path}"
            path.unlink()
            return f"[SUCCESS] Deleted: {file_path}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to delete: {e}"


def _move_file(self, source: str, destination: str) -> str:
        try:
            shutil.move(source, destination)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Moved: {source} -> {destination}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to move: {e}"


def _copy_file(self, source: str, destination: str) -> str:
        try:
            shutil.copy2(source, destination)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Copied: {source} -> {destination}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to copy: {e}"


def _create_directory(self, dir_path: str, parents: bool = False) -> str:
        try:
            Path(dir_path).mkdir(parents=parents, exist_ok=True)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Created directory: {dir_path}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to create directory: {e}"


def _write_file(self, file_path: str, content: str, encoding: str = 'utf-8') -> str:
        try:
            Path(file_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            with open(file_path, 'w', encoding=encoding) as f:
                f.write(content)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Written {len(content)} chars to: {file_path}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to write file: {e}"


def _append_file(self, file_path: str, content: str, encoding: str = 'utf-8') -> str:
        try:
            Path(file_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            with open(file_path, 'a', encoding=encoding) as f:
                f.write(content)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Appended {len(content)} chars to: {file_path}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to append to file: {e}"


def _get_file_info(self, file_path: str) -> str:
        try:
            path = Path(file_path)
            if not path.exists():
                return f"[ERROR] File not found: {file_path}"
            stat_info = path.stat()
            return f"""[FILE INFO] {file_path}
    Size: {stat_info.st_size:,} bytes
    Created: {datetime.fromtimestamp(stat_info.st_ctime).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}
    Modified: {datetime.fromtimestamp(stat_info.st_mtime).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}
    Accessed: {datetime.fromtimestamp(stat_info.st_atime).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}
    Attributes: {oct(stat_info.st_mode)}"""
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get file info: {e}"


def _list_directory(self, dir_path: str, recursive: bool = False, pattern: str = '') -> str:
        try:
            path = Path(dir_path)
            if not path.exists():
                return f"[ERROR] Directory not found: {dir_path}"
            result = f"[DIRECTORY] {dir_path}\n"
            if recursive:
                if pattern:
                    items = list(path.rglob(pattern))
                else:
                    items = list(path.rglob('*'))
                for item in items[:500]:
                    result += f"  {item.relative_to(path)}\n"
                if len(items) > 500:
                    result += f"  ... ({len(items)-500} more)\n"
                result += f"Total: {len(items)} items"
            else:
                if pattern:
                    items = list(path.glob(pattern))
                else:
                    items = list(path.iterdir())
                dirs = [i for i in items if i.is_dir()]
                files = [i for i in items if i.is_file()]
                result += f"Directories: {len(dirs)}\nFiles: {len(files)}\n\n"
                for d in sorted(dirs)[:20]:
                    result += f"  [DIR]  {d.name}/\n"
                if len(dirs) > 20:
                    result += f"  ... ({len(dirs)-20} more dirs)\n"
                for f in sorted(files)[:30]:
                    size = f.stat().st_size
                    size_str = f"{size:,}B" if size < 1024 else f"{size/1024:.1f}KB" if size < 1048576 else f"{size/1048576:.1f}MB"
                    result += f"  [FILE] {f.name} ({size_str})\n"
                if len(files) > 30:
                    result += f"  ... ({len(files)-30} more files)\n"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to list directory: {e}"


def _find_files(self, dir_path: str, name_pattern: str = '', extension: str = '',
                    min_size_mb: float = 0, max_size_mb: float = 0,
                    newer_than_days: int = 0, older_than_days: int = 0) -> str:
        try:
            path = Path(dir_path)
            if not path.exists():
                return f"[ERROR] Directory not found: {dir_path}"
            results = []
            for item in path.rglob('*'):
                if not item.is_file():
                    continue
                if name_pattern and name_pattern.lower() not in item.name.lower():
                    continue
                if extension and not item.name.endswith(extension):
                    continue
                size_mb = item.stat().st_size / 1048576
                if min_size_mb and size_mb < min_size_mb:
                    continue
                if max_size_mb and size_mb > max_size_mb:
                    continue
                if newer_than_days:
                    age_days = (datetime.now() - datetime.fromtimestamp(item.stat().st_mtime)).days
                    if age_days > newer_than_days:
                        continue
                if older_than_days:
                    age_days = (datetime.now() - datetime.fromtimestamp(item.stat().st_mtime)).days
                    if age_days < older_than_days:
                        continue
                results.append(item)
            if not results:
                return f"[EMPTY] No files matching criteria in: {dir_path}"
            result = f"[FOUND] {len(results)} files in {dir_path}\n"
            for r in results[:50]:
                size_str = f"{r.stat().st_size/1048576:.2f}MB" if r.stat().st_size > 1048576 else f"{r.stat().st_size/1024:.1f}KB" if r.stat().st_size > 1024 else f"{r.stat().st_size}B"
                result += f"  {r.relative_to(path)} ({size_str})\n"
            if len(results) > 50:
                result += f"  ... ({len(results)-50} more)\n"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to find files: {e}"


def _quick_find(self, pattern: str = '', directory: str = '', max_results: int = 50) -> str:
        """利用Windows搜索引擎全盘快速找文件, 全盘搜索只需0.1秒"""
        result_parts = []
        try:
            if not WIN32_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] 需要 pywin32 模块"
            import win32com.client
            scope = f"file:{directory}" if directory else "file:"
            like_pattern = pattern.replace('*', '%').replace('?', '_') if pattern else '%'
            sql = f"""SELECT System.ItemPathDisplay, System.Size, System.DateModified, System.FileName 
FROM SYSTEMINDEX 
WHERE SCOPE='{scope}' 
  AND System.FileName LIKE '{like_pattern}'
  AND System.ItemType='File'
ORDER BY System.DateModified DESC"""
            conn = win32com.client.Dispatch("ADODB.Connection")
            conn.Open("Provider=Search.CollatorDSO;Extended Properties='Application=Windows';")
            rs = conn.Execute(sql)
            results = []
            count = 0
            while not rs.EOF and count < max_results:
                try:
                    path = str(rs.Fields(0).Value or '')
                    size = rs.Fields(1).Value or 0
                    modified = str(rs.Fields(2).Value or '')[:19]
                    name = str(rs.Fields(3).Value or '')
                    results.append((path, size, modified, name))
                    count += 1
                except Exception: pass
                rs.MoveNext()
            rs.Close()
            conn.Close()
            scope_label = directory if directory else "所有磁盘"
            result_parts.append(f"[QUICK FIND] 搜索: \"{pattern or '*.*'}\" 范围: {scope_label}")
            result_parts.append(f"  耗时: 极短(Windows索引查询)")
            result_parts.append(f"  结果数: {count}")
            result_parts.append("-" * 60)
            if not results:
                result_parts.append("  (未找到匹配文件)")
                result_parts.append("  [提示] 如果文件是新创建的, Windows索引可能尚未更新")
                result_parts.append("  [提示] 请确保 Windows Search 服务正在运行")
                return '\n'.join(result_parts)
            for path, size, modified, name in results:
                size_str = f"{size / 1048576:.1f}MB" if size > 1048576 else f"{size / 1024:.1f}KB" if size > 1024 else f"{size}B"
                result_parts.append(f"  {name:<35} {size_str:>8}  {modified}")
            result_parts.append("-" * 60)
            result_parts.append("[QUICK FIND] 完成")
            return '\n'.join(result_parts)
        except Exception as e:
            err_msg = str(e)
            if "Provider cannot be found" in err_msg or "未找到提供程序" in err_msg:
                return """[ERROR] Windows Search 索引不可用.
请确保:
  1. Windows Search 服务正在运行 (services.msc -> Windows Search -> 启动)
  2. 磁盘已加入索引 (控制面板 -> 索引选项)
  3. 以管理员身份运行本程序
或者使用普通 search/find_files 命令(较慢但不需要索引):
  find_files dir_path=C:\\ name_pattern=data"""
            return f"[ERROR] Quick find failed: {e}"


def _zip_files(self, source_path: str, zip_path: str) -> str:
        try:
            source = Path(source_path)
            if not source.exists():
                return f"[ERROR] Source not found: {source_path}"
            with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zf:
                if source.is_file():
                    zf.write(source, source.name)
                else:
                    for item in source.rglob('*'):
                        zf.write(item, item.relative_to(source.parent))
            return f"[SUCCESS] Created ZIP: {zip_path}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to create ZIP: {e}"


def _unzip_files(self, zip_path: str, extract_to: str) -> str:
        try:
            with zipfile.ZipFile(zip_path, 'r') as zf:
                zf.extractall(extract_to)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Extracted {zip_path} to {extract_to} ({len(zf.namelist())} files)"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to extract ZIP: {e}"


def _calculate_checksum(self, file_path: str, algorithm: str = 'md5') -> str:
        try:
            path = Path(file_path)
            if not path.exists():
                return f"[ERROR] File not found: {file_path}"
            algo_map = {'md5': hashlib.md5, 'sha1': hashlib.sha1, 'sha256': hashlib.sha256}
            if algorithm not in algo_map:
                return f"[ERROR] Unsupported algorithm: {algorithm}. Use md5, sha1, or sha256."
            hasher = algo_map[algorithm]()
            with open(path, 'rb') as f:
                for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(8192), b''):
                    hasher.update(chunk)
            return f"[{algorithm.upper()}] {hasher.hexdigest()}  {file_path}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to calculate checksum: {e}"


def _read_registry(self, key_path: str, value_name: str, hive: str = 'HKLM') -> str:
        try:
            hive_handle = self._get_hive(hive)
            key = win32api.RegOpenKeyEx(hive_handle, key_path, 0, win32con.KEY_READ)
            value, reg_type = win32api.RegQueryValueEx(key, value_name)
            win32api.RegCloseKey(key)
            type_str = {1: 'REG_SZ', 2: 'REG_EXPAND_SZ', 3: 'REG_BINARY', 4: 'REG_DWORD', 7: 'REG_MULTI_SZ'}.get(reg_type, f'REG_TYPE_{reg_type}')
            return f"[REGISTRY] {hive}\\\\{key_path}\\\\{value_name} = {value} ({type_str})"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to read registry: {e}"


def _write_registry(self, key_path: str, value_name: str, value_data: str, value_type: str = 'REG_SZ', hive: str = 'HKLM') -> str:
        try:
            type_map = {'REG_SZ': win32con.REG_SZ, 'REG_DWORD': win32con.REG_DWORD,
                        'REG_BINARY': win32con.REG_BINARY, 'REG_EXPAND_SZ': win32con.REG_EXPAND_SZ}
            reg_type = type_map.get(value_type.upper(), win32con.REG_SZ)
            if reg_type == win32con.REG_DWORD:
                value_data = int(value_data)
            hive_handle = self._get_hive(hive)
            key = win32api.RegCreateKeyEx(hive_handle, key_path)
            win32api.RegSetValueEx(key, value_name, 0, reg_type, value_data)
            win32api.RegCloseKey(key)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Set {hive}\\\\{key_path}\\\\{value_name} = {value_data} ({value_type})"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to write registry: {e}"


def _delete_registry_key(self, key_path: str, hive: str = 'HKLM') -> str:
        try:
            hive_handle = self._get_hive(hive)
            win32api.RegDeleteKey(hive_handle, key_path)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Deleted registry key: {hive}\\\\{key_path}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to delete registry key: {e}"


def _delete_registry_value(self, key_path: str, value_name: str, hive: str = 'HKLM') -> str:
        try:
            hive_handle = self._get_hive(hive)
            key = win32api.RegOpenKeyEx(hive_handle, key_path, 0, win32con.KEY_SET_VALUE)
            win32api.RegDeleteValue(key, value_name)
            win32api.RegCloseKey(key)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Deleted registry value: {hive}\\\\{key_path}\\\\{value_name}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to delete registry value: {e}"


def _list_registry_keys(self, key_path: str, hive: str = 'HKLM') -> str:
        try:
            hive_handle = self._get_hive(hive)
            key = win32api.RegOpenKeyEx(hive_handle, key_path, 0, win32con.KEY_READ)
            i = 0
            result = f"[REGISTRY KEYS] {hive}\\\\{key_path}\n"
            count = 0
            while True:
                try:
                    subkey = win32api.RegEnumKey(key, i)
                    result += f"  {subkey}\n"
                    i += 1
                    count += 1
                    if count > 100:
                        result += "  ... (more than 100 keys)\n"
                        break
                except pywintypes.error:
                    break
            win32api.RegCloseKey(key)
            result += f"Total: {count} subkeys"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to list registry keys: {e}"


def _list_registry_values(self, key_path: str, hive: str = 'HKLM') -> str:
        try:
            hive_handle = self._get_hive(hive)
            key = win32api.RegOpenKeyEx(hive_handle, key_path, 0, win32con.KEY_READ)
            result = f"[REGISTRY VALUES] {hive}\\\\{key_path}\n"
            i = 0
            while True:
                try:
                    value_name, value_data, reg_type = win32api.RegEnumValue(key, i)
                    type_str = {1: 'SZ', 2: 'EXPAND_SZ', 3: 'BINARY', 4: 'DWORD', 7: 'MULTI_SZ'}.get(reg_type, str(reg_type))
                    result += f"  {value_name} = {value_data} ({type_str})\n"
                    i += 1
                except pywintypes.error:
                    break
            win32api.RegCloseKey(key)
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to list registry values: {e}"


def _get_process_details(self, process_name: str = '', pid: int = 0) -> str:
        try:
            if not PSUTIL_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] psutil not available"
            target = None
            if pid:
                try:
                    target = psutil.Process(pid)
                except Exception: pass
            elif process_name:
                for p in psutil.process_iter(['pid', 'name']):
                    try:
                        if p.info['name'].lower() == process_name.lower():
                            target = p
                            break
                    except Exception:
                        continue
            if not target:
                return f"[ERROR] Process not found: {process_name if process_name else pid}"
            with target.oneshot():
                info = target.as_dict(['pid', 'name', 'exe', 'cmdline', 'ppid', 'create_time',
                                       'username', 'memory_info', 'cpu_percent', 'num_threads',
                                       'num_handles', 'connections'])
            result = f"""[PROCESS DETAILS] {info['name']} (PID {info['pid']})
    Executable: {info['exe']}
    Command Line: {' '.join(info['cmdline']) if info['cmdline'] else '(none)'}
    Parent PID: {info['ppid']}
    User: {info['username']}
    Created: {datetime.fromtimestamp(info['create_time']).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}
    Memory: {info['memory_info'].rss/1048576:.2f} MB
    CPU: {info['cpu_percent']:.1f}%
    Threads: {info['num_threads']}
    Handles: {info['num_handles']}
    Connections: {len(info['connections']) if info['connections'] else 0}"""
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get process details: {e}"


def _set_process_priority(self, process_name: str, priority: str) -> str:
        try:
            if not PSUTIL_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] psutil not available"
            prio_map = {
                'idle': psutil.IDLE_PRIORITY_CLASS,
                'below_normal': psutil.BELOW_NORMAL_PRIORITY_CLASS,
                'normal': psutil.NORMAL_PRIORITY_CLASS,
                'above_normal': psutil.ABOVE_NORMAL_PRIORITY_CLASS,
                'high': psutil.HIGH_PRIORITY_CLASS,
                'realtime': psutil.REALTIME_PRIORITY_CLASS
            }
            if priority.lower() not in prio_map:
                return f"[ERROR] Invalid priority. Use: idle, below_normal, normal, above_normal, high, realtime"
            for p in psutil.process_iter(['pid', 'name']):
                try:
                    if p.info['name'].lower() == process_name.lower():
                        proc = psutil.Process(p.info['pid'])
                        proc.nice(prio_map[priority.lower()])
                        return f"[SUCCESS] Set {process_name} (PID {p.info['pid']}) priority to {priority}"
                except Exception:
                    continue
            return f"[ERROR] Process not found: {process_name}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to set priority: {e}"


def _set_process_affinity(self, process_name: str, cpu_mask: int) -> str:
        try:
            if not PSUTIL_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] psutil not available"
            for p in psutil.process_iter(['pid', 'name']):
                try:
                    if p.info['name'].lower() == process_name.lower():
                        proc = psutil.Process(p.info['pid'])
                        proc.cpu_affinity([i for i in range(psutil.cpu_count()) if cpu_mask & (1 << i)])
                        return f"[SUCCESS] Set {process_name} (PID {p.info['pid']}) affinity to mask {cpu_mask}"
                except Exception:
                    continue
            return f"[ERROR] Process not found: {process_name}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to set affinity: {e}"


def _get_process_environment(self, pid: int) -> str:
        try:
            if not PSUTIL_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] psutil not available"
            proc = psutil.Process(pid)
            env = proc.environ()
            result = f"[ENVIRONMENT] PID {pid}\n"
            for k, v in sorted(env.items())[:50]:
                result += f"  {k} = {v[:100]}{'...' if len(v) > 100 else ''}\n"
            if len(env) > 50:
                result += f"  ... ({len(env)-50} more)"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get environment: {e}"


def _wait_for_process(self, process_name: str, timeout_seconds: int = 60) -> str:
        try:
            if not PSUTIL_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] psutil not available"
            start_time = time.time()
            while time.time() - start_time < timeout_seconds:
                found = False
                for p in psutil.process_iter(['pid', 'name']):
                    try:
                        if p.info['name'].lower() == process_name.lower():
                            found = True
                            break
                    except Exception:
                        continue
                if not found:
                    return f"[SUCCESS] Process {process_name} exited after {time.time()-start_time:.1f}s"
                time.sleep(1)
            return f"[TIMEOUT] Process {process_name} still running after {timeout_seconds}s"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to wait for process: {e}"


def _list_windows(self, filter_title: str = '') -> str:
        try:
            windows = []
            def callback(hwnd, extra):
                if win32gui.IsWindowVisible(hwnd):
                    title = win32gui.GetWindowText(hwnd)
                    if filter_title and filter_title.lower() not in title.lower():
                        return True
                    if title:
                        class_name = win32gui.GetClassName(hwnd)
                        rect = win32gui.GetWindowRect(hwnd)
                        windows.append({
                            'hwnd': hex(hwnd),
                            'title': title[:60],
                            'class': class_name,
                            'x': rect[0], 'y': rect[1], 'w': rect[2]-rect[0], 'h': rect[3]-rect[1]
                        })
                return True
            win32gui.EnumWindows(callback, None)
            result = f"[WINDOWS] {len(windows)} visible windows (含坐标与尺寸)"
            if filter_title:
                result += f" (filter: '{filter_title}')"
            result += "\n句柄 | X,Y | 宽x高 | 类名 | 标题\n"
            for w in windows[:30]:
                result += f"  {w['hwnd']} | ({w['x']},{w['y']}) | {w['w']}x{w['h']} | {w['class']} | {w['title']}\n"
            if len(windows) > 30:
                result += f"  ... ({len(windows)-30} more)\n"
            result += "说明：X,Y 为窗口左上角坐标，宽x高为尺寸。可直接引用，无需再用其他工具获取坐标。\n"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to list windows: {e}"


def _focus_window(self, window_title: str, select: str = '') -> str:
        try:
            hwnd = self._find_window_by_title(window_title, select=select)
            if not hwnd:
                wins = self._find_window_by_title(window_title, select=select, all_matches=True) or []
                all_wins = self._find_window_by_title('', all_matches=True) or []
                sample = [win32gui.GetWindowText(h)[:40] for h in all_wins[:15]]
                return (f"[ERROR] Window not found: {window_title}\n"
                        f"当前可见窗口 {len(all_wins)} 个，示例: {sample}")
            if win32gui.IsIconic(hwnd):
                win32gui.ShowWindow(hwnd, win32con.SW_RESTORE)
            win32gui.SetForegroundWindow(hwnd)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Focused window: {win32gui.GetWindowText(hwnd)} (hwnd={hwnd})"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to focus window: {e}"


def _minimize_window(self, window_title: str) -> str:
        try:
            hwnd = self._find_window_by_title(window_title)
            if not hwnd:
                return f"[ERROR] Window not found: {window_title}"
            win32gui.ShowWindow(hwnd, win32con.SW_MINIMIZE)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Minimized window: {window_title}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to minimize window: {e}"


def _maximize_window(self, window_title: str) -> str:
        try:
            hwnd = self._find_window_by_title(window_title)
            if not hwnd:
                return f"[ERROR] Window not found: {window_title}"
            win32gui.ShowWindow(hwnd, win32con.SW_MAXIMIZE)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Maximized window: {window_title}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to maximize window: {e}"


def _close_window(self, window_title: str) -> str:
        try:
            hwnd = self._find_window_by_title(window_title)
            if not hwnd:
                return f"[ERROR] Window not found: {window_title}"
            win32gui.PostMessage(hwnd, win32con.WM_CLOSE, 0, 0)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Closed window: {window_title}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to close window: {e}"


def _send_keys(self, window_title: str = None, keys: str = '', interval: float = 0.05,
                   select: str = '') -> str:
        """序列化键盘发送：一次调用执行整串动作。
        - keys 用 || 分隔多条动作，按顺序执行
        - 单条支持 {TEXT:...}（整段打字）与 WScript SendKeys 语法（^s、{ENTER} 等）
        - select 窗口选择：'0'/'1' 序号、'#精确标题'、'@进程名'
        """
        try:
            if not keys:
                return "[ERROR] keys is required"
            if window_title:
                hwnd = self._find_window_by_title(window_title, select=select)
                if not hwnd:
                    return f"[ERROR] Window not found: {window_title}"
                if win32gui.IsIconic(hwnd):
                    win32gui.ShowWindow(hwnd, win32con.SW_RESTORE)
                win32gui.SetForegroundWindow(hwnd)
                time.sleep(0.15)
            shell = None
            try:
                import pythoncom
                pythoncom.CoInitialize()
                shell = Dispatch("WScript.Shell")
            except Exception:
                shell = Dispatch("WScript.Shell")
            steps = [s for s in keys.split('||')]
            done, failed = 0, []
            for i, step in enumerate(steps):
                step = step.strip()
                if not step:
                    continue
                try:
                    if step.startswith('{TEXT:') and step.endswith('}'):
                        text = step[6:-1]
                        if all(ord(ch) < 128 for ch in text):
                            shell.SendKeys(text, 1)  # 纯 ASCII 走键盘逐字
                        else:
                            self._send_text_via_clipboard(text)
                    else:
                        shell.SendKeys(step)
                    done += 1
                except Exception as _se:
                    failed.append(f"step{i + 1}({step[:30]}): {_se}")
                if i < len(steps) - 1 and interval and interval > 0:
                    time.sleep(min(float(interval), 1.0))
            out = f"[SUCCESS] Sent {done}/{len(steps)} key-steps"
            if window_title:
                out += f" to '{window_title}'"
            if failed:
                out += "\n[WARN] 失败步骤: " + '; '.join(failed)
            return out
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to send keys: {e}"


def _get_window_text(self, window_title: str) -> str:
        try:
            hwnd = self._find_window_by_title(window_title)
            if not hwnd:
                return f"[ERROR] Window not found: {window_title}"
            text = win32gui.GetWindowText(hwnd)
            return f"[WINDOW TEXT] {window_title}\n{text}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get window text: {e}"


def _get_window_position(self, window_title: str) -> str:
        try:
            hwnd = self._find_window_by_title(window_title)
            if not hwnd:
                return f"[ERROR] Window not found: {window_title}"
            rect = win32gui.GetWindowRect(hwnd)
            return f"""[WINDOW POSITION] {window_title}
    X: {rect[0]}
    Y: {rect[1]}
    Width: {rect[2]-rect[0]}
    Height: {rect[3]-rect[1]}"""
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get window position: {e}"


def _set_window_position(self, window_title: str, x: int, y: int, width: int, height: int) -> str:
        try:
            hwnd = self._find_window_by_title(window_title)
            if not hwnd:
                return f"[ERROR] Window not found: {window_title}"
            win32gui.SetWindowPos(hwnd, None, x, y, width, height, win32con.SWP_NOZORDER)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Set window position: {window_title} ({x},{y}) {width}x{height}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to set window position: {e}"


def _get_system_info(self) -> str:
        try:
            result = f"""[SYSTEM INFORMATION]
    OS: {platform.system()} {platform.release()} ({platform.version()})
    Machine: {platform.machine()}
    Processor: {platform.processor()}
    Hostname: {platform.node()}
    """
            mem = psutil.virtual_memory()
            result += f"""Memory Total: {mem.total/1073741824:.2f} GB
    Memory Available: {mem.available/1073741824:.2f} GB
    Memory Used: {mem.used/1073741824:.2f} GB ({mem.percent}%)
    CPU Cores: {psutil.cpu_count(logical=True)} logical, {psutil.cpu_count(logical=False)} physical
    """
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get system info: {e}"


def _get_disk_usage(self, drive: str = '') -> str:
        try:
            if drive:
                parts = [drive]
            else:
                parts = [d.device for d in psutil.disk_partitions()]
            result = "[DISK USAGE]\n"
            for part in parts:
                try:
                    usage = psutil.disk_usage(part)
                    result += f"{part}\n"
                    result += f"  Total: {usage.total/1073741824:.2f} GB\n"
                    result += f"  Used: {usage.used/1073741824:.2f} GB ({usage.percent}%)\n"
                    result += f"  Free: {usage.free/1073741824:.2f} GB\n"
                except Exception:
                    continue
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get disk usage: {e}"


def _get_network_interfaces(self) -> str:
        try:
            result = "[NETWORK INTERFACES]\n"
            for iface, addrs in psutil.net_if_addrs().items():
                result += f"\n{iface}:\n"
                for addr in addrs:
                    result += f"  {addr.family.name}: {addr.address}\n"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get network interfaces: {e}"


def _get_environment_variables(self, filter: str = '') -> str:
        try:
            result = "[ENVIRONMENT VARIABLES]\n"
            for k, v in sorted(os.environ.items()):
                if filter and filter.lower() not in k.lower():
                    continue
                result += f"  {k} = {v[:100]}{'...' if len(v) > 100 else ''}\n"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get environment variables: {e}"


def _set_environment_variable(self, name: str, value: str, scope: str = 'user') -> str:
        try:
            if scope.lower() == 'system':
                key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, r'SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Control\Session Manager\Environment', 0, winreg.KEY_SET_VALUE)
                winreg.SetValueEx(key, name, 0, winreg.REG_EXPAND_SZ, value)
                winreg.CloseKey(key)
            else:
                key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER, r'Environment', 0, winreg.KEY_SET_VALUE)
                winreg.SetValueEx(key, name, 0, winreg.REG_EXPAND_SZ, value)
                winreg.CloseKey(key)
            os.environ[name] = value
            return f"[SUCCESS] Set environment variable {name} = {value} ({scope} scope)"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to set environment variable: {e}"


def _get_uptime(self) -> str:
        try:
            boot_time = psutil.boot_time()
            uptime_seconds = time.time() - boot_time
            days = int(uptime_seconds // 86400)
            hours = int((uptime_seconds % 86400) // 3600)
            minutes = int((uptime_seconds % 3600) // 60)
            return f"[UPTIME] {days} days, {hours} hours, {minutes} minutes"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get uptime: {e}"


def _get_logged_in_users(self) -> str:
        try:
            result = "[LOGGED IN USERS]\n"
            for user in psutil.users():
                result += f"  {user.name} | {user.terminal or 'Console'} | {datetime.fromtimestamp(user.started).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}\n"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get logged in users: {e}"


def _get_installed_software(self, filter: str = '') -> str:
        try:
            result = "[INSTALLED SOFTWARE]\n"
            keys = [
                (win32con.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, r'SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Uninstall'),
                (win32con.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, r'SOFTWARE\WOW6432Node\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Uninstall'),
                (win32con.HKEY_CURRENT_USER, r'SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Uninstall'),
            ]
            found = 0
            for hive, path in keys:
                try:
                    key = win32api.RegOpenKeyEx(hive, path, 0, win32con.KEY_READ)
                    i = 0
                    while True:
                        try:
                            subkey_name = win32api.RegEnumKey(key, i)
                            subkey = win32api.RegOpenKeyEx(hive, f"{path}\\{subkey_name}", 0, win32con.KEY_READ)
                            try:
                                display_name, _ = win32api.RegQueryValueEx(subkey, 'DisplayName')
                                if display_name:
                                    if filter and filter.lower() not in display_name.lower():
                                        i += 1
                                        win32api.RegCloseKey(subkey)
                                        continue
                                    display_version = ''
                                    try:
                                        display_version, _ = win32api.RegQueryValueEx(subkey, 'DisplayVersion')
                                    except Exception: pass
                                    install_location = ''
                                    for loc_key in ('InstallLocation', 'DisplayIcon', 'UninstallString'):
                                        try:
                                            v, _ = win32api.RegQueryValueEx(subkey, loc_key)
                                            if v:
                                                install_location = v.split('",')[0].strip('"').split(',')[0].strip()
                                                break
                                        except Exception: pass
                                    result += f"  {display_name} {display_version} | {install_location or 'N/A'}\n"
                                    found += 1
                            except Exception: pass
                            win32api.RegCloseKey(subkey)
                            i += 1
                        except pywintypes.error:
                            break
                    win32api.RegCloseKey(key)
                except Exception:
                    continue
            result += f"Total: {found} installed applications"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get installed software: {e}"


def _list_services(self, filter: str = '', state: str = 'all') -> str:
        try:
            result = f"[SERVICES]"
            if filter:
                result += f" (filter: '{filter}')"
            result += "\n"
            scm = win32service.OpenSCManager(None, None, win32service.SC_MANAGER_ENUMERATE_SERVICE)
            status_types = {
                'all': win32service.SERVICE_STATE_ALL,
                'running': win32service.SERVICE_ACTIVE,
                'stopped': win32service.SERVICE_INACTIVE
            }
            services = win32service.EnumServicesStatus(scm, status_types.get(state.lower(), win32service.SERVICE_STATE_ALL))
            count = 0
            for service in services:
                name = service[0]
                display = service[1]
                if filter and filter.lower() not in name.lower() and filter.lower() not in display.lower():
                    continue
                status = {1: 'STOPPED', 2: 'START_PENDING', 3: 'STOP_PENDING', 4: 'RUNNING', 5: 'CONTINUE_PENDING', 6: 'PAUSE_PENDING', 7: 'PAUSED'}.get(service[3], 'UNKNOWN')
                result += f"  {name} ({display}) - {status}\n"
                count += 1
                if count > 100:
                    result += f"  ... ({len(services)-count} more)\n"
                    break
            win32service.CloseServiceHandle(scm)
            result += f"Total: {count} services"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to list services: {e}"


def _start_service(self, service_name: str) -> str:
        try:
            scm = win32service.OpenSCManager(None, None, win32service.SC_MANAGER_CONNECT)
            handle = win32service.OpenService(scm, service_name, win32service.SERVICE_START)
            win32service.StartService(handle, None)
            win32service.CloseServiceHandle(handle)
            win32service.CloseServiceHandle(scm)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Started service: {service_name}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to start service: {e}"


def _stop_service(self, service_name: str) -> str:
        try:
            scm = win32service.OpenSCManager(None, None, win32service.SC_MANAGER_CONNECT)
            handle = win32service.OpenService(scm, service_name, win32service.SERVICE_STOP)
            win32service.ControlService(handle, win32service.SERVICE_CONTROL_STOP)
            win32service.CloseServiceHandle(handle)
            win32service.CloseServiceHandle(scm)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Stopped service: {service_name}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to stop service: {e}"


def _restart_service(self, service_name: str) -> str:
        try:
            self._stop_service(service_name)
            time.sleep(2)
            self._start_service(service_name)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Restarted service: {service_name}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to restart service: {e}"


def _set_service_startup(self, service_name: str, startup_type: str) -> str:
        try:
            type_map = {'automatic': win32service.SERVICE_AUTO_START,
                        'manual': win32service.SERVICE_DEMAND_START,
                        'disabled': win32service.SERVICE_DISABLED}
            if startup_type.lower() not in type_map:
                return f"[ERROR] Invalid startup type. Use: automatic, manual, disabled"
            scm = win32service.OpenSCManager(None, None, win32service.SC_MANAGER_CONNECT)
            handle = win32service.OpenService(scm, service_name, win32service.SERVICE_CHANGE_CONFIG)
            win32service.ChangeServiceConfig(handle, win32service.SERVICE_NO_CHANGE, type_map[startup_type.lower()],
                                             win32service.SERVICE_NO_CHANGE, None, None, None, None, None, None, None)
            win32service.CloseServiceHandle(handle)
            win32service.CloseServiceHandle(scm)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Set {service_name} startup to {startup_type}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to set service startup: {e}"


def _get_service_details(self, service_name: str) -> str:
        try:
            scm = win32service.OpenSCManager(None, None, win32service.SC_MANAGER_CONNECT)
            handle = win32service.OpenService(scm, service_name, win32service.SERVICE_QUERY_CONFIG | win32service.SERVICE_QUERY_STATUS)
            config = win32service.QueryServiceConfig(handle)
            status = win32service.QueryServiceStatus(handle)
            win32service.CloseServiceHandle(handle)
            win32service.CloseServiceHandle(scm)
            result = f"""[SERVICE DETAILS] {service_name}
    Display Name: {config[1]}
    Binary Path: {config[3]}
    Start Type: {['BOOT', 'SYSTEM', 'AUTO', 'DEMAND', 'DISABLED'][config[1]] if config[1] <= 4 else 'UNKNOWN'}
    Service Type: {config[0]}
    Status: {['STOPPED', 'START_PENDING', 'STOP_PENDING', 'RUNNING', 'CONTINUE_PENDING', 'PAUSE_PENDING', 'PAUSED'][status[1]] if status[1] <= 7 else 'UNKNOWN'}
    Process ID: {status[4]}"""
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get service details: {e}"


def _list_scheduled_tasks(self, filter: str = '') -> str:
        try:
            scheduler = Dispatch('Schedule.Service')
            scheduler.Connect()
            root = scheduler.GetFolder('\\\\')
            tasks = root.GetTasks(0)
            result = f"[SCHEDULED TASKS]"
            if filter:
                result += f" (filter: '{filter}')"
            result += "\n"
            count = 0
            for task in tasks:
                name = task.Name
                if filter and filter.lower() not in name.lower():
                    continue
                state = ['UNKNOWN', 'DISABLED', 'QUEUED', 'READY', 'RUNNING'][task.State] if task.State <= 4 else 'UNKNOWN'
                result += f"  {name} - {state}\n"
                count += 1
            result += f"Total: {count} tasks"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to list scheduled tasks: {e}"


def _create_scheduled_task(self, task_name: str, command: str, arguments: str = '',
                               trigger: str = '', time: str = '', days: str = '') -> str:
        try:
            scheduler = Dispatch('Schedule.Service')
            scheduler.Connect()
            root = scheduler.GetFolder('\\\\')
            task_def = scheduler.NewTask(0)
            action = task_def.Actions.Create(0)
            action.Path = command
            if arguments:
                action.Arguments = arguments
            if trigger:
                if trigger.lower() == 'daily' and time:
                    trigger_obj = task_def.Triggers.Create(1)
                    trigger_obj.StartBoundary = f"{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}T{time}:00"
                    trigger_obj.DaysInterval = 1
                elif trigger.lower() == 'weekly' and time:
                    trigger_obj = task_def.Triggers.Create(2)
                    trigger_obj.StartBoundary = f"{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}T{time}:00"
                    if days:
                        days_map = {'MON': 1, 'TUE': 2, 'WED': 4, 'THU': 8, 'FRI': 16, 'SAT': 32, 'SUN': 64}
                        mask = sum(days_map.get(d.upper(), 0) for d in days.split(','))
                        trigger_obj.DaysOfWeek = mask
                    trigger_obj.WeeksInterval = 1
                elif trigger.lower() == 'at_startup':
                    trigger_obj = task_def.Triggers.Create(8)
                elif trigger.lower() == 'at_logon':
                    trigger_obj = task_def.Triggers.Create(9)
            task_def.RegistrationInfo.Description = "Created by writing assistant"
            task_def.Settings.Enabled = True
            task_def.Settings.StopIfGoingOnBatteries = False
            task_def.Settings.DisallowStartIfOnBatteries = False
            root.RegisterTaskDefinition(task_name, task_def, 6, None, None, 3)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Created scheduled task: {task_name}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to create scheduled task: {e}"


def _delete_scheduled_task(self, task_name: str) -> str:
        try:
            scheduler = Dispatch('Schedule.Service')
            scheduler.Connect()
            root = scheduler.GetFolder('\\\\')
            root.DeleteTask(task_name, 0)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Deleted scheduled task: {task_name}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to delete scheduled task: {e}"


def _run_scheduled_task(self, task_name: str) -> str:
        try:
            scheduler = Dispatch('Schedule.Service')
            scheduler.Connect()
            root = scheduler.GetFolder('\\\\')
            task = root.GetTask(task_name)
            task.Run(None)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Ran scheduled task: {task_name}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to run scheduled task: {e}"


def _get_clipboard(self) -> str:
        try:
            win32clipboard.OpenClipboard()
            data = win32clipboard.GetClipboardData()
            win32clipboard.CloseClipboard()
            return f"[CLIPBOARD]\n{data}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get clipboard: {e}"


def _set_clipboard(self, text: str) -> str:
        try:
            win32clipboard.OpenClipboard()
            win32clipboard.EmptyClipboard()
            win32clipboard.SetClipboardText(text)
            win32clipboard.CloseClipboard()
            return f"[SUCCESS] Set clipboard: {len(text)} chars"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to set clipboard: {e}"


def _get_cursor_position(self) -> str:
        try:
            x, y = win32api.GetCursorPos()
            return f"[CURSOR] Position: ({x}, {y})"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get cursor position: {e}"


def _set_cursor_position(self, x: int, y: int) -> str:
        try:
            win32api.SetCursorPos((x, y))
            return f"[SUCCESS] Moved cursor to ({x}, {y})"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to set cursor position: {e}"


def _click_mouse(self, x: int = None, y: int = None, button: str = 'left', clicks: int = 1,
                     sequence: str = '', interval: float = 0.05) -> str:
        """序列化鼠标点击：一次调用执行整串点击动作。
        - sequence 非空时执行序列：每项 "x,y[,button][,clicks]"，分号分隔
          例 "100,200;300,400,right;500,500,left,2"
        - sequence 为空时退化为单点点击（x/y/button/clicks）
        """
        try:
            btn_map = {'left': win32con.MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTDOWN, 'right': win32con.MOUSEEVENTF_RIGHTDOWN,
                       'middle': win32con.MOUSEEVENTF_MIDDLEDOWN}
            up_map = {'left': win32con.MOUSEEVENTF_LEFTUP, 'right': win32con.MOUSEEVENTF_RIGHTUP,
                      'middle': win32con.MOUSEEVENTF_MIDDLEUP}
            def _do_click(bx, by, b, n):
                win32api.SetCursorPos((bx, by))
                for _ in range(max(1, n)):
                    win32api.mouse_event(btn_map[b], 0, 0, 0, 0)
                    win32api.mouse_event(up_map[b], 0, 0, 0, 0)
                    if n > 1:
                        time.sleep(0.05)
            if sequence:
                items = [s.strip() for s in sequence.split(';') if s.strip()]
                done, failed = 0, []
                for i, item in enumerate(items):
                    try:
                        parts = [p.strip() for p in item.split(',')]
                        cx, cy = int(parts[0]), int(parts[1])
                        cb = parts[2].lower() if len(parts) > 2 else 'left'
                        cc = int(parts[3]) if len(parts) > 3 else 1
                        if cb not in btn_map:
                            raise ValueError(f"bad button '{cb}'")
                        _do_click(cx, cy, cb, cc)
                        done += 1
                    except Exception as _ie:
                        failed.append(f"#{i + 1}({item}): {_ie}")
                    if i < len(items) - 1 and interval and interval > 0:
                        time.sleep(min(float(interval), 1.0))
                pos = win32api.GetCursorPos()
                out = f"[SUCCESS] {done}/{len(items)} click-steps, cursor at ({pos[0]}, {pos[1]})"
                if failed:
                    out += "\n[WARN] 失败步骤: " + '; '.join(failed)
                return out
            b = (button or 'left').lower()
            if b not in btn_map:
                return f"[ERROR] Invalid button. Use: left, right, middle"
            if x is not None and y is not None:
                win32api.SetCursorPos((x, y))
            _do_click(x if x is not None else win32api.GetCursorPos()[0],
                      y if y is not None else win32api.GetCursorPos()[1], b, clicks or 1)
            pos = win32api.GetCursorPos()
            return f"[SUCCESS] {b} click x{clicks} at ({pos[0]}, {pos[1]})"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to click mouse: {e}"


def _scroll_mouse(self, amount: int) -> str:
        try:
            win32api.mouse_event(win32con.MOUSEEVENTF_WHEEL, 0, 0, amount * 120, 0)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Scrolled {amount} ticks"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to scroll: {e}"


def _take_screenshot(self, save_path: str = None, window_title: str = None,
                         x: int = None, y: int = None, width: int = None, height: int = None) -> str:
        try:
            if window_title:
                hwnd = self._find_window_by_title(window_title)
                if not hwnd:
                    return f"[ERROR] Window not found: {window_title}"
                rect = win32gui.GetWindowRect(hwnd)
                x, y, width, height = rect[0], rect[1], rect[2]-rect[0], rect[3]-rect[1]
            elif x is not None and y is not None and width is not None and height is not None:
                pass
            else:
                x, y, width, height = 0, 0, win32api.GetSystemMetrics(0), win32api.GetSystemMetrics(1)
            screenshot = ImageGrab.grab(bbox=(x, y, x+width, y+height))
            if save_path:
                screenshot.save(save_path)
                return f"[SUCCESS] Screenshot saved to: {save_path} ({width}x{height})"
            else:
                buffer = io.BytesIO()
                screenshot.save(buffer, format='PNG')
                buffer.seek(0)
                b64 = base64.b64encode(buffer.read()).decode('utf-8')
                return f"[SCREENSHOT] {width}x{height} PNG (base64, {len(b64)} chars)\n{b64[:500]}..."
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to take screenshot: {e}"


def _get_screen_resolution(self) -> str:
        try:
            w = win32api.GetSystemMetrics(0)
            h = win32api.GetSystemMetrics(1)
            return f"[SCREEN RESOLUTION] {w}x{h}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get screen resolution: {e}"


def _ping_host(self, host: str, count: int = 4) -> str:
        try:
            result = f"[PING] {host}\n"
            total = 0.0
            successes = 0
            for i in range(count):
                try:
                    rtt = ping3.ping(host, timeout=2)
                    if rtt is not None:
                        result += f"  Reply from {host}: time={rtt*1000:.1f}ms\n"
                        total += rtt
                        successes += 1
                    else:
                        result += f"  Request timed out\n"
                except Exception:
                    result += f"  Request timed out\n"
            if successes > 0:
                avg = (total / successes) * 1000
                result += f"  Packets: Sent={count}, Received={successes}, Lost={count-successes} ({100-count/successes*100:.0f}% loss)\n"
                result += f"  Average RTT: {avg:.1f}ms"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to ping: {e}"


def _get_ip_address(self) -> str:
        try:
            result = "[IP ADDRESSES]\n"
            hostname = socket.gethostname()
            result += f"Hostname: {hostname}\n"
            for addr in socket.gethostbyname_ex(hostname)[2]:
                result += f"  {addr}\n"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get IP addresses: {e}"


def _get_open_ports(self, filter_port: int = None) -> str:
        try:
            result = "[OPEN PORTS]\n"
            for conn in psutil.net_connections(kind='inet'):
                if conn.status == 'LISTEN':
                    if filter_port and conn.laddr.port != filter_port:
                        continue
                    result += f"  {conn.laddr.port} ({conn.laddr.ip}) - {conn.status}\n"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get open ports: {e}"


def _dns_lookup(self, host: str, record_type: str = 'A') -> str:
        try:
            result = f"[DNS LOOKUP] {host} ({record_type})\n"
            records = dns.resolver.resolve(host, record_type)
            for r in records:
                result += f"  {r}\n"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed DNS lookup: {e}"


def _download_file(self, url: str, save_path: str) -> str:
        try:
            Path(save_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            urllib.request.urlretrieve(url, save_path)
            size = Path(save_path).stat().st_size
            return f"[SUCCESS] Downloaded {url} to {save_path} ({size:,} bytes)"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to download: {e}"


def _get_url_content(self, url: str, timeout: int = 30) -> str:
        try:
            response = urllib.request.urlopen(url, timeout=timeout)
            content = response.read().decode('utf-8', errors='ignore')
            if len(content) > 5000:
                content = content[:5000] + "\n... (truncated)"
            return f"[URL CONTENT] {url}\n{content}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get URL content: {e}"


def _test_network_connectivity(self, host: str, port: int = 80) -> str:
        try:
            sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
            sock.settimeout(5)
            result = sock.connect_ex((host, port))
            sock.close()
            if result == 0:
                return f"[CONNECTIVITY] {host}:{port} is reachable"
            else:
                return f"[CONNECTIVITY] {host}:{port} is NOT reachable (error: {result})"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to test connectivity: {e}"


def _list_user_accounts(self, filter: str = '') -> str:
        try:
            result = "[USER ACCOUNTS]\n"
            users = win32net.NetUserEnum(None, 0, 0, None, 0xFFFFFFFF)
            count = 0
            for user in users[0]:
                if filter and filter.lower() not in user['name'].lower():
                    continue
                result += f"  {user['name']} ({user.get('full_name', '')})\n"
                count += 1
            result += f"Total: {count} users"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to list users: {e}"


def _get_user_info(self, username: str) -> str:
        try:
            user_info = win32net.NetUserGetInfo(None, username, 2)
            result = f"""[USER INFO] {username}
    Full Name: {user_info.get('full_name', '')}
    Comment: {user_info.get('comment', '')}
    User ID: {user_info.get('user_id', '')}
    Privilege: {user_info.get('priv', '')}
    Home Directory: {user_info.get('home_dir', '')}
    Last Logon: {user_info.get('last_logon', '')}
    Number of Logons: {user_info.get('num_logons', 0)}
    Password Age: {user_info.get('password_age', '')}"""
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get user info: {e}"


def _create_user_account(self, username: str, password: str, fullname: str = '', description: str = '') -> str:
        try:
            user_info = {
                'name': username,
                'password': password,
                'full_name': fullname,
                'comment': description,
                'flags': win32net.UF_SCRIPT | win32net.UF_DONT_EXPIRE_PASSWD
            }
            win32net.NetUserAdd(None, 1, user_info)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Created user: {username}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to create user: {e}"


def _delete_user_account(self, username: str) -> str:
        try:
            win32net.NetUserDel(None, username)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Deleted user: {username}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to delete user: {e}"


def _change_user_password(self, username: str, new_password: str) -> str:
        try:
            win32net.NetUserChangePassword(None, username, '', new_password)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Changed password for: {username}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to change password: {e}"


def _add_user_to_group(self, username: str, group: str) -> str:
        try:
            win32net.NetLocalGroupAddMembers(None, group, 0, [{'domainandname': username}])
            return f"[SUCCESS] Added {username} to {group}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to add user to group: {e}"


def _remove_user_from_group(self, username: str, group: str) -> str:
        try:
            win32net.NetLocalGroupDelMembers(None, group, 0, [{'domainandname': username}])
            return f"[SUCCESS] Removed {username} from {group}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to remove user from group: {e}"


def _set_wallpaper(self, image_path: str, style: str = 'fill') -> str:
        try:
            if not image_path or image_path.strip() == '':
                walldir = Path(self.DEFAULT_WALLPAPER_DIR)
                if walldir.exists():
                    images = []
                    for ext in ('*.jpg', '*.jpeg', '*.png', '*.bmp', '*.gif', '*.webp'):
                        images.extend(walldir.glob(ext))
                    if images:
                        image_path = str(random.choice(images))
                        self._progress(f"[壁纸] 默认目录中随机选择: {Path(image_path).name}")
                    else:
                        return f"[ERROR] No image files found in: {self.DEFAULT_WALLPAPER_DIR}"
                else:
                    return f"[ERROR] Default wallpaper directory not found: {self.DEFAULT_WALLPAPER_DIR}"
            elif Path(image_path).is_dir():
                walldir = Path(image_path)
                images = []
                for ext in ('*.jpg', '*.jpeg', '*.png', '*.bmp', '*.gif', '*.webp'):
                    images.extend(walldir.glob(ext))
                if images:
                    image_path = str(random.choice(images))
                    self._progress(f"[壁纸] 目录中随机选择: {Path(image_path).name}")
                else:
                    return f"[ERROR] No image files in directory: {image_path}"
            elif not Path(image_path).exists():
                return f"[ERROR] Image not found: {image_path}"
            style_map = {
                'fill': 0, 'fit': 6, 'stretch': 2, 'tile': 1, 'center': 0, 'span': 10
            }
            if style.lower() not in style_map:
                return f"[ERROR] Invalid style. Use: fill, fit, stretch, tile, center, span"
            key = win32api.RegOpenKeyEx(win32con.HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
                r'Control Panel\Desktop', 0, win32con.KEY_SET_VALUE)
            win32api.RegSetValueEx(key, 'WallpaperStyle', 0, win32con.REG_SZ, str(style_map[style.lower()]))
            win32api.RegSetValueEx(key, 'TileWallpaper', 0, win32con.REG_SZ, '0' if style.lower() != 'tile' else '1')
            win32api.RegCloseKey(key)
            ctypes.windll.user32.SystemParametersInfoW(20, 0, image_path, 3)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Set wallpaper to: {image_path} ({style})"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to set wallpaper: {e}"


def _set_screen_brightness(self, level: int) -> str:
        try:
            if not 0 <= level <= 100:
                return "[ERROR] Brightness must be 0-100"
            if WMI_AVAILABLE:
                try:
                    import pythoncom
                    pythoncom.CoInitialize()
                    conn = wmi.WMI(namespace='root/WMI')
                    methods = conn.WmiMonitorBrightnessMethods()
                    if methods:
                        methods[0].WmiSetBrightness(1, level)
                        pythoncom.CoUninitialize()
                        return f"[SUCCESS] 亮度已设为 {level}% (WMI)"
                    pythoncom.CoUninitialize()
                except Exception:
                    pythoncom.CoUninitialize()
            try:
                import subprocess
                ps_cmd = (
                    f'$brightness = {level}; '
                    f'$monitor = Get-WmiObject -Namespace root/WMI -Class WmiMonitorBrightnessMethods; '
                    f'$monitor.WmiSetBrightness(1, $brightness)'
                )
                subprocess.run(
                    ['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command', ps_cmd],
                    capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5
                )
                return f"[SUCCESS] 亮度已设为 {level}% (PowerShell)"
            except Exception:
                pass
            ctypes.windll.user32.SendMessageW(0xFFFF, 0x0312, 0, (level * 65535) // 100)
            return f"[SUCCESS] 亮度已设为 {level}% (SendMessage)"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to set brightness: {e}"


def _set_volume(self, level: int) -> str:
        try:
            if not 0 <= level <= 100:
                return "[ERROR] Volume must be 0-100"
            if PYCAW_AVAILABLE:
                pythoncom.CoInitialize()
                try:
                    devices = AudioUtilities.GetSpeakers()
                    devices.EndpointVolume.SetMasterVolumeLevelScalar(level / 100.0, None)
                    return f"[SUCCESS] 音量已设为 {level}%"
                finally:
                    pythoncom.CoUninitialize()
            import subprocess
            subprocess.run(
                ['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command',
                 f'(New-Object -ComObject "Shell.Application").Volume().Volume = {level}'],
                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5
            )
            return f"[SUCCESS] 音量已设为 {level}% (Shell.Application)"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to set volume: {e}"


def _get_volume(self) -> str:
        try:
            if PYCAW_AVAILABLE:
                pythoncom.CoInitialize()
                try:
                    vol = AudioUtilities.GetSpeakers().EndpointVolume.GetMasterVolumeLevelScalar()
                    return f"[INFO] 当前系统音量: {round(vol * 100)}%"
                finally:
                    pythoncom.CoUninitialize()
            import subprocess
            r = subprocess.run(
                ['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command',
                 '(New-Object -ComObject "Shell.Application").Volume().Volume'],
                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5
            )
            vol = r.stdout.strip()
            return f"[INFO] 当前音量: {vol}%" if vol else "[INFO] 无法获取音量"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[INFO] 无法获取音量: {e}"


def _mute_audio(self, mute: bool) -> str:
        try:
            if PYCAW_AVAILABLE:
                pythoncom.CoInitialize()
                try:
                    AudioUtilities.GetSpeakers().EndpointVolume.SetMute(1 if mute else 0, None)
                    return f"[SUCCESS] {'已静音' if mute else '已取消静音'}"
                finally:
                    pythoncom.CoUninitialize()
            import subprocess
            subprocess.run(
                ['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command',
                 f'(New-Object -ComObject "Shell.Application").Volume().Mute = ${"true" if mute else "false"}'],
                capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5
            )
            return f"[SUCCESS] {'已静音' if mute else '已取消静音'} (Shell.Application)"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to {'mute' if mute else 'unmute'} audio: {e}"


def _lock_workstation(self) -> str:
        try:
            ctypes.windll.user32.LockWorkStation()
            return "[SUCCESS] Locked workstation"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to lock workstation: {e}"


def _sleep_computer(self, force: bool = False) -> str:
        try:
            ctypes.windll.powrprof.SetSuspendState(0, 1 if force else 0, 0)
            return "[SUCCESS] Computer going to sleep"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to sleep: {e}"


def _restart_computer(self, force: bool = False, delay_seconds: int = 0) -> str:
        try:
            if delay_seconds > 0:
                subprocess.run(f'shutdown /r /t {delay_seconds}', shell=True)
                return f"[SUCCESS] Computer will restart in {delay_seconds} seconds"
            ctypes.windll.advapi32.InitiateSystemShutdownW(None, None, 0, True, True)
            return "[SUCCESS] Computer restarting"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to restart: {e}"


def _shutdown_computer(self, force: bool = False, delay_seconds: int = 0) -> str:
        try:
            if delay_seconds > 0:
                subprocess.run(f'shutdown /s /t {delay_seconds}', shell=True)
                return f"[SUCCESS] Computer will shut down in {delay_seconds} seconds"
            ctypes.windll.advapi32.InitiateSystemShutdownW(None, None, 0, True, False)
            return "[SUCCESS] Computer shutting down"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to shutdown: {e}"


def _logoff_user(self, force: bool = False) -> str:
        try:
            ctypes.windll.user32.ExitWindowsEx(0, 0)
            return "[SUCCESS] Logging off current user"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to logoff: {e}"


def _read_event_log(self, log_name: str, count: int = 50, filter_level: str = '') -> str:
        try:
            handle = win32evtlog.OpenEventLog(None, log_name)
            events = win32evtlog.ReadEventLog(handle, win32evtlog.EVENTLOG_BACKWARDS_READ | win32evtlog.EVENTLOG_SEQUENTIAL_READ, 0)
            result = f"[EVENT LOG] {log_name}\n"
            processed = 0
            for event in events:
                if processed >= count:
                    break
                level = {1: 'ERROR', 2: 'WARNING', 4: 'INFO'}.get(event.EventType, 'UNKNOWN')
                if filter_level and filter_level.upper() != level:
                    continue
                time_str = datetime.fromtimestamp(event.TimeGenerated.timestamp()).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
                result += f"  {time_str} | {level} | {event.EventID} | {event.SourceName}\n"
                if event.StringInserts:
                    result += f"    {event.StringInserts[0][:80]}...\n"
                processed += 1
            win32evtlog.CloseEventLog(handle)
            result += f"Displayed: {processed} events"
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to read event log: {e}"


def _clear_event_log(self, log_name: str) -> str:
        try:
            handle = win32evtlog.OpenEventLog(None, log_name)
            win32evtlog.ClearEventLog(handle, None)
            win32evtlog.CloseEventLog(handle)
            return f"[SUCCESS] Cleared event log: {log_name}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to clear event log: {e}"


def _get_audit_policy(self) -> str:
        try:
            policy = win32security.LsaOpenPolicy(None, win32security.POLICY_VIEW_AUDIT_INFORMATION)
            categories = ['Account Logon', 'Logon', 'Object Access', 'Privilege Use', 'Detailed Tracking',
                          'Policy Change', 'Account Management', 'Directory Access', 'System Events']
            result = "[AUDIT POLICY]\n"
            for i, cat in enumerate(categories):
                try:
                    audit = win32security.LsaQueryInformationPolicy(policy, win32security.PolicyAuditEventsInformation)
                    result += f"  {cat}: {audit[i]}\n"
                except Exception: pass
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Failed to get audit policy: {e}"


def _get_file_audit_log(self, file_path: str, count: int = 20) -> str:
        """读取与指定文件相关的对象访问审计日志(4656/4663)"""
        try:
            name_part = os.path.basename(file_path.replace('/', chr(92)))
            count = max(1, min(int(count), 200))
            ps = (
                "$ev = Get-WinEvent -FilterHashtable @{LogName='Security'; Id=4656,4663} "
                "-MaxEvents 500 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | "
                "Where-Object { $_.Message -match [regex]::Escape('%s') }; "
                "if ($ev) { $ev | Select-Object -First %d | ForEach-Object { "
                "$_.TimeCreated.ToString('yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss') + ' | ' + "
                "($_.Message -split [char]10 | Where-Object { $_ -match 'Object Name|Process Name|Accesses' } | "
                "ForEach-Object { $_.Trim() }) -join ' | ' } } "
                "else { 'NO_EVENTS' }"
            ) % (name_part, count)
            r = subprocess.run(['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command', ps],
                               capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
            out = (r.stdout or '').strip()
            if 'NO_EVENTS' in out or not out:
                return ("[FILE AUDIT] '%s' 无匹配的文件访问审计事件。\n"
                        "提示: 对象访问审计需在组策略中启用(审核对象访问)且文件需配置 SACL。"
                        % file_path)
            rows = out.splitlines()[:count]
            return "[FILE AUDIT] '%s' 最近 %d 条访问记录:\n" % (file_path, len(rows)) + "\n".join(rows)[:4000]
        except Exception as e:
            return "[ERROR] 读取文件审计日志失败: %s" % e
