            r = subprocess.run('vssadmin delete shadows /all /quiet', shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
            return f"[SHADOW] Deleted all shadow copies: {r.stdout.strip() or r.stderr.strip() or 'Done'}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Shadow copy delete failed: {e}"


def _hibernate_clear(self) -> str:
        """休眠文件清除"""
        try:
            r = subprocess.run('powercfg -h off', shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15)
            return f"[HIBERNATE] Disabled hibernation (hiberfil.sys deleted): {r.stdout.strip() or r.stderr.strip() or 'Done'}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Hibernate clear failed: {e}"


def _tor_proxy(self, action: str, url: str = None) -> str:
        """TOR代理"""
        try:
            if action == "start":
                r = subprocess.run('tasklist /fi "imagename eq tor.exe"', shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
                if 'tor.exe' in r.stdout.lower():
                    return "[TOR] Already running"
                tor_path = r"C:\Tor\tor.exe"
                if os.path.exists(tor_path):
                    subprocess.Popen([tor_path], creationflags=0x8)
                    return "[TOR] Started"
                else:
                    return "[TOR] tor.exe not found at C:\\Tor\\tor.exe. Install TOR Browser."
            elif action == "stop":
                subprocess.run('taskkill /f /im tor.exe', shell=True, capture_output=True, timeout=5)
                return "[TOR] Stopped"
            elif action == "check":
                if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
                    return "[ERROR] requests not available"
                try:
                    r = requests.get(url or "https://check.torproject.org/api/ip", proxies={'http': 'socks5://127.0.0.1:9050', 'https': 'socks5://127.0.0.1:9050'}, timeout=10)
                    return f"[TOR] Check: {r.text[:200]}"
                except Exception:
                    return "[TOR] Not connected (proxy at 127.0.0.1:9050 unreachable)"
            else:
                return f"[ERROR] Unknown action: {action}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] TOR proxy failed: {e}"


def _proxy_rotate(self, count: int = None, test_url: str = None) -> str:
        """代理轮换"""
        try:
            if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] requests not available"
            n = count or 5
            url = test_url or "http://httpbin.org/ip"
            results = []
            try:
                r = requests.get("https://api.proxyscrape.com/v2/?request=displayproxies&protocol=http&timeout=5000", timeout=10)
                proxies = [p.strip() for p in r.text.strip().split('\n') if p.strip()][:n]
            except Exception:
                proxies = []
            for proxy in proxies:
                try:
                    r = requests.get(url, proxies={'http': f'http://{proxy}', 'https': f'http://{proxy}'}, timeout=5)
                    results.append(f"[OK] {proxy} -> {r.text[:100]}")
                except Exception:
                    results.append(f"[FAIL] {proxy}")
            return f"[PROXY ROTATE] Tested {len(proxies)} proxies:\n" + "\n".join(results) if results else "[PROXY] No proxies available"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Proxy rotate failed: {e}"


def _ua_randomize(self, count: int = None) -> str:
        """User-Agent随机化"""
        try:
            n = count or 5
            ua_pool = [
                'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 Chrome/125.0.0.0 Safari/537.36',
                'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64; rv:126.0) Gecko/20100101 Firefox/126.0',
                'Mozilla/5.0 (Macintosh; Intel Mac OS X 14_5) AppleWebKit/605.1.15 Version/17.5 Safari/605.1.15',
                'Mozilla/5.0 (X11; Linux x86_64) AppleWebKit/537.36 Chrome/124.0.0.0 Safari/537.36',
                'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 Chrome/125.0.0.0 Safari/537.36 Edg/125.0.0.0',
                'Mozilla/5.0 (iPhone; CPU iPhone OS 17_5 like Mac OS X) AppleWebKit/605.1.15 Version/17.5 Mobile/15E148 Safari/604.1',
                'Mozilla/5.0 (iPad; CPU OS 17_5 like Mac OS X) AppleWebKit/605.1.15 Version/17.5 Mobile/15E148 Safari/604.1',
                'Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 Chrome/124.0.0.0 Safari/537.36 OPR/110.0.0.0',
            ]
            selected = [random.choice(ua_pool) for _ in range(n)]
            return "[UA RANDOM] " + "\n".join(selected)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] UA randomize failed: {e}"


def _request_time_jitter(self, urls: str, delay_range: str = None) -> str:
        """请求时间扰动"""
        try:
            if not REQUESTS_AVAILABLE:
                return "[ERROR] requests not available"
            url_list = [u.strip() for u in urls.split(',') if u.strip()]
            lo, hi = 1, 5
            if delay_range and '-' in delay_range:
                parts = delay_range.split('-')
                lo, hi = float(parts[0]), float(parts[1])
            results = []
            for url in url_list:
                delay = random.uniform(lo, hi)
                time.sleep(delay)
                try:
                    r = requests.get(url, timeout=10, verify=False)
                    results.append(f"[{delay:.1f}s] {url} -> {r.status_code}")
                except Exception as ex:
                    results.append(f"[{delay:.1f}s] {url} -> ERROR: {ex}")
            return "[JITTER] Results:\n" + "\n".join(results)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] Request time jitter failed: {e}"


def _check_dll_hijack(self, path: str) -> str:
        """扫描系统路径中的可劫持DLL"""
        try:
            results = []
            target = path.strip('"')
            if os.path.isfile(target):
                target_dir = os.path.dirname(target)
                exe_name = os.path.basename(target).lower()
            else:
                target_dir = target
                exe_name = None
            if not os.path.isdir(target_dir):
                return f"[ERROR] 目录不存在: {target_dir}"
            sys_dirs = [os.environ.get('SystemRoot', r'C:\Windows') + '\\System32',
                        os.environ.get('SystemRoot', r'C:\Windows')]
            dlls_in_dir = [f for f in os.listdir(target_dir) if f.lower().endswith(('.dll', '.ocx', '.sys'))]
            for dll in dlls_in_dir:
                found_in_sys = False
                for sd in sys_dirs:
                    if os.path.exists(os.path.join(sd, dll)):
                        found_in_sys = True
                        break
                if not found_in_sys:
                    results.append(f"[HIJACKABLE] {dll} — 不在System32中，可被劫持")
            if not results:
                return "[OK] 未发现可劫持DLL"
            return "[DLL HIJACK SCAN]\n" + "\n".join(results)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] DLL劫持扫描失败: {e}"


def _list_drivers(self) -> str:
        """列出所有已加载的内核驱动程序"""
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(['driverquery', '/FO', 'CSV'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15)
            if result.returncode == 0:
                return "[DRIVERS]\n" + result.stdout[:4000]
            return "[ERROR] driverquery 执行失败"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 列出驱动失败: {e}"


def _get_network_stats(self) -> str:
        """获取实时网络流量统计（按进程）"""
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(['netstat', '-b', '-n'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
            lines = result.stdout.strip().split('\n')
            proc_connections = {}
            current_proc = None
            for line in lines:
                if line.startswith('  ') and '[' in line:
                    current_proc = line.strip().strip('[]')
                elif line.startswith('  ') and current_proc:
                    parts = line.split()
                    if len(parts) >= 3:
                        proc_connections.setdefault(current_proc, []).append(parts[2])
            output = []
            for proc, conns in sorted(proc_connections.items(), key=lambda x: -len(x[1]))[:20]:
                output.append(f"{proc}: {len(conns)} 连接")
            return "[NET STATS]\n" + "\n".join(output) if output else "[OK] 无活跃网络连接"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 网络统计失败: {e}"


def _analyze_pe(self, file_path: str) -> str:
        """分析EXE/DLL文件的PE结构"""
        try:
            fp = file_path.strip('"')
            if not os.path.isfile(fp):
                return f"[ERROR] 文件不存在: {fp}"
            with open(fp, 'rb') as f:
                mz = f.read(2)
                if mz != b'MZ':
                    return "[ERROR] 不是有效的PE文件"
                f.seek(0x3C)
                pe_offset = int.from_bytes(f.read(4), 'little')
                f.seek(pe_offset)
                pe_sig = f.read(4)
                if pe_sig != b'PE\x00\x00':
                    return "[ERROR] PE签名无效"
                machine = int.from_bytes(f.read(2), 'little')
                num_sections = int.from_bytes(f.read(2), 'little')
                timestamp = int.from_bytes(f.read(4), 'little')
                f.read(8)
                optional_size = int.from_bytes(f.read(2), 'little')
                characteristics = int.from_bytes(f.read(2), 'little')
            machine_map = {0x14c: 'x86', 0x8664: 'x64', 0x1c0: 'ARM', 0xaa64: 'ARM64'}
            chars = []
            if characteristics & 0x0002: chars.append('EXECUTABLE')
            if characteristics & 0x0020: chars.append('LARGE_ADDRESS_AWARE')
            if characteristics & 0x2000: chars.append('DLL')
            from datetime import datetime as _dt
            try:
                ts_str = _dt.utcfromtimestamp(timestamp).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC')
            except Exception:
                ts_str = str(timestamp)
            return (f"[PE ANALYSIS] {os.path.basename(fp)}\n"
                    f"  Machine: {machine_map.get(machine, hex(machine))}\n"
                    f"  Sections: {num_sections}\n"
                    f"  Compile Time: {ts_str}\n"
                    f"  Characteristics: {', '.join(chars) if chars else 'NONE'}\n"
                    f"  Optional Header Size: {optional_size}")
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] PE分析失败: {e}"


def _scan_lsa_protection(self) -> str:
        """检测LSA保护（PPL）状态"""
        try:
            key_path = r"SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Control\Lsa"
            key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, key_path, 0, winreg.KEY_READ)
            run_as_ppl, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(key, "RunAsPPL")
            winreg.CloseKey(key)
            if run_as_ppl == 2:
                return "[LSA] PPL 已启用 (RunAsPPL=2, Protected Process Light)"
            elif run_as_ppl == 1:
                return "[LSA] PPL 已启用 (RunAsPPL=1, Protected Process)"
            else:
                return f"[LSA] PPL 未启用 (RunAsPPL={run_as_ppl})"
        except FileNotFoundError:
            return "[LSA] RunAsPPL 键不存在 — PPL 未配置"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] LSA检测失败: {e}"


def _list_hidden_users(self) -> str:
        """检测隐藏的本地用户账户"""
        try:
            key_path = r"SAM\SAM\Domains\Account\Users"
            key = winreg.OpenKey(winreg.HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE, key_path, 0, winreg.KEY_READ)
            rid_names = {}
            i = 0
            while True:
                try:
                    subkey_name = winreg.EnumKey(key, i)
                    try:
                        rid = int(subkey_name, 16)
                    except ValueError:
                        i += 1; continue
                    subkey = winreg.OpenKey(key, subkey_name, 0, winreg.KEY_READ)
                    try:
                        v, _ = winreg.QueryValueEx(subkey, "V")
                        if isinstance(v, bytes) and len(v) > 0xCC:
                            name_offset = int.from_bytes(v[0xCC:0xD0], 'little')
                            name_len = int.from_bytes(v[0xD0:0xD4], 'little')
                            if name_len > 0:
                                name = v[name_offset:name_offset+name_len].decode('utf-16-le', errors='replace')
                                rid_names[rid] = name
                    except Exception: pass
                    winreg.CloseKey(subkey)
                    i += 1
                except OSError:
                    break
            winreg.CloseKey(key)
            hidden = []
            for rid, name in rid_names.items():
                if rid >= 1000 and (rid & 0x3E8) != 0x3E8:
                    hidden.append(f"RID={rid:#x} Name={name}")
            if not hidden:
                return "[OK] 未发现隐藏用户"
            return "[HIDDEN USERS]\n" + "\n".join(hidden)
        except PermissionError:
            return "[ERROR] 需要SYSTEM权限访问SAM"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 检测隐藏用户失败: {e}"


def _enable_admin_hidden(self, enable: str) -> str:
        """启用或禁用内置管理员账户"""
        try:
            action = "/active:yes" if enable.lower() in ("yes", "y", "1") else "/active:no"
            result = subprocess.run(['net', 'user', 'administrator', action], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
            state = "启用" if "yes" in action else "禁用"
            if result.returncode == 0:
                return f"[OK] 内置Administrator账户已{state}"
            return f"[ERROR] 操作失败: {result.stderr.strip()}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 管理员账户操作失败: {e}"


def _get_privileges(self) -> str:
        """列出当前进程的所有权限"""
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(['whoami', '/priv'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
            if result.returncode == 0:
                return "[PRIVILEGES]\n" + result.stdout.strip()
            return "[ERROR] whoami /priv 执行失败"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 获取权限失败: {e}"


def _take_ownership(self, target: str) -> str:
        """夺取文件或文件夹的所有权"""
        try:
            t = target.strip('"')
            result = subprocess.run(['takeown', '/f', t, '/a'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15)
            if result.returncode == 0:
                icacls = subprocess.run(['icacls', t, '/grant', 'Administrators:F'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15)
                return f"[OK] 已夺取所有权并授予完全控制: {t}"
            return f"[ERROR] takeown失败: {result.stderr.strip()}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 夺取所有权失败: {e}"


def _clear_dns_cache(self) -> str:
        """清除DNS缓存"""
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(['ipconfig', '/flushdns'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
            if result.returncode == 0:
                return "[OK] DNS缓存已清除"
            return f"[ERROR] {result.stderr.strip()}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] DNS清除失败: {e}"


def _clear_temp_files(self) -> str:
        """清理系统临时文件夹"""
        try:
            total = 0
            dirs = [os.environ.get('TEMP', ''), os.environ.get('TMP', ''),
                    r'C:\Windows\Temp', r'C:\Windows\Prefetch']
            for d in dirs:
                if not d or not os.path.isdir(d):
                    continue
                for root, _, files in os.walk(d):
                    for f in files:
                        try:
                            fp = os.path.join(root, f)
                            sz = os.path.getsize(fp)
                            os.remove(fp)
                            total += sz
                        except Exception: pass
            return f"[OK] 临时文件已清理，释放 {total/1024/1024:.1f} MB"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 清理失败: {e}"


def _analyze_boot_time(self) -> str:
        """分析系统启动耗时"""
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(['wmic', 'startup', 'list', 'brief'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
            wevt = subprocess.run(['wevtutil', 'qe', 'System', '/q:*[System[(EventID=100)]]', '/c:1', '/f:text'],
                                  capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
            boot_info = wevt.stdout.strip() if wevt.stdout else "N/A"
            return f"[BOOT TIME]\n启动项:\n{result.stdout.strip()[:2000]}\n\n最近启动事件:\n{boot_info[:1000]}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 启动分析失败: {e}"


def _list_recent_docs(self) -> str:
        """列出最近打开的文档"""
        try:
            recent_dir = os.path.join(os.environ.get('APPDATA', ''), 'Microsoft', 'Windows', 'Recent')
            if not os.path.isdir(recent_dir):
                return "[ERROR] 最近文档目录不存在"
            items = []
            for f in os.listdir(recent_dir):
                fp = os.path.join(recent_dir, f)
                mtime = os.path.getmtime(fp)
                items.append((mtime, f))
            items.sort(reverse=True)
            from datetime import datetime as _dt
            output = []
            for mtime, name in items[:30]:
                ts = _dt.fromtimestamp(mtime).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M')
                output.append(f"  [{ts}] {name}")
            return "[RECENT DOCS]\n" + "\n".join(output) if output else "[OK] 无最近文档"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 列出最近文档失败: {e}"


def _clean_recycle_bin(self, shred: str = "no") -> str:
        """清空回收站"""
        try:
            if shred.lower() in ("yes", "y", "1"):
                recycle_dirs = [r'C:\$Recycle.Bin']
                for rd in recycle_dirs:
                    if not os.path.isdir(rd):
                        continue
                    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(rd):
                        for f in files:
                            try:
                                fp = os.path.join(root, f)
                                sz = os.path.getsize(fp)
                                with open(fp, 'wb') as fh:
                                    fh.write(os.urandom(sz))
                                os.remove(fp)
                            except Exception: pass
                return "[OK] 回收站已粉碎清空"
            else:
                result = subprocess.run(['rd', '/s', '/q', r'C:\$Recycle.Bin'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
                return "[OK] 回收站已清空"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 清空回收站失败: {e}"


def _decrypt_browser(self, browser: str) -> str:
        """解密浏览器保存的密码"""
        try:
            b = browser.lower().strip()
            if b == 'chrome':
                login_db = os.path.join(os.environ.get('LOCALAPPDATA', ''),
                                        'Google', 'Chrome', 'User Data', 'Default', 'Login Data')
            elif b == 'edge':
                login_db = os.path.join(os.environ.get('LOCALAPPDATA', ''),
                                        'Microsoft', 'Edge', 'User Data', 'Default', 'Login Data')
            elif b == 'firefox':
                profile_dir = os.path.join(os.environ.get('APPDATA', ''), 'Mozilla', 'Firefox', 'Profiles')
                if os.path.isdir(profile_dir):
                    profiles = [d for d in os.listdir(profile_dir) if d.endswith('.default-release')]
                    if profiles:
                        login_db = os.path.join(profile_dir, profiles[0], 'logins.json')
                    else:
                        return "[ERROR] 未找到Firefox默认配置"
                else:
                    return "[ERROR] Firefox配置目录不存在"
            else:
                return f"[ERROR] 不支持的浏览器: {browser}"
            if not os.path.isfile(login_db):
                return f"[ERROR] 密码数据库不存在: {login_db}"
            import shutil, tempfile
            tmp = tempfile.mktemp(suffix='.db')
            shutil.copy2(login_db, tmp)
            import sqlite3
            conn = sqlite3.connect(tmp)
            cur = conn.cursor()
            try:
                cur.execute("SELECT origin_url, username_value, password_value FROM logins")
                rows = cur.fetchall()
            except Exception:
                cur.execute("SELECT hostname, encryptedUsername, encryptedPassword FROM moz_logins")
                rows = cur.fetchall()
            conn.close()
            os.unlink(tmp)
            output = []
            for row in rows[:20]:
                output.append(f"  URL: {row[0]}\n  User: {row[1]}\n  Pass: [加密，需DPAPI解密]")
            return f"[BROWSER DECRYPT] {browser} — 找到{len(rows)}条记录\n" + "\n".join(output)
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 浏览器解密失败: {e}"


def _credential_unlock(self, data: str, key: str = None,
                           entropy: str = None, mode: str = 'auto') -> str:
        """解密凭据：给密文进，明文出。

        以前模型拿到浏览器dump只会看到 [encrypted:Nbytes]，还得自己去写
        DPAPI 脚本绕。这里把解密做成一步到位的工具：
          mode=dpapi      纯 DPAPI blob（base64/hex 均可）
          mode=gcm        v10/v11 的 chromium 值，需要提供 key（base64 主密钥）
          mode=auto       自动识别：带 v10/v11 前缀走 GCM，否则走 DPAPI
        返回明文；解不开会明确说清是哪一步失败。"""
        try:
            raw = base64.b64decode(data) if data.startswith(('DPAPI', 'v1', 'v2')) \
                or len(data) % 4 == 0 else bytes.fromhex(data)
        except Exception:
            return "[ERROR] data 既不是合法 base64 也不是合法 hex"
        if not raw:
            return "[ERROR] data 为空"
        try:
            key_b = base64.b64decode(key) if key else None
            ent_b = base64.b64decode(entropy) if entropy else None
            if mode == 'dpapi':
                plain = self._dpapi_unprotect(raw, ent_b)
            elif mode == 'gcm':
                if not key_b:
                    return "[SYNTAX] mode=gcm 必须提供 key（base64 的 AES 主密钥）"
                if len(raw) < 3 + 12 + 16:
                    return "[ERROR] 数据过短，不像 v10/v11 格式"
                plain = self._aes_gcm_decrypt(key_b, raw[3:15], raw[15:-16], raw[-16:])
            else:
                if raw[:3] == b'v20':
                    return ("[未解密: App-Bound 加密] v20 需要浏览器 Elevation "
                            "Service 在 SYSTEM 上下文解密，用户态无法完成；"
                            "这不是密钥问题，勿重试")
                if raw[:3] in (b'v10', b'v11'):
                    if not key_b:
                        return ("[NEED KEY] 这是 v10/v11 密文，需要 AES 主密钥。"
                                "用 key=<Local State 里 os_crypt.encrypted_key "
                                "经 DPAPI 解出的 base64 密钥>，或直接调用 "
                                "decrypt_browser 让工具自动取")
                    plain = self._aes_gcm_decrypt(key_b, raw[3:15], raw[15:-16],
                                                  raw[-16:])
                else:
                    plain = self._dpapi_unprotect(raw, ent_b)
            try:
                text = plain.decode('utf-8')
            except UnicodeDecodeError:
                text = plain.hex()
            return f"[CRED] 解密成功（{len(plain)} 字节）\n明文: {text}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 解密失败: {e}"


def _verify_signature(self, file_path: str) -> str:
        """验证文件的数字签名"""
        try:
            fp = file_path.strip('"')
            result = subprocess.run(['sigverif', '/verify'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
            try:
                import ctypes
                sig = ctypes.windll.wintrust
                from ctypes import wintypes
                WINTRUST_ACTION_GENERIC_VERIFY_V2 = ctypes.cGUID(0xaac56b, 0xcd44, 0x11d0,
                    0x8c, 0xc2, 0x00, 0xc0, 0x4f, 0xc2, 0x95, 0xee)
                path_w = ctypes.c_wchar_p(fp)
                r = ctypes.windll.crypt32.CryptVerifyMessageSignature(0, None, None, 0, None, None, None)
            except Exception: pass
            result2 = subprocess.run(['powershell', '-Command',
                         f'(Get-AuthenticodeSignature "{fp}").Status'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
            status = result2.stdout.strip() if result2.stdout else "Unknown"
            return f"[SIGNATURE] {os.path.basename(fp)}: {status}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 签名验证失败: {e}"


def _generate_cert(self, cn: str = "localhost", days: str = "365") -> str:
        """生成自签名SSL证书"""
        try:
            common_name = cn or "localhost"
            cert_days = int(days) if days.isdigit() else 365
            key_file = "selfsigned.key"
            cert_file = "selfsigned.pem"
            result = subprocess.run(['openssl', 'req', '-x509', '-newkey', 'rsa:2048',
                                     '-keyout', key_file, '-out', cert_file,
                                     '-days', str(cert_days), '-nodes',
                                     '-subj', f'/CN={common_name}'],
                                    capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15)
            if result.returncode == 0:
                return f"[OK] 证书已生成: {cert_file}, 密钥: {key_file}\nCN={common_name}, 有效期={cert_days}天"
            return f"[ERROR] openssl失败: {result.stderr.strip()}"
        except FileNotFoundError:
            return "[ERROR] openssl未安装"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 证书生成失败: {e}"


def _list_encrypted_files(self, path: str) -> str:
        """列出被EFS加密的文件"""
        try:
            p = path.strip('"')
            results = []
            for root, dirs, files in os.walk(p):
                for f in files:
                    fp = os.path.join(root, f)
                    try:
                        import ctypes
                        encrypted = ctypes.windll.advapi32.FileEncryptionStatusW(fp, ctypes.byref(ctypes.c_uint()))
                        if encrypted == 0:
                            results.append(fp)
                    except Exception: pass
            if not results:
                return "[OK] 未发现EFS加密文件"
            return f"[EFS ENCRYPTED] 发现{len(results)}个加密文件:\n" + "\n".join(results[:30])
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] EFS扫描失败: {e}"


def _test_sqlite_injection(self, db_path: str, table: str = "") -> str:
        """本地SQLite文件注入测试"""
        try:
            dp = db_path.strip('"')
            import sqlite3
            conn = sqlite3.connect(dp)
            cur = conn.cursor()
            if table:
                payloads = ["' OR '1'='1", "1; DROP TABLE--", "' UNION SELECT * FROM sqlite_master--",
                            "1 AND 1=1", "' OR 1=1 --"]
                results = []
                for payload in payloads:
                    try:
                        cur.execute(f"SELECT * FROM {table} WHERE rowid='{payload}'")
                        r = cur.fetchall()
                        results.append(f"  [{payload}] → {len(r)} rows (可能注入)")
                    except Exception as ex:
                        results.append(f"  [{payload}] → ERROR: {ex} (已过滤)")
                conn.close()
                return "[SQL INJECTION TEST]\n" + "\n".join(results)
            else:
                cur.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")
                tables = [r[0] for r in cur.fetchall()]
                conn.close()
                return f"[SQLITE] 表列表: {', '.join(tables)}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] SQLite注入测试失败: {e}"


def _fuzz_local_api(self, url: str, count: str = "50") -> str:
        """对本地HTTP API进行模糊测试"""
        try:
            n = int(count) if count.isdigit() else 50
            import urllib.request, urllib.error, random, string
            results = {"crash": 0, "error_5xx": 0, "error_4xx": 0, "ok": 0}
            for i in range(n):
                payload = ''.join(random.choices(string.printable, k=random.randint(1, 200)))
                try:
                    req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=payload.encode(), method='POST')
                    req.add_header('Content-Type', 'application/octet-stream')
                    resp = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=3)
                    results["ok"] += 1
                except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
                    if e.code >= 500:
                        results["error_5xx"] += 1
                    else:
                        results["error_4xx"] += 1
                except Exception:
                    results["crash"] += 1
            return (f"[FUZZ] {url} × {n}次\n"
                    f"  OK: {results['ok']}\n"
                    f"  4xx: {results['error_4xx']}\n"
                    f"  5xx: {results['error_5xx']}\n"
                    f"  Crash/Timeout: {results['crash']}")
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 模糊测试失败: {e}"


def _check_smb_signing(self) -> str:
        """检查SMB签名状态"""
        try:
            result = subprocess.run(['powershell', '-Command',
                         'Get-SmbServerConfiguration | Select-Object RequireSecuritySignature, EnableSecuritySignature'],
                        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
            if result.returncode == 0:
                return "[SMB SIGNING]\n" + result.stdout.strip()
            reg = subprocess.run(['reg', 'query', r'HKLM\SYSTEM\CurrentControlSet\Services\LanmanServer\Parameters',
                                  '/v', 'requiresecuritysignature'], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=5)
            return "[SMB SIGNING] (Registry)\n" + reg.stdout.strip()
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] SMB签名检查失败: {e}"


def _attach_debugger(self, pid: str) -> str:
        """附加调试器到进程"""
        try:
            p = int(pid)
            import ctypes
            kernel32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
            PROCESS_ALL_ACCESS = 0x1F0FFF
            handle = kernel32.OpenProcess(PROCESS_ALL_ACCESS, False, p)
            if not handle:
                return f"[ERROR] 无法打开进程 PID={p} (权限不足)"
            DebugActiveProcess = kernel32.DebugActiveProcess
            DebugActiveProcess.argtypes = [ctypes.c_uint]
            DebugActiveProcess.restype = ctypes.c_bool
            if DebugActiveProcess(p):
                kernel32.CloseHandle(handle)
                return f"[OK] 调试器已附加到 PID={p}"
            kernel32.CloseHandle(handle)
            return f"[ERROR] 调试附加失败 PID={p} (可能已是PPL)"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 调试附加失败: {e}"


def _dump_memory(self, pid: str, output: str = "") -> str:
        """转储进程内存"""
        try:
            p = int(pid)
            out = output.strip('"') if output else f"memdump_{p}.bin"
            import ctypes
            kernel32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
            PROCESS_VM_READ = 0x0010
            PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION = 0x0400
            handle = kernel32.OpenProcess(PROCESS_VM_READ | PROCESS_QUERY_INFORMATION, False, p)
            if not handle:
                return f"[ERROR] 无法打开进程 PID={p}"
            ReadProcessMemory = kernel32.ReadProcessMemory
            ReadProcessMemory.argtypes = [ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_void_p, ctypes.c_size_t, ctypes.POINTER(ctypes.c_size_t)]
            ReadProcessMemory.restype = ctypes.c_bool
            dumped = 0
            with open(out, 'wb') as f:
                for addr in range(0x10000, 0x7FFF0000, 0x10000):
                    buf = ctypes.create_string_buffer(0x10000)
                    read = ctypes.c_size_t()
                    if ReadProcessMemory(handle, addr, buf, 0x10000, ctypes.byref(read)):
                        if read.value > 0:
                            f.write(buf.raw[:read.value])
                            dumped += read.value
            kernel32.CloseHandle(handle)
            return f"[OK] 内存转储完成: {out} ({dumped/1024/1024:.1f} MB)"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 内存转储失败: {e}"


def _list_handles(self, pid: str) -> str:
        """列出进程打开的系统句柄"""
        try:
            p = int(pid)
            result = subprocess.run(['handle', '-p', str(p), '-nobanner'],
                                    capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
            if result.returncode == 0:
                return f"[HANDLES] PID={p}\n" + result.stdout[:3000]
            result2 = subprocess.run(['powershell', '-Command',
                         f'Get-Process -Id {p} | Select-Object -ExpandProperty Handles'],
                        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10)
            return f"[HANDLES] PID={p} (PowerShell)\n句柄数: {result2.stdout.strip()}"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 句柄枚举失败: {e}"


def _trace_syscalls(self, pid: str, duration: str = "5") -> str:
        """追踪进程的系统调用"""
        try:
            p = int(pid)
            dur = int(duration) if duration.isdigit() else 5
            result = subprocess.run(['powershell', '-Command',
                         f'Get-WinEvent -FilterHashtable @{{LogName="Microsoft-Windows-Kernel-Process/Operational"; TimeSpan=(New-TimeSpan -Seconds {dur})}} -MaxEvents 50 2>$null | Format-Table TimeCreated, Message -AutoSize'],
                        capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=dur+10)
            if result.stdout.strip():
                return f"[SYSTRACE] PID={p}, {dur}s\n" + result.stdout[:3000]
            return f"[SYSTRACE] PID={p}, {dur}s — 无ETW事件捕获（可能需要管理员权限或ETW提供者未启用）"
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] syscall追踪失败: {e}"


def _cancel_task(self, task_id: str) -> str:
        """取消后台任务"""
        tid = task_id.strip()
        if tid not in self._bg_tasks:
            return f"[ERROR] 任务不存在: {tid}"
        info = self._bg_tasks[tid]
        if info['status'] != 'running':
            return f"[WARN] 任务 {tid} 当前状态: {info['status']}，无法取消"
        info['status'] = 'cancelled'
        info['end_time'] = time.time()
        info['result'] = '[CANCELLED] 任务已被用户取消'
        return f"[OK] 任务 {tid} 已取消"


def _self_write_plugin(self, tool_name: str, code: str, description: str = '',
                           params: str = '', required: str = '', lifetime: str = 'long') -> str:
        """自我扩展：将 LLM 编写的插件源码注册为工具。long=写入 plugins/ 永久保留；short=仅内存注册，程序关闭即消失。"""
        import json as _json
        import re as _re
        try:
            tool_name = (tool_name or '').strip()
            if not _re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]{1,40}', tool_name):
                return f"[ERROR] 非法工具名: {tool_name!r}（须为英文标识符）"
            code = (code or '').strip()
            if not code:
                return "[ERROR] code 为空"
            try:
                import warnings as _w
                with _w.catch_warnings():
                    _w.simplefilter('ignore', SyntaxWarning)
                    compile(code, f'<plugin:{tool_name}>', 'exec')
            except SyntaxError as se:
                return f"[ERROR] 插件代码语法错误: line {se.lineno}: {se.msg}"
            expected = '_' + tool_name
            if not _re.search(r'def\s+%s\s*\(' % _re.escape(expected), code):
                m = _re.search(r'def\s+([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)\s*\(', code)
                if m:
                    code = code[:m.start()] + code[m.start():].replace('def ' + m.group(1) + '(', 'def ' + expected + '(', 1)
                else:
                    return "[ERROR] 插件代码中未找到函数定义（须有 def _%s）" % tool_name
            try:
                props = _json.loads(params) if isinstance(params, str) and params.strip() else {}
                if not isinstance(props, dict):
                    props = {}
            except Exception:
                props = {}
            if isinstance(required, str) and required.strip():
                try:
                    req = _json.loads(required)
                    if not isinstance(req, list):
                        req = [s.strip() for s in required.split(',') if s.strip()]
                except Exception:
                    req = [s.strip() for s in required.split(',') if s.strip()]
            else:
                req = []
            schema = {
                "type": "function",
                "function": {
                    "name": tool_name,
                    "description": description or ("Self-written plugin: %s" % tool_name),
                    "parameters": {"type": "object", "properties": props, "required": req}
                }
            }
            if lifetime == 'short':
                fpath = f'<memory:{tool_name}>'
            else:
                os.makedirs(self._plugin_dir, exist_ok=True)
                stamp = time.strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')
                fpath = os.path.join(self._plugin_dir, f'self_{tool_name}_{stamp}.plugin')
                with open(fpath, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    f.write("[SCHEMA]\n" + _json.dumps(schema, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
                            + "\n\n[CODE]\n" + code + "\n\n[MAP]\n" + tool_name + ": " + expected + "\n")
            existing = {t['function']['name'] for t in self.tools if 'function' in t}
            if tool_name in existing:
                self.tools = [t for t in self.tools if t.get('function', {}).get('name') != tool_name]
            self.tools.append(schema)
            ns = {}
            import warnings as _w
            with _w.catch_warnings():
                _w.simplefilter('ignore', SyntaxWarning)
                exec(compile(code, fpath, 'exec'), globals(), ns)
            bound = False
            for fname_in_ns, func_obj in ns.items():
                if callable(func_obj):
                    setattr(self, fname_in_ns, func_obj.__get__(self, type(self)))
                    if fname_in_ns == expected:
                        bound = True
            if not bound:
                return f"[WARN] 插件文件已写入 {fpath}，但未绑定到 {expected}（函数名不匹配）"
            self._plugin_map[tool_name] = expected
            self._plugin_lifetime[tool_name] = 'short' if lifetime == 'short' else 'long'
            if getattr(self, 'sched', None) is not None:
                try:
                    self.sched.register(schema, getattr(self, expected), source='plugin')
                    # 热注册必须顺手钉住：只 register 不 activate 的话，工具
                    # 只进了注册表、没进可见层，模型目录里看得见名字却调不动，
                    # 与下面那句"立即可用"完全不符。
                    self._dyn_pin([tool_name])
                except Exception:
                    pass
            self._dyn_catalog_cache = None
            self._dyn_catalog_cache_n = 0
            try:
                m = getattr(self, 'mesh', None)
                if m is not None:
                    m.ext.record(tool_name, code, schema, lifetime)
            except Exception:
                pass
            kind = '短期插件（内存注册，关闭程序即消失）' if lifetime == 'short' else '长期插件（已写入 plugins/ 目录，重启后自动加载）'
            return (f"[PLUGIN OK] {kind}：工具 {tool_name!r} 已注册并立即可用（共 {len(self.tools)} 个工具）。\n"
                    f"文件: {fpath}\n下一轮对话可直接调用 {tool_name}。")
        except Exception as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 插件注册失败: {e}"


def _self_list_plugins(self) -> str:
        """列出所有已注册的自定义插件。"""
        if not self._plugin_map:
            return "[PLUGIN] 当前没有已注册的自定义插件。plugins/ 目录: " + self._plugin_dir
        lines = ["[PLUGIN] 已注册 %d 个自定义插件:" % len(self._plugin_map)]
        for tname, mname in sorted(self._plugin_map.items()):
            tag = '短期' if self._plugin_lifetime.get(tname) == 'short' else '长期'
            lines.append(f"  [{tag}] {tname} -> {mname}")
        lines.append("插件目录: " + self._plugin_dir)
        return "\n".join(lines)


def _local_monitor_snapshot(self, sections: str = '', top_n: int = 5) -> str:
        """采样本机 CPU/内存/磁盘/网络/进程TOP，返回纯文本快照。"""
        if not LOCAL_MONITOR_CONFIG.get('enabled', True):
            return "[MONITOR] 功能已关闭（LOCAL_MONITOR_CONFIG['enabled']=False）"
        if not PSUTIL_AVAILABLE or psutil is None:
            return "[ERROR] psutil 不可用，无法采样本机状态"
        try:
            top_n = max(1, min(int(top_n or 5), LOCAL_MONITOR_CONFIG['max_top_n']))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            top_n = LOCAL_MONITOR_CONFIG['default_top_n']
        want = {s.strip().lower() for s in (sections or LOCAL_MONITOR_CONFIG['default_sections']).split(',') if s.strip()}
        out = ["=" * 50, "[本机实时快照] " + datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'), "=" * 50]
        try:
            if 'cpu' in want:
                per = psutil.cpu_percent(interval=LOCAL_MONITOR_CONFIG['cpu_sample_seconds'], percpu=True)
                out.append(f"CPU 总占用: {sum(per)/max(1,len(per)):.1f}%  (核数 {psutil.cpu_count(logical=True)})")
                cores = ' '.join(f"{p:.0f}" for p in per)
                out.append(f"CPU 分核: {cores}")
                freq = psutil.cpu_freq()
                if freq:
                    out.append(f"CPU 频率: 当前 {freq.current:.0f}MHz / 最大 {freq.max or 0:.0f}MHz")
            if 'mem' in want:
                m = psutil.virtual_memory()
                sw = psutil.swap_memory()
                out.append(f"内存: {m.percent}% 已用 ({m.used/2**30:.1f}/{m.total/2**30:.1f} GB)  交换: {sw.percent}%")
            if 'disk' in want:
                for p in psutil.disk_partitions(all=False):
                    if p.fstype:
                        try:
                            u = psutil.disk_usage(p.mountpoint)
                            out.append(f"磁盘 {p.device} [{p.fstype}]: {u.percent}% 已用 ({u.used/2**30:.0f}/{u.total/2**30:.0f} GB)")
                        except (PermissionError, OSError):
                            continue
            if 'net' in want:
                io1 = psutil.net_io_counters()
                time.sleep(0.2)
                io2 = psutil.net_io_counters()
                out.append(f"网络: ↑{(io2.bytes_sent-io1.bytes_sent)*5/1024:.0f}KB/s ↓{(io2.bytes_recv-io1.bytes_recv)*5/1024:.0f}KB/s  (累计 ↑{io2.bytes_sent/2**30:.2f}GB ↓{io2.bytes_recv/2**30:.2f}GB)")
                conns = psutil.net_connections(kind='inet')
                est = sum(1 for c in conns if c.status == 'ESTABLISHED')
                out.append(f"连接: ESTABLISHED {est} 个 / 总 {len(conns)} 个")
            if 'proc' in want:
                procs = []
                for pr in psutil.process_iter(['pid', 'name', 'cpu_percent', 'memory_percent']):
                    try:
                        procs.append(pr.info)
                    except (psutil.NoSuchProcess, psutil.AccessDenied):
                        continue
                top_cpu = sorted([p for p in procs if p['cpu_percent'] is not None],
                                 key=lambda x: x['cpu_percent'], reverse=True)[:top_n]
                out.append(f"进程数: {len(procs)}  CPU TOP{top_n}:")
                for p in top_cpu:
                    out.append(f"  [{p['pid']:>6}] {str(p['name'])[:28]:<28} CPU {p['cpu_percent']:.1f}%  MEM {p['memory_percent'] or 0:.1f}%")
        except Exception as e:
            out.append(f"[ERROR] 采样失败: {e}")
        out.append("=" * 50)
        return "\n".join(out)


def _local_index(self, action: str = 'search', root: str = '', query: str = '',
                     ext: str = '', limit: int = 10) -> str:
        """本地文件全文索引：build/search/stats，纯本地无网络。"""
        cfg = LOCAL_INDEX_CONFIG
        if not cfg.get('enabled', True):
            return "[INDEX] 功能已关闭（LOCAL_INDEX_CONFIG['enabled']=False）"
        action = (action or 'search').lower().strip()
        try:
            limit = max(1, min(int(limit or cfg['default_limit']), cfg['max_limit']))
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            limit = cfg['default_limit']
        if action == 'build':
            root = os.path.abspath(root) if root else os.path.expanduser('~')
            if not os.path.isdir(root):
                return f"[ERROR] 目录不存在: {root}"
            t0 = time.time()
            files = self._local_index_scan(root)
            data = {"root": root, "built_at": time.time(), "files": files}
            try:
                with open(self._local_index_path(), 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False)
            except OSError as e:
                return f"[ERROR] 索引写入失败: {e}"
            dt = time.time() - t0
            total_kb = sum(v['size'] for v in files.values()) / 1024
            return (f"[INDEX OK] 已索引 {len(files)} 个文件 ({total_kb:.0f} KB) 于 {root}\n"
                    f"耗时 {dt:.2f}s，索引文件: {self._local_index_path()}")
        ipath = self._local_index_path()
        if not os.path.exists(ipath):
            return "[ERROR] 尚未建立索引。请先调用 action=build（可指定 root 目录）"
        try:
            with open(ipath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                data = json.load(f)
        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as e:
            return f"[ERROR] 索引文件损坏: {e}。请重新 action=build"
        if action == 'stats':
            files = data.get('files', {})
            total_kb = sum(v.get('size', 0) for v in files.values()) / 1024
            built = datetime.fromtimestamp(data.get('built_at', 0)).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')
            ext_stat = {}
            for fp in files:
                e = os.path.splitext(fp)[1].lower()
                ext_stat[e] = ext_stat.get(e, 0) + 1
            top_ext = sorted(ext_stat.items(), key=lambda x: -x[1])[:8]
            lines = [f"[INDEX STATS] 根目录: {data.get('root')}",
                     f"建立时间: {built}  文件数: {len(files)}  总量: {total_kb:.0f} KB",
                     "扩展名分布: " + ', '.join(f"{e}×{n}" for e, n in top_ext)]
            return "\n".join(lines)
        query = (query or '').strip()
        if not query:
            return "[ERROR] action=search 需要 query 参数"
        q_lower = query.lower()
        ext_filter = {e.strip().lower() for e in (ext or '').replace('，', ',').split(',') if e.strip()}
        hits = []
        for fp, info in data.get('files', {}).items():
            if ext_filter and os.path.splitext(fp)[1].lower() not in ext_filter:
                continue
            snippet = info.get('snippet', '')
            fname_hit = 1 if q_lower in os.path.basename(fp).lower() else 0
            body_hit = snippet.lower().count(q_lower)
            if fname_hit == 0 and body_hit == 0:
                continue
            ctx = ''
            pos = snippet.lower().find(q_lower)
            if pos >= 0:
                s = max(0, pos - 40)
                ctx = snippet[s:pos + len(query) + 60].replace('\n', ' ').strip()
            score = fname_hit * 10 + min(body_hit, 20)
            hits.append((score, fp, body_hit, ctx))
        hits.sort(key=lambda x: -x[0])
        if not hits:
            return f"[INDEX] 未命中: {query!r}（索引 {len(data.get('files', {}))} 个文件）。可尝试 action=build 更新索引或换关键词"
        out = [f"[INDEX SEARCH] {query!r} → 命中 {len(hits)} 个文件，显示前 {min(limit, len(hits))}:"]
        for score, fp, n, ctx in hits[:limit]:
            out.append(f"\n[{os.path.basename(fp)}]  (命中 {n} 次, 相关度 {score})")
            out.append(f"  路径: {fp}")
            if ctx:
                out.append(f"  片段: ...{ctx}...")
        return "\n".join(out)
